### tubeshock/NOTES.md
     1	Tubeshock is a portrait tube-shooter prototype.
     2	Slide or drag along the lower play area to move the claw around the rim.
     3	The claw auto-fires; press SURGE to clear enemies near the rim.
     4	Keyboard: arrows/WASD move, Space or Enter activates SURGE.
     5	Reach higher levels, protect 3 lives, and beat the best score saved on-device.

### stacklock/NOTES.md
     1	STACKLOCK
     2	Controls: tap left/right board halves to shift, tap board or ROTATE to turn, swipe down to drop, swipe up to hold.
     3	Keyboard: arrows/WASD move, Up/W/Z rotate, Space hard drops, C holds, Enter restarts.
     4	Loop: clear lines to level up in Marathon, or clear 40 lines for the fastest Sprint time.
     5	Best Marathon score and Sprint time persist locally in the browser.

### mazerunner-prime/NOTES.md
     1	Swipe anywhere to queue a turn; arrow keys and WASD also work.
     2	Collect every dot to advance into a faster seeded maze.
     3	Power nodes make the quartet flee for 8 seconds; touch them while glowing to score.
     4	Collect orange data shards for bonus points; you have three lives.
     5	Tap the run-ended card or press Enter/Space to restart; best score is saved locally.

### mazerunner-prime/index.html
     1	<!doctype html>
     2	<html lang="en">

### corridor-crawl/NOTES.md
     1	# Corridor Crawl
     2	
     3	Controls: tap an adjacent tile to move or bump-attack, tap yourself to wait, tap a bottom-bar slot to use an item, long-press any tile or slot to inspect. Desktop: arrows/WASD/QEZC/hjkl to move, space or `.` to wait, 1-6 to use items, M to mute, R to restart.
     4	Loop: seeded rooms-and-corridors floors with fog of war; fight six monster types (pack rats, a splitting ooze, a ranged archer, an adjacent-only stalker, a shoving brute, a thief that steals and flees); potions and scrolls stay unidentified until used; hunger slows regeneration and then bites.
     5	Goal: step on the down stairs to descend, take the Crown of Echoes on depth 8, then climb all the way back out through an angrier dungeon.
     6	Fail/win: permadeath — score is depth x10 + kills x3 + gold (+100 for escaping); best score persists in localStorage; tap anywhere on the end screen to restart instantly.

### spire-ascent/NOTES.md
     1	# Spire Ascent
     2	Controls: TAP anywhere = jump (hold = higher); DOUBLE-TAP in mid-air = dash — dash into a wall for a wall-kick. Desktop: Space/W/Up/Enter (hold), double-press to dash, A/D or arrows nudge direction, R restarts.
     3	Loop: you auto-run and flip direction off the walls; climb seeded tower rows of ledges, crumblers, springs, movers, wind zones and spike bands while the lava doom line rises faster over time.
     4	Scoring: score = climb height + combo bonus for consecutive landings on non-crumbling platforms; falling into the lava or touching spikes ends the run.
     5	Persistence: best score in localStorage for ENDLESS and separately for DAILY SEED (mode toggled on the game-over screen); tap anywhere to restart instantly.

### bulwark/NOTES.md
     1	# Bulwark
     2	Touch: tap an empty cell, then a chip (WALL/ARROW/FROST/SPLASH/ZAP/BANK) to build; double-tap a cell to rebuild the last type; tap a tower for upgrade/sell; tap a wall to refund it; GO calls the wave early for bonus gold.
     3	Keys: arrows/WASD move cursor, 1-6 pick chip, space/enter build or open sheet, U upgrade, X sell, G go, M mute, R restart.
     4	Loop: walls and towers reroute live pathfinding, so maze the creeps into your guns. Banked gold earns interest between waves; banks add more.
     5	Fliers ignore the maze, tanks have armour, shielded regenerate, a boss lands every 10th wave.
     6	Fail at 20 leaks, win at wave 30 (endless continues). Score = waves + lives left, best kept in localStorage.

### dirt-rocket/NOTES.md
     1	# Dirt Rocket
     2	Side-view motocross physics racer. Landscape, touch-first, no build step.
     3	CONTROLS: hold anywhere on the right half = GAS; BOOST bubble (right) while the heat ring is below redline; BRAKE circle (bottom-left); drag up/down anywhere on the left half = rider lean (wheelies + air rotation). Keys: Right/D gas, Left/A brake, Up-Down/W-S lean, Shift/Space boost, R restart, Enter continue.
     4	LOOP: 3 seeded tracks per event, times summed. Land flat for a speed kick, sloppy = wobble, nose-first = crash (2s remount). Wheelie distance buys seconds off.
     5	WIN: finish all 3 tracks for a bronze/silver/gold medal vs par; best event total is saved to localStorage.

### vector-storm/NOTES.md
     1	# Vector Storm
     2	
     3	Twin-stick arena shooter. Boots straight into play; no build step, no deps.
     4	Controls: drag anywhere on the LEFT half to move, RIGHT half to aim + auto-fire; BOMB button clears the screen. Keyboard: WASD move, arrow keys aim/fire, Space/B bomb, any key restarts.
     5	Loop: seeded spawn grammar escalates waves of drifters, dodging weavers, segmented snakes, mini-emitting spawners, and gravity wells. Kills drop crystals; every 8 collected raises the score multiplier, which resets on death.
     6	Fail: 3 lives, then a score screen with best persisted to localStorage.

### serpentine/NOTES.md
     1	Serpentine is a portrait snake/light-cycle arena survival game.
     2	Swipe horizontally or vertically to turn; arrow keys and WASD also work.
     3	Collect charge pips to grow, avoid every wall, and outlive the rival hunters.
     4	Closed gates, speed pads, and a shrinking arena make each seeded round tighter.
     5	Tap or press Space/Enter after a crash to restart; best score persists locally.

### crossfire-hopper/NOTES.md
     1	Controls: swipe or drag up/down/left/right; tap the on-screen arrows; keyboard arrows/WASD also work.
     2	Loop: hop across procedural roads, rivers, rail lines, and safe medians while climbing upward.
     3	Rivers need a log or lily pad; warning lights mean a train is coming, and coins add to your score.
     4	Keep moving or a swooping sky predator will catch you; squash or drown ends the run.
     5	Best score persists locally; after a fail, tap, swipe, or press any direction for an instant restart.

    59	  <script>
    60	  (() => {
    61	    'use strict';
    62	
    63	    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    64	    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
    65	    const BASE_W = 390;
    66	    const BASE_H = 700;
    67	    const TOP_H = 72;
    68	    const ROW_H = 62;
    69	    const PLAYER_ANCHOR = 530;
    70	    const GRID_LEFT = 13;
    71	    const CELL_W = 52;
    72	    const COLS = 7;
    73	    const BOARD_W = CELL_W * COLS;
    74	    const BEST_KEY = 'crossfire-hopper-best-v1';
    75	
    76	    let cssScale = 1;
    77	    let renderScale = 1;
    78	    let cameraRow = 0;
    79	    let lastTime = 0;
    80	    let elapsed = 0;
    81	    let distance = 0;
    82	    let coins = 0;
    83	    let best = readBest();
    84	    let idle = 0;
    85	    let shake = 0;
    86	    let flash = 0;
    87	    let state = 'running';
    88	    let rows = new Map();
    89	    let particles = [];
    90	    let eagle = null;
    91	    let audioContext = null;
    92	    let pointer = null;
    93	    let player;
    94	
    95	    const colors = {
    96	      ink: '#07111f',
    97	      ink2: '#0c1a2b',
    98	      mint: '#74f3b0',
    99	      mint2: '#c0ffe0',
   100	      yellow: '#ffd668',
   101	      coral: '#ff756e',
   102	      water: '#17657c',
   103	      water2: '#238ba0',
   104	      road: '#293442',
   105	      rail: '#343847',
   106	      median: '#21584f'
   107	    };
   108	
   109	    const buttons = {
   110	      up: { x: 165, y: 590, w: 60, h: 52, dx: 0, dy: 1 },
   111	      left: { x: 99, y: 646, w: 60, h: 48, dx: -1, dy: 0 },
   112	      down: { x: 165, y: 646, w: 60, h: 48, dx: 0, dy: -1 },
   113	      right: { x: 231, y: 646, w: 60, h: 48, dx: 1, dy: 0 }
   114	    };
   115	
   116	    function readBest() {
   117	      try { return Number(localStorage.getItem(BEST_KEY)) || 0; }
   118	      catch (_) { return 0; }
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    function saveBest(value) {
   122	      best = Math.max(best, value);
   123	      try { localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(best)); }
   124	      catch (_) { /* Storage may be unavailable in private frames. */ }
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    function hash(value) {
   128	      const x = Math.sin(value * 127.371 + 19.17) * 43758.5453;
   129	      return x - Math.floor(x);
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    function wrap(value, width = BASE_W) {
   133	      return ((value % width) + width) % width;
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    function wrappedDistance(a, b, width = BASE_W) {
   137	      const d = Math.abs(a - b) % width;
   138	      return Math.min(d, width - d);
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    function cellX(col) {
   142	      return GRID_LEFT + col * CELL_W + CELL_W / 2;
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    function nearestCol(x) {
   146	      let found = 0;
   147	      let nearest = Infinity;
   148	      for (let col = 0; col < COLS; col++) {
   149	        const d = Math.abs(cellX(col) - x);
   150	        if (d < nearest) { nearest = d; found = col; }
   151	      }
   152	      return found;
   153	    }
   154	
   155	    function roundedRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   156	      const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   157	      ctx.beginPath();
   158	      ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
   159	      ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius);
   160	      ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius);
   161	      ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius);
   162	      ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius);
   163	      ctx.closePath();
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
   167	
   168	    function rowScreenY(rowIndex) {
   169	      return PLAYER_ANCHOR - (rowIndex - cameraRow) * ROW_H;
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    function makeRow(index) {
   173	      if (rows.has(index)) return rows.get(index);
   174	      const r = { index, type: 'road', vehicles: [], supports: [], coin: null };
   175	      const n = hash(index * 4.17 + 2.3);
   176	      const previous = rows.get(index - 1);
   177	
   178	      if (index === 0 || index % 7 === 0 || index % 13 === 0) {
   179	        r.type = 'median';
   180	      } else if (previous && previous.type === 'river' && n < .68) {
   181	        r.type = 'road';
   182	      } else if (n < .26) {
   183	        r.type = 'river';
   184	      } else if (n < .41) {
   185	        r.type = 'rail';
   186	      } else {
   187	        r.type = 'road';
   188	      }
   189	
   190	      if (r.type === 'road') {
   191	        const count = 2 + Math.floor(hash(index * 2.8) * 2);
   192	        const direction = hash(index * 7.11) > .5 ? 1 : -1;
   193	        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   194	          const truck = hash(index * 10 + i * 3.1) > .68;
   195	          r.vehicles.push({
   196	            x: hash(index * 31 + i * 11) * BASE_W,
   197	            width: truck ? 58 : 40 + hash(index * 17 + i) * 12,
   198	            height: truck ? 25 : 21,
   199	            speed: direction * (38 + hash(index * 5.7 + i) * 48),
   200	            truck
   201	          });
   202	        }
   203	      }
   204	
   205	      if (r.type === 'river') {
   206	        r.current = (hash(index * 8.13) > .5 ? 1 : -1) * (7 + hash(index * 3.4) * 13);
   207	        const count = 4;
   208	        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   209	          const pad = hash(index * 20 + i * 4.9) > .64;
   210	          r.supports.push({
   211	            x: -45 + i * 101 + hash(index * 18 + i) * 45,
   212	            width: pad ? 42 + hash(index + i * 8) * 16 : 67 + hash(index + i * 8) * 41,
   213	            speed: r.current * (.65 + hash(index * 7 + i) * .7),
   214	            pad,
   215	            phase: hash(index * 12 + i * 2.6) * 6.28
   216	          });
   217	        }
   218	      }
   219	
   220	      if (r.type === 'rail') {
   221	        r.train = {
   222	          direction: hash(index * 1.7) > .5 ? 1 : -1,
   223	          phase: hash(index * 9.6) * 6,
   224	          speed: .86 + hash(index * 2.2) * .5
   225	        };
   226	      }
   227	
   228	      if (r.type !== 'median' && hash(index * 14.6 + 4) > .31) {
   229	        r.coin = {
   230	          col: Math.floor(hash(index * 25.3 + 8) * COLS),
   231	          phase: hash(index * 9.2) * 6.28,
   232	          collected: false
   233	        };
   234	      }
   235	
   236	      rows.set(index, r);
   237	      return r;
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    function ensureRows(maxIndex) {
   241	      for (let i = 0; i <= maxIndex; i++) makeRow(i);
   242	    }
   243

   245	      rows = new Map();

    29	  let active = null;
    30	  let queue = [];
    31	  let hold = null;
    32	  let holdUsed = false;
    33	  let bag = [];
    34	  let score = 0;
    35	  let lines = 0;
    36	  let level = 1;
    37	  let dropClock = 0;
    38	  let startedAt = 0;
    39	  let elapsed = 0;
    40	  let lastFrame = performance.now();
    41	  let running = true;
    42	  let result = '';
    43	  let shake = 0;
    44	  let flash = 0;
    45	  let particles = [];
    46	  let layout = { x: 0, y: 0, cell: 20, boardW: 200, boardH: 400, sideX: 220, sideW: 100 };
    47	  let pointer = null;
    48	
    49	  const key = (suffix) => `stacklock:${suffix}`;
    50	  const bestScore = () => Number(localStorage.getItem(key('marathon-best')) || 0);
    51	  const bestTime = () => Number(localStorage.getItem(key('sprint-best')) || 0);
    52	
    53	  function emptyBoard() { return Array.from({ length: ROWS }, () => Array(COLS).fill(null)); }
    54	
    55	  function cloneMatrix(matrix) { return matrix.map(row => row.slice()); }
    56	
    57	  function rotateMatrix(matrix) {
    58	    const h = matrix.length;
    59	    const w = matrix[0].length;
    60	    const rotated = Array.from({ length: w }, () => Array(h).fill(0));
    61	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) rotated[x][h - 1 - y] = matrix[y][x];
    62	    return rotated;
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  function shuffledBag() {
    66	    const next = PIECES.map((_, i) => i);
    67	    for (let i = next.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    68	      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    69	      [next[i], next[j]] = [next[j], next[i]];
    70	    }
    71	    return next;
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  function nextType() {
    75	    if (!bag.length) bag = shuffledBag();
    76	    return bag.pop();
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  function makePiece(type) {
    80	    const shape = cloneMatrix(PIECES[type].shape);
    81	    return { type, shape, x: Math.floor((COLS - shape[0].length) / 2), y: 0 };
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  function fillQueue() { while (queue.length < 3) queue.push(nextType()); }
    85	
    86	  function beginGame(nextMode = mode) {
    87	    mode = nextMode;
    88	    board = emptyBoard();
    89	    bag = [];
    90	    queue = [];
    91	    fillQueue();
    92	    hold = null;
    93	    holdUsed = false;
    94	    score = 0;
    95	    lines = 0;
    96	    level = 1;
    97	    dropClock = 0;
    98	    elapsed = 0;
    99	    startedAt = performance.now();
   100	    running = true;
   101	    result = '';
   102	    shake = 0;
   103	    flash = 0;
   104	    particles = [];
   105	    spawnPiece();
   106	    statusLine.textContent = mode === 'sprint' ? 'Clear 40 lines as fast as you can.' : 'Build a clean stack.';
   107	    updateHud();
   108	    canvas.focus({ preventScroll: true });
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  function spawnPiece(type = queue.shift()) {
   112	    fillQueue();
   113	    active = makePiece(type);
   114	    fillQueue();
   115	    holdUsed = false;
   116	    if (collides(active, 0, 0, active.shape)) finish('STACK FULL');
   117	  }
   118	
   119	  function collides(piece, dx = 0, dy = 0, shape = piece.shape) {
   120	    for (let y = 0; y < shape.length; y++) {
   121	      for (let x = 0; x < shape[y].length; x++) {
   122	        if (!shape[y][x]) continue;
   123	        const nx = piece.x + x + dx;
   124	        const ny = piece.y + y + dy;
   125	        if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny >= ROWS) return true;
   126	        if (ny >= 0 && board[ny][nx]) return true;
   127	      }
   128	    }
   129	    return false;
   130	  }
   131	
   132	  function move(dx) {
   133	    if (!running || !active) return false;
   134	    if (!collides(active, dx, 0)) { active.x += dx; return true; }
   135	    return false;
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  function rotate() {
   139	    if (!running || !active) return;
   140	    const turned = rotateMatrix(active.shape);
   141	    const kicks = [0, -1, 1, -2, 2];
   142	    for (const kick of kicks) {
   143	      if (!collides(active, kick, 0, turned)) {
   144	        active.shape = turned;
   145	        active.x += kick;
   146	        flash = Math.max(flash, .12);
   147	        return;
   148	      }
   149	    }
   150	  }
   151	
   152	  function softDrop() {
   153	    if (!running || !active) return;
   154	    if (!collides(active, 0, 1)) { active.y++; score++; dropClock = 0; updateHud(); }
   155	    else lockPiece();
   156	  }
   157	
   158	  function hardDrop() {
   159	    if (!running || !active) return;
   160	    let distance = 0;
   161	    while (!collides(active, 0, 1)) { active.y++; distance++; }
   162	    score += distance * 2;
   163	    shake = Math.max(shake, Math.min(6, 2 + distance * .16));
   164	    lockPiece();
   165	  }
   166	
   167	  function holdPiece() {
   168	    if (!running || !active || holdUsed) return;
   169	    const currentType = active.type;
   170	    if (hold === null) { hold = currentType; spawnPiece(); }
   171	    else { const heldType = hold; hold = currentType; active = makePiece(heldType); if (collides(active)) finish('STACK FULL'); }
   172	    holdUsed = true;
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  function lockPiece() {
   176	    if (!running || !active) return;
   177	    for (let y = 0; y < active.shape.length; y++) {
   178	      for (let x = 0; x < active.shape[y].length; x++) {
   179	        if (!active.shape[y][x]) continue;
   180	        const by = active.y + y;
   181	        const bx = active.x + x;
   182	        if (by < 0) return finish('STACK FULL');
   183	        board[by][bx] = active.type;
   184	      }
   185	    }
   186	    const cleared = clearLines();
   187	    if (cleared) {
   188	      const weights = [0, 100, 300, 500, 800];
   189	      score += weights[cleared] * level;
   190	      lines += cleared;
   191	      level = Math.floor(lines / 10) + 1;
   192	      flash = .42;
   193	      shake = Math.min(13, 4 + cleared * 2.8);
   194	      emitLineParticles(cleared);
   195	      if (mode === 'sprint' && lines >= 40) return finish('SPRINT CLEAR');
   196	    }
   197	    spawnPiece();
   198	    updateHud();
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  function clearLines() {
   202	    const full = [];
   203	    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) if (board[y].every(Boolean)) full.push(y);
   204	    if (!full.length) return 0;
   205	    for (let i = full.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) board.splice(full[i], 1);
   206	    while (board.length < ROWS) board.unshift(Array(COLS).fill(null));
   207	    return full.length;
   208	  }
   209	
   210	  function emitLineParticles(count) {
   211	    const yBase = layout.y + layout.boardH - Math.max(0, count - 1) * layout.cell;
   212	    for (let i = 0; i < count * 18; i++) {
   213	      particles.push({
   214	        x: layout.x + Math.random() * layout.boardW,
   215	        y: yBase + (Math.random() - .5) * layout.cell * count,
   216	        vx: (Math.random() - .5) * 160,
   217	        vy: -40 - Math.random() * 170,
   218	        life: .35 + Math.random() * .5,
   219	        max: .85,
   220	        size: 1 + Math.random() * 3,
   221	        color: ['#6be7bb', '#f6cf57', '#b48cff', '#60d8ee'][i % 4]
   222	      });
   223	    }
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  function finish(message) {
   227	    if (!running) return;
   228	    running = false;
   229	    result = message;
   230	    if (mode === 'marathon') {
   231	      if (score > bestScore()) localStorage.setItem(key('marathon-best'), String(score));
   232	      statusLine.textContent = message === 'SPRINT CLEAR' ? 'New run ready.' : 'Tap the board or press enter to restart.';
   233	    } else {
   234	      if (message === 'SPRINT CLEAR' && (!bestTime() || elapsed < bestTime())) localStorage.setItem(key('sprint-best'), String(elapsed));
   235	      statusLine.textContent = message === 'SPRINT CLEAR' ? 'Tap the board or press enter to run again.' : 'Tap the board or press enter to retry.';
   236	    }
   237	    updateHud();
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  function gravityMs() { return Math.max(85, 720 - (level - 1) * 54); }
   241	
   242	  function updateHud() {
   243	    metricLabel.textContent = mode === 'marathon' ? 'SCORE' : 'TIME';
   244	    metricValue.textContent = mode === 'marathon' ? String(score).padStart(5, '0') : formatTime(elapsed);
   245	    secondaryLabel.textContent = mode === 'marathon' ? 'BEST' : 'BEST TIME';
   246	    secondaryValue.textContent = mode === 'marathon' ? String(bestScore()).padStart(5, '0') : (bestTime() ? formatTime(bestTime()) : '--:--.--');
   247	    levelValue.textContent = mode === 'sprint' ? `${Math.min(lines, 40)}/40` : String(level);
   248	  }
   249	
   250	  function formatTime(ms) {
   251	    const centis = Math.floor(ms / 10) % 100;
   252	    const seconds = Math.floor(ms / 1000) % 60;
   253	    const minutes = Math.floor(ms / 60000);
   254	    return `${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(centis).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   255	  }
   256	
   257	  function resize() {
   258	    const rect = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
   259	    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   260	    const cap = 960;
   261	    const scale = Math.min(dpr, cap / Math.max(rect.width, rect.height));
   262	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(rect.width * scale));
   263	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(rect.height * scale));
   264	    ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
   265	    const w = rect.width;
   266	    const h = rect.height;
   267	    const sideW = Math.min(112, Math.max(91, w * .275));
   268	    const usableBoardW = w - sideW - 25;
   269	    const cell = Math.max(13, Math.floor(Math.min(usableBoardW / COLS, (h - 25) / ROWS)));
   270	    const boardW = cell * COLS;
   271	    const boardH = cell * ROWS;
   272	    const x = Math.max(8, (w - (boardW + sideW + 15)) / 2);
   273	    const y = Math.max(10, (h - boardH) / 2);
   274	    layout = { x, y, cell, boardW, boardH, sideX: x + boardW + 15, sideW };
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  function roundedRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   278	    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   279	    ctx.beginPath();
   280	    ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
   281	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius);
   282	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius);
   283	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius);
   284	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius);
   285	    ctx.closePath();
   286	  }
   287	
   288	  function draw() {
   289	    const w = stage.clientWidth;
   290	    const h = stage.clientHeight;
   291	    ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / w, 0, 0, canvas.height / h, 0, 0);
   292	    const pulse = Math.sin(elapsed * .003) * .5 + .5;
   293	    const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h);
   294	    bg.addColorStop(0, '#0b1729'); bg.addColorStop(.54, '#091321'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#10192b');
   295	    ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   296	    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(w * .32, h * .1, 0, w * .32, h * .1, w * .78);
   297	    glow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(107,231,187,${.075 + pulse * .02})`); glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(107,231,187,0)');
   298	    ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   299	
   300	    const offsetX = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake : 0;
   301	    const offsetY = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake : 0;
   302	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(offsetX, offsetY);
   303	    drawBoard();
   304	    drawSidePanel();
   305	    ctx.restore();
   306	    drawParticles(w, h);
   307	    if (!running) drawResult(w, h);
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  function drawBoard() {
   311	    const { x, y, cell, boardW, boardH } = layout;
   312	    ctx.save();
   313	    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.35)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22; ctx.shadowOffsetY = 8;
   314	    roundedRect(x - 4, y - 4, boardW + 8, boardH + 8, 13);
   315	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 10, 19, .78)'; ctx.fill();
   316	    ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
   317	    roundedRect(x - 3, y - 3, boardW + 6, boardH + 6, 12);
   318	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(145, 181, 220, .18)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   319	    for (let row = 0; row < ROWS; row++) {
   320	      for (let col = 0; col < COLS; col++) {
   321	        const gx = x + col * cell, gy = y + row * cell;
   322	        ctx.fillStyle = (row + col) % 2 ? 'rgba(28, 48, 76, .22)' : 'rgba(19, 36, 60, .22)';
   323	        ctx.fillRect(gx + 1, gy + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2);
   324	        const tile = board[row][col];
   325	        if (tile !== null) drawTile(gx, gy, cell, PIECES[tile], 1);
   326	      }
   327	    }
   328	    if (active && running) {
   329	      const ghostY = ghostLanding();
   330	      if (ghostY !== active.y) drawPiece(active, ghostY, .14, true);
   331	      drawPiece(active, active.y, 1, false);
   332	    }
   333	    if (flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(219,255,244,${flash * .16})`; ctx.fillRect(x, y, boardW, boardH); }
   334	    ctx.restore();
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  function ghostLanding() {
   338	    let dy = 0;
   339	    while (!collides(active, 0, dy + 1)) dy++;
   340	    return active.y + dy;
   341	  }
   342	
   343	  function drawPiece(piece, yOverride, alpha, ghost) {
   344	    const { x, y, cell } = layout;
   345	    const data = PIECES[piece.type];
   346	    for (let py = 0; py < piece.shape.length; py++) for (let px = 0; px < piece.shape[py].length; px++) {
   347	      if (piece.shape[py][px]) drawTile(x + (piece.x + px) * cell, y + (yOverride + py) * cell, cell, data, alpha, ghost);
   348	    }
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  function drawTile(x, y, size, data, alpha = 1, ghost = false) {
   352	    const pad = Math.max(1.5, size * .075);
   353	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
   354	    if (ghost) {
   355	      roundedRect(x + pad, y + pad, size - pad * 2, size - pad * 2, size * .18);
   356	      ctx.fillStyle = data.color; ctx.fill();
   357	      ctx.strokeStyle = data.color; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size * .07); ctx.stroke();
   358	      ctx.restore(); return;
   359	    }
   360	    ctx.shadowColor = data.color; ctx.shadowBlur = size * .18;
   361	    roundedRect(x + pad, y + pad, size - pad * 2, size - pad * 2, size * .18);
   362	    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + size, y + size);
   363	    grad.addColorStop(0, data.glow); grad.addColorStop(.2, data.color); grad.addColorStop(1, shade(data.color, -.22));
   364	    ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
   365	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.26)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size * .035); ctx.stroke();
   366	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.2)';
   367	    roundedRect(x + pad * 2, y + pad * 1.6, size - pad * 4, Math.max(1.5, size * .08), size * .04); ctx.fill();
   368	    ctx.restore();
   369	  }
   370	
   371	  function shade(hex, amount) {
   372	    const value = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
   373	    const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, (value >> 16) + 255 * amount));
   374	    const g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, ((value >> 8) & 255) + 255 * amount));
   375	    const b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, (value & 255) + 255 * amount));
   376	    return `rgb(${r|0},${g|0},${b|0})`;
   377	  }
   378	
   379	  function drawSidePanel() {
   380	    const { sideX, sideW, y, boardH, cell } = layout;
   381	    const labelSize = Math.max(7, Math.min(9, cell * .34));
   382	    drawMiniPanel(sideX, y + 1, sideW, Math.min(boardH * .23, 112), 'HOLD', hold, true, labelSize);
   383	    drawMiniPanel(sideX, y + Math.min(boardH * .23, 112) + 12, sideW, Math.min(boardH * .49, 220), 'NEXT', queue, false, labelSize);
   384	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(167, 188, 216, .52)'; ctx.font = `700 ${labelSize}px system-ui`; ctx.letterSpacing = '.12em';
   385	    ctx.fillText(mode === 'sprint' ? `${Math.min(lines,40)} / 40 LINES` : `${lines} LINES`, sideX + 4, y + boardH - 26);
   386	  }
   387	
   388	  function drawMiniPanel(x, y, w, h, label, content, isHold, labelSize) {
   389	    roundedRect(x, y, w, h, 10); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(23, 39, 65, .55)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(145,181,220,.14)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   390	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(173, 197, 226, .63)'; ctx.font = `800 ${labelSize}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText(label, x + 7, y + 14);
   391	    if (isHold) { if (content !== null) drawMiniPiece(content, x + w / 2, y + h * .61, Math.min(15, w / 7)); return; }
   392	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (content[i] !== undefined) drawMiniPiece(content[i], x + w / 2, y + 31 + i * Math.min(48, h / 3.2), Math.min(11.5, w / 9));
   393	  }
   394	
   395	  function drawMiniPiece(type, cx, cy, cell) {
   396	    const shape = PIECES[type].shape;
   397	    const width = shape[0].length * cell, height = shape.length * cell;
   398	    for (let py = 0; py < shape.length; py++) for (let px = 0; px < shape[py].length; px++) if (shape[py][px]) drawTile(cx - width / 2 + px * cell, cy - height / 2 + py * cell, cell, PIECES[type], .95);
   399	  }
   400	
   401	  function drawParticles(w, h) {
   402	    ctx.save();
   403	    for (const particle of particles) {
   404	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, particle.life / particle.max);
   405	      ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
   406	      ctx.fillRect(particle.x, particle.y, particle.size, particle.size);
   407	    }
   408	    ctx.restore();
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  function drawResult(w, h) {
   412	    ctx.save();
   413	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 10, 19, .64)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   414	    const cx = w * .5, cy = h * .48;
   415	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   416	    ctx.fillStyle = result === 'SPRINT CLEAR' ? '#6be7bb' : '#f6cf57'; ctx.font = `850 ${Math.max(24, Math.min(38, w * .105))}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText(result, cx, cy - 13);
   417	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(235,244,255,.82)'; ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(9, Math.min(12, w * .03))}px system-ui`;
   418	    ctx.fillText(mode === 'marathon' ? `${score.toLocaleString()} POINTS` : formatTime(elapsed), cx, cy + 19);
   419	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(190,211,238,.58)'; ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(8, Math.min(10, w * .025))}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText('TAP TO RESTART', cx, cy + 47);
   420	    ctx.restore();
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function update(dt, now) {
   424	    if (running) {
   425	      elapsed = now - startedAt;
   426	      dropClock += dt;
   427	      if (dropClock >= gravityMs()) { dropClock = 0; if (!collides(active, 0, 1)) active.y++; else lockPiece(); }
   428	      updateHud();
   429	    }
   430	    shake *= Math.pow(.04, dt / 1000);
   431	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt / 1000);
   432	    for (const particle of particles) { particle.life -= dt / 1000; particle.x += particle.vx * dt / 1000; particle.y += particle.vy * dt / 1000; particle.vy += 260 * dt / 1000; }
   433	    particles = particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   434	  }
   435	
   436	  function loop(now) {
   437	    const dt = Math.min(50, now - lastFrame); lastFrame = now;
   438	    update(dt, now); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function handleAction(action) {
   442	    if (!running) { beginGame(mode); return; }
   443	    if (action === 'hold') holdPiece();
   444	    if (action === 'rotate') rotate();
   445	    if (action === 'drop') hardDrop();
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => handleAction(button.dataset.action)));
   449	  modeButtons.forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
   450	    modeButtons.forEach(item => item.classList.toggle('active', item === button));
   451	    beginGame(button.dataset.mode);
   452	  }));
   453	
   454	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
   455	    const k = event.key.toLowerCase();
   456	    if (['arrowleft','arrowright','arrowdown','arrowup',' ','c','z','a','d','s','w','enter'].includes(k)) event.preventDefault();
   457	    if (!running && (k === 'enter' || k === ' ')) return beginGame(mode);
   458	    if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') move(-1);
   459	    else if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') move(1);
   460	    else if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') softDrop();
   461	    else if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w' || k === 'z') rotate();
   462	    else if (k === ' ') hardDrop();
   463	    else if (k === 'c') holdPiece();
   464	  }, { passive: false });
   465	
   466	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
   467	    event.preventDefault();
   468	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   469	    pointer = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, t: performance.now() };
   470	  });
   471	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', (event) => {
   472	    event.preventDefault();
   473	    if (!pointer) return;
   474	    const dx = event.clientX - pointer.x, dy = event.clientY - pointer.y, dt = performance.now() - pointer.t;
   475	    pointer = null;
   476	    if (!running) return beginGame(mode);
   477	    const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   478	    if (distance > 24 && dy < -24) holdPiece();
   479	    else if (distance > 24 && dy > 24) { if (dt < 260 || Math.abs(dy) > 130) hardDrop(); else softDrop(); }
   480	    else if (Math.abs(dx) > 22) move(dx < 0 ? -1 : 1);
   481	    else {
   482	      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   483	      const localX = event.clientX - rect.left;
   484	      const localY = event.clientY - rect.top;
   485	      if (activePieceContains(localX, localY)) rotate();
   486	      else if (localX < rect.width * .5) move(-1); else move(1);
   487	    }
   488	  });
   489	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointer = null; });
   490	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   491	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   492	
   493	  function activePieceContains(localX, localY) {
   494	    if (!active) return false;
   495	    const boardX = Math.floor((localX - layout.x) / layout.cell);
   496	    const boardY = Math.floor((localY - layout.y) / layout.cell);
   497	    const px = boardX - active.x;
   498	    const py = boardY - active.y;
   499	    return py >= 0 && py < active.shape.length && px >= 0 && px < active.shape[py].length && Boolean(active.shape[py][px]);
   500	  }
   501	
   502	  resize();
   503	  beginGame('marathon');
   504	  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   505	})();

    57	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)); }
    58	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    59	  function wrap(v, n) { return ((v % n) + n) % n; }
    60	  function shortestLane(a, b) {
    61	    let d = a - b;
    62	    while (d > LANES / 2) d -= LANES;
    63	    while (d < -LANES / 2) d += LANES;
    64	    return d;

    90	  function tubeMetrics() {
    91	    return { cx: W * .5, cy: H * .39, rx: Math.min(W * .44, H * .43), ry: Math.min(W * .27, H * .24) };
    92	  }

    94	  function laneAngle(lane) {
    95	    if (geometryName() === 'OPEN ARC') return -Math.PI * .88 + (clamp(lane, 0, LANES - 1) / (LANES - 1)) * Math.PI * 1.55;
    96	    return -Math.PI * .5 + (wrap(lane, LANES) / LANES) * TAU;
    97	  }

    99	  function geometryName() { return geometries[(level - 1) % geometries.length]; }

   101	  function project(t, lane, extraAngle) {
   102	    const m = tubeMetrics();
   103	    const p = ease(clamp(t, 0, 1.12));
   104	    const a = laneAngle(lane) + (extraAngle || 0);
   105	    const deep = geometryName() === 'RIBBON';
   106	    let rx = lerp(18, m.rx, p);
   107	    let ry = lerp(12, m.ry, p);
   108	    let x = m.cx + Math.cos(a) * rx;
   109	    let y = m.cy + p * H * .16 + Math.sin(a) * ry;
   110	    if (geometryName() === 'STAR') {
   111	      const tooth = 1 + Math.cos(a * 5 + level * .48) * .12 * p;
   112	      x = m.cx + Math.cos(a) * rx * tooth;
   113	      y = m.cy + p * H * .16 + Math.sin(a) * ry * tooth;
   114	    } else if (deep) {
   115	      y += Math.sin(a * 2 + p * 4) * 11 * p;
   116	      x += Math.sin(a + p * 2) * 5 * p;
   117	      ry *= .64;
   118	      y = m.cy + p * H * .16 + Math.sin(a) * ry;
   119	    }
   120	    return { x: x, y: y };
   121	  }

   123	  function traceRing(t, openExtra) {
   124	    const name = geometryName();
   125	    const segments = name === 'OPEN ARC' ? 34 : 40;
   126	    const m = tubeMetrics();
   127	    const p = ease(clamp(t, 0, 1.12));
   128	    const start = name === 'OPEN ARC' ? -Math.PI * .88 : -Math.PI * .5;
   129	    const span = name === 'OPEN ARC' ? Math.PI * 1.55 : TAU;
   130	    ctx.beginPath();
   131	    for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
   132	      const a = start + span * (i / segments);
   133	      const fakeLane = name === 'OPEN ARC' ? (i / segments) * (LANES - 1) : (i / segments) * LANES;
   134	      const pt = project(p, fakeLane, name === 'OPEN ARC' ? 0 : 0);
   135	      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(pt.x, pt.y); else ctx.lineTo(pt.x, pt.y);
   136	    }
   137	    if (openExtra && name !== 'OPEN ARC') ctx.closePath();
   138	    void m;
   139	  }

   141	  function playTone(freq, duration, type, volume) {
   142	    if (!audio) return;
   143	    const now = audio.currentTime;
   144	    const osc = audio.createOscillator();
   145	    const gain = audio.createGain();
   146	    osc.type = type || 'sine';
   147	    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);
   148	    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(45, freq * .7), now + duration);
   149	    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume || .025, now);
   150	    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, now + duration);
   151	    osc.connect(gain).connect(audio.destination);
   152	    osc.start(now);
   153	    osc.stop(now + duration + .02);
   154	  }

   226	  function spawnEnemy() {

   235	  function fire() {

   240	  function hitEnemy(e, index) {
   241	    const points = (e.type === 'pulsar' ? 150 : e.type === 'zapper' ? 110 : e.type === 'spinner' ? 90 : 60) * level;
   242	    score += points;

   250	  function burstAtEnemy(e, amount, color) {
   251	    const p = project(e.t, e.lane);
   252	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i++) {
   253	      const a = Math.random() * TAU;

   256	    }
   257	  }

   269	    }

   293	  function finishWarp() {

   304	  function update(dt) {
   305	    time += dt;
   306	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.8);
   307	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 1.8);
   308	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   309	      const p = particles[i];
   310	      p.life -= dt;
   311	      p.x += p.vx * dt;
   312	      p.y += p.vy * dt;
   313	      p.vx *= 1 - dt * 1.7;
   314	      p.vy *= 1 - dt * 1.7;
   315	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   316	    }
   317	    if (state === 'warp') {
   318	      warpTime += dt;
   319	      if (warpTime > 1.45) finishWarp();
   320	      return;
   321	    }
   322	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   323	
   324	    const chase = shortestLane(targetLane, playerLane);
   325	    playerLane = wrap(playerLane + chase * Math.min(1, dt * 13), LANES);
   326	    levelTime += dt;
   327	    const levelDuration = Math.max(12, 19 - level * .55);
   328	    spawnTimer -= dt;
   329	    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
   330	      spawnEnemy();
   331	      const intensity = Math.min(.86, .94 - level * .035);
   332	      spawnTimer = rand(intensity * .7, intensity * 1.12);
   333	      if (level > 4 && Math.random() < .17) spawnTimer *= .4;
   334	    }
   335	    fireTimer -= dt;
   336	    if (fireTimer <= 0) {
   337	      fire();
   338	      fireTimer = Math.max(.19, .37 - level * .012);
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    for (let i = bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   342	      const b = bullets[i];
   343	      b.t -= dt * 1.55;
   344	      b.life += dt;
   345	      let used = false;
   346	      for (let j = enemies.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   347	        const e = enemies[j];
   348	        if (Math.abs(b.t - e.t) < .06 && Math.abs(shortestLane(b.lane, e.lane)) < .62) {
   349	          hitEnemy(e, j);
   350	          used = true;
   351	          break;
   352	        }
   353	      }
   354	      if (used || b.t < -.08) bullets.splice(i, 1);
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    for (let i = enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   358	      const e = enemies[i];
   359	      e.age += dt;
   360	      e.hit = Math.max(0, e.hit - dt);
   361	      e.t += e.speed * dt;
   362	      if (e.type === 'spinner') e.lane = wrap(e.lane + Math.sin(e.age * 3.4 + e.phase) * dt * (1.5 + level * .04), LANES);
   363	      if (e.type === 'zapper') {
   364	        e.t = .82 + Math.sin(e.age * 2 + e.phase) * .065;
   365	        e.lane = wrap(e.lane + dt * (1.15 + level * .06), LANES);
   366	        if (Math.abs(shortestLane(e.lane, playerLane)) < .63 && e.hit <= 0) {
   367	          e.hit = 1.2;
   368	          loseLife('zapper');
   369	          burstAtEnemy(e, 12, colors.pink);
   370	        }
   371	      }
   372	      if (e.type === 'pulsar') e.t += Math.sin(e.age * 5 + e.phase) * dt * .016;
   373	      if (e.t >= 1.05 && e.type !== 'zapper') {
   374	        burstAtEnemy(e, 7, colors.red);
   375	        enemies.splice(i, 1);
   376	        loseLife('breach');
   377	      }
   378	    }
   379	    if (state === 'playing' && levelTime >= levelDuration) startWarp();
   380	    updateHud();
   381	  }

   383	  function drawBackground() {
   384	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   385	    g.addColorStop(0, '#050816');
   386	    g.addColorStop(.52, '#0a1725');
   387	    g.addColorStop(1, '#090b18');
   388	    ctx.fillStyle = g;
   389	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   390	    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .5, H * .38, 4, W * .5, H * .38, Math.max(W, H) * .7);
   391	    glow.addColorStop(0, '#1b446032');
   392	    glow.addColorStop(1, '#060a1500');
   393	    ctx.fillStyle = glow;
   394	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   395	    ctx.fillStyle = '#a9eaf0';
   396	    for (const s of backgroundBits) {
   397	      s.y += s.drift * .015;
   398	      if (s.y > H + 5) s.y = -5;
   399	      ctx.globalAlpha = s.z * (.28 + Math.sin(time * 1.5 + s.x) * .12);
   400	      ctx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, s.r, s.r);
   401	    }
   402	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   403	  }

   405	  function drawTube() {
   406	    const m = tubeMetrics();
   407	    const name = geometryName();
   408	    ctx.save();
   409	    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   410	    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   411	    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
   412	    ctx.shadowColor = name === 'STAR' ? colors.violet : name === 'RIBBON' ? colors.gold : colors.cyan;
   413	    ctx.strokeStyle = name === 'STAR' ? '#a58dff88' : name === 'RIBBON' ? '#ffd56b88' : '#63e8f688';
   414	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   415	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
   416	      const t = i / 5;
   417	      traceRing(t, true);
   418	      ctx.globalAlpha = .32 + t * .38;
   419	      ctx.stroke();
   420	    }
   421	    ctx.shadowBlur = 4;
   422	    ctx.globalAlpha = .45;
   423	    for (let lane = 0; lane < LANES; lane++) {
   424	      ctx.beginPath();
   425	      for (let i = 0; i <= 14; i++) {
   426	        const p = project(i / 14, lane);
   427	        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); else ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
   428	      }
   429	      ctx.stroke();
   430	    }
   431	    ctx.globalAlpha = .98;
   432	    ctx.lineWidth = 2.3;
   433	    traceRing(1, true);
   434	    ctx.stroke();
   435	    ctx.globalAlpha = .4;
   436	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   437	    traceRing(.03, true);
   438	    ctx.stroke();
   439	    ctx.restore();
   440	    drawCenterCore(m);
   441	  }
   442	
   443	  function drawCenterCore(m) {
   444	    ctx.save();
   445	    const r = 15 + Math.sin(time * 2.2) * 2;
   446	    ctx.shadowColor = colors.cyan;
   447	    ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
   448	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#86ffffaa';
   449	    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
   450	    ctx.beginPath();
   451	    ctx.ellipse(m.cx, m.cy, r * 1.5, r, 0, 0, TAU);
   452	    ctx.stroke();
   453	    ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
   454	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d4ffff';
   455	    ctx.beginPath();
   456	    ctx.arc(m.cx, m.cy, r * .45, time * .7, time * .7 + Math.PI * 1.35);
   457	    ctx.stroke();
   458	    ctx.restore();
   459	  }
   460	
   461	  function drawBullet(b) {
   462	    const p = project(b.t, b.lane);
   463	    const q = project(Math.min(1.08, b.t + .075), b.lane);
   464	    ctx.save();
   465	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#eaffff';
   466	    ctx.shadowColor = colors.aqua;
   467	    ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   468	    ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
   469	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.lineTo(q.x, q.y); ctx.stroke();
   470	    ctx.restore();
   471	  }
   472	
   473	  function drawEnemy(e) {
   474	    const p = project(e.t, e.lane);
   475	    const size = lerp(4, 17, clamp(e.t, 0, 1));
   476	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(time * 5 + e.phase) * .1;
   477	    ctx.save();
   478	    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
   479	    ctx.rotate(e.age * (e.type === 'spinner' ? 4 : .7) + e.phase);
   480	    ctx.globalAlpha = e.hit > 0 ? .45 : 1;
   481	    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   482	    if (e.type === 'crawler') {
   483	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#75f1df'; ctx.fillStyle = '#1e7f84'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.aqua; ctx.shadowBlur = 13; ctx.lineWidth = 1.7;
   484	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -size); ctx.lineTo(size * .78, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, size); ctx.lineTo(-size * .78, 0); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   485	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-size * .7, -size * .4); ctx.lineTo(-size * 1.4, -size * .9); ctx.moveTo(size * .7, size * .4); ctx.lineTo(size * 1.4, size * .9); ctx.stroke();
   486	    } else if (e.type === 'spinner') {
   487	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#bf9aff'; ctx.fillStyle = '#503f91'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.violet; ctx.shadowBlur = 15; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   488	      ctx.beginPath();
   489	      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 4; const rr = i % 2 ? size * .48 : size * 1.05; const x = Math.cos(a) * rr, y = Math.sin(a) * rr; if (!i) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y); }
   490	      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   491	      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, size * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   492	    } else if (e.type === 'zapper') {
   493	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff719a'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.pink; ctx.shadowBlur = 17; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   494	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, size * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   495	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-size * 1.4, 0); ctx.lineTo(-size * .5, -size * .5); ctx.lineTo(size * .1, size * .45); ctx.lineTo(size * 1.4, 0); ctx.stroke();
   496	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd7e5'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, size * .16, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   497	    } else {
   498	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd56e'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.gold; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   499	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, size * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   500	      ctx.globalAlpha = .6;
   501	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, size * .55 + Math.sin(time * 7 + e.phase) * size * .2, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   502	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   503	      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const a = i * TAU / 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a) * size, Math.sin(a) * size); ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * size * 1.55, Math.sin(a) * size * 1.55); ctx.stroke(); }
   504	    }
   505	    ctx.restore();
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  function drawClaw() {
   509	    const p = project(1.055, playerLane);
   510	    const q = project(.95, playerLane);
   511	    const angle = laneAngle(playerLane);
   512	    ctx.save();
   513	    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
   514	    ctx.rotate(angle + Math.PI / 2);
   515	    ctx.fillStyle = '#dfffff'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#5bf4ef'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.aqua; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
   516	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -12); ctx.lineTo(8, 9); ctx.lineTo(0, 5); ctx.lineTo(-8, 9); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   517	    ctx.restore();
   518	    ctx.save();
   519	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d6fffd88'; ctx.shadowColor = colors.aqua; ctx.shadowBlur = 9; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   520	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(q.x, q.y); ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.stroke();
   521	    ctx.restore();
   522	  }
   523	
   524	  function drawParticles() {
   525	    ctx.save();
   526	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
   527	    for (const p of particles) {
   528	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1);
   529	      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
   530	      ctx.shadowColor = p.color;
   531	      ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
   532	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   533	    }
   534	    ctx.restore();
   535	  }
   536	
   537	  function drawWarp() {
   538	    const m = tubeMetrics();
   539	    const t = clamp(warpTime / 1.45, 0, 1);
   540	    ctx.save();
   541	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
   542	    ctx.translate(m.cx, m.cy + H * .12);
   543	    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
   544	      const p = (i / 14 + t * .8) % 1;
   545	      const rx = 10 + p * W * .7;
   546	      const ry = 7 + p * H * .35;
   547	      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - p) * .7;
   548	      ctx.strokeStyle = i % 2 ? colors.violet : colors.cyan;
   549	      ctx.lineWidth = 1 + (1 - p) * 2;
   550	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, rx, ry, Math.sin(i) * .5, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   551	    }
   552	    ctx.restore();
   553	    ctx.save();
   554	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   555	    ctx.fillStyle = '#dfffff';
   556	    ctx.shadowColor = colors.cyan; ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
   557	    ctx.font = '800 12px ui-monospace, monospace';
   558	    ctx.letterSpacing = '4px';
   559	    ctx.fillText('WARPING TO LEVEL ' + String(level + 1).padStart(2, '0'), m.cx, H * .78);
   560	    ctx.restore();
   561	  }
   562	
   563	  function draw() {
   564	    drawBackground();
   565	    ctx.save();
   566	    if (shake > 0) ctx.translate(rand(-1, 1) * shake * 10, rand(-1, 1) * shake * 10);
   567	    drawTube();
   568	    for (const b of bullets) drawBullet(b);
   569	    for (const e of enemies) drawEnemy(e);
   570	    drawClaw();
   571	    drawParticles();
   572	    if (state === 'warp') drawWarp();
   573	    ctx.restore();
   574	    if (flash > 0) {
   575	      ctx.fillStyle = state === 'gameover' ? '#ff42551e' : '#bfffff' + Math.floor(clamp(flash, 0, 1) * 24).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
   576	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   577	    }
   578	  }
   579	
   580	  function frame(now) {
   581	    const dt = Math.min(.034, Math.max(0, (now - last) / 1000));
   582	    last = now;
   583	    update(dt);
   584	    draw();
   585	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   586	  }
   587	
   588	  function pointerStart(e) {
   589	    if (state === 'gameover') return;
   590	    if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;
   591	    if (e.clientY > H * .58 || e.pointerType === 'mouse') {
   592	      drag = true;
   593	      pointerId = e.pointerId;
   594	      setLaneFromPointer(e.clientX);
   595	      unlockAudio();
   596	      try { canvas.setPointerCapture(pointerId); } catch (err) { /* optional */ }
   597	      e.preventDefault();
   598	    }
   599	  }
   600	
   601	  function pointerMove(e) {
   602	    if (!drag || (pointerId !== null && e.pointerId !== pointerId)) return;
   603	    setLaneFromPointer(e.clientX);
   604	    e.preventDefault();
   605	  }
   606	
   607	  function pointerEnd(e) {
   608	    if (pointerId !== null && e.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
   609	    drag = false;
   610	    pointerId = null;
   611	  }
   612	
   613	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerStart, { passive: false });
   614	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   615	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerEnd, { passive: false });
   616	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerEnd, { passive: false });
   617	  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   618	  surgeBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); activateSurge(); });
   619	  restartBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); resetGame(); });
   620	  restartBtn.addEventListener('click', resetGame);
   621	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   622	    if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'a', 'd', 'A', 'D', 'w', 's', 'W', 'S', ' ', 'Enter'].indexOf(e.key) !== -1) e.preventDefault();
   623	    if (state === 'gameover' && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { resetGame(); return; }
   624	    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A' || e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'W') moveLane(-1);
   625	    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D' || e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') moveLane(1);
   626	    if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') activateSurge();
   627	  }, { passive: false });
   628	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   629	  window.addEventListener('blur', function () { drag = false; });
   630	
   631	  resize();
   632	  resetGame();
   633	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   634	})();

    98	  function createMaze() {
    99	    const cols = 17;
   100	    const rows = 25;
   101	    const walls = Array.from({ length: rows }, () => Array(cols).fill(true));
   102	    const cellW = (cols - 1) / 2;
   103	    const cellH = (rows - 1) / 2;
   104	    const seen = Array.from({ length: cellH }, () => Array(cellW).fill(false));
   105	    const stack = [{ x: Math.floor(cellW / 2), y: Math.floor(cellH / 2) }];
   106	    const carveCell = (cx, cy) => { walls[cy * 2 + 1][cx * 2 + 1] = false; };
   107	    carveCell(stack[0].x, stack[0].y);
   108	    seen[stack[0].y][stack[0].x] = true;
   109	    while (stack.length) {
   110	      const current = stack[stack.length - 1];
   111	      const candidates = [];
   112	      for (const d of [{ x: 0, y: -1 }, { x: 1, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 1 }, { x: -1, y: 0 }]) {
   113	        const nx = current.x + d.x;
   114	        const ny = current.y + d.y;
   115	        if (nx >= 0 && nx < cellW && ny >= 0 && ny < cellH && !seen[ny][nx]) candidates.push({ x: nx, y: ny, d });
   116	      }
   117	      if (!candidates.length) { stack.pop(); continue; }
   118	      const choice = candidates[Math.floor(random() * candidates.length)];
   119	      const gx = current.x * 2 + 1;
   120	      const gy = current.y * 2 + 1;
   121	      walls[gy + choice.d.y][gx + choice.d.x] = false;
   122	      carveCell(choice.x, choice.y);
   123	      seen[choice.y][choice.x] = true;
   124	      stack.push({ x: choice.x, y: choice.y });
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    // Add selective cross-links so the route has honest loops and alternate escapes.
   128	    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
   129	      const x = 1 + Math.floor(random() * (cols - 2));
   130	      const y = 1 + Math.floor(random() * (rows - 2));
   131	      if ((x + y) % 2 === 1) {
   132	        if (x < cols - 2 && walls[y][x + 1]) walls[y][x + 1] = false;
   133	        else if (y < rows - 2 && walls[y + 1][x]) walls[y + 1][x] = false;
   134	      }
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    const warpRow = rows % 2 === 0 ? rows / 2 : rows - 2;
   138	    walls[warpRow][0] = false;
   139	    walls[warpRow][1] = false;
   140	    walls[warpRow][cols - 2] = false;
   141	    walls[warpRow][cols - 1] = false;
   142	    // Keep the hub and its four exits legible on every seed.
   143	    const hub = { x: Math.floor(cols / 2), y: Math.floor(rows / 2) };
   144	    walls[hub.y][hub.x] = false;
   145	    for (const d of DIR_LIST) {
   146	      const nx = hub.x + d.x;
   147	      const ny = hub.y + d.y;
   148	      if (nx > 0 && nx < cols - 1 && ny > 0 && ny < rows - 1) walls[ny][nx] = false;
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    const powerNodes = [
   152	      { x: 1, y: 1, active: true }, { x: cols - 2, y: 1, active: true },
   153	      { x: 1, y: rows - 2, active: true }, { x: cols - 2, y: rows - 2, active: true }
   154	    ];
   155	    for (const node of powerNodes) walls[node.y][node.x] = false;
   156	
   157	    const pellets = new Set();
   158	    for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) {
   159	      for (let x = 0; x < cols; x++) {
   160	        if (!walls[y][x] && !(x === hub.x && y === hub.y) && !powerNodes.some(p => p.x === x && p.y === y)) pellets.add(key(x, y));
   161	      }
   162	    }
   163	    return { cols, rows, walls, warpRow, hub, powerNodes, pellets, totalPellets: pellets.size };
   164	  }

   184	  function makeChaser(name, type, color, speed, offset) {
   185	    const h = maze.hub;
   186	    const home = { x: h.x + (offset % 2 ? 0 : (offset === 0 ? -1 : 1)), y: h.y + (offset > 1 ? 1 : -1) };
   187	    const chaser = { name, type, color, speed, x: home.x, y: home.y, dir: DIRS.up, nextDir: DIRS.up, home, eaten: false, respawn: 0, locked: false, wanderTarget: null, pulse: random() * TAU };
   188	    if (!canMove(home.x, home.y)) { chaser.home = { x: h.x, y: h.y }; chaser.x = h.x; chaser.y = h.y; }
   189	    return chaser;
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  function canMove(x, y) {
   193	    if (!maze) return false;
   194	    if (y === maze.warpRow && (x < 0 || x >= maze.cols)) return true;
   195	    if (x < 0 || x >= maze.cols || y < 0 || y >= maze.rows) return false;
   196	    return !maze.walls[y][x];
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  function wrappedX(x) {
   200	    if (x < 0) return x + maze.cols;
   201	    if (x >= maze.cols) return x - maze.cols;
   202	    return x;
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  function legalDirs(x, y) {
   206	    return DIR_LIST.filter(d => canMove(x + d.x, y + d.y));
   207	  }
   208	
   209	  function tileDistance(a, b) {
   210	    let dx = Math.abs(a.x - b.x);
   211	    if (maze && dx > maze.cols / 2) dx = maze.cols - dx;
   212	    return dx + Math.abs(a.y - b.y);
   213	  }
