   215	  function targetFor(chaser) {
   216	    const px = Math.round(player.x);
   217	    const py = Math.round(player.y);
   218	    if (chaser.type === 'direct') return { x: px, y: py };
   219	    if (chaser.type === 'ahead') return { x: px + player.dir.x * 5, y: py + player.dir.y * 5 };
   220	    if (chaser.type === 'flank') return { x: px - player.dir.x * 6, y: py - player.dir.y * 6 };
   221	    if (!chaser.locked && tileDistance(chaser, player) < 8) chaser.locked = true;
   222	    if (chaser.locked) return { x: px, y: py };
   223	    if (!chaser.wanderTarget || tileDistance(chaser, chaser.wanderTarget) < 2 || !canMove(chaser.wanderTarget.x, chaser.wanderTarget.y)) {
   224	      const open = [];
   225	      for (let y = 1; y < maze.rows - 1; y++) for (let x = 1; x < maze.cols - 1; x++) if (!maze.walls[y][x]) open.push({ x, y });
   226	      chaser.wanderTarget = open[Math.floor(random() * open.length)];
   227	    }
   228	    return chaser.wanderTarget;
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  function bfsDirection(startX, startY, target, flee) {
   232	    const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.cols - 1, Math.round(startX)));
   233	    const sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.rows - 1, Math.round(startY)));
   234	    let tx = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.cols - 1, Math.round(target.x)));
   235	    let ty = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.rows - 1, Math.round(target.y)));
   236	    if (!canMove(tx, ty)) { tx = maze.hub.x; ty = maze.hub.y; }
   237	    const origin = key(sx, sy);
   238	    const queue = [{ x: sx, y: sy }];
   239	    const came = new Map([[origin, null]]);
   240	    while (queue.length) {
   241	      const current = queue.shift();
   242	      if (current.x === tx && current.y === ty) break;
   243	      for (const d of DIR_LIST) {
   244	        let nx = current.x + d.x;
   245	        const ny = current.y + d.y;
   246	        if (ny === maze.warpRow && (nx < 0 || nx >= maze.cols)) nx = wrappedX(nx);
   247	        if (!canMove(nx, ny)) continue;
   248	        const k = key(nx, ny);
   249	        if (!came.has(k)) { came.set(k, { x: current.x, y: current.y, d }); queue.push({ x: nx, y: ny }); }
   250	      }
   251	    }
   252	    const endKey = key(tx, ty);
   253	    if (!came.has(endKey)) return null;
   254	    let cursor = { x: tx, y: ty };
   255	    let first = null;
   256	    while (key(cursor.x, cursor.y) !== origin) {
   257	      const step = came.get(key(cursor.x, cursor.y));
   258	      if (!step) break;
   259	      first = step.d;
   260	      cursor = { x: step.x, y: step.y };
   261	    }
   262	    if (!flee) return first;
   263	    return null;
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  function fleeDirection(chaser) {
   267	    const options = legalDirs(Math.round(chaser.x), Math.round(chaser.y));
   268	    if (!options.length) return DIRS.none;
   269	    let bestDir = options[0];
   270	    let bestDistance = -Infinity;
   271	    for (const d of options) {
   272	      const nx = wrappedX(Math.round(chaser.x) + d.x);
   273	      const ny = Math.round(chaser.y) + d.y;
   274	      const distance = tileDistance({ x: nx, y: ny }, { x: player.x, y: player.y });
   275	      const jitter = random() * .35;
   276	      if (distance + jitter > bestDistance) { bestDistance = distance + jitter; bestDir = d; }
   277	    }
   278	    return bestDir;
   279	  }
   280	
   281	  function chooseChaserDir(chaser) {
   282	    const x = Math.round(chaser.x);
   283	    const y = Math.round(chaser.y);
   284	    let chosen = powerTimer > 0 ? fleeDirection(chaser) : bfsDirection(x, y, targetFor(chaser), false);
   285	    const options = legalDirs(x, y);
   286	    if (!chosen || !canMove(x + chosen.x, y + chosen.y)) {
   287	      const filtered = options.filter(d => !(d.x === -chaser.dir.x && d.y === -chaser.dir.y));
   288	      chosen = filtered[Math.floor(random() * Math.max(1, filtered.length))] || options[0] || DIRS.none;
   289	    }
   290	    chaser.nextDir = chosen;
   291	  }
   292	
   293	  function updateActor(actor, dt, isPlayer) {
   294	    const speed = actor.speed;
   295	    let remaining = speed * dt;
   296	    let guard = 0;
   297	    while (remaining > 0 && guard++ < 3) {
   298	      const tx = Math.round(actor.x);
   299	      const ty = Math.round(actor.y);
   300	      const atTile = Math.abs(actor.x - tx) < .04 && Math.abs(actor.y - ty) < .04;
   301	      if (atTile) {
   302	        actor.x = tx; actor.y = ty;
   303	        const requested = actor.nextDir || DIRS.none;
   304	        if (canMove(tx + requested.x, ty + requested.y)) actor.dir = requested;
   305	        else if (!canMove(tx + actor.dir.x, ty + actor.dir.y)) actor.dir = DIRS.none;
   306	        if (!isPlayer) chooseChaserDir(actor);
   307	        if (isPlayer && canMove(tx + actor.nextDir.x, ty + actor.nextDir.y)) actor.dir = actor.nextDir;
   308	        if (actor.dir.x === 0 && actor.dir.y === 0) break;
   309	      }
   310	      const step = Math.min(remaining, .18);
   311	      const nx = actor.x + actor.dir.x * step;
   312	      const ny = actor.y + actor.dir.y * step;
   313	      if (actor.dir.x && !canMove(Math.round(actor.x + actor.dir.x * .51), Math.round(actor.y))) {
   314	        actor.x = Math.round(actor.x); actor.dir = DIRS.none; break;
   315	      }
   316	      if (actor.dir.y && !canMove(Math.round(actor.x), Math.round(actor.y + actor.dir.y * .51))) {
   317	        actor.y = Math.round(actor.y); actor.dir = DIRS.none; break;
   318	      }
   319	      actor.x = nx; actor.y = ny;
   320	      if (actor.y === maze.warpRow && actor.x < 0) actor.x += maze.cols;
   321	      if (actor.y === maze.warpRow && actor.x >= maze.cols) actor.x -= maze.cols;
   322	      remaining -= step;
   323	    }
   324	  }
   325	
   326	  function addScore(points) {
   327	    score += points;
   328	    saveBest();
   329	    updateHud();
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  function updateHud() {
   333	    scoreEl.textContent = String(score).padStart(6, '0');
   334	    bestEl.textContent = String(best).padStart(6, '0');
   335	    levelEl.textContent = String(level).padStart(2, '0');
   336	    livesEl.textContent = '●'.repeat(Math.max(0, lives));
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  function spawnBurst(x, y, color, count, force) {
   340	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   341	      const angle = random() * TAU;
   342	      const speed = force * (.5 + random());
   343	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, life: .35 + random() * .55, max: .9, color, size: .06 + random() * .1 });
   344	    }
   345	  }
   346	
   347	  function loseLife() {
   348	    if (hitCooldown > 0 || gameState !== 'playing') return;
   349	    lives -= 1;
   350	    hitCooldown = 2;
   351	    shake = .55;
   352	    spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, '#ff648c', 26, 3.2);
   353	    updateHud();
   354	    if (lives <= 0) {
   355	      gameState = 'gameover';
   356	      showState('RUN ENDED', 'Final score ' + String(score).padStart(6, '0') + ' · best ' + String(best).padStart(6, '0'));
   357	      return;
   358	    }
   359	    player.x = maze.hub.x; player.y = maze.hub.y; player.dir = DIRS.left; player.nextDir = DIRS.left;
   360	    for (const chaser of chasers) { chaser.x = chaser.home.x; chaser.y = chaser.home.y; chaser.eaten = false; chaser.respawn = 0; }
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  function showState(title, copy) {
   364	    stateTitle.textContent = title;
   365	    stateCopy.textContent = copy;
   366	    stateCard.classList.add('visible');
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function hideState() { stateCard.classList.remove('visible'); }
   370	
   371	  function restart() {
   372	    score = 0; lives = 3; level = 1; baseSeed = (Date.now() ^ 0x9e3779b9) >>> 0;
   373	    gameState = 'playing'; hintTimer = 8; hideState(); buildLevel();
   374	  }
   375	
   376	  function activateDirection(dir) {
   377	    if (gameState === 'gameover') { restart(); return; }
   378	    if (!dir) return;
   379	    player.nextDir = dir;
   380	    hintTimer = 0;
   381	    tryStartAudio();
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  function checkPickups() {
   385	    const px = Math.round(player.x);
   386	    const py = Math.round(player.y);
   387	    const pelletKey = key(px, py);
   388	    if (maze.pellets.delete(pelletKey)) {
   389	      addScore(10);
   390	      spawnBurst(px, py, '#bfeaff', 3, .8);
   391	      if (maze.pellets.size === 0) {
   392	        addScore(500 * level);
   393	        level += 1;
   394	        buildLevel();
   395	        return;
   396	      }
   397	    }
   398	    for (const node of maze.powerNodes) {
   399	      if (node.active && node.x === px && node.y === py) {
   400	        node.active = false; powerTimer = 8; addScore(50); shake = .18;
   401	        spawnBurst(node.x, node.y, '#ffe36e', 18, 2.4);
   402	      }
   403	    }
   404	    if (shard.active && tileDistance(player, shard) < .75) {
   405	      shard.active = false; shardCooldown = 13 + random() * 7; addScore(250); shake = .25;
   406	      spawnBurst(shard.x, shard.y, '#ffbd62', 24, 2.6);
   407	    }
   408	  }
   409	
   410	  function checkChaserHits() {
   411	    if (hitCooldown > 0) return;
   412	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   413	      if (chaser.eaten || chaser.respawn > 0) continue;
   414	      if (tileDistance(player, chaser) < .58) {
   415	        if (powerTimer > 0) {
   416	          chaser.eaten = true; chaser.respawn = 1.15; chaser.x = maze.hub.x; chaser.y = maze.hub.y;
   417	          addScore(200); shake = .28; spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, chaser.color, 20, 2.7);
   418	        } else { loseLife(); }
   419	        break;
   420	      }
   421	    }
   422	  }
   423	
   424	  function update(dt) {
   425	    elapsed += dt;
   426	    const step = Math.min(dt, .045);
   427	    if (bannerTimer > 0) bannerTimer -= step;
   428	    if (hitCooldown > 0) hitCooldown -= step;
   429	    if (shake > 0) shake -= step;
   430	    if (powerTimer > 0) powerTimer = Math.max(0, powerTimer - step);
   431	    if (hintTimer > 0) { hintTimer -= step; if (hintTimer <= 0) hintEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
   432	    if (gameState !== 'playing') { updateParticles(step); return; }
   433	
   434	    updateActor(player, step, true);
   435	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   436	      if (chaser.eaten) { chaser.respawn -= step; if (chaser.respawn <= 0) { chaser.eaten = false; chaser.x = maze.hub.x; chaser.y = maze.hub.y; chaser.dir = DIRS.up; } continue; }
   437	      updateActor(chaser, step, false);
   438	    }
   439	    checkPickups();
   440	    checkChaserHits();
   441	    if (shardCooldown > 0) shardCooldown -= step;
   442	    if (!shard.active && shardCooldown <= 0) { shard.active = true; shard.x = maze.hub.x; shard.y = maze.hub.y; shard.phase = elapsed; }
   443	    updateParticles(step);
   444	  }
   445	
   446	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   447	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   448	      const p = particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .95; p.vy *= .95;
   449	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   450	    }
   451	    if (particles.length > 180) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 180);
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function layout() {
   455	    const tile = Math.max(12, Math.min((width - 26) / maze.cols, (height - 160) / maze.rows));
   456	    return { tile, x: (width - tile * maze.cols) / 2, y: Math.max(92, (height - tile * maze.rows) / 2 + 4) };
   457	  }
   458	
   459	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   460	    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   461	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius); ctx.closePath();
   462	  }
   463	
   464	  function drawBackground() {
   465	    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
   466	    gradient.addColorStop(0, '#070b1c'); gradient.addColorStop(.5, '#0d1230'); gradient.addColorStop(1, '#180a27');
   467	    ctx.fillStyle = gradient; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
   468	    ctx.globalAlpha = .16;
   469	    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
   470	      const x = (i * 83 + 31) % width; const y = (i * 137 + 21) % height;
   471	      ctx.fillStyle = i % 3 === 0 ? '#72f4e1' : '#778bff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, (i % 3) + .6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   472	    }
   473	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   474	  }
   475	
   476	  function drawMaze(box) {
   477	    const t = box.tile;
   478	    ctx.save();
   479	    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(61, 99, 223, .28)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
   480	    ctx.fillStyle = '#080d27'; roundRect(box.x - 5, box.y - 5, maze.cols * t + 10, maze.rows * t + 10, 16); ctx.fill();
   481	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   482	    for (let y = 0; y < maze.rows; y++) {
   483	      for (let x = 0; x < maze.cols; x++) {
   484	        const px = box.x + x * t; const py = box.y + y * t;
   485	        if (maze.walls[y][x]) {
   486	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wall; roundRect(px + t * .08, py + t * .08, t * .84, t * .84, t * .17); ctx.fill();
   487	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wallHi; ctx.globalAlpha = .42; roundRect(px + t * .17, py + t * .14, t * .5, t * .09, t * .04); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   488	        } else {
   489	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.corridor; ctx.fillRect(px, py, t, t);
   490	        }
   491	      }
   492	    }
   493	    // Warp tunnel glows show the cross-screen escape clearly.
   494	    const wy = box.y + maze.warpRow * t + t / 2;
   495	    const warpGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(box.x, 0, box.x + t * 2, 0);
   496	    warpGradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(114,244,225,.1)'); warpGradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(114,244,225,0)');
   497	    ctx.fillStyle = warpGradient; ctx.fillRect(box.x, wy - t * .4, t * 2, t * .8);
   498	    const warpGradient2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(box.x + maze.cols * t, 0, box.x + (maze.cols - 2) * t, 0);
   499	    warpGradient2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(114,244,225,.1)'); warpGradient2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(114,244,225,0)');
   500	    ctx.fillStyle = warpGradient2; ctx.fillRect(box.x + (maze.cols - 2) * t, wy - t * .4, t * 2, t * .8);
   501	    ctx.restore();
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  function drawPickups(box) {
   505	    const t = box.tile;
   506	    for (const p of maze.pellets) {
   507	      const split = p.split(','); const x = Number(split[0]); const y = Number(split[1]);
   508	      ctx.fillStyle = '#b9eaff'; ctx.globalAlpha = .82; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (x + .5) * t, box.y + (y + .5) * t, Math.max(1.4, t * .075), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   509	    }
   510	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   511	    for (const node of maze.powerNodes) {
   512	      if (!node.active) continue;
   513	      const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 5 + node.x) * .13;
   514	      ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe36e'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   515	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe36e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (node.x + .5) * t, box.y + (node.y + .5) * t, t * .27 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   516	    }
   517	    if (shard && shard.active) {
   518	      const sx = box.x + (shard.x + .5) * t; const sy = box.y + (shard.y + .5) * t;
   519	      const spin = elapsed * 2.8 + shard.phase; const r = t * .36;
   520	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy); ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4 + Math.sin(spin) * .2); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffbd62'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffbd62'; roundRect(-r * .68, -r * .68, r * 1.36, r * 1.36, r * .18); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff2c9'; roundRect(-r * .28, -r * .5, r * .25, r * .72, r * .1); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   521	    }
   522	  }
   523	
   524	  function drawPlayer(box) {
   525	    if (hitCooldown > 0 && Math.floor(elapsed * 13) % 2 === 0) return;
   526	    const t = box.tile; const x = box.x + (player.x + .5) * t; const y = box.y + (player.y + .5) * t; const r = t * .42;
   527	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(player.dir.angle); ctx.shadowColor = COLORS.player; ctx.shadowBlur = 17; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.player; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * .72, -r * .82); ctx.lineTo(-r * .48, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * .72, r * .82); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.playerHi; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .12, 0, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   528	  }
   529	
   530	  function drawChasers(box) {
   531	    const t = box.tile;
   532	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   533	      if (chaser.eaten) continue;
   534	      const x = box.x + (chaser.x + .5) * t; const y = box.y + (chaser.y + .5) * t; const r = t * .39;
   535	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.shadowColor = powerTimer > 0 ? '#6d80ff' : chaser.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 13;
   536	      ctx.fillStyle = powerTimer > 0 ? (powerTimer < 2 && Math.floor(elapsed * 9) % 2 ? '#eaf0ff' : '#5a68c8') : chaser.color;
   537	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -r * .08, r, Math.PI, 0); ctx.lineTo(r, r * .75); ctx.lineTo(r * .5, r * .52); ctx.lineTo(0, r * .78); ctx.lineTo(-r * .5, r * .52); ctx.lineTo(-r, r * .75); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   538	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f9ff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .32, -r * .08, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(r * .32, -r * .08, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#14204d'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .28 + chaser.dir.x * 2, -r * .08 + chaser.dir.y * 2, r * .08, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(r * .36 + chaser.dir.x * 2, -r * .08 + chaser.dir.y * 2, r * .08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   539	    }
   540	  }
   541	
   542	  function drawParticles(box) {
   543	    const t = box.tile;
   544	    for (const p of particles) {
   545	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.max); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (p.x + .5) * t, box.y + (p.y + .5) * t, p.size * t, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   546	    }
   547	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   548	  }
   549	
   550	  function draw() {
   551	    drawBackground();
   552	    if (!maze) return;
   553	    const box = layout();
   554	    const sx = shake > 0 ? (random() - .5) * shake * 12 : 0;
   555	    const sy = shake > 0 ? (random() - .5) * shake * 12 : 0;
   556	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   557	    drawMaze(box); drawPickups(box); drawParticles(box); drawChasers(box); drawPlayer(box);
   558	    ctx.restore();
   559	    if (bannerTimer > 0 && gameState === 'playing') {
   560	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, bannerTimer * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#eaf2ff'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '900 12px system-ui'; ctx.letterSpacing = '2px'; ctx.fillText('LEVEL ' + String(level).padStart(2, '0'), width / 2, box.y - 14); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   561	    }
   562	    if (powerTimer > 0) {
   563	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe36e'; ctx.globalAlpha = .9; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '900 10px system-ui'; ctx.fillText('HUNT ' + powerTimer.toFixed(1), width / 2, height - 111); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   564	    }
   565	  }
   566	
   567	  function frame(time) {
   568	    if (!lastTime) lastTime = time;
   569	    const dt = Math.min(.05, (time - lastTime) / 1000);
   570	    lastTime = time;
   571	    update(dt); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   572	  }
   573	
   574	  let audio = null;
   575	  function tryStartAudio() {
   576	    if (audio || !window.AudioContext && !window.webkitAudioContext) return;
   577	    try { audio = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (_) { audio = null; }
   578	  }
   579	
   580	  function pointerDown(event) {
   581	    if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' && event.button !== 0) return;
   582	    pointerStart = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, time: performance.now() };
   583	    if (event.target.closest && event.target.closest('.pad')) return;
   584	    event.preventDefault();
   585	  }
   586	
   587	  function pointerMove(event) { if (pointerStart) event.preventDefault(); }
   588	
   589	  function pointerUp(event) {
   590	    if (!pointerStart) return;
   591	    const start = pointerStart; pointerStart = null;
   592	    const dx = event.clientX - start.x; const dy = event.clientY - start.y;
   593	    if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < 18) { if (gameState === 'gameover') restart(); return; }
   594	    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) activateDirection(dx > 0 ? DIRS.right : DIRS.left);
   595	    else activateDirection(dy > 0 ? DIRS.down : DIRS.up);
   596	    event.preventDefault();
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  function onKey(event) {
   600	    if (inputDirs[event.key]) { event.preventDefault(); activateDirection(inputDirs[event.key]); return; }
   601	    if (event.key === ' ' || event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); if (gameState === 'gameover') restart(); else tryStartAudio(); }
   602	  }
   603	
   604	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   605	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   606	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   607	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointerStart = null; });
   608	  document.addEventListener('touchmove', event => { if (event.target === canvas || event.target.closest('#controls')) event.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   609	  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, { passive: false });
   610	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   611	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', updateOrientation);
   612	  stateCard.addEventListener('pointerup', event => { event.preventDefault(); restart(); });
   613	  stateCard.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') restart(); });
   614	  document.querySelectorAll('.pad').forEach(button => {
   615	    const activate = event => { event.preventDefault(); activateDirection(DIRS[button.dataset.dir]); };
   616	    button.addEventListener('pointerdown', activate, { passive: false });
   617	    button.addEventListener('click', activate, { passive: false });
   618	  });
   619

   306	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.8);

    57	  function rotateMatrix(matrix) {
    58	    const h = matrix.length;
    59	    const w = matrix[0].length;
    60	    const rotated = Array.from({ length: w }, () => Array(h).fill(0));
    61	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) rotated[x][h - 1 - y] = matrix[y][x];

    66	    const next = PIECES.map((_, i) => i);

    68	      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    69	      [next[i], next[j]] = [next[j], next[i]];
    70	    }

   119	  function collides(piece, dx = 0, dy = 0, shape = piece.shape) {
   120	    for (let y = 0; y < shape.length; y++) {
   121	      for (let x = 0; x < shape[y].length; x++) {
   122	        if (!shape[y][x]) continue;
   123	        const nx = piece.x + x + dx;
   124	        const ny = piece.y + y + dy;
   125	        if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny >= ROWS) return true;
   126	        if (ny >= 0 && board[ny][nx]) return true;
   127	      }
   128	    }
   129	    return false;
   130	  }

   132	  function move(dx) {
   133	    if (!running || !active) return false;
   134	    if (!collides(active, dx, 0)) { active.x += dx; return true; }
   135	    return false;

   139	    if (!running || !active) return;
   140	    const turned = rotateMatrix(active.shape);
   141	    const kicks = [0, -1, 1, -2, 2];

   143	      if (!collides(active, kick, 0, turned)) {

   154	    if (!collides(active, 0, 1)) { active.y++; score++; dropClock = 0; updateHud(); }
   155	    else lockPiece();

   158	  function hardDrop() {
   159	    if (!running || !active) return;
   160	    let distance = 0;
   161	    while (!collides(active, 0, 1)) { active.y++; distance++; }
   162	    score += distance * 2;

   169	    const currentType = active.type;
   170	    if (hold === null) { hold = currentType; spawnPiece(); }
   171	    else { const heldType = hold; hold = currentType; active = makePiece(heldType); if (collides(active)) finish('STACK FULL'); }

   175	  function lockPiece() {
   176	    if (!running || !active) return;
   177	    for (let y = 0; y < active.shape.length; y++) {
   178	      for (let x = 0; x < active.shape[y].length; x++) {
   179	        if (!active.shape[y][x]) continue;
   180	        const by = active.y + y;

   201	  function clearLines() {
   202	    const full = [];
   203	    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) if (board[y].every(Boolean)) full.push(y);
   204	    if (!full.length) return 0;
   205	    for (let i = full.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) board.splice(full[i], 1);
   206	    while (board.length < ROWS) board.unshift(Array(COLS).fill(null));
   207	    return full.length;
   208	  }

   210	  function emitLineParticles(count) {

   222	      });
   223	    }
   224	  }

   236	    }

   277	  function roundedRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   278	    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   279	    ctx.beginPath();
   280	    ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
   281	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius);
   282	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius);
   283	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius);
   284	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius);
   285	    ctx.closePath();
   286	  }

   288	  function draw() {

   295	    ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   296	    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(w * .32, h * .1, 0, w * .32, h * .1, w * .78);
   297	    glow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(107,231,187,${.075 + pulse * .02})`); glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(107,231,187,0)');
   298	    ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   299	
   300	    const offsetX = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake : 0;
   301	    const offsetY = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake : 0;
   302	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(offsetX, offsetY);
   303	    drawBoard();
   304	    drawSidePanel();
   305	    ctx.restore();
   306	    drawParticles(w, h);
   307	    if (!running) drawResult(w, h);
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  function drawBoard() {
   311	    const { x, y, cell, boardW, boardH } = layout;
   312	    ctx.save();
   313	    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.35)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22; ctx.shadowOffsetY = 8;
   314	    roundedRect(x - 4, y - 4, boardW + 8, boardH + 8, 13);
   315	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 10, 19, .78)'; ctx.fill();
   316	    ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
   317	    roundedRect(x - 3, y - 3, boardW + 6, boardH + 6, 12);
   318	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(145, 181, 220, .18)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   319	    for (let row = 0; row < ROWS; row++) {
   320	      for (let col = 0; col < COLS; col++) {
   321	        const gx = x + col * cell, gy = y + row * cell;
   322	        ctx.fillStyle = (row + col) % 2 ? 'rgba(28, 48, 76, .22)' : 'rgba(19, 36, 60, .22)';
   323	        ctx.fillRect(gx + 1, gy + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2);
   324	        const tile = board[row][col];
   325	        if (tile !== null) drawTile(gx, gy, cell, PIECES[tile], 1);
   326	      }
   327	    }
   328	    if (active && running) {
   329	      const ghostY = ghostLanding();
   330	      if (ghostY !== active.y) drawPiece(active, ghostY, .14, true);
   330	      if (ghostY !== active.y) drawPiece(active, ghostY, .14, true);
   331	      drawPiece(active, active.y, 1, false);
   332	    }
   333	    if (flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(219,255,244,${flash * .16})`; ctx.fillRect(x, y, boardW, boardH); }
   334	    ctx.restore();
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  function ghostLanding() {
   338	    let dy = 0;
   339	    while (!collides(active, 0, dy + 1)) dy++;
   340	    return active.y + dy;
   341	  }
   342	
   343	  function drawPiece(piece, yOverride, alpha, ghost) {
   344	    const { x, y, cell } = layout;
   345	    const data = PIECES[piece.type];
   346	    for (let py = 0; py < piece.shape.length; py++) for (let px = 0; px < piece.shape[py].length; px++) {
   347	      if (piece.shape[py][px]) drawTile(x + (piece.x + px) * cell, y + (yOverride + py) * cell, cell, data, alpha, ghost);
   348	    }
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  function drawTile(x, y, size, data, alpha = 1, ghost = false) {
   352	    const pad = Math.max(1.5, size * .075);
   353	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
   354	    if (ghost) {
   355	      roundedRect(x + pad, y + pad, size - pad * 2, size - pad * 2, size * .18);
   356	      ctx.fillStyle = data.color; ctx.fill();
   357	      ctx.strokeStyle = data.color; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size * .07); ctx.stroke();
   358	      ctx.restore(); return;
   359	    }
   360	    ctx.shadowColor = data.color; ctx.shadowBlur = size * .18;
   361	    roundedRect(x + pad, y + pad, size - pad * 2, size - pad * 2, size * .18);
   362	    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + size, y + size);
   363	    grad.addColorStop(0, data.glow); grad.addColorStop(.2, data.color); grad.addColorStop(1, shade(data.color, -.22));
   364	    ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
   365	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.26)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size * .035); ctx.stroke();
   366	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.2)';
   367	    roundedRect(x + pad * 2, y + pad * 1.6, size - pad * 4, Math.max(1.5, size * .08), size * .04); ctx.fill();
   368	    ctx.restore();
   369	  }
   370	
   371	  function shade(hex, amount) {
   372	    const value = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
   373	    const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, (value >> 16) + 255 * amount));
   374	    const g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, ((value >> 8) & 255) + 255 * amount));
   375	    const b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, (value & 255) + 255 * amount));
   376	    return `rgb(${r|0},${g|0},${b|0})`;
   377	  }
   378	
   379	  function drawSidePanel() {
   380	    const { sideX, sideW, y, boardH, cell } = layout;
   381	    const labelSize = Math.max(7, Math.min(9, cell * .34));
   382	    drawMiniPanel(sideX, y + 1, sideW, Math.min(boardH * .23, 112), 'HOLD', hold, true, labelSize);
   383	    drawMiniPanel(sideX, y + Math.min(boardH * .23, 112) + 12, sideW, Math.min(boardH * .49, 220), 'NEXT', queue, false, labelSize);
   384	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(167, 188, 216, .52)'; ctx.font = `700 ${labelSize}px system-ui`; ctx.letterSpacing = '.12em';
   385	    ctx.fillText(mode === 'sprint' ? `${Math.min(lines,40)} / 40 LINES` : `${lines} LINES`, sideX + 4, y + boardH - 26);
   386	  }
   387	
   388	  function drawMiniPanel(x, y, w, h, label, content, isHold, labelSize) {
   389	    roundedRect(x, y, w, h, 10); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(23, 39, 65, .55)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(145,181,220,.14)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   390	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(173, 197, 226, .63)'; ctx.font = `800 ${labelSize}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText(label, x + 7, y + 14);
   391	    if (isHold) { if (content !== null) drawMiniPiece(content, x + w / 2, y + h * .61, Math.min(15, w / 7)); return; }
   392	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (content[i] !== undefined) drawMiniPiece(content[i], x + w / 2, y + 31 + i * Math.min(48, h / 3.2), Math.min(11.5, w / 9));
   393	  }
   394	
   395	  function drawMiniPiece(type, cx, cy, cell) {
   396	    const shape = PIECES[type].shape;
   397	    const width = shape[0].length * cell, height = shape.length * cell;
   398	    for (let py = 0; py < shape.length; py++) for (let px = 0; px < shape[py].length; px++) if (shape[py][px]) drawTile(cx - width / 2 + px * cell, cy - height / 2 + py * cell, cell, PIECES[type], .95);
   399	  }
   400	
   401	  function drawParticles(w, h) {
   402	    ctx.save();
   403	    for (const particle of particles) {
   404	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, particle.life / particle.max);
   405	      ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
   406	      ctx.fillRect(particle.x, particle.y, particle.size, particle.size);
   407	    }
   408	    ctx.restore();
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  function drawResult(w, h) {
   412	    ctx.save();
   413	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 10, 19, .64)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   414	    const cx = w * .5, cy = h * .48;
   415	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   416	    ctx.fillStyle = result === 'SPRINT CLEAR' ? '#6be7bb' : '#f6cf57'; ctx.font = `850 ${Math.max(24, Math.min(38, w * .105))}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText(result, cx, cy - 13);
   417	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(235,244,255,.82)'; ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(9, Math.min(12, w * .03))}px system-ui`;
   418	    ctx.fillText(mode === 'marathon' ? `${score.toLocaleString()} POINTS` : formatTime(elapsed), cx, cy + 19);
   419	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(190,211,238,.58)'; ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(8, Math.min(10, w * .025))}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText('TAP TO RESTART', cx, cy + 47);
   420	    ctx.restore();
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function update(dt, now) {
   424	    if (running) {
   425	      elapsed = now - startedAt;
   426	      dropClock += dt;
   427	      if (dropClock >= gravityMs()) { dropClock = 0; if (!collides(active, 0, 1)) active.y++; else lockPiece(); }
   428	      updateHud();
   429	    }
   430	    shake *= Math.pow(.04, dt / 1000);
   431	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt / 1000);
   432	    for (const particle of particles) { particle.life -= dt / 1000; particle.x += particle.vx * dt / 1000; particle.y += particle.vy * dt / 1000; particle.vy += 260 * dt / 1000; }
   433	    particles = particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   434	  }
   435	
   436	  function loop(now) {
   437	    const dt = Math.min(50, now - lastFrame); lastFrame = now;
   438	    update(dt, now); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function handleAction(action) {
   442	    if (!running) { beginGame(mode); return; }
   443	    if (action === 'hold') holdPiece();
   444	    if (action === 'rotate') rotate();
   445	    if (action === 'drop') hardDrop();
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => handleAction(button.dataset.action)));
   449	  modeButtons.forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
   450	    modeButtons.forEach(item => item.classList.toggle('active', item === button));
   451	    beginGame(button.dataset.mode);
   452	  }));
   453	
   454	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
   455	    const k = event.key.toLowerCase();
   456	    if (['arrowleft','arrowright','arrowdown','arrowup',' ','c','z','a','d','s','w','enter'].includes(k)) event.preventDefault();
   457	    if (!running && (k === 'enter' || k === ' ')) return beginGame(mode);
   458	    if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') move(-1);
   459	    else if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') move(1);
   460	    else if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') softDrop();
   461	    else if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w' || k === 'z') rotate();
   462	    else if (k === ' ') hardDrop();
   463	    else if (k === 'c') holdPiece();
   464	  }, { passive: false });
   465	
   466	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
   467	    event.preventDefault();
   468	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   469	    pointer = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, t: performance.now() };
   470	  });
   471	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', (event) => {
   472	    event.preventDefault();
   473	    if (!pointer) return;
   474	    const dx = event.clientX - pointer.x, dy = event.clientY - pointer.y, dt = performance.now() - pointer.t;
   475	    pointer = null;
   476	    if (!running) return beginGame(mode);
   477	    const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   478	    if (distance > 24 && dy < -24) holdPiece();
   479	    else if (distance > 24 && dy > 24) { if (dt < 260 || Math.abs(dy) > 130) hardDrop(); else softDrop(); }
   480	    else if (Math.abs(dx) > 22) move(dx < 0 ? -1 : 1);
   481	    else {
   482	      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   483	      const localX = event.clientX - rect.left;
   484	      const localY = event.clientY - rect.top;
   485	      if (activePieceContains(localX, localY)) rotate();
   486	      else if (localX < rect.width * .5) move(-1); else move(1);
   487	    }
   488	  });
   489	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointer = null; });
   490	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   491	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   492	
   493	  function activePieceContains(localX, localY) {
   494	    if (!active) return false;
   495	    const boardX = Math.floor((localX - layout.x) / layout.cell);
   496	    const boardY = Math.floor((localY - layout.y) / layout.cell);
   497	    const px = boardX - active.x;
   498	    const py = boardY - active.y;
   499	    return py >= 0 && py < active.shape.length && px >= 0 && px < active.shape[py].length && Boolean(active.shape[py][px]);
   500	  }
   501	
   502	  resize();
   503	  beginGame('marathon');
   504	  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   505	})();

    83	    backingScale = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2, 960 / cssLong);
    84	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * backingScale));
    85	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * backingScale));
    86	    canvas.style.width = width + 'px';
    87	    canvas.style.height = height + 'px';
    88	    ctx.setTransform(backingScale, 0, 0, backingScale, 0, 0);
    89	    updateOrientation();
    90	  }
    91	
    92	  function updateOrientation() {
    93	    rotateCard.classList.toggle('visible', window.innerWidth > window.innerHeight);
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  function key(x, y) { return x + ',' + y; }
    97	
    98	  function createMaze() {
    99	    const cols = 17;
   100	    const rows = 25;
   101	    const walls = Array.from({ length: rows }, () => Array(cols).fill(true));
   102	    const cellW = (cols - 1) / 2;
   103	    const cellH = (rows - 1) / 2;
   104	    const seen = Array.from({ length: cellH }, () => Array(cellW).fill(false));
   105	    const stack = [{ x: Math.floor(cellW / 2), y: Math.floor(cellH / 2) }];
   106	    const carveCell = (cx, cy) => { walls[cy * 2 + 1][cx * 2 + 1] = false; };
   107	    carveCell(stack[0].x, stack[0].y);
   108	    seen[stack[0].y][stack[0].x] = true;
   109	    while (stack.length) {
   110	      const current = stack[stack.length - 1];
   111	      const candidates = [];
   112	      for (const d of [{ x: 0, y: -1 }, { x: 1, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 1 }, { x: -1, y: 0 }]) {
   113	        const nx = current.x + d.x;
   114	        const ny = current.y + d.y;
   115	        if (nx >= 0 && nx < cellW && ny >= 0 && ny < cellH && !seen[ny][nx]) candidates.push({ x: nx, y: ny, d });
   116	      }
   117	      if (!candidates.length) { stack.pop(); continue; }
   118	      const choice = candidates[Math.floor(random() * candidates.length)];
   119	      const gx = current.x * 2 + 1;
   120	      const gy = current.y * 2 + 1;
   121	      walls[gy + choice.d.y][gx + choice.d.x] = false;
   122	      carveCell(choice.x, choice.y);
   123	      seen[choice.y][choice.x] = true;
   124	      stack.push({ x: choice.x, y: choice.y });
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    // Add selective cross-links so the route has honest loops and alternate escapes.
   128	    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
   129	      const x = 1 + Math.floor(random() * (cols - 2));
   130	      const y = 1 + Math.floor(random() * (rows - 2));
   131	      if ((x + y) % 2 === 1) {
   132	        if (x < cols - 2 && walls[y][x + 1]) walls[y][x + 1] = false;
   133	        else if (y < rows - 2 && walls[y + 1][x]) walls[y + 1][x] = false;
   134	      }
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    const warpRow = rows % 2 === 0 ? rows / 2 : rows - 2;
   138	    walls[warpRow][0] = false;
   139	    walls[warpRow][1] = false;
   140	    walls[warpRow][cols - 2] = false;
   141	    walls[warpRow][cols - 1] = false;
   142	    // Keep the hub and its four exits legible on every seed.
   143	    const hub = { x: Math.floor(cols / 2), y: Math.floor(rows / 2) };
   144	    walls[hub.y][hub.x] = false;
   145	    for (const d of DIR_LIST) {
   146	      const nx = hub.x + d.x;
   147	      const ny = hub.y + d.y;
   148	      if (nx > 0 && nx < cols - 1 && ny > 0 && ny < rows - 1) walls[ny][nx] = false;
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    const powerNodes = [
   152	      { x: 1, y: 1, active: true }, { x: cols - 2, y: 1, active: true },
   153	      { x: 1, y: rows - 2, active: true }, { x: cols - 2, y: rows - 2, active: true }
   154	    ];
   155	    for (const node of powerNodes) walls[node.y][node.x] = false;
   156	
   157	    const pellets = new Set();
   158	    for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) {
   159	      for (let x = 0; x < cols; x++) {
   160	        if (!walls[y][x] && !(x === hub.x && y === hub.y) && !powerNodes.some(p => p.x === x && p.y === y)) pellets.add(key(x, y));
   161	      }
   162	    }
   163	    return { cols, rows, walls, warpRow, hub, powerNodes, pellets, totalPellets: pellets.size };
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  function buildLevel() {
   167	    setupRng((baseSeed + Math.imul(level, 2654435761)) >>> 0);
   168	    maze = createMaze();
   169	    player = { x: maze.hub.x, y: maze.hub.y, dir: DIRS.left, nextDir: DIRS.left, speed: 4.25 + level * .18 };
   170	    chasers = [
   171	      makeChaser('Vector', 'direct', COLORS.direct, 3.14 + level * .15, 0),
   172	      makeChaser('Fore', 'ahead', COLORS.ahead, 3.02 + level * .15, 1),
   173	      makeChaser('Tailwind', 'flank', COLORS.flank, 2.9 + level * .15, 2),
   174	      makeChaser('Driftlock', 'wander', COLORS.wander, 2.72 + level * .14, 3)
   175	    ];
   176	    powerTimer = 0;
   177	    hitCooldown = 1.1;
   178	    bannerTimer = 1.35;
   179	    shardCooldown = 8 + random() * 4;
   180	    shard = { active: false, x: maze.hub.x, y: maze.hub.y, phase: random() * TAU };
   181	    updateHud();
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  function makeChaser(name, type, color, speed, offset) {
   185	    const h = maze.hub;
   186	    const home = { x: h.x + (offset % 2 ? 0 : (offset === 0 ? -1 : 1)), y: h.y + (offset > 1 ? 1 : -1) };
   187	    const chaser = { name, type, color, speed, x: home.x, y: home.y, dir: DIRS.up, nextDir: DIRS.up, home, eaten: false, respawn: 0, locked: false, wanderTarget: null, pulse: random() * TAU };
   188	    if (!canMove(home.x, home.y)) { chaser.home = { x: h.x, y: h.y }; chaser.x = h.x; chaser.y = h.y; }
   189	    return chaser;
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  function canMove(x, y) {
   193	    if (!maze) return false;
   194	    if (y === maze.warpRow && (x < 0 || x >= maze.cols)) return true;
   195	    if (x < 0 || x >= maze.cols || y < 0 || y >= maze.rows) return false;
   196	    return !maze.walls[y][x];
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  function wrappedX(x) {
   200	    if (x < 0) return x + maze.cols;
   201	    if (x >= maze.cols) return x - maze.cols;
   202	    return x;
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  function legalDirs(x, y) {
   206	    return DIR_LIST.filter(d => canMove(x + d.x, y + d.y));
   207	  }
   208	
   209	  function tileDistance(a, b) {
   210	    let dx = Math.abs(a.x - b.x);
   211	    if (maze && dx > maze.cols / 2) dx = maze.cols - dx;
   212	    return dx + Math.abs(a.y - b.y);
   213	  }
   214	
   215	  function targetFor(chaser) {
   216	    const px = Math.round(player.x);
   217	    const py = Math.round(player.y);
   218	    if (chaser.type === 'direct') return { x: px, y: py };
   219	    if (chaser.type === 'ahead') return { x: px + player.dir.x * 5, y: py + player.dir.y * 5 };
   220	    if (chaser.type === 'flank') return { x: px - player.dir.x * 6, y: py - player.dir.y * 6 };
   221	    if (!chaser.locked && tileDistance(chaser, player) < 8) chaser.locked = true;
   222	    if (chaser.locked) return { x: px, y: py };
   223	    if (!chaser.wanderTarget || tileDistance(chaser, chaser.wanderTarget) < 2 || !canMove(chaser.wanderTarget.x, chaser.wanderTarget.y)) {
   224	      const open = [];
   225	      for (let y = 1; y < maze.rows - 1; y++) for (let x = 1; x < maze.cols - 1; x++) if (!maze.walls[y][x]) open.push({ x, y });
   226	      chaser.wanderTarget = open[Math.floor(random() * open.length)];
   227	    }
   228	    return chaser.wanderTarget;
   229	  }
   230	
   230	
   231	  function bfsDirection(startX, startY, target, flee) {
   232	    const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.cols - 1, Math.round(startX)));
   233	    const sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.rows - 1, Math.round(startY)));
   234	    let tx = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.cols - 1, Math.round(target.x)));
   235	    let ty = Math.max(0, Math.min(maze.rows - 1, Math.round(target.y)));
   236	    if (!canMove(tx, ty)) { tx = maze.hub.x; ty = maze.hub.y; }
   237	    const origin = key(sx, sy);
   238	    const queue = [{ x: sx, y: sy }];
   239	    const came = new Map([[origin, null]]);
   240	    while (queue.length) {
   241	      const current = queue.shift();
   242	      if (current.x === tx && current.y === ty) break;
   243	      for (const d of DIR_LIST) {
   244	        let nx = current.x + d.x;
   245	        const ny = current.y + d.y;
   246	        if (ny === maze.warpRow && (nx < 0 || nx >= maze.cols)) nx = wrappedX(nx);
   247	        if (!canMove(nx, ny)) continue;
   248	        const k = key(nx, ny);
   249	        if (!came.has(k)) { came.set(k, { x: current.x, y: current.y, d }); queue.push({ x: nx, y: ny }); }
   250	      }
   251	    }
   252	    const endKey = key(tx, ty);
   253	    if (!came.has(endKey)) return null;
   254	    let cursor = { x: tx, y: ty };
   255	    let first = null;
   256	    while (key(cursor.x, cursor.y) !== origin) {
   257	      const step = came.get(key(cursor.x, cursor.y));
   258	      if (!step) break;
   259	      first = step.d;
   260	      cursor = { x: step.x, y: step.y };
   261	    }
   262	    if (!flee) return first;
   263	    return null;
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  function fleeDirection(chaser) {
   267	    const options = legalDirs(Math.round(chaser.x), Math.round(chaser.y));
   268	    if (!options.length) return DIRS.none;
   269	    let bestDir = options[0];
   270	    let bestDistance = -Infinity;
   271	    for (const d of options) {
   272	      const nx = wrappedX(Math.round(chaser.x) + d.x);
   273	      const ny = Math.round(chaser.y) + d.y;
   274	      const distance = tileDistance({ x: nx, y: ny }, { x: player.x, y: player.y });
   275	      const jitter = random() * .35;
   276	      if (distance + jitter > bestDistance) { bestDistance = distance + jitter; bestDir = d; }
   277	    }
   278	    return bestDir;
   279	  }
   280	
   281	  function chooseChaserDir(chaser) {
   282	    const x = Math.round(chaser.x);
   283	    const y = Math.round(chaser.y);
   284	    let chosen = powerTimer > 0 ? fleeDirection(chaser) : bfsDirection(x, y, targetFor(chaser), false);
   285	    const options = legalDirs(x, y);
   286	    if (!chosen || !canMove(x + chosen.x, y + chosen.y)) {
   287	      const filtered = options.filter(d => !(d.x === -chaser.dir.x && d.y === -chaser.dir.y));
   288	      chosen = filtered[Math.floor(random() * Math.max(1, filtered.length))] || options[0] || DIRS.none;
   289	    }
   290	    chaser.nextDir = chosen;
   291	  }
   292	
   293	  function updateActor(actor, dt, isPlayer) {
   294	    const speed = actor.speed;
   295	    let remaining = speed * dt;
   296	    let guard = 0;
   297	    while (remaining > 0 && guard++ < 3) {
   298	      const tx = Math.round(actor.x);
   299	      const ty = Math.round(actor.y);
   300	      const atTile = Math.abs(actor.x - tx) < .04 && Math.abs(actor.y - ty) < .04;
   301	      if (atTile) {
   302	        actor.x = tx; actor.y = ty;
   303	        const requested = actor.nextDir || DIRS.none;
   304	        if (canMove(tx + requested.x, ty + requested.y)) actor.dir = requested;
   305	        else if (!canMove(tx + actor.dir.x, ty + actor.dir.y)) actor.dir = DIRS.none;
   306	        if (!isPlayer) chooseChaserDir(actor);
   307	        if (isPlayer && canMove(tx + actor.nextDir.x, ty + actor.nextDir.y)) actor.dir = actor.nextDir;
   308	        if (actor.dir.x === 0 && actor.dir.y === 0) break;
   309	      }
   310	      const step = Math.min(remaining, .18);
   311	      const nx = actor.x + actor.dir.x * step;
   312	      const ny = actor.y + actor.dir.y * step;
   313	      if (actor.dir.x && !canMove(Math.round(actor.x + actor.dir.x * .51), Math.round(actor.y))) {
   314	        actor.x = Math.round(actor.x); actor.dir = DIRS.none; break;
   315	      }
   316	      if (actor.dir.y && !canMove(Math.round(actor.x), Math.round(actor.y + actor.dir.y * .51))) {
   317	        actor.y = Math.round(actor.y); actor.dir = DIRS.none; break;
   318	      }
   319	      actor.x = nx; actor.y = ny;
   320	      if (actor.y === maze.warpRow && actor.x < 0) actor.x += maze.cols;
   321	      if (actor.y === maze.warpRow && actor.x >= maze.cols) actor.x -= maze.cols;
   322	      remaining -= step;
   323	    }
   324	  }
   325	
   326	  function addScore(points) {
   327	    score += points;
   328	    saveBest();
   329	    updateHud();
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  function updateHud() {
   333	    scoreEl.textContent = String(score).padStart(6, '0');
   334	    bestEl.textContent = String(best).padStart(6, '0');
   335	    levelEl.textContent = String(level).padStart(2, '0');
   336	    livesEl.textContent = '●'.repeat(Math.max(0, lives));
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  function spawnBurst(x, y, color, count, force) {
   340	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   341	      const angle = random() * TAU;
   342	      const speed = force * (.5 + random());
   343	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, life: .35 + random() * .55, max: .9, color, size: .06 + random() * .1 });
   344	    }
   345	  }
   346	
   347	  function loseLife() {
   348	    if (hitCooldown > 0 || gameState !== 'playing') return;
   349	    lives -= 1;
   350	    hitCooldown = 2;
   351	    shake = .55;
   352	    spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, '#ff648c', 26, 3.2);
   353	    updateHud();
   354	    if (lives <= 0) {
   355	      gameState = 'gameover';
   356	      showState('RUN ENDED', 'Final score ' + String(score).padStart(6, '0') + ' · best ' + String(best).padStart(6, '0'));
   357	      return;
   358	    }
   359	    player.x = maze.hub.x; player.y = maze.hub.y; player.dir = DIRS.left; player.nextDir = DIRS.left;
   360	    for (const chaser of chasers) { chaser.x = chaser.home.x; chaser.y = chaser.home.y; chaser.eaten = false; chaser.respawn = 0; }
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  function showState(title, copy) {
   364	    stateTitle.textContent = title;
   365	    stateCopy.textContent = copy;
   366	    stateCard.classList.add('visible');
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function hideState() { stateCard.classList.remove('visible'); }
   370	
   371	  function restart() {
   372	    score = 0; lives = 3; level = 1; baseSeed = (Date.now() ^ 0x9e3779b9) >>> 0;
   373	    gameState = 'playing'; hintTimer = 8; hideState(); buildLevel();
   374	  }
   375	
   376	  function activateDirection(dir) {
   377	    if (gameState === 'gameover') { restart(); return; }
   378	    if (!dir) return;
   379	    player.nextDir = dir;
   380	    hintTimer = 0;
   381	    tryStartAudio();
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  function checkPickups() {
   385	    const px = Math.round(player.x);
   386	    const py = Math.round(player.y);
   387	    const pelletKey = key(px, py);
   388	    if (maze.pellets.delete(pelletKey)) {
   389	      addScore(10);
   390	      spawnBurst(px, py, '#bfeaff', 3, .8);
   391	      if (maze.pellets.size === 0) {
   392	        addScore(500 * level);
   393	        level += 1;
   394	        buildLevel();
   395	        return;
   396	      }
   397	    }
   398	    for (const node of maze.powerNodes) {
   399	      if (node.active && node.x === px && node.y === py) {
   400	        node.active = false; powerTimer = 8; addScore(50); shake = .18;
   401	        spawnBurst(node.x, node.y, '#ffe36e', 18, 2.4);
   402	      }
   403	    }
   404	    if (shard.active && tileDistance(player, shard) < .75) {
   405	      shard.active = false; shardCooldown = 13 + random() * 7; addScore(250); shake = .25;
   406	      spawnBurst(shard.x, shard.y, '#ffbd62', 24, 2.6);
   407	    }
   408	  }
   409	
   410	  function checkChaserHits() {
   411	    if (hitCooldown > 0) return;
   412	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   413	      if (chaser.eaten || chaser.respawn > 0) continue;
   414	      if (tileDistance(player, chaser) < .58) {
   415	        if (powerTimer > 0) {
   416	          chaser.eaten = true; chaser.respawn = 1.15; chaser.x = maze.hub.x; chaser.y = maze.hub.y;
   417	          addScore(200); shake = .28; spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, chaser.color, 20, 2.7);
   418	        } else { loseLife(); }
   419	        break;
   420	      }
   421	    }
   422	  }
   423	
   424	  function update(dt) {
   425	    elapsed += dt;
   426	    const step = Math.min(dt, .045);
   427	    if (bannerTimer > 0) bannerTimer -= step;
   428	    if (hitCooldown > 0) hitCooldown -= step;
   429	    if (shake > 0) shake -= step;
   430	    if (powerTimer > 0) powerTimer = Math.max(0, powerTimer - step);
   430	    if (powerTimer > 0) powerTimer = Math.max(0, powerTimer - step);
   431	    if (hintTimer > 0) { hintTimer -= step; if (hintTimer <= 0) hintEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
   432	    if (gameState !== 'playing') { updateParticles(step); return; }
   433	
   434	    updateActor(player, step, true);
   435	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   436	      if (chaser.eaten) { chaser.respawn -= step; if (chaser.respawn <= 0) { chaser.eaten = false; chaser.x = maze.hub.x; chaser.y = maze.hub.y; chaser.dir = DIRS.up; } continue; }
   437	      updateActor(chaser, step, false);
   438	    }
   439	    checkPickups();
   440	    checkChaserHits();
   441	    if (shardCooldown > 0) shardCooldown -= step;
   442	    if (!shard.active && shardCooldown <= 0) { shard.active = true; shard.x = maze.hub.x; shard.y = maze.hub.y; shard.phase = elapsed; }
   443	    updateParticles(step);
   444	  }
   445	
   446	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   447	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   448	      const p = particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .95; p.vy *= .95;
   449	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   450	    }
   451	    if (particles.length > 180) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 180);
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function layout() {
   455	    const tile = Math.max(12, Math.min((width - 26) / maze.cols, (height - 160) / maze.rows));
   456	    return { tile, x: (width - tile * maze.cols) / 2, y: Math.max(92, (height - tile * maze.rows) / 2 + 4) };
   457	  }
   458	
   459	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   460	    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   461	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius); ctx.closePath();
   462	  }
   463	
   464	  function drawBackground() {
   465	    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
   466	    gradient.addColorStop(0, '#070b1c'); gradient.addColorStop(.5, '#0d1230'); gradient.addColorStop(1, '#180a27');
   467	    ctx.fillStyle = gradient; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
   468	    ctx.globalAlpha = .16;
   469	    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
   470	      const x = (i * 83 + 31) % width; const y = (i * 137 + 21) % height;
   471	      ctx.fillStyle = i % 3 === 0 ? '#72f4e1' : '#778bff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, (i % 3) + .6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   472	    }
   473	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   474	  }
   475	
   476	  function drawMaze(box) {
   477	    const t = box.tile;
   478	    ctx.save();
   479	    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(61, 99, 223, .28)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
   480	    ctx.fillStyle = '#080d27'; roundRect(box.x - 5, box.y - 5, maze.cols * t + 10, maze.rows * t + 10, 16); ctx.fill();
   481	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   482	    for (let y = 0; y < maze.rows; y++) {
   483	      for (let x = 0; x < maze.cols; x++) {
   484	        const px = box.x + x * t; const py = box.y + y * t;
   485	        if (maze.walls[y][x]) {
   486	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wall; roundRect(px + t * .08, py + t * .08, t * .84, t * .84, t * .17); ctx.fill();
   487	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wallHi; ctx.globalAlpha = .42; roundRect(px + t * .17, py + t * .14, t * .5, t * .09, t * .04); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   488	        } else {
   489	          ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.corridor; ctx.fillRect(px, py, t, t);
   490	        }
   491	      }
   492	    }
   493	    // Warp tunnel glows show the cross-screen escape clearly.
   494	    const wy = box.y + maze.warpRow * t + t / 2;
   495	    const warpGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(box.x, 0, box.x + t * 2, 0);
   496	    warpGradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(114,244,225,.1)'); warpGradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(114,244,225,0)');
   497	    ctx.fillStyle = warpGradient; ctx.fillRect(box.x, wy - t * .4, t * 2, t * .8);
   498	    const warpGradient2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(box.x + maze.cols * t, 0, box.x + (maze.cols - 2) * t, 0);
   499	    warpGradient2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(114,244,225,.1)'); warpGradient2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(114,244,225,0)');
   500	    ctx.fillStyle = warpGradient2; ctx.fillRect(box.x + (maze.cols - 2) * t, wy - t * .4, t * 2, t * .8);
   501	    ctx.restore();
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  function drawPickups(box) {
   505	    const t = box.tile;
   506	    for (const p of maze.pellets) {
   507	      const split = p.split(','); const x = Number(split[0]); const y = Number(split[1]);
   508	      ctx.fillStyle = '#b9eaff'; ctx.globalAlpha = .82; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (x + .5) * t, box.y + (y + .5) * t, Math.max(1.4, t * .075), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   509	    }
   510	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   511	    for (const node of maze.powerNodes) {
   512	      if (!node.active) continue;
   513	      const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 5 + node.x) * .13;
   514	      ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe36e'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   515	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe36e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (node.x + .5) * t, box.y + (node.y + .5) * t, t * .27 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   516	    }
   517	    if (shard && shard.active) {
   518	      const sx = box.x + (shard.x + .5) * t; const sy = box.y + (shard.y + .5) * t;
   519	      const spin = elapsed * 2.8 + shard.phase; const r = t * .36;
   520	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy); ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4 + Math.sin(spin) * .2); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffbd62'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffbd62'; roundRect(-r * .68, -r * .68, r * 1.36, r * 1.36, r * .18); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff2c9'; roundRect(-r * .28, -r * .5, r * .25, r * .72, r * .1); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   521	    }
   522	  }
   523	
   524	  function drawPlayer(box) {
   525	    if (hitCooldown > 0 && Math.floor(elapsed * 13) % 2 === 0) return;
   526	    const t = box.tile; const x = box.x + (player.x + .5) * t; const y = box.y + (player.y + .5) * t; const r = t * .42;
   527	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(player.dir.angle); ctx.shadowColor = COLORS.player; ctx.shadowBlur = 17; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.player; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * .72, -r * .82); ctx.lineTo(-r * .48, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * .72, r * .82); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.playerHi; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .12, 0, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   528	  }
   529	
   530	  function drawChasers(box) {
   531	    const t = box.tile;
   532	    for (const chaser of chasers) {
   533	      if (chaser.eaten) continue;
   534	      const x = box.x + (chaser.x + .5) * t; const y = box.y + (chaser.y + .5) * t; const r = t * .39;
   535	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.shadowColor = powerTimer > 0 ? '#6d80ff' : chaser.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 13;
   536	      ctx.fillStyle = powerTimer > 0 ? (powerTimer < 2 && Math.floor(elapsed * 9) % 2 ? '#eaf0ff' : '#5a68c8') : chaser.color;
   537	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -r * .08, r, Math.PI, 0); ctx.lineTo(r, r * .75); ctx.lineTo(r * .5, r * .52); ctx.lineTo(0, r * .78); ctx.lineTo(-r * .5, r * .52); ctx.lineTo(-r, r * .75); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   538	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f9ff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .32, -r * .08, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(r * .32, -r * .08, r * .18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#14204d'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * .28 + chaser.dir.x * 2, -r * .08 + chaser.dir.y * 2, r * .08, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(r * .36 + chaser.dir.x * 2, -r * .08 + chaser.dir.y * 2, r * .08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   539	    }
   540	  }
   541	
   542	  function drawParticles(box) {
   543	    const t = box.tile;
   544	    for (const p of particles) {
   545	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.max); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(box.x + (p.x + .5) * t, box.y + (p.y + .5) * t, p.size * t, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   546	    }
   547	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   548	  }
   549	
   550	  function draw() {
   551	    drawBackground();
   552	    if (!maze) return;
   553	    const box = layout();
   554	    const sx = shake > 0 ? (random() - .5) * shake * 12 : 0;
   555	    const sy = shake > 0 ? (random() - .5) * shake * 12 : 0;
   556	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   557	    drawMaze(box); drawPickups(box); drawParticles(box); drawChasers(box); drawPlayer(box);
   558	    ctx.restore();
   559	    if (bannerTimer > 0 && gameState === 'playing') {
   560	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, bannerTimer * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#eaf2ff'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '900 12px system-ui'; ctx.letterSpacing = '2px'; ctx.fillText('LEVEL ' + String(level).padStart(2, '0'), width / 2, box.y - 14); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   561	    }
   562	    if (powerTimer > 0) {
   563	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe36e'; ctx.globalAlpha = .9; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '900 10px system-ui'; ctx.fillText('HUNT ' + powerTimer.toFixed(1), width / 2, height - 111); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   564	    }
   565	  }
   566	
   567	  function frame(time) {
   568	    if (!lastTime) lastTime = time;
   569	    const dt = Math.min(.05, (time - lastTime) / 1000);
   570	    lastTime = time;
   571	    update(dt); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   572	  }
   573	
   574	  let audio = null;
   575	  function tryStartAudio() {
   576	    if (audio || !window.AudioContext && !window.webkitAudioContext) return;
   577	    try { audio = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (_) { audio = null; }
   578	  }
   579	
   580	  function pointerDown(event) {
   581	    if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' && event.button !== 0) return;
   582	    pointerStart = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, time: performance.now() };
   583	    if (event.target.closest && event.target.closest('.pad')) return;
   584	    event.preventDefault();
   585	  }
   586	
   587	  function pointerMove(event) { if (pointerStart) event.preventDefault(); }
   588	
   589	  function pointerUp(event) {
   590	    if (!pointerStart) return;
   591	    const start = pointerStart; pointerStart = null;
   592	    const dx = event.clientX - start.x; const dy = event.clientY - start.y;
   593	    if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < 18) { if (gameState === 'gameover') restart(); return; }
   594	    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) activateDirection(dx > 0 ? DIRS.right : DIRS.left);
   595	    else activateDirection(dy > 0 ? DIRS.down : DIRS.up);
   596	    event.preventDefault();
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  function onKey(event) {
   600	    if (inputDirs[event.key]) { event.preventDefault(); activateDirection(inputDirs[event.key]); return; }
   601	    if (event.key === ' ' || event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); if (gameState === 'gameover') restart(); else tryStartAudio(); }
   602	  }
   603	
   604	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   605	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   606	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   607	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointerStart = null; });
   608	  document.addEventListener('touchmove', event => { if (event.target === canvas || event.target.closest('#controls')) event.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   609	  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, { passive: false });
   610	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   611	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', updateOrientation);
   612	  stateCard.addEventListener('pointerup', event => { event.preventDefault(); restart(); });
   613	  stateCard.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') restart(); });
   614	  document.querySelectorAll('.pad').forEach(button => {
   615	    const activate = event => { event.preventDefault(); activateDirection(DIRS[button.dataset.dir]); };
   616	    button.addEventListener('pointerdown', activate, { passive: false });
   617	    button.addEventListener('click', activate, { passive: false });
   618	  });
   619	
   620	  resize();
   621	  buildLevel();
   622	  updateHud();
   623	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   624	})();

     1	/* Corridor Crawl - core utilities: rng, math, audio, particles */
     2	(function (g) {
     3	  'use strict';
     4	
     5	  function RNG(seed) { this.s = (seed >>> 0) || 0x9e3779b9; }
     6	  RNG.prototype.next = function () {
     7	    var x = this.s;
     8	    x ^= x << 13; x >>>= 0;
     9	    x ^= x >>> 17;
    10	    x ^= x << 5; x >>>= 0;
    11	    this.s = x; return x;
    12	  };
    13	  RNG.prototype.f = function () { return this.next() / 4294967296; };
    14	  RNG.prototype.int = function (a, b) { return a + Math.floor(this.f() * (b - a + 1)); };
    15	  RNG.prototype.pick = function (a) { return a[this.int(0, a.length - 1)]; };
    16	  RNG.prototype.chance = function (p) { return this.f() < p; };
    17	  RNG.prototype.shuffle = function (a) {
    18	    for (var i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { var j = this.int(0, i), t = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = t; }
    19	    return a;
    20	  };
    21	
    22	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
    23	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    24	  function sign(v) { return v < 0 ? -1 : (v > 0 ? 1 : 0); }
    25	  function dist(ax, ay, bx, by) { return Math.max(Math.abs(ax - bx), Math.abs(ay - by)); }
    26	
    27	  /* ---- audio: tiny WebAudio blip synth, no files ---- */
    28	  var AC = null, muted = false;
    29	  function audioInit() {
    30	    if (AC) return;
    31	    try {
    32	      var C = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    33	      if (C) AC = new C();
    34	    } catch (e) { AC = null; }
    35	  }
    36	  function resumeAudio() { if (AC && AC.state === 'suspended') AC.resume(); }
    37	  function blip(freq, dur, type, vol, slide) {
    38	    if (!AC || muted) return;
    39	    try {
    40	      var t = AC.currentTime;
    41	      var o = AC.createOscillator(), gn = AC.createGain();
    42	      o.type = type || 'square';
    43	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    44	      if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, freq * slide), t + dur);
    45	      gn.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
    46	      gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || 0.10, t + 0.008);
    47	      gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    48	      o.connect(gn); gn.connect(AC.destination);
    49	      o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    50	    } catch (e) { }
    51	  }
    52	  function noise(dur, vol) {
    53	    if (!AC || muted) return;
    54	    try {
    55	      var n = Math.floor(AC.sampleRate * dur);
    56	      var buf = AC.createBuffer(1, n, AC.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
    57	      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
    58	      var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = buf;
    59	      var gn = AC.createGain(); gn.gain.value = vol || 0.08;
    60	      var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = 1400;
    61	      s.connect(f); f.connect(gn); gn.connect(AC.destination); s.start();
    62	    } catch (e) { }
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  var SFX = {
    66	    step: function () { blip(180, 0.05, 'triangle', 0.05, 0.9); },
    67	    hit: function () { noise(0.10, 0.10); blip(240, 0.08, 'square', 0.07, 0.5); },
    68	    hurt: function () { blip(150, 0.18, 'sawtooth', 0.11, 0.4); },
    69	    kill: function () { blip(420, 0.16, 'square', 0.09, 0.35); noise(0.14, 0.09); },
    70	    pickup: function () { blip(660, 0.07, 'triangle', 0.08); blip(880, 0.09, 'triangle', 0.07); },
    71	    quaff: function () { blip(300, 0.16, 'sine', 0.09, 2.2); },
    72	    scroll: function () { blip(520, 0.22, 'sawtooth', 0.07, 1.8); },
    73	    stairs: function () { blip(220, 0.20, 'sine', 0.09, 1.9); },
    74	    bad: function () { blip(110, 0.30, 'sawtooth', 0.10, 0.6); },
    75	    win: function () { [523, 659, 784, 1047].forEach(function (f, i) { setTimeout(function () { blip(f, 0.28, 'triangle', 0.10); }, i * 130); }); },
    76	    die: function () { [330, 262, 196, 130].forEach(function (f, i) { setTimeout(function () { blip(f, 0.34, 'sawtooth', 0.10); }, i * 150); }); }
    77	  };
    78	
    79	  /* ---- particles ---- */
    80	  function Particles() { this.list = []; }
    81	  Particles.prototype.burst = function (x, y, color, n, spd, life) {
    82	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    83	      var a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, s = (0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6) * (spd || 90);
    84	      this.list.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, l: (life || 0.45) * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.6), m: (life || 0.45), c: color, r: 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.5 });
    85	    }
    86	  };
    87	  Particles.prototype.text = function (x, y, str, color) {
    88	    this.list.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: 0, vy: -34, l: 0.75, m: 0.75, c: color, txt: str });
    89	  };
    90	  Particles.prototype.update = function (dt) {
    91	    for (var i = this.list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    92	      var p = this.list[i];
    93	      p.l -= dt;
    94	      if (p.l <= 0) { this.list.splice(i, 1); continue; }
    95	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
    96	      if (!p.txt) { p.vy += 150 * dt; p.vx *= 0.96; }
    97	    }
    98	  };
    99	  Particles.prototype.draw = function (ctx) {
   100	    for (var i = 0; i < this.list.length; i++) {
   101	      var p = this.list[i], a = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p.l / p.m));
   102	      ctx.globalAlpha = a;
   103	      if (p.txt) {
   104	        ctx.fillStyle = p.c;
   105	        ctx.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace';
   106	        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   107	        ctx.fillText(p.txt, p.x, p.y);
   108	      } else {
   109	        ctx.fillStyle = p.c;
   110	        ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.r, p.y - p.r, p.r * 2, p.r * 2);
   111	      }
   112	    }
   113	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   114	  };
   115	  Particles.prototype.clear = function () { this.list.length = 0; };
   116	
   117	  g.CC = {
   118	    RNG: RNG, clamp: clamp, lerp: lerp, sign: sign, dist: dist,
   119	    audioInit: audioInit, resumeAudio: resumeAudio, SFX: SFX,
   120	    Particles: Particles,
   121	    setMuted: function (v) { muted = v; }, isMuted: function () { return muted; }
   122	  };
   123	})(window);
