    84	  function newGame() {
    85	    parts.clear(); shake = 0; flash = 0;
    86	    var seed = (Date.now() ^ (Math.random() * 0xffffffff)) >>> 0;
    87	    S = {
    88	      seed: seed, rng: new CC.RNG(seed),
    89	      depth: 1, maxDepth: 1, turn: 0, kills: 0, gold: 0,
    90	      state: 'play', log: [], hasCrown: false, ascending: false,
    91	      p: {
    92	        x: 0, y: 0, ax: 0, ay: 0, hp: 26, maxhp: 26, atk: [2, 5], def: 0,
    93	        lvl: 1, xp: 0, next: 12, food: 900, might: 0, haste: 0, ward: 0, regen: 0
    94	      },
    95	      inv: [], ident: {}, shade: {}, glyph: {},
    96	      level: null, mons: [], projectiles: [], hintT: 0, inspect: null
    97	    };
    98	    // randomised appearances per run
    99	    var sh = S.rng.shuffle(SHADES.slice()), gl = S.rng.shuffle(GLYPHS.slice()), i = 0;
   100	    for (var k in POTIONS) S.shade[k] = sh[i++];
   101	    i = 0; for (var k2 in SCROLLS) S.glyph[k2] = gl[i++];
   102	    S.inv.push({ key: 'ration', kind: 'food', n: 1 });
   103	    S.inv.push({ key: 'mend', kind: 'potion', n: 1 });
   104	    genFloor(1, false);
   105	    log('Depth 1. Something below is humming.', '#9fb0c8');
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  function genFloor(depth, arriveAtDown) {
   109	    var lvl = new CC.Level(depth, S.rng);
   110	    S.level = lvl; S.mons = []; S.projectiles = [];
   111	    lvl.items = []; lvl.golds = [];
   112	    var p = S.p;
   113	    if (arriveAtDown) { p.x = lvl.downx; p.y = lvl.downy; }
   114	    else { p.x = lvl.upx; p.y = lvl.upy; }
   115	    p.ax = p.x; p.ay = p.y;
   116	
   117	    var extra = S.hasCrown ? 4 : 0;
   118	    var count = 4 + Math.floor(depth * 0.9) + extra;
   119	    var cap = 20 + extra;
   120	    var avoid = [{ x: p.x, y: p.y }];
   121	    var attempts = 0;
   122	    for (var i = 0; i < count && S.mons.length < cap; i++) {
   123	      var key = pickMonster(depth);
   124	      var spot = lvl.randomFloor(S.rng, avoid);
   125	      if (CC.dist(spot.x, spot.y, p.x, p.y) < 6 && attempts++ < 60) { i--; avoid.push(spot); continue; }
   126	      if (monAt(spot.x, spot.y)) { if (attempts++ < 60) { i--; } continue; }
   127	      spawnMon(key, spot.x, spot.y, depth);
   128	      if (MON[key].pack) {
   129	        var n = S.rng.int(MON[key].pack[0], MON[key].pack[1]);
   130	        for (var j = 0; j < n; j++) {
   131	          var d = S.rng.pick(DIRS), nx = spot.x + d[0], ny = spot.y + d[1];
   132	          if (lvl.walkable(nx, ny) && !monAt(nx, ny)) spawnMon(key, nx, ny, depth);
   133	        }
   134	      }
   135	    }
   136	    // items
   137	    var nit = S.rng.int(2, 4) + (depth > 3 ? 1 : 0);
   138	    for (var q = 0; q < nit; q++) {
   139	      var s2 = lvl.randomFloor(S.rng);
   140	      lvl.items.push({ x: s2.x, y: s2.y, key: randomItemKey() });
   141	    }
   142	    var ng = S.rng.int(2, 4);
   143	    for (var q2 = 0; q2 < ng; q2++) {
   144	      var s3 = lvl.randomFloor(S.rng);
   145	      lvl.golds.push({ x: s3.x, y: s3.y, amt: S.rng.int(4, 12) + depth * 3 });
   146	    }
   147	    if (depth >= 8 && !S.hasCrown) {
   148	      var r = lvl.rooms[lvl.rooms.length - 1];
   149	      lvl.items.push({ x: r.cx, y: r.cy - 1 >= r.y ? r.cy - 1 : r.cy, key: 'crown' });
   150	      for (var b = 0; b < 3; b++) {
   151	        var d2 = DIRS[b * 2], bx = r.cx + d2[0] * 2, by = r.cy + d2[1] * 2;
   152	        if (lvl.walkable(bx, by) && !monAt(bx, by)) spawnMon('brute', bx, by, depth);
   153	      }
   154	    }
   155	    lvl.computeFov(p.x, p.y, fovRadius());
   156	    updateMonsterSight();
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  function fovRadius() { return 7; }
   160	
   161	  function pickMonster(depth) {
   162	    var pool = [];
   163	    for (var i = 0; i < MONKEYS.length; i++) {
   164	      var k = MONKEYS[i], m = MON[k];
   165	      if (depth < m.min) continue;
   166	      var w = m.w + (depth >= m.min + 2 ? 1 : 0);
   167	      for (var j = 0; j < w; j++) pool.push(k);
   168	    }
   169	    if (!pool.length) pool.push('rat');
   170	    return S.rng.pick(pool);
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  function spawnMon(key, x, y, depth, hpOverride, size) {
   174	    var t = MON[key];
   175	    var hp = hpOverride != null ? hpOverride : t.hp + Math.floor((depth - 1) * 1.6);
   176	    var m = {
   177	      key: key, name: t.name, x: x, y: y, ax: x, ay: y,
   178	      hp: hp, maxhp: Math.max(hp, t.hp), dmg: [t.dmg[0] + Math.floor((depth - 1) * 0.4), t.dmg[1] + Math.floor((depth - 1) * 0.6)],
   179	      def: t.def + (depth > 5 ? 1 : 0), xp: t.xp, spd: t.spd, col: t.col,
   180	      energy: 0, awake: false, fear: 0, loot: null, size: size == null ? 2 : size, hurt: 0
   181	    };
   182	    S.mons.push(m);
   183	    return m;
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  function randomItemKey() {
   187	    var r = S.rng.f();
   188	    if (r < 0.20) return 'ration';
   189	    if (r < 0.62) {
   190	      var pk = Object.keys(POTIONS);
   191	      var wts = { mend: 5, fury: 2, quick: 2, bile: 2, sight: 2 };
   192	      var pool = []; pk.forEach(function (k) { for (var i = 0; i < wts[k]; i++) pool.push(k); });
   193	      return S.rng.pick(pool);
   194	    }
   195	    return S.rng.pick(Object.keys(SCROLLS));
   196	  }
   197	
   198	  function itemKind(key) {
   199	    if (key === 'ration') return 'food';
   200	    if (key === 'crown') return 'crown';
   201	    if (POTIONS[key]) return 'potion';
   202	    return 'scroll';
   203	  }
   204	  function itemName(key) {
   205	    var k = itemKind(key);
   206	    if (k === 'food') return 'Dry Ration';
   207	    if (k === 'crown') return 'Crown of Echoes';
   208	    if (k === 'potion') return S.ident[key] ? 'Potion of ' + POTIONS[key].name : S.shade[key] + ' Potion';
   209	    return S.ident[key] ? 'Scroll of ' + SCROLLS[key].name : 'Scroll "' + S.glyph[key] + '"';
   210	  }
   211	  function itemDesc(key) {
   212	    var k = itemKind(key);
   213	    if (k === 'food') return 'Chewy. Quiets the hunger.';
   214	    if (k === 'crown') return 'It repeats what it hears. Carry it up and out.';
   215	    if (!S.ident[key]) return 'You have not tried this one yet.';
   216	    return (POTIONS[key] || SCROLLS[key]).desc;
   217	  }
   218	  function itemColor(key) {
   219	    var k = itemKind(key);
   220	    if (k === 'food') return '#c9a86a';
   221	    if (k === 'crown') return '#ffd24d';
   222	    if (k === 'potion') return POTIONS[key].col;
   223	    return '#dfe6f2';
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  function addItem(key) {
   227	    for (var i = 0; i < S.inv.length; i++) if (S.inv[i].key === key) { S.inv[i].n++; return true; }
   228	    if (S.inv.length >= 6) return false;
   229	    S.inv.push({ key: key, kind: itemKind(key), n: 1 });
   230	    return true;
   230	    return true;
   231	  }
   232	
   233	  function monAt(x, y) {
   234	    for (var i = 0; i < S.mons.length; i++) if (S.mons[i].x === x && S.mons[i].y === y && S.mons[i].hp > 0) return S.mons[i];
   235	    return null;
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   239	   * Player actions
   240	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   241	  function playerMove(dx, dy) {
   242	    if (S.state !== 'play') return;
   243	    var p = S.p, nx = p.x + dx, ny = p.y + dy;
   244	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) { useStairsIfAny(true); return; }
   245	    var m = monAt(nx, ny);
   246	    if (m) { playerAttack(m); endTurn(); return; }
   247	    if (!S.level.walkable(nx, ny)) { return; }
   248	    // no cutting diagonal corners through walls
   249	    if (dx !== 0 && dy !== 0 && !S.level.walkable(p.x + dx, p.y) && !S.level.walkable(p.x, p.y + dy)) return;
   250	    p.x = nx; p.y = ny;
   251	    CC.SFX.step();
   252	    pickupHere();
   253	    endTurn();
   254	    useStairsIfAny(false);
   255	  }
   256	
   257	  function pickupHere() {
   258	    var lvl = S.level, p = S.p, i;
   259	    for (i = lvl.golds.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   260	      if (lvl.golds[i].x === p.x && lvl.golds[i].y === p.y) {
   261	        S.gold += lvl.golds[i].amt;
   262	        log('+' + lvl.golds[i].amt + ' gold.', '#ffd24d');
   263	        parts.text(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y), '+' + lvl.golds[i].amt, '#ffd24d');
   264	        lvl.golds.splice(i, 1); CC.SFX.pickup();
   265	      }
   266	    }
   267	    for (i = lvl.items.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   268	      var it = lvl.items[i];
   269	      if (it.x !== p.x || it.y !== p.y) continue;
   270	      if (it.key === 'crown') {
   271	        S.hasCrown = true; lvl.items.splice(i, 1);
   272	        log('The Crown of Echoes is yours. CLIMB.', '#ffd24d');
   273	        CC.SFX.win(); shake = 14;
   274	        parts.burst(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y) + L.ts / 2, '#ffd24d', 34, 170, 0.9);
   275	        // the floor wakes up
   276	        for (var q = 0; q < S.mons.length; q++) S.mons[q].awake = true;
   277	        continue;
   278	      }
   279	      if (addItem(it.key)) {
   280	        log('Picked up ' + itemName(it.key) + '.', '#a9e6ff');
   281	        CC.SFX.pickup(); lvl.items.splice(i, 1);
   282	      } else { log('Your pack is full.', '#ff8a6a'); }
   283	    }
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  function useStairsIfAny(explicit) {
   287	    if (S.state !== 'play') return false;
   288	    var t = S.level.at(S.p.x, S.p.y);
   289	    if (t === T.DOWN) { descend(); return true; }
   290	    if (t === T.UP) { ascend(); return true; }
   291	    if (explicit) { endTurn(); }
   292	    return false;
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  function descend() {
   296	    S.depth++; if (S.depth > S.maxDepth) S.maxDepth = S.depth;
   297	    CC.SFX.stairs();
   298	    genFloor(S.depth, false);
   299	    log('Depth ' + S.depth + '.', '#9fb0c8');
   300	    if (S.depth === 8 && !S.hasCrown) log('The humming is right here.', '#ffd24d');
   301	  }
   302	  function ascend() {
   303	    if (S.depth === 1) {
   304	      if (S.hasCrown) { winGame(); }
   305	      else { log('The way out stays shut without the Crown.', '#ff8a6a'); }
   306	      return;
   307	    }
   308	    S.depth--; CC.SFX.stairs();
   309	    genFloor(S.depth, true);
   310	    log('Depth ' + S.depth + (S.hasCrown ? '. It follows you up.' : '.'), S.hasCrown ? '#ffb04d' : '#9fb0c8');
   311	  }
   312	
   313	  function playerAttack(m) {
   314	    var p = S.p;
   315	    var dmg = S.rng.int(p.atk[0], p.atk[1]) + p.might;
   316	    dmg = Math.max(1, dmg - S.rng.int(0, m.def));
   317	    hurtMon(m, dmg, true);
   318	  }
   319	
   320	  function hurtMon(m, dmg, byPlayer) {
   321	    m.hp -= dmg; m.awake = true; m.hurt = 0.22;
   322	    parts.text(sx(m.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(m.y) + L.ts * 0.3, '' + dmg, '#ffdf6a');
   323	    parts.burst(sx(m.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(m.y) + L.ts / 2, m.col, 8, 100, 0.35);
   324	    shake = Math.max(shake, 5);
   325	    CC.SFX.hit();
   326	    if (m.key === 'ooze' && m.hp > 1 && m.size > 0) splitOoze(m);
   327	    if (m.hp <= 0) killMon(m, byPlayer);
   328	  }
   329	
   330	  function splitOoze(m) {
   331	    var free = [];
   332	    for (var i = 0; i < DIRS.length; i++) {
   333	      var nx = m.x + DIRS[i][0], ny = m.y + DIRS[i][1];
   334	      if (S.level.walkable(nx, ny) && !monAt(nx, ny) && !(S.p.x === nx && S.p.y === ny)) free.push([nx, ny]);
   335	    }
   336	    if (!free.length) return;
   337	    var half = Math.max(1, Math.floor(m.hp / 2));
   338	    m.hp = Math.max(1, m.hp - half);
   339	    m.size--;
   340	    var spot = S.rng.pick(free);
   341	    var c = spawnMon('ooze', spot[0], spot[1], S.depth, half, m.size);
   342	    c.awake = true; c.name = 'Split Ooze';
   343	    parts.burst(sx(spot[0]) + L.ts / 2, sy(spot[1]) + L.ts / 2, '#66d97a', 10, 110, 0.4);
   344	    log('The ooze splits.', '#66d97a');
   345	  }
   346	
   347	  function killMon(m, byPlayer) {
   348	    m.hp = 0;
   349	    parts.burst(sx(m.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(m.y) + L.ts / 2, m.col, 18, 150, 0.6);
   350	    CC.SFX.kill();
   351	    if (m.loot) {
   352	      if (m.loot.gold) { S.gold += m.loot.gold; log('You take back ' + m.loot.gold + ' gold.', '#ffd24d'); }
   353	      if (m.loot.item) { addItem(m.loot.item); log('You take back the ' + itemName(m.loot.item) + '.', '#a9e6ff'); }
   354	    }
   355	    if (byPlayer) {
   356	      S.kills++;
   357	      S.p.xp += m.xp;
   358	      while (S.p.xp >= S.p.next) {
   359	        S.p.xp -= S.p.next; S.p.lvl++; S.p.next = 10 + S.p.lvl * 8;
   360	        S.p.maxhp += 5; S.p.hp = Math.min(S.p.maxhp, S.p.hp + 5);
   361	        S.p.atk[0]++; S.p.atk[1]++;
   362	        log('You steady. Level ' + S.p.lvl + '.', '#4ee08a');
   363	        parts.text(sx(S.p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(S.p.y) - 6, 'LEVEL UP', '#4ee08a');
   364	      }
   365	    }
   366	    var i = S.mons.indexOf(m); if (i >= 0) S.mons.splice(i, 1);
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function hurtPlayer(dmg, src) {
   370	    var p = S.p;
   371	    dmg = Math.max(1, dmg - p.ward - S.rng.int(0, p.def));
   372	    p.hp -= dmg;
   373	    parts.text(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y) + L.ts * 0.3, '-' + dmg, '#ff6a6a');
   374	    shake = Math.max(shake, 8); flash = 0.25; flashCol = '#ff3b3b';
   375	    CC.SFX.hurt();
   376	    if (p.hp <= 0) { p.hp = 0; die(src); }
   377	  }
   378	
   379	  function die(src) {
   380	    if (S.state !== 'play') return;
   381	    S.state = 'dead';
   382	    S.finalScore = score();
   383	    saveBest(S.finalScore);
   384	    S.deathBy = src || 'the dark';
   385	    CC.SFX.die(); shake = 16;
   386	  }
   387	  function winGame() {
   388	    S.state = 'won';
   389	    S.finalScore = score() + 100;
   390	    saveBest(S.finalScore);
   391	    CC.SFX.win(); shake = 10;
   392	  }
   393	  function score() { return S.maxDepth * 10 + S.kills * 3 + S.gold; }
   394	  function saveBest(v) {
   395	    if (v > best) { best = v; try { localStorage.setItem('cc_best', String(best)); } catch (e) { } }
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   399	   * Items in use
   400	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   401	  function useSlot(i) {
   402	    if (S.state !== 'play') return;
   403	    var slot = S.inv[i]; if (!slot) return;
   404	    var key = slot.key;
   405	    slot.n--; if (slot.n <= 0) S.inv.splice(i, 1);
   406	    applyItem(key);
   407	    endTurn();
   408	  }
   409	
   410	  function applyItem(key) {
   411	    var p = S.p, kind = itemKind(key);
   412	    if (kind === 'food') {
   413	      p.food = Math.min(1200, p.food + 550);
   414	      log('You eat. Steadier now.', '#c9a86a'); CC.SFX.quaff(); return;
   415	    }
   416	    var wasNew = !S.ident[key];
   417	    S.ident[key] = true;
   418	    if (kind === 'potion') {
   419	      CC.SFX.quaff();
   420	      parts.burst(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y) + L.ts / 2, POTIONS[key].col, 16, 110, 0.6);
   421	      if (key === 'mend') {
   422	        var heal = Math.floor((p.maxhp - p.hp) * 0.6) + 8;
   423	        p.hp = Math.min(p.maxhp, p.hp + heal);
   424	        log('Mending: +' + heal + ' HP.', '#4ee08a');
   425	      } else if (key === 'fury') { p.might += 3; p.furyT = 24; log('Fury: your swings bite.', '#ff7a4d'); }
   426	      else if (key === 'quick') { p.haste = 14; log('Quickening: the dungeon lags.', '#ffe14d'); }
   427	      else if (key === 'bile') { log('Bile! That was a mistake.', '#ff6a6a'); CC.SFX.bad(); hurtPlayer(6 + S.depth, 'a bad draught'); }
   428	      else if (key === 'sight') {
   429	        p.sight = 40; log('Clarity: you see further.', '#4dc9ff');
   430	      }
--- game loop/render ---
   430	      }
   431	    } else {
   432	      CC.SFX.scroll();
   433	      parts.burst(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y) + L.ts / 2, '#dfe6f2', 16, 120, 0.6);
   434	      if (key === 'blink') {
   435	        var s = S.level.randomFloor(S.rng);
   436	        p.x = s.x; p.y = s.y; p.ax = s.x; p.ay = s.y;
   437	        log('Displacement: elsewhere.', '#a9e6ff');
   438	      } else if (key === 'flame') {
   439	        var n = 0;
   440	        for (var i = S.mons.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   441	          var m = S.mons[i];
   442	          if (CC.dist(m.x, m.y, p.x, p.y) <= 5 && S.level.los(p.x, p.y, m.x, m.y)) {
   443	            hurtMon(m, 9 + S.depth, true); n++;
   444	          }
   445	        }
   446	        shake = 12; flash = 0.2; flashCol = '#ff9a3b';
   447	        log(n ? 'Scorching: ' + n + ' seared.' : 'Scorching: nothing to burn.', '#ff9a3b');
   448	      } else if (key === 'ward') { p.ward = 3; p.wardT = 22; log('Warding: blows glance off.', '#4dc9ff'); }
   449	      else if (key === 'terror') {
   450	        var c = 0;
   451	        for (var j = 0; j < S.mons.length; j++) {
   452	          var mm = S.mons[j];
   453	          if (CC.dist(mm.x, mm.y, p.x, p.y) <= 7) { mm.fear = 12; c++; }
   454	        }
   455	        log(c ? 'Terror: ' + c + ' turn to flee.' : 'Terror: nothing hears it.', '#c96de0');
   456	      } else if (key === 'mapping') {
   457	        S.level.seen.fill(1);
   458	        log('Surveying: the floor unfolds.', '#a9e6ff');
   459	      }
   460	    }
   461	    if (wasNew) log('It was ' + itemName(key) + '.', '#9fb0c8');
   462	  }
   463	
   464	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   465	   * Turn resolution
   466	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   467	  function endTurn() {
   468	    if (S.state !== 'play') return;
   469	    var p = S.p;
   470	    S.turn++;
   471	
   472	    // hunger + regen
   473	    p.food--;
   474	    if (p.food <= 0) {
   475	      p.food = 0;
   476	      if (S.turn % 6 === 0) { p.hp -= 1; parts.text(sx(p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(p.y), 'starving', '#ff8a6a'); if (p.hp <= 0) { die('hunger'); return; } }
   477	    } else {
   478	      var rate = p.food > 300 ? 11 : 22;
   479	      p.regen++;
   480	      if (p.regen >= rate && p.hp < p.maxhp) { p.regen = 0; p.hp++; }
   481	    }
   482	    if (p.food === 200) log('Hunger gnaws. Regeneration slows.', '#ff8a6a');
   483	    if (p.food === 1) log('You are starving.', '#ff6a6a');
   484	
   485	    // buff timers
   486	    if (p.furyT > 0) { p.furyT--; if (p.furyT === 0) { p.might = Math.max(0, p.might - 3); log('The fury fades.', '#9fb0c8'); } }
   487	    if (p.wardT > 0) { p.wardT--; if (p.wardT === 0) { p.ward = 0; log('The ward fades.', '#9fb0c8'); } }
   488	    if (p.haste > 0) p.haste--;
   489	    if (p.sight > 0) p.sight--;
   490	
   491	    S.level.computeFov(p.x, p.y, p.sight > 0 ? 12 : fovRadius());
   492	    updateMonsterSight();
   493	
   494	    // monsters act
   495	    var gain = p.haste > 0 ? 0.5 : 1;
   496	    for (var i = S.mons.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   497	      var m = S.mons[i];
   498	      if (m.hp <= 0) continue;
   499	      m.energy += m.spd * gain;
   500	      var guard = 0;
   501	      while (m.energy >= 1 && m.hp > 0 && S.state === 'play' && guard++ < 3) {
   502	        m.energy -= 1;
   503	        monTurn(m);
   504	      }
   505	      if (m.fear > 0) m.fear--;
   506	    }
   507	    S.level.computeFov(p.x, p.y, p.sight > 0 ? 12 : fovRadius());
   508	    updateMonsterSight();
   509	  }
   510	
   511	  function updateMonsterSight() {
   512	    for (var i = 0; i < S.mons.length; i++) {
   513	      var m = S.mons[i];
   514	      if (!m.awake && CC.dist(m.x, m.y, S.p.x, S.p.y) <= 8 && S.level.los(m.x, m.y, S.p.x, S.p.y)) m.awake = true;
   515	    }
   516	  }
   517	
   518	  function monTurn(m) {
   519	    var p = S.p, d = CC.dist(m.x, m.y, p.x, p.y);
   520	    if (!m.awake) {
   521	      if (S.rng.chance(0.25)) wander(m);
   522	      return;
   523	    }
   524	    if (m.fear > 0) { fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y); return; }
   525	
   526	    switch (m.key) {
   527	      case 'archer': return archerTurn(m, d);
   528	      case 'thief': return thiefTurn(m, d);
   529	      case 'rat': return ratTurn(m, d);
   530	      case 'brute': return bruteTurn(m, d);
   531	      case 'stalker': return stalkerTurn(m, d);
   532	      default:
   533	        if (d <= 1) monAttack(m); else moveToward(m, p.x, p.y);
   534	    }
   535	  }
   536	
   537	  function ratTurn(m, d) {
   538	    if (d <= 1) {
   539	      var bonus = 0;
   540	      for (var i = 0; i < S.mons.length; i++) {
   541	        var o = S.mons[i];
   542	        if (o !== m && o.key === 'rat' && CC.dist(o.x, o.y, S.p.x, S.p.y) <= 1) bonus++;
   543	      }
   544	      monAttack(m, bonus, bonus ? 'the pack' : null);
   545	    } else moveToward(m, S.p.x, S.p.y);
   546	  }
   547	
   548	  function bruteTurn(m, d) {
   549	    if (d <= 1) {
   550	      monAttack(m, 0, null, true);
   551	    } else moveToward(m, S.p.x, S.p.y);
   552	  }
   553	
   554	  function stalkerTurn(m, d) {
   555	    if (d <= 1) monAttack(m);
   556	    else moveToward(m, S.p.x, S.p.y);
   557	  }
   558	
   559	  function archerTurn(m, d) {
   560	    var p = S.p;
   561	    if (d <= 1) { fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y); return; }
   562	    if (d <= 6 && S.level.los(m.x, m.y, p.x, p.y)) {
   563	      if (d < 3 && S.rng.chance(0.5)) { fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y); return; }
   564	      if (S.rng.chance(0.7)) {
   565	        for (var q = 1; q <= 6; q++) {
   566	          var f = q / 7;
   567	          parts.burst(sx(m.x + (p.x - m.x) * f) + L.ts / 2, sy(m.y + (p.y - m.y) * f) + L.ts / 2, '#e0d24a', 2, 22, 0.3);
   568	        }
   569	        var dmg = S.rng.int(m.dmg[0], m.dmg[1]);
   570	        log(m.name + ' looses a quill.', '#e0d24a');
   571	        hurtPlayer(dmg, m.name);
   572	        return;
   573	      }
   574	      return; // hold position
   575	    }
   576	    moveToward(m, p.x, p.y);
   577	  }
   578	
   579	  function thiefTurn(m, d) {
   580	    var p = S.p;
   581	    if (m.loot) {
   582	      fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y); fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y);
   583	      if (CC.dist(m.x, m.y, p.x, p.y) >= 13) {
   584	        log(m.name + ' vanishes with your things.', '#ff8a6a');
   585	        var i = S.mons.indexOf(m); if (i >= 0) S.mons.splice(i, 1);
   586	      }
   587	      return;
   588	    }
   589	    if (d <= 1) {
   590	      if (S.gold > 0 && S.rng.chance(0.6)) {
   591	        var take = Math.max(1, Math.floor(S.gold * 0.5));
   592	        S.gold -= take; m.loot = { gold: take };
   593	        log(m.name + ' snatches ' + take + ' gold!', '#ff8a6a');
   594	      } else if (S.inv.length) {
   595	        var si = S.rng.int(0, S.inv.length - 1), s = S.inv[si];
   596	        m.loot = { item: s.key };
   597	        s.n--; if (s.n <= 0) S.inv.splice(si, 1);
   598	        log(m.name + ' lifts your ' + itemName(m.loot.item) + '!', '#ff8a6a');
   599	      } else { monAttack(m); return; }
   600	      CC.SFX.bad(); flash = 0.2; flashCol = '#5ad6d6';
   601	      fleeFrom(m, p.x, p.y);
   602	      return;
   603	    }
   604	    moveToward(m, p.x, p.y);
   605	  }
   606	
   607	  function monAttack(m, bonus, why, knock) {
   608	    var dmg = S.rng.int(m.dmg[0], m.dmg[1]) + (bonus || 0);
   609	    parts.burst(sx(S.p.x) + L.ts / 2, sy(S.p.y) + L.ts / 2, m.col, 8, 90, 0.35);
   610	    log(m.name + (why ? ' (with ' + why + ')' : '') + ' hits you.', '#ff8a6a');
   611	    hurtPlayer(dmg, m.name);
   612	    if (knock && S.state === 'play') {
   613	      var dx = CC.sign(S.p.x - m.x), dy = CC.sign(S.p.y - m.y);
   614	      var nx = S.p.x + dx, ny = S.p.y + dy;
   615	      if (S.level.walkable(nx, ny) && !monAt(nx, ny)) {
   616	        S.p.x = nx; S.p.y = ny; shake = 12;
   617	        log('You are shoved back.', '#9aa4b4');
   618	        S.level.computeFov(S.p.x, S.p.y, S.p.sight > 0 ? 12 : fovRadius());
   619	      }
   620	    }
   621	  }
   622	
   623	  function stepOk(m, nx, ny) {
   624	    if (!S.level.walkable(nx, ny)) return false;
   625	    if (monAt(nx, ny)) return false;
   626	    if (S.p.x === nx && S.p.y === ny) return false;
   627	    return true;
   628	  }
   629	
   630	  function moveToward(m, tx, ty) {
   631	    var dx = CC.sign(tx - m.x), dy = CC.sign(ty - m.y);
   632	    var opts = [[dx, dy], [dx, 0], [0, dy]];
   633	    if (Math.abs(tx - m.x) < Math.abs(ty - m.y)) opts = [[dx, dy], [0, dy], [dx, 0]];
   634	    opts.push([dx, dy === 0 ? (S.rng.chance(0.5) ? 1 : -1) : 0]);
   635	    for (var i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
   636	      var o = opts[i];
   637	      if (o[0] === 0 && o[1] === 0) continue;
   638	      var nx = m.x + o[0], ny = m.y + o[1];
   639	      if (nx === S.p.x && ny === S.p.y) { monAttack(m); return; }
   640	      if (stepOk(m, nx, ny)) { m.x = nx; m.y = ny; return; }
   641	    }
   642	  }
   643	
   644	  function fleeFrom(m, tx, ty) {
   645	    var dx = -CC.sign(tx - m.x), dy = -CC.sign(ty - m.y);
   646	    var opts = [[dx, dy], [dx, 0], [0, dy], [dy, dx], [-dy, -dx]];
   647	    for (var i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
   648	      var o = opts[i];
   649	      if (o[0] === 0 && o[1] === 0) continue;
   650	      var nx = m.x + o[0], ny = m.y + o[1];
   651	      if (stepOk(m, nx, ny)) { m.x = nx; m.y = ny; return; }
   652	    }
   653	  }
   654	
   655	  function wander(m) {
   656	    var d = S.rng.pick(DIRS);
   657	    if (stepOk(m, m.x + d[0], m.y + d[1])) { m.x += d[0]; m.y += d[1]; }
   658	  }
   659	
   660	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   661	   * Camera & coords
   662	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   663	  var cam = { x: 0, y: 0 };
   664	  function updateCam(inst) {
   665	    var tx = CC.clamp(S.p.ax - (L.cols - 1) / 2, 0, Math.max(0, CC.MAPW - L.cols));
   666	    var ty = CC.clamp(S.p.ay - (L.rows - 1) / 2, 0, Math.max(0, CC.MAPH - L.rows));
   667	    if (inst) { cam.x = tx; cam.y = ty; }
   668	    else { cam.x = CC.lerp(cam.x, tx, 0.25); cam.y = CC.lerp(cam.y, ty, 0.25); }
   669	  }
   670	  function sx(tx) { return L.mapX0 + (tx - cam.x) * L.ts; }
   671	  function sy(ty) { return L.mapY0 + (ty - cam.y) * L.ts; }
   672	  function screenToTile(px, py) {
   673	    return { x: Math.floor((px - L.mapX0) / L.ts + cam.x), y: Math.floor((py - L.mapY0) / L.ts + cam.y) };
   674	  }
   675	
   676	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   677	   * Input
   678	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   679	  var press = null, LONG = 430;
   680	
   681	  function canvasPos(ev, touch) {
   682	    var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   683	    var cx = touch ? touch.clientX : ev.clientX, cy = touch ? touch.clientY : ev.clientY;
   684	    return { x: cx - r.left, y: cy - r.top };
   685	  }
   686	
   687	  function onDown(x, y) {
   688	    CC.audioInit(); CC.resumeAudio();
   689	    press = { x: x, y: y, t: performance.now(), moved: false, fired: false };
   690	  }
   691	  function onMove(x, y) {
   692	    if (!press) return;
   693	    if (Math.abs(x - press.x) > 14 || Math.abs(y - press.y) > 14) press.moved = true;
   694	  }
   695	  function onUp(x, y) {
   696	    if (!press) return;
   697	    var p = press; press = null;
   698	    if (p.fired) return;
   699	    handleTap(p.x, p.y);
   700	  }
   701	
   702	  function handleTap(x, y) {
   703	    if (S.inspect) { S.inspect = null; return; }
   704	    if (S.state === 'dead' || S.state === 'won') { newGame(); updateCam(true); return; }
   705	    // mute toggle
   706	    if (x > L.w - 46 && y < 46) { CC.setMuted(!CC.isMuted()); return; }
   707	    if (y >= L.barY) { tapBar(x, y); return; }
   708	    if (y < L.mapY) return;
   709	    var t = screenToTile(x, y);
   710	    var dx = CC.clamp(t.x - S.p.x, -1, 1), dy = CC.clamp(t.y - S.p.y, -1, 1);
   711	    if (t.x === S.p.x && t.y === S.p.y) { playerMove(0, 0); return; }
   712	    playerMove(dx, dy);
   713	  }
   714	
   715	  function tapBar(x, y) {
   716	    var n = 6, pad = 6;
   717	    var slotW = (L.w - pad * (n + 1)) / n;
   718	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   719	      var bx = pad + i * (slotW + pad);
   720	      if (x >= bx - pad / 2 && x <= bx + slotW + pad / 2) { useSlot(i); return; }
   721	    }
   722	  }
   723	
   724	  function inspectAt(x, y) {
   725	    if (y >= L.barY) {
   726	      var n = 6, pad = 6, slotW = (L.w - pad * (n + 1)) / n;
   727	      var i = Math.floor((x - pad) / (slotW + pad));
   728	      var s = S.inv[i];
   729	      if (s) S.inspect = { title: itemName(s.key), body: itemDesc(s.key) + '\n\nYou carry ' + s.n + '.' };
   730	      return;
   731	    }
   732	    if (y < L.mapY) return;
   733	    var t = screenToTile(x, y);
   734	    var lvl = S.level;
   735	    if (t.x < 0 || t.y < 0 || t.x >= CC.MAPW || t.y >= CC.MAPH || !lvl.seen[t.y * CC.MAPW + t.x]) {
   736	      S.inspect = { title: 'Unknown', body: 'You have not been there.' }; return;
   737	    }
   738	    if (t.x === S.p.x && t.y === S.p.y) {
   739	      S.inspect = {
   740	        title: 'You', body: 'Level ' + S.p.lvl + '  HP ' + S.p.hp + '/' + S.p.maxhp +
   741	          '\nDamage ' + (S.p.atk[0] + S.p.might) + '-' + (S.p.atk[1] + S.p.might) +
   742	          '\nGold ' + S.gold + '  Kills ' + S.kills + (S.hasCrown ? '\nYou carry the Crown of Echoes.' : '')
   743	      };
   744	      return;
   745	    }
   746	    var m = monAt(t.x, t.y);
   747	    var vis = lvl.vis[t.y * CC.MAPW + t.x];
   748	    if (m && vis && (m.key !== 'stalker' || CC.dist(m.x, m.y, S.p.x, S.p.y) <= 1)) {
   749	      S.inspect = { title: m.name, body: monDesc(m.key) + '\n\nHP ' + m.hp + '/' + m.maxhp + '  hits for ' + m.dmg[0] + '-' + m.dmg[1] };
   750	      return;
   751	    }
   752	    var it = null;
   753	    for (var i = 0; i < lvl.items.length; i++) if (lvl.items[i].x === t.x && lvl.items[i].y === t.y) it = lvl.items[i];
   754	    if (it) { S.inspect = { title: itemName(it.key), body: itemDesc(it.key) }; return; }
   755	    for (var j = 0; j < lvl.golds.length; j++) if (lvl.golds[j].x === t.x && lvl.golds[j].y === t.y) {
   756	      S.inspect = { title: 'Gold', body: 'A loose pile: ' + lvl.golds[j].amt + '.' }; return;
   757	    }
   758	    var tt = lvl.at(t.x, t.y);
   759	    if (tt === T.WALL) S.inspect = { title: 'Wall', body: 'Cold cut stone.' };
   760	    else if (tt === T.DOWN) S.inspect = { title: 'Stairs Down', body: 'Step on them to go deeper.' };
--- game touch/keys/end ---
  1030	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 1.5, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
  1031	    }
  1032	    if (S.p.haste > 0) {
  1033	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,225,77,0.5)';
  1034	      ctx.fillRect(cx - r * 1.4, cy + r * 1.2, r * 2.8, 2);
  1035	    }
  1036	  }

  1081	  }

  1083	  function drawLog() {
  1084	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0d15'; ctx.fillRect(0, L.hudH, L.w, L.logH);
  1085	    ctx.font = '11px ' + F; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  1086	    var n = S.log.length;
  1087	    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
  1088	      var e = S.log[n - 2 + i];

  1090	      ctx.globalAlpha = i === 1 ? 1 : 0.55;
  1091	      ctx.fillStyle = e.c;
  1092	      ctx.fillText(clip(e.t, L.w - 16), 8, L.hudH + 9 + i * 14);

  1096	  function clip(t, w) {
  1097	    var max = Math.floor(w / 6.2);
  1098	    return t.length > max ? t.slice(0, max - 1) + '…' : t;
  1099	  }

  1101	  function drawBar() {
  1102	    var w = L.w, y = L.barY, h = L.barH;
  1103	    ctx.fillStyle = '#101420'; ctx.fillRect(0, y, w, h);

  1120	    }
  1121	    var lbl = S.inv.length ? 'tap an item to use · hold anything to inspect' : 'no items — walk over things to pick them up';
  1122	    ctx.fillStyle = '#5b6883'; ctx.font = '10px ' + F; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
  1123	    ctx.fillText(lbl, w / 2, y + h - 3);
  1124	  }

  1126	  function drawInspect() {
  1127	    var w = L.w;
  1128	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,6,12,0.82)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, L.h);
  1129	    var bw = Math.min(320, w - 40), bh = 190, bx = (w - bw) / 2, by = (L.h - bh) / 2;
  1130	    ctx.fillStyle = '#141a28'; ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
  1131	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#38445e'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
  1132	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e9f2ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 15px ' + F; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
  1133	    ctx.fillText(S.inspect.title, w / 2, by + 18);
  1134	    ctx.font = '12px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#a9b6cc';
  1135	    wrapText(S.inspect.body, w / 2, by + 52, bw - 34, 17);
  1136	    ctx.fillStyle = '#5b6883'; ctx.font = '11px ' + F;
  1137	    ctx.fillText('tap to close', w / 2, by + bh - 24);
  1138	  }
  1139	
  1140	  function wrapText(text, cx, y, maxw, lh) {
  1141	    var paras = String(text).split('\n');
  1142	    for (var p = 0; p < paras.length; p++) {
  1143	      var words = paras[p].split(' '), line = '';
  1144	      for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
  1145	        var test = line ? line + ' ' + words[i] : words[i];
  1146	        if (ctx.measureText(test).width > maxw && line) { ctx.fillText(line, cx, y); y += lh; line = words[i]; }
  1147	        else line = test;
  1148	      }
  1149	      ctx.fillText(line, cx, y); y += lh;
  1150	    }
  1151	    return y;
  1152	  }
  1153	
  1154	  function drawEnd(won) {
  1155	    var w = L.w;
  1156	    ctx.fillStyle = won ? 'rgba(20,16,4,0.88)' : 'rgba(10,4,6,0.88)';
  1157	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, L.h);
  1158	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
  1159	    ctx.fillStyle = won ? '#ffd24d' : '#ff6a6a';
  1160	    ctx.font = 'bold 26px ' + F;
  1161	    ctx.fillText(won ? 'YOU ESCAPED' : 'YOU DIED', w / 2, L.h * 0.24);
  1162	    ctx.font = '12px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#a9b6cc';
  1163	    var sub = won ? 'The Crown of Echoes is out of the dark.' : 'Killed by ' + (S.deathBy || 'the dark') + ' on depth ' + S.depth + '.';
  1164	    wrapText(sub, w / 2, L.h * 0.24 + 40, w - 60, 17);
  1165	    ctx.font = '13px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#dfe8f7';
  1166	    var y = L.h * 0.44;
  1167	    ctx.fillText('deepest  ' + S.maxDepth, w / 2, y);
  1168	    ctx.fillText('kills  ' + S.kills, w / 2, y + 22);
  1169	    ctx.fillText('gold  ' + S.gold, w / 2, y + 44);
  1170	    ctx.font = 'bold 20px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#4ee08a';
  1171	    ctx.fillText('SCORE ' + S.finalScore, w / 2, y + 78);
  1172	    ctx.font = '12px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#9fb0c8';
  1173	    ctx.fillText('best ' + best, w / 2, y + 106);
  1174	    ctx.font = '13px ' + F; ctx.fillStyle = '#e9f2ff';
  1175	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 350);
  1176	    ctx.fillText('tap anywhere to crawl again', w / 2, L.h * 0.80);
  1177	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  1178	  }
  1179	
  1180	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
  1181	   * Main loop
  1182	   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  1183	  var last = 0;
  1184	  function frame(now) {
  1185	    var dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000 || 0); last = now;
  1186	
  1187	    // long-press detection
  1188	    if (press && !press.fired && !press.moved && now - press.t > LONG) {
  1189	      press.fired = true; inspectAt(press.x, press.y);
  1190	    }
  1191	
  1192	    // ease display positions
  1193	    var k = 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt);
  1194	    S.p.ax = CC.lerp(S.p.ax, S.p.x, Math.min(1, k * 1.6));
  1195	    S.p.ay = CC.lerp(S.p.ay, S.p.y, Math.min(1, k * 1.6));
  1196	    for (var i = 0; i < S.mons.length; i++) {
  1197	      var m = S.mons[i];
  1198	      m.ax = CC.lerp(m.ax, m.x, Math.min(1, k * 1.4));
  1199	      m.ay = CC.lerp(m.ay, m.y, Math.min(1, k * 1.4));
  1200	      if (m.hurt > 0) m.hurt -= dt;
  1201	    }
  1202	    if (shake > 0) shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 42);
  1203	    if (flash > 0) flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 1.6);
  1204	    parts.update(dt);
  1205	    updateCam(false);
  1206	
  1207	    draw(dt);
  1208	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  1209	  }
  1210	
  1211	  resize();
  1212	  newGame();
  1213	  updateCam(true);
  1214	  requestAnimationFrame(function (t) { last = t; frame(t); });
  1215	})(window);

   241	  function playerMove(dx, dy) {
   242	    if (S.state !== 'play') return;
   243	    var p = S.p, nx = p.x + dx, ny = p.y + dy;
   244	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) { useStairsIfAny(true); return; }
   245	    var m = monAt(nx, ny);
   246	    if (m) { playerAttack(m); endTurn(); return; }
   247	    if (!S.level.walkable(nx, ny)) { return; }
   248	    // no cutting diagonal corners through walls
   249	    if (dx !== 0 && dy !== 0 && !S.level.walkable(p.x + dx, p.y) && !S.level.walkable(p.x, p.y + dy)) return;

   252	    pickupHere();
   253	    endTurn();

   302	  function ascend() {

   313	  function playerAttack(m) {
   314	    var p = S.p;
   315	    var dmg = S.rng.int(p.atk[0], p.atk[1]) + p.might;
   316	    dmg = Math.max(1, dmg - S.rng.int(0, m.def));
   317	    hurtMon(m, dmg, true);

   966	  function drawMon(m) {
   967	    var ts = L.ts, cx = sx(m.ax) + ts / 2, cy = sy(m.ay) + ts / 2, r = ts * 0.34;

   980	      case 'archer':
   981	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - r); ctx.lineTo(cx + r * 0.85, cy + r * 0.7);

   998	        break;

   702	  function handleTap(x, y) {

   711	    if (t.x === S.p.x && t.y === S.p.y) { playerMove(0, 0); return; }

   715	  function tapBar(x, y) {

    17	  S = cv.width / VW;
    18	  VH = cv.height / S;
    19	  g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
    20	}
    21	window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    22	window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { setTimeout(resize, 120); });
    23	resize();
    24	
    25	/* ---------- audio ---------- */
    26	var AC = null;
    27	function audioInit() {
    28	  if (AC) return;
    29	  try { AC = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (e) { AC = null; }
    30	}
    31	function sfx(f0, f1, dur, type, vol) {
    32	  if (!AC || AC.state === 'suspended') { if (AC) AC.resume(); }
    33	  if (!AC) return;
    34	  var t = AC.currentTime;
    35	  var o = AC.createOscillator(), gn = AC.createGain();
    36	  o.type = type || 'square';
    37	  o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
    38	  o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, f1), t + dur);
    39	  gn.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
    40	  gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || 0.08, t + 0.012);
    41	  gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    42	  o.connect(gn); gn.connect(AC.destination);
    43	  o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    44	}
    45	function noise(dur, vol) {
    46	  if (!AC) return;
    47	  var n = Math.floor(AC.sampleRate * dur);
    48	  var buf = AC.createBuffer(1, n, AC.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
    49	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
    50	  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = buf;
    51	  var gn = AC.createGain(); gn.gain.value = vol || 0.10;
    52	  s.connect(gn); gn.connect(AC.destination); s.start();
    53	}
    54	
    55	/* ---------- constants ---------- */
    56	var GRAV = 2100, RUN = 178, JUMP = 640, HOLD_G = 0.42, HOLD_MAX = 0.20;
    57	var SPRING = 1010, DASH_V = 470, DASH_T = 0.20, WKICK = 620, CRUMBLE = 0.45;
    58	var HW = 9, PHH = 22, START_Y = 0;
    59	
    60	/* ---------- state ---------- */
    61	var mode = 'endless';
    62	var world, P, cam, lava, lavaSpd, elapsed, dead, deadT, score, best, combo, bestCombo, bonus = 0;
    63	var parts = [], shake = 0, tint = 0, tm = 0, hintT, flashT = 0, newBest = false, seedUsed = 0;
    64	
    65	function LS(k, v) {
    66	  try {
    67	    if (v === undefined) return localStorage.getItem(k);
    68	    localStorage.setItem(k, v);
    69	  } catch (e) { }
    70	  return null;
    71	}
    72	function bestKey() { return mode === 'daily' ? 'spire.best.d' + dailySeed() : 'spire.best'; }
    73	function loadBest() { best = parseInt(LS(bestKey()) || '0', 10) || 0; }
    74	
    75	function reset() {
    76	  seedUsed = mode === 'daily' ? dailySeed() : (Math.random() * 4294967295) >>> 0;
    77	  world = new World(seedUsed, START_Y);
    78	  P = { x: VW / 2, y: START_Y, vx: 0, vy: 0, dir: 1, plat: null, coyote: 0, buf: 0,
    79	        dashT: 0, dashOk: true, dashMemo: 0, holdT: 0, holding: false, bestY: START_Y, sq: 0 };
    80	  cam = START_Y - VH * 0.60;
    81	  lava = START_Y + VH * 0.80;
    82	  lavaSpd = 44; elapsed = 0; dead = false; deadT = 0; score = 0; combo = 0; bestCombo = 0; bonus = 0;
    83	  parts.length = 0; shake = 0; tint = 0; hintT = 5.2; newBest = false;
    84	  loadBest();
    85	}
    86	
    87	/* ---------- particles ---------- */
    88	function burst(x, y, n, col, spd, up) {
    89	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    90	    var a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    91	    parts.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * spd * (0.4 + Math.random()),
    92	      vy: Math.sin(a) * spd * (0.4 + Math.random()) - (up || 0),
    93	      life: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.45, max: 0.8, c: col, s: 2 + Math.random() * 3, gr: 1 });
    94	  }
    95	}
    96	
    97	/* ---------- input ---------- */
    98	var lastTap = 0;
    99	function pressJump() {
   100	  audioInit();
   101	  if (dead) { if (deadT > 0.35) restartTap(); return; }
   102	  var now = tm;
   103	  if (!P.plat && P.coyote <= 0 && (now - lastTap) < 0.30 && P.dashOk) { doDash(); lastTap = 0; }
   104	  else { P.buf = 0.13; P.holding = true; lastTap = now; }
   105	}
   106	function releaseJump() { P.holding = false; }
   107	
   108	function doDash() {
   109	  P.dashT = DASH_T; P.dashOk = false; P.dashMemo = 0.34;
   110	  P.vy = -90; P.vx = P.dir * DASH_V;
   111	  burst(P.x - P.dir * 8, P.y - PHH / 2, 10, '#8fe9ff', 90);
   112	  shake = Math.max(shake, 3); sfx(340, 880, 0.13, 'sawtooth', 0.07);
   113	}
   114	
   115	var overlayBtn = null;
   116	function pointAt(e) {
   117	  var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   118	  var cx = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * VW;
   119	  var cy = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * VH;
   120	  return { x: cx, y: cy };
   121	}
   122	function restartTap() { reset(); }
   123	
   124	function onDown(e) {
   125	  e.preventDefault();
   126	  audioInit();
   127	  if (dead && overlayBtn) {
   128	    var p = pointAt(e.touches ? e.touches[0] : e);
   129	    if (p.x > overlayBtn.x && p.x < overlayBtn.x + overlayBtn.w &&
   130	        p.y > overlayBtn.y && p.y < overlayBtn.y + overlayBtn.h) {
   131	      mode = (mode === 'daily') ? 'endless' : 'daily';
   132	      loadBest(); sfx(500, 700, 0.08, 'triangle', 0.06); reset(); return;
   133	    }
   134	  }
   135	  pressJump();
   136	}
   137	function onUp(e) { if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault(); releaseJump(); }
   138	
   139	var wrap = document.getElementById('wrap');
   140	wrap.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {
   141	  e.preventDefault();
   142	  var t = e.changedTouches[0];
   143	  onDown({ clientX: t.clientX, clientY: t.clientY, preventDefault: function () { } });
   144	}, { passive: false });
   145	wrap.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: false });
   146	wrap.addEventListener('touchcancel', onUp, { passive: false });
   147	wrap.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   148	wrap.addEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
   149	window.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
   150	wrap.addEventListener('contextmenu', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   151	
   152	window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   153	  var k = e.key;
   154	  if (k === ' ' || k === 'Spacebar' || k === 'ArrowUp' || k === 'w' || k === 'W' || k === 'Enter') {
   155	    e.preventDefault(); if (!e.repeat) pressJump();
   156	  } else if (k === 'ArrowLeft' || k === 'a' || k === 'A') { if (!dead) P.dir = -1; }
   157	  else if (k === 'ArrowRight' || k === 'd' || k === 'D') { if (!dead) P.dir = 1; }
   158	  else if (k === 'r' || k === 'R') { reset(); }
   159	});
   160	window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) {
   161	  var k = e.key;
   162	  if (k === ' ' || k === 'Spacebar' || k === 'ArrowUp' || k === 'w' || k === 'W' || k === 'Enter') releaseJump();
   163	});
   164	
   165	/* ---------- death ---------- */
   166	var deathReason='';
   167	function die(reason) {
   168	  if (dead) return;
   169	  dead = true; deadT = 0; deathReason = reason;
   170	  burst(P.x, P.y - PHH / 2, 26, '#ff5a4a', 180, 60);
   171	  shake = 12; noise(0.45, 0.16); sfx(320, 60, 0.5, 'sawtooth', 0.09);
   172	  var total = Math.floor(score);
   173	  if (total > best) { best = total; newBest = true; LS(bestKey(), String(best)); }
   174	}
   175	
   176	/* ---------- update ---------- */
   177	function step(dt) {
   178	  tm += dt;
   179	  if (shake > 0) shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 26);
   180	  for (var i = parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   181	    var q = parts[i];
   182	    q.life -= dt; if (q.life <= 0) { parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   183	    q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt; q.vy += 700 * q.gr * dt;
   184	    q.vx *= (1 - 1.6 * dt);
   185	  }
   186	  if (dead) { deadT += dt; return; }
   187	
   188	  elapsed += dt; hintT -= dt; if (flashT > 0) flashT -= dt;
   189	  world.update(dt);
   190	
   191	  // ---- horizontal ----
   192	  P.dashT = Math.max(0, P.dashT - dt);
   193	  P.dashMemo = Math.max(0, P.dashMemo - dt);
   194	  var wind = world.windAt(P.y - PHH / 2);
   195	  var vx = P.dashT > 0 ? P.vx : P.dir * RUN;
   196	  if (P.dashT > 0) P.vx *= (1 - 1.2 * dt);
   197	  P.x += (vx + wind) * dt;
   198	  if (P.plat) P.x += P.plat.dx || 0;
   199	
   200	  // ---- wall spikes (stick 14px out of the wall) ----
   201	  for (var s = 0; s < world.spikes.length; s++) {
   202	    var sp = world.spikes[s];
   203	    if (P.y < sp.y || P.y - PHH > sp.y + sp.h) continue;
   204	    if (sp.side === 0 ? (P.x - HW < WALL + 13) : (P.x + HW > VW - WALL - 13)) { die('spike'); return; }
   205	  }
   206	
   207	  // ---- walls ----
   208	  var hitWall = 0;
   209	  if (P.x - HW < WALL) { P.x = WALL + HW; hitWall = -1; }
   210	  else if (P.x + HW > VW - WALL) { P.x = VW - WALL - HW; hitWall = 1; }
   211	  if (hitWall) {
   212	    if (P.dashT > 0 || P.dashMemo > 0) {
   213	      P.vy = -WKICK; P.dashT = 0; P.dashMemo = 0; P.dashOk = true; P.plat = null;
   214	      P.dir = -hitWall; P.holdT = 0; P.holding = false;
   215	      burst(P.x + hitWall * 8, P.y - PHH / 2, 12, '#ffe27a', 150);
   216	      shake = Math.max(shake, 5); sfx(520, 1000, 0.12, 'square', 0.08); flashT = 0.18;
   217	    } else if (P.dir === hitWall) {
   218	      P.dir = -hitWall;
   219	      if (P.dashT <= 0) P.vx = 0;
   220	      burst(P.x + hitWall * 8, P.y - PHH / 2, 3, '#b9a7ff', 50);
   221	      sfx(220, 180, 0.05, 'triangle', 0.03);
   222	    }
   223	  }
   224	
   225	  // ---- vertical ----
   226	  var prevY = P.y;
   227	  if (P.plat) {
   228	    if (P.plat.dead) { P.plat = null; P.coyote = 0.05; }
   229	    else {
   230	      P.y = P.plat.y;
   231	      if (P.x + HW < P.plat.x + 1 || P.x - HW > P.plat.x + P.plat.w - 1) { P.plat = null; P.coyote = 0.09; }
   232	    }
   233	  }
   234	  if (!P.plat) {
   235	    var gm = 1;
   236	    if (P.dashT > 0) gm = 0.18;
   237	    else if (P.holding && P.vy < 0 && P.holdT < HOLD_MAX) { gm = HOLD_G; P.holdT += dt; }
   238	    P.vy += GRAV * gm * dt;
   239	    if (P.vy > 1250) P.vy = 1250;
   240	    P.y += P.vy * dt;
   241	    P.coyote = Math.max(0, P.coyote - dt);
   242	  } else { P.vy = 0; P.coyote = 0.09; }
   243	
   244	  // ---- jump ----
   245	  P.buf = Math.max(0, P.buf - dt);
   246	  if (P.buf > 0 && (P.plat || P.coyote > 0)) {
   247	    P.vy = -JUMP; P.plat = null; P.coyote = 0; P.buf = 0; P.holdT = 0; P.sq = -1;
   248	    burst(P.x, P.y, 6, '#cfe0ff', 70);
   249	    sfx(400, 720, 0.09, 'square', 0.055);
   250	  }
--- spire game 2 ---
   250	  }
   251	
   252	  // ---- landing ----
   253	  if (!P.plat && P.vy > 0) {
   254	    for (var i2 = 0; i2 < world.plats.length; i2++) {
   255	      var p = world.plats[i2];
   256	      if (p.dead) continue;
   257	      if (prevY <= p.y + 1 && P.y >= p.y && P.x + HW > p.x && P.x - HW < p.x + p.w) {
   258	        land(p); break;
   259	      }
   260	    }
   261	    if (dead) return;
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  // ---- height / score ----
   265	  if (P.y < P.bestY) P.bestY = P.y;
   266	  var h = Math.max(0, (START_Y - P.bestY) / 11);
   267	  score = Math.max(score, h + bonus);
   268	
   269	  // ---- doom ----
   270	  lavaSpd = 36 + Math.min(elapsed, 150) * 0.60;
   271	  lava -= lavaSpd * dt;
   272	  if (lava > P.bestY + 760) lava = P.bestY + 760;
   273	  if (P.y > lava) { die('lava'); return; }
   274	
   275	  // ---- camera ----
   276	  var target = P.y - VH * 0.60;
   277	  if (target < cam) cam += (target - cam) * Math.min(1, dt * 9);
   278	  if (P.y - cam > VH * 0.86) cam = P.y - VH * 0.86;
   279	  if (lava - cam < VH * 0.30) cam = lava - VH * 0.30;
   280	
   281	  tint = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - (lava - P.y) / 320));
   282	
   283	  world.ensure(cam - VH);
   284	  world.cull(lava + 120);
   285	  if (P.dashT <= 0 && (P.plat || P.coyote > 0)) P.dashOk = true;
   286	  if (P.sq > 0) P.sq = Math.max(0, P.sq - dt * 5);
   287	  else if (P.sq < 0) P.sq = Math.min(0, P.sq + dt * 5);
   288	}
   289	
   290	var bonus = 0;
   291	function land(p) {
   292	  P.y = p.y; P.vy = 0; P.plat = p; P.dashOk = true; P.dashT = 0; P.holdT = 0; P.sq = 1;
   293	  if (p.spike) {
   294	    var sx = p.spike === 'L' ? p.x : p.x + p.w - p.spw;
   295	    if (P.x + HW * 0.5 > sx && P.x - HW * 0.5 < sx + p.spw) { die('spike'); return; }
   296	  }
   297	  if (p.t === 'spring') {
   298	    P.vy = -SPRING; P.plat = null; P.coyote = 0; P.dashOk = true;
   299	    combo++; bonusAdd(combo);
   300	    burst(P.x, p.y, 12, '#4dffa6', 130, 40);
   301	    shake = Math.max(shake, 4); sfx(300, 1200, 0.16, 'triangle', 0.08);
   302	    return;
   303	  }
   304	  if (p.t === 'crumble') {
   305	    if (p.tmr <= 0) p.tmr = CRUMBLE;
   306	    if (combo > 2) sfx(180, 90, 0.14, 'sawtooth', 0.05);
   307	    combo = 0;
   308	    burst(P.x, p.y, 6, '#e8a35c', 60);
   309	  } else {
   310	    combo++; bonusAdd(combo);
   311	    if (combo > bestCombo) bestCombo = combo;
   312	    burst(P.x, p.y, 4, '#cfe0ff', 50);
   313	    sfx(combo > 1 ? Math.min(1200, 260 + combo * 40) : 260, 200, 0.05, 'triangle', 0.04);
   314	    if (combo > 0 && combo % 5 === 0) { flashT = 0.25; shake = Math.max(shake, 3); sfx(700, 1400, 0.16, 'square', 0.07); }
   315	  }
   316	}
   317	function bonusAdd(c) { bonus += c * 1.5; }
   318	
   319	/* ---------- render ---------- */
   320	function fmt(n) { return String(Math.floor(n)); }
   321	
   322	function draw() {
   323	  g.setTransform(S, 0, 0, S, 0, 0);
   324	  var sx = 0, sy = 0;
   325	  if (shake > 0) { sx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake; sy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake; }
   326	  g.save(); g.translate(sx, sy);
   327	
   328	  world.drawBack(g, cam, VH, tint);
   329	  world.drawWinds(g, cam, VH);
   330	  world.drawPlats(g, cam, VH);
   331	  world.drawSpikes(g, cam, VH, tm);
   332	
   333	  // player
   334	  if (!dead) {
   335	    var py = P.y - cam, px = P.x;
   336	    var sqz = P.sq > 0 ? P.sq : 0, str = P.sq < 0 ? -P.sq : 0;
   337	    var w = (HW * 2) * (1 + sqz * 0.35 - str * 0.2);
   338	    var h = PHH * (1 - sqz * 0.28 + str * 0.22);
   339	    // trail
   340	    if (P.dashT > 0) {
   341	      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(140,235,255,0.30)';
   342	      for (var t = 1; t <= 3; t++) g.fillRect(px - w / 2 - P.dir * t * 11, py - h, w, h);
   343	    }
   344	    var pg = g.createLinearGradient(0, py - h, 0, py);
   345	    pg.addColorStop(0, P.dashT > 0 ? '#c9f6ff' : '#ffe9a8');
   346	    pg.addColorStop(1, P.dashT > 0 ? '#3fb6d8' : '#ff9c3c');
   347	    g.fillStyle = pg;
   348	    g.fillRect(px - w / 2, py - h, w, h);
   349	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
   350	    g.fillRect(px - w / 2 + (P.dir > 0 ? w * 0.52 : w * 0.16), py - h * 0.78, w * 0.3, 4);
   351	    if (P.dashOk && !P.plat && P.dashT <= 0) {
   352	      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,240,255,0.75)'; g.lineWidth = 2;
   353	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(px, py - h / 2, w * 0.95, 0, 6.283); g.stroke();
   354	    }
   355	  }
   356	
   357	  // particles
   358	  for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
   359	    var q = parts[i];
   360	    g.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, q.life / 0.5));
   361	    g.fillStyle = q.c; g.fillRect(q.x - q.s / 2, q.y - cam - q.s / 2, q.s, q.s);
   362	  }
   363	  g.globalAlpha = 1;
   364	
   365	  // lava
   366	  var ly = lava - cam;
   367	  if (ly < VH + 40) {
   368	    var lg = g.createLinearGradient(0, ly - 46, 0, ly + 60);
   369	    lg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,110,30,0)');
   370	    lg.addColorStop(0.42, 'rgba(255,120,40,0.55)');
   371	    lg.addColorStop(0.62, '#ff5a10');
   372	    lg.addColorStop(1, '#8a1200');
   373	    g.fillStyle = lg; g.fillRect(0, ly - 46, VW, VH - ly + 90);
   374	    g.fillStyle = '#ffd27a';
   375	    for (var x = 0; x < VW; x += 13) {
   376	      var wv = Math.sin(tm * 5 + x * 0.09) * 5 + Math.sin(tm * 2.3 + x * 0.03) * 3;
   377	      g.fillRect(x, ly + wv, 13, 4);
   378	    }
   379	  }
   380	  g.restore();
   381	
   382	  drawHud();
   383	}
   384	
   385	function rr(x, y, w, h, r) {
   386	  g.beginPath();
   387	  g.moveTo(x + r, y); g.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   388	  g.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); g.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); g.closePath();
   389	}
   390	
   391	function drawHud() {
   392	  var F = 'system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';
   393	  g.textBaseline = 'top';
   394	  if (flashT > 0) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (flashT * 0.5).toFixed(3) + ')'; g.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH); }
   395	
   396	  var top = 10;
   397	  g.textAlign = 'left';
   398	  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; rr(8, top, 128, 42, 8); g.fill();
   399	  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)'; g.font = '600 11px ' + F;
   400	  g.fillText('SCORE', 16, top + 5);
   401	  g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = '700 24px ' + F;
   402	  g.fillText(fmt(score), 16, top + 16);
   403	
   404	  g.textAlign = 'right';
   405	  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; rr(VW - 140, top, 132, 42, 8); g.fill();
   406	  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)'; g.font = '700 15px ' + F;
   407	  g.fillText('BEST ' + best, VW - 16, top + 6);
   408	  g.fillStyle = mode === 'daily' ? '#7de3ff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
   409	  g.font = '600 11px ' + F;
   410	  g.fillText(mode === 'daily' ? 'DAILY SEED' : 'ENDLESS', VW - 16, top + 26);
   411	
   412	  if (!dead && combo > 1) {
   413	    g.textAlign = 'center';
   414	    var pul = 1 + 0.12 * Math.sin(tm * 12);
   415	    g.save(); g.translate(VW / 2, top + 54); g.scale(pul, pul);
   416	    g.fillStyle = combo >= 5 ? '#ffd84d' : '#9fe8ff';
   417	    g.font = '800 24px ' + F;
   418	    g.fillText('x' + combo, 0, 0);
   419	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; g.font = '600 10px ' + F;
   420	    g.fillText('COMBO', 0, 26);
   421	    g.restore();
   422	  }
   423	
   424	  if (hintT > 0 && !dead) {
   425	    g.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, hintT / 1.2);
   426	    g.textAlign = 'center';
   427	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)'; rr(VW / 2 - 155, VH - 92, 310, 34, 10); g.fill();
   428	    g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = '600 14px ' + F;
   429	    g.fillText('TAP to jump (hold = higher) · DOUBLE-TAP to dash', VW / 2, VH - 82);
   430	    g.globalAlpha = 1;
   431	  }
   432	
   433	  if (dead) {
   434	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,4,14,0.72)'; g.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
   435	    var cy = VH / 2 - 110;
   436	    g.textAlign = 'center';
   437	    g.fillStyle = '#ff6a4a'; g.font = '800 34px ' + F;
   438	    g.fillText('THE SPIRE WINS', VW / 2, cy);
   439	    g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = '800 62px ' + F;
   440	    g.fillText(fmt(score), VW / 2, cy + 48);
   441	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)'; g.font = '600 12px ' + F;
   442	    g.fillText('SCORE', VW / 2, cy + 116);
   443	    g.fillStyle = newBest ? '#ffd84d' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)'; g.font = '700 17px ' + F;
   444	    g.fillText(newBest ? 'NEW BEST!' : 'BEST ' + best, VW / 2, cy + 140);
   445	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)'; g.font = '600 13px ' + F;
   446	    g.fillText('height ' + Math.floor(Math.max(0, (START_Y - P.bestY) / 11)) + 'm · best combo x' + bestCombo, VW / 2, cy + 168);
   447	
   448	    var bw = 220, bh = 52, bx = VW / 2 - bw / 2, by = cy + 202;
   449	    overlayBtn = { x: bx, y: by, w: bw, h: bh };
   450	    g.fillStyle = mode === 'daily' ? 'rgba(125,227,255,0.18)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)';
   451	    rr(bx, by, bw, bh, 12); g.fill();
   452	    g.strokeStyle = mode === 'daily' ? '#7de3ff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; g.lineWidth = 2;
   453	    rr(bx, by, bw, bh, 12); g.stroke();
   454	    g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = '700 15px ' + F;
   455	    g.fillText(mode === 'daily' ? 'MODE: DAILY SEED' : 'MODE: ENDLESS', VW / 2, by + 11);
   456	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; g.font = '600 11px ' + F;
   457	    g.fillText('tap to switch', VW / 2, by + 31);
   458	
   459	    if (deadT > 0.35) {
   460	      g.globalAlpha = 0.65 + 0.35 * Math.sin(tm * 5);
   461	      g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = '700 18px ' + F;
   462	      g.fillText('TAP ANYWHERE TO CLIMB AGAIN', VW / 2, by + bh + 34);
   463	      g.globalAlpha = 1;
   464	    }
   465	  }
   466	}
   467	
   468	/* ---------- loop ---------- */
   469	var prev = 0, acc = 0;
   470	function frame(t) {
   471	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   472	  if (!prev) prev = t;
   473	  var dt = (t - prev) / 1000; prev = t;
   474	  if (dt > 0.1) dt = 0.1;
   475	  acc += dt;
   476	  var guard = 0;
   477	  while (acc > 1 / 120 && guard++ < 8) { var s = Math.min(1 / 60, acc); step(s); acc -= s; }
   478	  draw();
   479	}
   480	
   481	window.SpireState = function () { return { P: P, world: world, lava: lava, cam: cam, dead: dead, score: score, VH: VH, why: deathReason }; };
   482	
   483	reset();
   484	requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   485	document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { prev = 0; acc = 0; });
   486	
   487	})();
--- spire game 3 ---
--- world ---
     1	/* Spire Ascent - world generation & rendering (original work) */
     2	'use strict';
     3	
     4	var VW = 390;          // virtual width
     5	var WALL = 15;         // wall thickness
     6	var ROW = 76;          // vertical spacing between generated rows
     7	var PT = 12;           // platform thickness
     8	
     9	function makeRng(seed) {
    10	  var a = (seed >>> 0) || 1;
    11	  return function () {
    12	    a |= 0; a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    13	    var t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    14	    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    15	    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    16	  };
    17	}
    18	
    19	function dailySeed() {
    20	  var d = new Date();
    21	  return d.getFullYear() * 10000 + (d.getMonth() + 1) * 100 + d.getDate();
    22	}
    23	
    24	function World(seed, startY) {
    25	  this.rng = makeRng(seed);
    26	  this.startY = startY;
    27	  this.plats = [];
    28	  this.winds = [];
    29	  this.spikes = [];   // wall spikes
    30	  this.genY = startY;
    31	  this.lastSpike = { 0: 1e9, 1: 1e9 };
    32	  // guaranteed safe launch pad
    33	  this.plats.push({ x: VW / 2 - 60, y: startY, w: 120, t: 'ledge', sp: 0, dead: 0, tmr: 0, ph: 0, amp: 0, bx: 0 });
    34	  for (var i = 0; i < 14; i++) this.genRow();
    35	}
    36	
    37	World.prototype.diffAt = function (y) {
    38	  return Math.min(1, (this.startY - y) / 7000);
    39	};
    40	
    41	World.prototype.mkPlat = function (y, w, d) {
    42	  var r = this.rng;
    43	  var minX = WALL + 8, maxX = VW - WALL - 8 - w;
    44	  var x = minX + r() * (maxX - minX);
    45	  // never park a platform inside a wall-spike band
    46	  for (var si = 0; si < this.spikes.length; si++) {
    47	    var sk = this.spikes[si];
    48	    if (y > sk.y - 26 && y < sk.y + sk.h + 26) {
    49	      if (sk.side === 0) x = Math.max(x, WALL + 46);
    50	      else x = Math.min(x, VW - WALL - 46 - w);
    51	    }
    52	  }
    53	  x = Math.max(minX, Math.min(maxX, x));
    54	  var p = { x: x, y: y, w: w, t: 'ledge', sp: 0, dead: 0, tmr: 0, ph: r() * 6.283, amp: 0, bx: x };
    55	  var q = r();
    56	  if (q < 0.08) p.t = 'spring';
    57	  else if (q < 0.20 + 0.20 * d) p.t = 'crumble';
    58	  else if (q < 0.30 + 0.36 * d) {
    59	    p.t = 'mover';
    60	    p.amp = Math.min(52 + r() * 45, (maxX - minX) / 2);
    61	    p.bx = Math.max(minX + p.amp, Math.min(maxX - p.amp, x));
    62	    p.sp = (0.6 + r() * 0.8) * (r() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1);
    63	  }
    64	  // spike strip on part of the top (never the whole platform)
    65	  if (p.t !== 'spring' && r() < 0.03 + 0.13 * d) {
    66	    p.spike = r() < 0.5 ? 'L' : 'R';
    67	    p.spw = p.w * (0.28 + r() * 0.14);
    68	  }
    69	  return p;
    70	};
    71	
    72	World.prototype.genRow = function () {
    73	  var r = this.rng;
    74	  this.genY -= ROW;
    75	  var y = this.genY;
    76	  var d = this.diffAt(y);
    77	
    78	  // wall spike band (placed above this row so platforms above route around it)
    79	  if (r() < 0.04 + 0.13 * d) {
    80	    var side = r() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1;
    81	    if (this.lastSpike[side] - (y - 30) > 270) {
    82	      var sh = 40 + r() * 52;
    83	      this.spikes.push({ side: side, y: y - 30 - sh, h: sh });
    84	      this.lastSpike[side] = y - 30 - sh;
    85	    }
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  var w1 = 62 + r() * 52 - 18 * d;
    89	  var a = this.mkPlat(y, Math.max(44, w1), d);
    90	  this.plats.push(a);
    91	
    92	  if (r() < 0.5 - 0.26 * d) {
    93	    var b = this.mkPlat(y - 12 - r() * 22, Math.max(42, 54 + r() * 44 - 16 * d), d);
    94	    if (Math.abs((b.x + b.w / 2) - (a.x + a.w / 2)) > 92) this.plats.push(b);
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  // wind zone
    98	  if (r() < 0.06 + 0.08 * d) {
    99	    this.winds.push({ y: y - 40, h: 150 + r() * 130, f: (r() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * (70 + r() * 70), t: 0 });
   100	  }
   101	};
   102	
   103	World.prototype.ensure = function (topY) {
   104	  var guard = 0;
   105	  while (this.genY > topY && guard++ < 400) this.genRow();
   106	};
   107	
   108	World.prototype.cull = function (botY) {
   109	  var f = function (o) { return o.y < botY + 200; };
   110	  if (this.plats.length > 90) this.plats = this.plats.filter(function (p) { return p.y < botY + 200; });
   111	  if (this.winds.length > 20) this.winds = this.winds.filter(f);
   112	  if (this.spikes.length > 20) this.spikes = this.spikes.filter(f);
   113	};
   114	
   115	World.prototype.update = function (dt) {
   116	  for (var i = 0; i < this.plats.length; i++) {
   117	    var p = this.plats[i];
   118	    p.px = p.x;
   119	    if (p.t === 'mover') {
   120	      p.ph += dt * p.sp * 1.5;
   121	      p.x = p.bx + Math.sin(p.ph) * p.amp;
   122	    }
   123	    if (p.t === 'crumble' && p.tmr > 0) {
   124	      p.tmr -= dt;
   125	      if (p.tmr <= 0) p.dead = 1;
   126	    }
   127	    p.dx = p.x - p.px;
   128	  }
   129	  for (var j = 0; j < this.winds.length; j++) this.winds[j].t += dt;
   130	};
   131	
   132	World.prototype.windAt = function (y) {
   133	  var f = 0;
   134	  for (var i = 0; i < this.winds.length; i++) {
   135	    var w = this.winds[i];
   136	    if (y < w.y + w.h && y > w.y) f += w.f;
   137	  }
   138	  return f;
   139	};
   140	
   141	/* ---------- drawing ---------- */
   142	
   143	World.prototype.drawBack = function (g, camY, H, tint) {
   144	  var grd = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   145	  grd.addColorStop(0, '#0a0713');
   146	  grd.addColorStop(1, '#1a1030');
   147	  g.fillStyle = grd; g.fillRect(0, 0, VW, H);
   148	
   149	  // parallax masonry
   150	  g.save();
   151	  var par = camY * 0.35, bh = 54, bw = 74;
   152	  var y0 = Math.floor((par - H) / bh) * bh;
   153	  for (var y = y0; y < par + H + bh; y += bh) {
   154	    var row = Math.floor(y / bh);
   155	    for (var x = -bw; x < VW + bw; x += bw) {
   156	      var sx = x + (row % 2 ? bw / 2 : 0);
   157	      g.fillStyle = ((row + Math.floor(sx / bw)) % 2) ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.022)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.010)';
   158	      g.fillRect(sx + 2, y - par + 2, bw - 4, bh - 4);
   159	    }
   160	  }
   161	  g.restore();
   162	
   163	  // side walls
   164	  var wg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, WALL, 0);
   165	  wg.addColorStop(0, '#2a1c4e'); wg.addColorStop(1, '#150d2b');
   166	  g.fillStyle = wg; g.fillRect(0, 0, WALL, H);
   167	  var wg2 = g.createLinearGradient(VW - WALL, 0, VW, 0);
   168	  wg2.addColorStop(0, '#150d2b'); wg2.addColorStop(1, '#2a1c4e');
   169	  g.fillStyle = wg2; g.fillRect(VW - WALL, 0, WALL, H);
   170	  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(140,110,255,0.20)';
   171	  g.fillRect(WALL - 2, 0, 2, H); g.fillRect(VW - WALL, 0, 2, H);
   172	
   173	  if (tint > 0) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,70,20,' + (tint * 0.20).toFixed(3) + ')'; g.fillRect(0, 0, VW, H); }
   174	};
   175	
   176	World.prototype.drawWinds = function (g, camY, H) {
   177	  for (var i = 0; i < this.winds.length; i++) {
   178	    var w = this.winds[i], sy = w.y - camY;
   179	    if (sy > H || sy + w.h < 0) continue;
   180	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(90,200,255,0.07)';
   181	    g.fillRect(WALL, sy, VW - WALL * 2, w.h);
   182	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(140,220,255,0.45)'; g.lineWidth = 2; g.lineCap = 'round';
   183	    var dir = w.f > 0 ? 1 : -1;
   184	    for (var k = 0; k < 7; k++) {
   185	      var ly = sy + ((k + 0.5) / 7) * w.h;
   186	      var t = (w.t * 130 * dir + k * 97) % (VW * 2);
   187	      var lx = dir > 0 ? (WALL + ((t) % (VW - WALL * 2 + 60)) - 30) : (VW - WALL - ((t * -1 + 9999) % (VW - WALL * 2 + 60)) + 30);
   188	      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(lx, ly); g.lineTo(lx + 26 * dir, ly); g.stroke();
   189	    }
   190	  }
   191	};
   192	
   193	function spikeTeeth(g, x, y, w, up, col) {
   194	  g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath();
   195	  var n = Math.max(2, Math.round(w / 9));
   196	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   197	    var sx = x + (i / n) * w, sw = w / n;
   198	    g.moveTo(sx, y); g.lineTo(sx + sw / 2, y - up); g.lineTo(sx + sw, y);
   199	  }
   200	  g.closePath(); g.fill();
   201	}
   202	
   203	World.prototype.drawSpikes = function (g, camY, H, tm) {
   204	  for (var i = 0; i < this.spikes.length; i++) {
   205	    var s = this.spikes[i], sy = s.y - camY;
   206	    if (sy > H || sy + s.h < 0) continue;
   207	    var pul = 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(tm * 6 + s.y * 0.05);
   208	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,60,80,' + (0.16 * pul).toFixed(3) + ')';
   209	    if (s.side === 0) g.fillRect(WALL, sy, 28, s.h); else g.fillRect(VW - WALL - 28, sy, 28, s.h);
   210	    var bx = s.side === 0 ? WALL : VW - WALL, d = s.side === 0 ? 14 : -14;
   211	    var n = Math.max(2, Math.round(s.h / 14)), th = s.h / n;
   212	    g.fillStyle = '#ff3c50'; g.beginPath();
   213	    for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
   214	      var ty = sy + k * th;
   215	      g.moveTo(bx, ty); g.lineTo(bx + d, ty + th / 2); g.lineTo(bx, ty + th);
   216	    }
   217	    g.closePath(); g.fill();
   218	  }
   219	};
   220	
   221	World.prototype.drawPlats = function (g, camY, H) {
   222	  for (var i = 0; i < this.plats.length; i++) {
   223	    var p = this.plats[i];
   224	    if (p.dead) continue;
   225	    var sy = p.y - camY;
   226	    if (sy > H + 20 || sy < -30) continue;
   227	    var x = p.x, w = p.w, col = '#7f8ea8', top = '#c9d6ea';
   228	
   229	    if (p.t === 'crumble') {
   230	      var shake = p.tmr > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3 : 0;
   231	      x += shake;
   232	      var f = p.tmr > 0 ? p.tmr / 0.45 : 1;
   233	      col = 'rgb(' + Math.round(150 + 80 * (1 - f)) + ',' + Math.round(105 * f + 40) + ',60)';
   234	      top = '#e8a35c';
   235	    } else if (p.t === 'spring') { col = '#1f7a52'; top = '#4dffa6'; }
   236	    else if (p.t === 'mover') { col = '#3a4f8a'; top = '#8fb6ff'; }
   237	
   238	    g.fillStyle = col; g.fillRect(x, sy, w, PT);
   239	    g.fillStyle = top; g.fillRect(x, sy, w, 3);
   240	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.28)'; g.fillRect(x, sy + PT - 3, w, 3);
   241	
   242	    if (p.t === 'spring') {
   243	      var cx = x + w / 2;
   244	      g.strokeStyle = '#4dffa6'; g.lineWidth = 3;
   245	      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - 11, sy - 3); g.lineTo(cx, sy - 12); g.lineTo(cx + 11, sy - 3); g.stroke();
   246	    }
   247	    if (p.t === 'crumble') {
   248	      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)';
   249	      for (var k = 1; k < 4; k++) g.fillRect(x + (w * k / 4), sy + 3, 2, PT - 5);
   250	    }
   251	    if (p.spike) {
   252	      var sx = p.spike === 'L' ? x : x + w - p.spw;
   253	      spikeTeeth(g, sx, sy, p.spw, 11, '#ff3c50');
   254	    }
   255	  }
   256	};

     1	/* Bulwark - lane tower defense with maze building. Vanilla canvas. */

     3	(function () {
     4	
     5	var cv = document.getElementById('c');
     6	var ctx = cv.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });

     8	/* ---------------- layout ---------------- */
     9	var L = { w: 390, h: 700, s: 1, hud: 58, bar: 96, cell: 34, cols: 11, rows: 15, gx: 0, gy: 0 };
    10	var rowsLocked = 0;
    11	
    12	function resize() {
    13	  var w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
    14	  L.w = w; L.h = h;
    15	  var dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    16	  var longAxis = Math.max(w, h);
    17	  var s = Math.min(dpr, 960 / longAxis);
    18	  s = Math.max(s, 0.6);
    19	  L.s = s;
    20	  cv.width = Math.round(w * s);
    21	  cv.height = Math.round(h * s);

    23	  L.hud = Math.round(Math.min(64, Math.max(52, h * 0.082)));

    33	  L.gy = L.hud + (gh - L.rows * L.cell) / 2;
