    62	function newGame(seed) {
    63	  resize();
    64	  G = {
    65	    seed: seed || (Date.now() & 0xffff),
    66	    map: new E.Map(L.cols, L.rows, seed || (Date.now() & 0xffff)),
    67	    towers: [], creeps: [], bullets: [], fx: [], parts: [], texts: [],
    68	    gold: 95, lives: 20, leaks: 0, wave: 0, wavesDone: 0,
    69	    spawnQ: [], spawnI: 0, waveT: 0, active: false, prep: 15,
    70	    phase: 'play', sel: -1, selTower: null, pending: 'arrow',
    71	    shake: 0, shakeX: 0, shakeY: 0, hint: 1, msg: '', msgT: 0,
    72	    kx: 5, ky: 7, kb: false, time: 0, endless: false
    73	  };
    74	  G.map.solve();
    75	}
    76	
    77	var best = 0;
    78	try { best = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('bulwark.best') || '0', 10) || 0; } catch (e) { }
    79	function saveBest(v) { if (v > best) { best = v; try { localStorage.setItem('bulwark.best', String(v)); } catch (e) { } } }
    80	
    81	/* ---------------- waves ---------------- */
    82	function buildWave(n) {
    83	  var r = E.rng(G.seed * 7919 + n * 104729);
    84	  var q = [];
    85	  var t = 0;
    86	  if (n % 10 === 0) {
    87	    var bhp = Math.pow(1.14, n - 1) * (0.5 + n / 30);
    88	    q.push({ type: 'boss', t: 0.6, hpm: bhp, spm: 1 });
    89	    var esc = 5 + n;
    90	    for (var e = 0; e < esc; e++) q.push({ type: r() < .5 ? 'runner' : 'grunt', t: 1.6 + e * 0.55, hpm: Math.pow(1.155, n), spm: 1 });
    91	    return q;
    92	  }
    93	  var count = Math.round(6 + n * 1.75);
    94	  var pool = ['grunt'];
    95	  if (n >= 3) pool.push('runner');
    96	  if (n >= 5) pool.push('flier');
    97	  if (n >= 7) pool.push('tank');
    98	  if (n >= 9) pool.push('shield');
    99	  if (n >= 12) pool.push('runner', 'flier');
   100	  if (n >= 16) pool.push('tank', 'shield');
   101	  var hpm = Math.pow(1.155, n - 1);
   102	  var spm = 1 + Math.min(0.45, n * 0.013);
   103	  for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   104	    var ty = pool[Math.floor(r() * pool.length)];
   105	    t += 0.34 + r() * 0.42;
   106	    q.push({ type: ty, t: t, hpm: hpm, spm: spm });
   107	  }
   108	  return q;
   109	}
   110	
   111	function startWave() {
   112	  G.wave++;
   113	  G.spawnQ = buildWave(G.wave);
   114	  G.spawnI = 0; G.waveT = 0; G.active = true;
   115	  toast('WAVE ' + G.wave + (G.wave % 10 === 0 ? '  — BOSS' : ''));
   116	  E.audio.wave();
   117	}
   118	
   119	function endWave() {
   120	  G.active = false;
   121	  G.wavesDone = G.wave;
   122	  var interest = Math.min(45, Math.floor(G.gold * 0.06));
   123	  var bankInc = 0, ipct = 0;
   124	  for (var i = 0; i < G.towers.length; i++) {
   125	    var tw = G.towers[i];
   126	    if (tw.t === 'bank') { bankInc += 4 * tw.lv; ipct += 0.015 * tw.lv; }
   127	  }
   128	  interest += Math.floor(G.gold * ipct);
   129	  var clear = 12 + G.wave * 3;
   130	  var tot = interest + bankInc + clear;
   131	  G.gold += tot;
   132	  E.audio.coin();
   133	  floatT(L.w / 2, L.gy + 40, '+' + tot + 'g  (interest ' + (interest + bankInc) + ')', '#ffe066');
   134	  saveBest(G.wavesDone + G.lives);
   135	  if (G.wave >= 30 && !G.endless) { G.phase = 'win'; E.audio.win(); return; }
   136	  G.prep = 9;
   137	}
   138	
   139	/* ---------------- helpers ---------------- */
   140	function cellCenter(i) { return { x: (i % L.cols) + 0.5, y: ((i / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5 }; }
   141	function sx(cx) { return L.gx + cx * L.cell; }
   142	function sy(cy) { return L.gy + cy * L.cell; }
   143	function cellAt(px, py) {
   144	  var x = Math.floor((px - L.gx) / L.cell), y = Math.floor((py - L.gy) / L.cell);
   145	  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= L.cols || y >= L.rows) return -1;
   146	  return y * L.cols + x;
   147	}
   148	function towerAt(i) { for (var k = 0; k < G.towers.length; k++) if (G.towers[k].i === i) return G.towers[k]; return null; }
   149	function toast(m) { G.msg = m; G.msgT = 2.0; }
   150	function floatT(x, y, txt, col) { G.texts.push({ x: x, y: y, t: txt, c: col || '#fff', life: 1.1, max: 1.1 }); }
   151	function burst(x, y, col, n, sp) {
   152	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   153	    var a = Math.random() * 6.283, v = (0.4 + Math.random()) * (sp || 90);
   154	    G.parts.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * v, vy: Math.sin(a) * v, life: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.35, max: 0.7, c: col, r: 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.5 });
   155	  }
   156	}
   157	function shake(m) { G.shake = Math.max(G.shake, m); }
   158	
   159	/* ---------------- build ---------------- */
   160	function occupiedCells() {
   161	  var a = [];
   162	  for (var i = 0; i < G.creeps.length; i++) {
   163	    var c = G.creeps[i];
   164	    if (c.fly || c.dead || c.cy < 0) continue;
   165	    var x = E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cx), 0, L.cols - 1), y = E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cy), 0, L.rows - 1);
   166	    a.push(y * L.cols + x);
   167	  }
   168	  return a;
   169	}
   170	
   171	function tryBuild(i, type) {
   172	  var d = T[type];
   173	  if (!d) return;
   174	  if (i < 0 || G.map.g[i] !== E.EMPTY) { E.audio.deny(); toast('BLOCKED'); return; }
   175	  if (G.gold < d.cost) { E.audio.deny(); toast('NEED ' + d.cost + 'g'); return; }
   176	  if (!G.map.canPlace(i, occupiedCells())) { E.audio.deny(); toast('WOULD SEAL THE PATH'); shake(4); return; }
   177	  G.gold -= d.cost;
   178	  G.map.g[i] = d.wall ? E.WALL : E.TOWER;
   179	  G.map.solve();
   180	  for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) G.creeps[k].tx = undefined;
   181	  if (!d.wall) {
   182	    G.towers.push({
   183	      i: i, t: type, lv: 1, cd: 0, spent: d.cost, ang: -Math.PI / 2, pulse: 0,
   184	      x: (i % L.cols) + 0.5, y: ((i / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5
   185	    });
   186	  }
   187	  var cc = cellCenter(i);
   188	  burst(sx(cc.x), sy(cc.y), d.col, 10, 70);
   189	  E.audio.build();
   190	  G.hint = 0;
   191	  G.sel = -1;
   192	}
   193	
   194	function statOf(tw) {
   195	  var d = T[tw.t], m = Math.pow(1.38, tw.lv - 1);
   196	  return {
   197	    dmg: d.dmg ? d.dmg * m : 0,
   198	    range: d.range ? d.range * (1 + (tw.lv - 1) * 0.09) : 0,
   199	    rate: d.rate ? d.rate * Math.pow(0.94, tw.lv - 1) : 0
   200	  };
   201	}
   202	function upCost(tw) { return Math.round(T[tw.t].cost * (0.85 + tw.lv * 0.75)); }
   203	function sellVal(tw) { return Math.floor(tw.spent * 0.6); }
   204	
   205	function upgrade(tw) {
   206	  if (tw.lv >= 5) { toast('MAX LEVEL'); E.audio.deny(); return; }
   207	  var c = upCost(tw);
   208	  if (G.gold < c) { E.audio.deny(); toast('NEED ' + c + 'g'); return; }
   209	  G.gold -= c; tw.spent += c; tw.lv++; tw.pulse = 1;
   210	  burst(sx(tw.x), sy(tw.y), T[tw.t].col, 12, 90);
   211	  E.audio.build();
   212	}
   213	function sell(tw) {
   214	  var v = sellVal(tw);
   215	  G.gold += v;
   216	  G.map.g[tw.i] = E.EMPTY; G.map.solve();
   217	  for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) G.creeps[k].tx = undefined;
   218	  G.towers.splice(G.towers.indexOf(tw), 1);
   219	  G.selTower = null;
   220	  floatT(sx(tw.x), sy(tw.y), '+' + v + 'g', '#ffe066');
   221	  E.audio.coin();
   222	}
   223	
   224	/* ---------------- creeps ---------------- */
   225	function spawn(s) {
   226	  var d = EN[s.type];
   227	  var hp = d.hp * (s.hpm || 1);
   228	  G.creeps.push({
   229	    type: s.type, cx: G.map.entryC + 0.5, cy: -0.7, hp: hp, hpMax: hp,
   230	    sh: d.shield ? d.shield * (s.hpm || 1) : 0, shMax: d.shield ? d.shield * (s.hpm || 1) : 0, shT: 0,
   231	    sp: d.sp * (s.spm || 1), r: d.r, col: d.col, gold: d.gold, leak: d.leak,
   232	    armor: d.armor || 0, fly: !!d.fly, boss: !!d.boss,
   233	    slowT: 0, slowF: 1, flash: 0, dead: false, tx: undefined, ty: 0, ang: Math.PI / 2
   234	  });
   235	}
   236	
   237	function bestNeighbor(i) {
   238	  var cols = L.cols, rows = L.rows, x = i % cols, y = (i / cols) | 0;
   239	  var bi = -1, bd = 1e9;
   240	  for (var k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
   241	    var ax = x + (k === 0 ? 1 : k === 1 ? -1 : 0), ay = y + (k === 2 ? 1 : k === 3 ? -1 : 0);
   242	    if (ax < 0 || ay < 0 || ax >= cols || ay >= rows) continue;
   243	    var j = ay * cols + ax;
   244	    if (G.map.g[j] !== E.EMPTY) continue;
   245	    var dv = G.map.dist[j];
   246	    if (dv >= 0 && dv < bd) { bd = dv; bi = j; }
   247	  }
   248	  return bi;
   249	}
   250	
   250	
   251	function pickTarget(c) {
   252	  if (c.cy < 0.5) { c.tx = G.map.entryC + 0.5; c.ty = 0.5; return; }
   253	  var x = E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cx), 0, L.cols - 1), y = E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cy), 0, L.rows - 1);
   254	  var i = y * L.cols + x;
   255	  var ni = G.map.next[i];
   256	  if (ni < 0 || G.map.g[i] !== E.EMPTY) ni = bestNeighbor(i);
   257	  if (ni < 0) { c.tx = c.cx; c.ty = c.cy; return; }
   258	  c.tx = (ni % L.cols) + 0.5; c.ty = ((ni / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5;
   259	}
   260	
   261	function leak(c) {
   262	  c.dead = true;
   263	  G.leaks += c.leak; G.lives -= c.leak;
   264	  shake(9); E.audio.leak();
   265	  burst(sx(c.cx), sy(c.cy), '#ff4444', 16, 130);
   266	  floatT(sx(c.cx), sy(c.cy) - 10, '-' + c.leak, '#ff5555');
   267	  if (G.lives <= 0) {
   268	    G.lives = 0; G.phase = 'lose';
   269	    saveBest(G.wavesDone + 0);
   270	    E.audio.lose();
   271	  }
   272	}
   273	
   274	function damage(c, amt, src) {
   275	  if (c.dead) return;
   276	  var d = Math.max(1, amt - c.armor);
   277	  if (c.sh > 0) {
   278	    c.sh -= d; c.shT = 2.6;
   279	    if (c.sh < 0) { c.hp += c.sh; c.sh = 0; }
   280	  } else c.hp -= d;
   281	  c.flash = 0.12;
   282	  if (c.hp <= 0) {
   283	    c.dead = true;
   284	    G.gold += c.gold;
   285	    burst(sx(c.cx), sy(c.cy), c.col, c.boss ? 34 : 9, c.boss ? 190 : 100);
   286	    floatT(sx(c.cx), sy(c.cy) - 8, '+' + c.gold, '#ffe066');
   287	    E.audio.kill();
   288	    if (c.boss) { shake(12); E.audio.boom(); }
   289	  }
   290	}
   291	
   292	/* ---------------- towers fire ---------------- */
   293	function nearestTarget(tw, range) {
   294	  var bi = null, bd = -1;
   295	  for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) {
   296	    var c = G.creeps[k];
   297	    if (c.dead || c.cy < -0.2) continue;
   298	    var dx = c.cx - tw.x, dy = c.cy - tw.y;
   299	    if (dx * dx + dy * dy > range * range) continue;
   300	    // prefer the creep furthest along toward the exit
   301	    var prog;
   302	    if (c.fly) {
   303	      prog = 1000 - Math.hypot(c.cx - ((G.map.exit % L.cols) + 0.5), c.cy - (((G.map.exit / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5));
   304	    } else {
   305	      var di = G.map.dist[E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cy), 0, L.rows - 1) * L.cols + E.clamp(Math.floor(c.cx), 0, L.cols - 1)];
   306	      prog = 1000 - (di >= 0 ? di : 999);
   307	    }
   308	    if (prog > bd) { bd = prog; bi = c; }
   309	  }
   310	  return bi;
   311	}
   312	
   313	function fire(tw, dt) {
   314	  var d = T[tw.t], st = statOf(tw);
   315	  if (d.kind === 'bank' || d.wall) return;
   316	  tw.cd -= dt;
   317	  if (tw.cd > 0) return;
   318	  var tgt = nearestTarget(tw, st.range);
   319	  if (!tgt) return;
   320	  tw.ang = Math.atan2(tgt.cy - tw.y, tgt.cx - tw.x);
   321	  tw.cd = st.rate;
   322	  tw.pulse = 1;
   323	  if (d.kind === 'shot') {
   324	    G.bullets.push({ x: tw.x, y: tw.y, tgt: tgt, sp: 13, dmg: st.dmg, col: d.col, r: 3, kind: 'shot' });
   325	    E.audio.shoot();
   326	  } else if (d.kind === 'lob') {
   327	    G.bullets.push({ x: tw.x, y: tw.y, tx: tgt.cx, ty: tgt.cy, sp: 8, dmg: st.dmg, col: d.col, r: 4.5, kind: 'lob', aoe: d.aoe * (1 + (tw.lv - 1) * 0.06) });
   328	    E.audio.shoot();
   329	  } else if (d.kind === 'beam') {
   330	    damage(tgt, st.dmg);
   331	    tgt.slowT = d.slowT; tgt.slowF = Math.min(tgt.slowF, d.slow);
   332	    G.fx.push({ kind: 'beam', x1: tw.x, y1: tw.y, x2: tgt.cx, y2: tgt.cy, life: 0.14, max: 0.14, col: d.col });
   333	    E.audio.frost();
   334	  } else if (d.kind === 'chain') {
   335	    var hit = [tgt], cur = tgt, dmg = st.dmg;
   336	    var n = d.chains + (tw.lv >= 4 ? 1 : 0);
   337	    damage(cur, dmg);
   338	    G.fx.push({ kind: 'bolt', x1: tw.x, y1: tw.y, x2: cur.cx, y2: cur.cy, life: 0.16, max: 0.16, col: d.col });
   339	    for (var h = 1; h < n; h++) {
   340	      var nb = null, nd = 2.4 * 2.4;
   341	      for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) {
   342	        var c2 = G.creeps[k];
   343	        if (c2.dead || hit.indexOf(c2) >= 0 || c2.cy < -0.2) continue;
   344	        var dd = E.dist2(c2.cx, c2.cy, cur.cx, cur.cy);
   345	        if (dd < nd) { nd = dd; nb = c2; }
   346	      }
   347	      if (!nb) break;
   348	      dmg *= 0.72;
   349	      damage(nb, dmg);
   350	      G.fx.push({ kind: 'bolt', x1: cur.cx, y1: cur.cy, x2: nb.cx, y2: nb.cy, life: 0.16, max: 0.16, col: d.col });
   351	      hit.push(nb); cur = nb;
   352	    }
   353	    E.audio.zap();
   354	  }
   355	}
   356	
   357	/* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   358	function update(dt) {
   359	  G.time += dt;
   360	  if (G.phase !== 'play') { updFx(dt); return; }
   361	
   362	  if (!G.active) {
   363	    G.prep -= dt;
   364	    if (G.prep <= 0) startWave();
   365	  } else {
   366	    G.waveT += dt;
   367	    while (G.spawnI < G.spawnQ.length && G.spawnQ[G.spawnI].t <= G.waveT) { spawn(G.spawnQ[G.spawnI]); G.spawnI++; }
   368	    if (G.spawnI >= G.spawnQ.length && G.creeps.length === 0) endWave();
   369	  }
   370	
   371	  // creeps
   372	  for (var i = G.creeps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   373	    var c = G.creeps[i];
   374	    if (c.dead) { G.creeps.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   375	    if (c.flash > 0) c.flash -= dt;
   376	    if (c.slowT > 0) { c.slowT -= dt; if (c.slowT <= 0) c.slowF = 1; }
   377	    if (c.shMax > 0) {
   378	      c.shT -= dt;
   379	      if (c.shT <= 0 && c.sh < c.shMax) c.sh = Math.min(c.shMax, c.sh + c.shMax * 0.35 * dt);
   380	    }
   381	    var sp = c.sp * c.slowF;
   382	    if (c.fly) {
   383	      var ex = (G.map.exit % L.cols) + 0.5, ey = ((G.map.exit / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5;
   384	      var dx = ex - c.cx, dy = ey - c.cy, dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   385	      c.ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
   386	      if (dl < 0.25) { leak(c); continue; }
   387	      c.cx += dx / dl * sp * dt; c.cy += dy / dl * sp * dt;
   388	    } else {
   389	      if (c.tx === undefined || Math.hypot(c.tx - c.cx, c.ty - c.cy) < 0.06) pickTarget(c);
   390	      var dx2 = c.tx - c.cx, dy2 = c.ty - c.cy, dl2 = Math.hypot(dx2, dy2);
   391	      if (dl2 > 0.0001) {
   392	        c.ang = Math.atan2(dy2, dx2);
   393	        var mv = Math.min(dl2, sp * dt);
   394	        c.cx += dx2 / dl2 * mv; c.cy += dy2 / dl2 * mv;
   395	      }
   396	      var ex2 = (G.map.exit % L.cols) + 0.5, ey2 = ((G.map.exit / L.cols) | 0) + 0.5;
   397	      if (E.dist2(c.cx, c.cy, ex2, ey2) < 0.09) { leak(c); continue; }
   398	    }
   399	  }
   400	
   401	  // towers
   402	  for (var t = 0; t < G.towers.length; t++) {
   403	    var tw = G.towers[t];
   404	    if (tw.pulse > 0) tw.pulse -= dt * 4;
   405	    fire(tw, dt);
   406	  }
   407	
   408	  // bullets
   409	  for (var b = G.bullets.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {
   410	    var bu = G.bullets[b];
   411	    if (bu.kind === 'shot') {
   412	      if (!bu.tgt || bu.tgt.dead) { G.bullets.splice(b, 1); continue; }
   413	      var bx = bu.tgt.cx - bu.x, by = bu.tgt.cy - bu.y, bl = Math.hypot(bx, by);
   414	      var step = bu.sp * dt;
   415	      if (bl <= step) {
   416	        damage(bu.tgt, bu.dmg);
   417	        burst(sx(bu.tgt.cx), sy(bu.tgt.cy), bu.col, 4, 60);
   418	        G.bullets.splice(b, 1); continue;
   419	      }
   420	      bu.x += bx / bl * step; bu.y += by / bl * step;
   421	      bu.a = Math.atan2(by, bx);
   422	    } else {
   423	      var lx = bu.tx - bu.x, ly = bu.ty - bu.y, ll = Math.hypot(lx, ly);
   424	      var st2 = bu.sp * dt;
   425	      if (ll <= st2) {
   426	        for (var k2 = 0; k2 < G.creeps.length; k2++) {
   427	          var cc = G.creeps[k2];
   428	          if (cc.dead) continue;
   429	          if (E.dist2(cc.cx, cc.cy, bu.tx, bu.ty) <= bu.aoe * bu.aoe) damage(cc, bu.dmg);
   430	        }
   431	        G.fx.push({ kind: 'ring', x: bu.tx, y: bu.ty, r: bu.aoe, life: 0.3, max: 0.3, col: bu.col });
   432	        burst(sx(bu.tx), sy(bu.ty), bu.col, 14, 130);
   433	        E.audio.boom(); shake(4);
   434	        G.bullets.splice(b, 1); continue;
   435	      }
   436	      bu.x += lx / ll * st2; bu.y += ly / ll * st2;
   437	    }
   438	  }
   439	  updFx(dt);
   440	}
   441	
   442	function updFx(dt) {
   443	  for (var i = G.fx.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { G.fx[i].life -= dt; if (G.fx[i].life <= 0) G.fx.splice(i, 1); }
   444	  for (var p = G.parts.length - 1; p >= 0; p--) {
   445	    var q = G.parts[p];
   446	    q.life -= dt;
   447	    if (q.life <= 0) { G.parts.splice(p, 1); continue; }
   448	    q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt; q.vx *= 0.92; q.vy *= 0.92;
   449	  }
   450	  for (var x = G.texts.length - 1; x >= 0; x--) {
   451	    var tt = G.texts[x]; tt.life -= dt; tt.y -= 26 * dt;
   452	    if (tt.life <= 0) G.texts.splice(x, 1);
   453	  }
   454	  if (G.msgT > 0) G.msgT -= dt;
   455	  if (G.shake > 0) {
   456	    G.shake = Math.max(0, G.shake - dt * 26);
   457	    G.shakeX = (Math.random() - 0.5) * G.shake;
   458	    G.shakeY = (Math.random() - 0.5) * G.shake;
   459	  } else { G.shakeX = 0; G.shakeY = 0; }
   460	}
   461	
   462	/* ---------------- draw ---------------- */
   463	function rr(x, y, w, h, r) {
   464	  ctx.beginPath();
   465	  r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   466	  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
   467	  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   468	  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   469	  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
   470	  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
   471	  ctx.closePath();
   472	}
   473	
   474	function draw() {
   475	  var w = L.w, h = L.h, cell = L.cell;
   476	  ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0f18';
   477	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   478	
   479	  ctx.save();
   480	  ctx.translate(G.shakeX, G.shakeY);
   481	
   482	  // field
   483	  var fw = L.cols * cell, fh = L.rows * cell;
   484	  var grd = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, L.gy, 0, L.gy + fh);
   485	  grd.addColorStop(0, '#16203a'); grd.addColorStop(1, '#101728');
   486	  ctx.fillStyle = grd;
   487	  ctx.fillRect(L.gx, L.gy, fw, fh);
   488	
   489	  // grid lines
   490	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.045)';
   491	  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   492	  ctx.beginPath();
   493	  for (var x = 0; x <= L.cols; x++) { ctx.moveTo(L.gx + x * cell, L.gy); ctx.lineTo(L.gx + x * cell, L.gy + fh); }
   494	  for (var y = 0; y <= L.rows; y++) { ctx.moveTo(L.gx, L.gy + y * cell); ctx.lineTo(L.gx + fw, L.gy + y * cell); }
   495	  ctx.stroke();
   496	
   497	  // path preview (flow toward exit from entry)
   498	  drawPath();
   499	
   500	  // cells
   501	  for (var i = 0; i < G.map.n; i++) {
   502	    var g = G.map.g[i];
   503	    if (g === E.EMPTY) continue;
   504	    var cx = L.gx + (i % L.cols) * cell, cy = L.gy + ((i / L.cols) | 0) * cell;
   505	    if (g === E.ROCK) {
   506	      ctx.fillStyle = '#28303f';
   507	      rr(cx + 2, cy + 2, cell - 4, cell - 4, 5); ctx.fill();
   508	      ctx.fillStyle = '#333d4f';
   509	      rr(cx + 5, cy + 5, cell - 12, cell - 13, 3); ctx.fill();
   510	    } else if (g === E.WALL) {
   511	      ctx.fillStyle = T.wall.col2;
   512	      rr(cx + 1.5, cy + 1.5, cell - 3, cell - 3, 4); ctx.fill();
   513	      ctx.fillStyle = T.wall.col;
   514	      rr(cx + 3.5, cy + 3.5, cell - 7, cell - 9, 3); ctx.fill();
   515	    }
   516	  }
   517	
   518	  // entry / exit
   519	  drawPort(G.map.entry, '#6fd8ff', 'IN');
   520	  drawPort(G.map.exit, '#ff6b6b', 'OUT');
--- bulwark update/render ---
   520	  drawPort(G.map.exit, '#ff6b6b', 'OUT');
   521	
   522	  // selection ring
   523	  if (G.sel >= 0) {
   524	    var s = cellCenter(G.sel);
   525	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   526	    ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]); ctx.lineDashOffset = -G.time * 20;
   527	    rr(sx(s.x) - cell / 2 + 2, sy(s.y) - cell / 2 + 2, cell - 4, cell - 4, 5); ctx.stroke();
   528	    ctx.setLineDash([]);
   529	    var pd = T[G.pending];
   530	    if (pd && pd.range) {
   531	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)';
   532	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(s.x), sy(s.y), pd.range * cell, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   533	    }
   534	  }
   535	  if (G.kb) {
   536	    var kc = G.ky * L.cols + G.kx, kk = cellCenter(kc);
   537	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   538	    rr(sx(kk.x) - cell / 2 + 3, sy(kk.y) - cell / 2 + 3, cell - 6, cell - 6, 4); ctx.stroke();
   539	  }
   540	
   541	  // towers
   542	  for (var t = 0; t < G.towers.length; t++) drawTower(G.towers[t]);
   543	
   544	  // selected tower range
   545	  if (G.selTower) {
   546	    var st = statOf(G.selTower);
   547	    if (st.range) {
   548	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
   549	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(G.selTower.x), sy(G.selTower.y), st.range * cell, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   550	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.stroke();
   551	    }
   552	  }
   553	
   554	  // fx beams
   555	  for (var f = 0; f < G.fx.length; f++) {
   556	    var fx = G.fx[f], al = fx.life / fx.max;
   557	    if (fx.kind === 'beam') {
   558	      ctx.strokeStyle = fx.col; ctx.globalAlpha = al; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   559	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx(fx.x1), sy(fx.y1)); ctx.lineTo(sx(fx.x2), sy(fx.y2)); ctx.stroke();
   560	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   561	    } else if (fx.kind === 'bolt') {
   562	      ctx.strokeStyle = fx.col; ctx.globalAlpha = al; ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
   563	      ctx.beginPath();
   564	      var x1 = sx(fx.x1), y1 = sy(fx.y1), x2 = sx(fx.x2), y2 = sy(fx.y2);
   565	      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
   566	      for (var seg = 1; seg < 4; seg++) {
   567	        var tt2 = seg / 4;
   568	        ctx.lineTo(E.lerp(x1, x2, tt2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 12, E.lerp(y1, y2, tt2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 12);
   569	      }
   570	      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   571	    } else if (fx.kind === 'ring') {
   572	      ctx.strokeStyle = fx.col; ctx.globalAlpha = al * 0.9; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   573	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(fx.x), sy(fx.y), fx.r * cell * (1.3 - al * 0.35), 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   574	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   575	    }
   576	  }
   577	
   578	  // bullets
   579	  for (var b = 0; b < G.bullets.length; b++) {
   580	    var bu = G.bullets[b];
   581	    ctx.fillStyle = bu.col;
   582	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(bu.x), sy(bu.y), bu.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   583	  }
   584	
   585	  // creeps
   586	  for (var c = 0; c < G.creeps.length; c++) drawCreep(G.creeps[c]);
   587	
   588	  // particles
   589	  for (var p = 0; p < G.parts.length; p++) {
   590	    var q = G.parts[p];
   591	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, q.life / q.max);
   592	    ctx.fillStyle = q.c;
   593	    ctx.fillRect(q.x - q.r / 2, q.y - q.r / 2, q.r, q.r);
   594	  }
   595	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   596	
   597	  // float texts
   598	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   599	  for (var tx = 0; tx < G.texts.length; tx++) {
   600	    var ft = G.texts[tx];
   601	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, ft.life / ft.max);
   602	    ctx.fillStyle = ft.c;
   603	    ctx.font = '700 13px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   604	    ctx.fillText(ft.t, ft.x, ft.y);
   605	  }
   606	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   607	
   608	  ctx.restore();
   609	
   610	  drawHUD();
   611	  drawBar();
   612	  if (G.phase !== 'play') drawEnd();
   613	}
   614	
   615	function drawPath() {
   616	  var i = G.map.entry, cell = L.cell, guard = 0;
   617	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,190,255,0.18)';
   618	  ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(3, cell * 0.42);
   619	  ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   620	  ctx.beginPath();
   621	  var cc = cellCenter(i);
   622	  ctx.moveTo(sx(cc.x), sy(cc.y));
   623	  while (i >= 0 && i !== G.map.exit && guard++ < 600) {
   624	    i = G.map.next[i];
   625	    if (i < 0) break;
   626	    var p = cellCenter(i);
   627	    ctx.lineTo(sx(p.x), sy(p.y));
   628	  }
   629	  ctx.stroke();
   630	  ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
   631	}
   632	
   633	function drawPort(i, col, label) {
   634	  var cell = L.cell, p = cellCenter(i);
   635	  var px = sx(p.x), py = sy(p.y);
   636	  ctx.fillStyle = col;
   637	  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22 + 0.12 * Math.sin(G.time * 3);
   638	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 1, py - cell / 2 + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2, 5); ctx.fill();
   639	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   640	  ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   641	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 1, py - cell / 2 + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2, 5); ctx.stroke();
   642	  ctx.fillStyle = col;
   643	  ctx.font = '700 9px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   644	  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   645	  ctx.fillText(label, px, py);
   646	}
   647	
   648	function drawTower(tw) {
   649	  var d = T[tw.t], cell = L.cell;
   650	  var px = sx(tw.x), py = sy(tw.y);
   651	  var pulse = Math.max(0, tw.pulse);
   652	  ctx.fillStyle = d.col2;
   653	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 2, py - cell / 2 + 2, cell - 4, cell - 4, 6); ctx.fill();
   654	  var r = cell * (0.30 + pulse * 0.05);
   655	  ctx.fillStyle = d.col;
   656	  if (tw.t === 'bank') {
   657	    rr(px - r, py - r * 0.8, r * 2, r * 1.6, 3); ctx.fill();
   658	    ctx.fillStyle = d.col2;
   659	    ctx.font = '700 ' + Math.round(cell * 0.4) + 'px -apple-system,sans-serif';
   660	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   661	    ctx.fillText('$', px, py + 1);
   662	  } else {
   663	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   664	    ctx.save();
   665	    ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(tw.ang);
   666	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0f18';
   667	    ctx.fillRect(r * 0.3, -cell * 0.075, cell * 0.30, cell * 0.15);
   668	    ctx.restore();
   669	  }
   670	  // level pips
   671	  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
   672	  for (var l = 0; l < tw.lv; l++) {
   673	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
   674	    ctx.fillRect(px - cell / 2 + 3 + l * 4.5, py + cell / 2 - 6, 3, 3);
   675	  }
   676	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   677	}
   678	
   679	function drawCreep(c) {
   680	  var cell = L.cell, px = sx(c.cx), py = sy(c.cy);
   681	  var r = c.r * cell;
   682	  if (c.fly) {
   683	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.28)';
   684	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(px + 5, py + 7, r * 0.9, r * 0.5, 0, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   685	  }
   686	  ctx.fillStyle = c.flash > 0 ? '#ffffff' : c.col;
   687	  if (c.boss) {
   688	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(G.time * 0.7);
   689	    ctx.beginPath();
   690	    for (var k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
   691	      var a = k / 6 * 6.283;
   692	      ctx[k ? 'lineTo' : 'moveTo'](Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r);
   693	    }
   694	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   695	  } else if (c.fly) {
   696	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(c.ang);
   697	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.7, -r * 0.8); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.3, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.7, r * 0.8);
   698	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   699	  } else if (c.type === 'tank') {
   700	    rr(px - r, py - r, r * 2, r * 2, 3); ctx.fill();
   701	  } else {
   702	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   703	  }
   704	  if (c.slowF < 1) {
   705	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#6fd8ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   706	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r + 2.5, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   707	  }
   708	  if (c.sh > 0) {
   709	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,190,255,' + (0.35 + 0.55 * (c.sh / c.shMax)) + ')';
   710	    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   711	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r + 4, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   712	  }
   713	  // hp bar
   714	  if (c.hp < c.hpMax) {
   715	    var bw = r * 2.2;
   716	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
   717	    ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, py - r - 7, bw, 3.5);
   718	    ctx.fillStyle = c.hp / c.hpMax > 0.4 ? '#7ee081' : '#ff6b6b';
   719	    ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, py - r - 7, bw * Math.max(0, c.hp / c.hpMax), 3.5);
   720	  }
   721	}
   722	
   723	/* ------------- HUD ------------- */
   724	var goBtn = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
   725	var sndBtn = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
   726	
   727	function drawHUD() {
   728	  var w = L.w, hud = L.hud;
   729	  ctx.fillStyle = '#0e1420';
   730	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, hud);
   731	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)';
   732	  ctx.fillRect(0, hud - 1, w, 1);
   733	  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   734	
   735	  var pad = 10, cy = hud * 0.36;
   736	  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   737	  ctx.font = '700 15px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   738	  ctx.fillStyle = G.lives <= 5 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#e6f0ff';
   739	  ctx.fillText('♥ ' + G.lives, pad, cy);
   740	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe066';
   741	  ctx.fillText('◆ ' + Math.floor(G.gold), pad + 62, cy);
   742	  var wn = Math.max(1, G.wave);
   743	  ctx.fillStyle = (wn % 10 === 0 && G.active) ? '#ff5cc8' : '#cfe3ff';
   744	  ctx.fillText('W ' + wn + (G.endless ? '' : '/30'), pad + 140, cy);
   745	
   746	  ctx.font = '500 10px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   747	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,220,255,0.5)';
   748	  ctx.fillText('BEST ' + best, pad + 200, cy);
   749	  if (G.msgT > 0) {
   750	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + Math.min(1, G.msgT) + ')';
   751	    ctx.font = '700 11px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   752	    ctx.fillText(G.msg, pad, hud * 0.75);
   753	  } else if (G.hint) {
   754	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,220,255,0.65)';
   755	    ctx.font = '500 11px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   756	    ctx.fillText('Tap a cell, then a chip — maze them in.', pad, hud * 0.75);
   757	  }
   758	
   759	  // GO / wave state
   760	  goBtn = { x: w - 90, y: 7, w: 82, h: hud - 14 };
   761	
   762	  // sound (left of GO)
   763	  sndBtn = { x: goBtn.x - 34, y: 7 + (goBtn.h - 26) / 2, w: 28, h: 26 };
   764	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
   765	  rr(sndBtn.x, sndBtn.y, sndBtn.w, sndBtn.h, 5); ctx.fill();
   766	  ctx.fillStyle = E.audio.isOn() ? '#7ee081' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
   767	  ctx.font = '700 9px -apple-system,sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   768	  ctx.fillText(E.audio.isOn() ? 'SND' : 'OFF', sndBtn.x + sndBtn.w / 2, sndBtn.y + sndBtn.h / 2);
   769	
   770	  if (!G.active && G.phase === 'play') {
   771	    var pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(G.time * 5);
   772	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(126,224,129,' + (0.2 + pulse * 0.2) + ')';
   773	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.fill();
   774	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#7ee081'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   775	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.stroke();
   776	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d9ffdc'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   777	    ctx.font = '700 12px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   778	    ctx.fillText('GO  ' + Math.ceil(Math.max(0, G.prep)) + 's', goBtn.x + goBtn.w / 2, goBtn.y + goBtn.h / 2);
   779	  } else {
   780	    var left = G.spawnQ.length - G.spawnI + G.creeps.length;
   781	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
   782	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.fill();
   783	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(220,235,255,0.8)'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   784	    ctx.font = '700 12px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   785	    ctx.fillText('LEFT ' + left, goBtn.x + goBtn.w / 2, goBtn.y + goBtn.h / 2);
   786	  }
   787	}
   788	
   789	/* ------------- build bar / tower sheet ------------- */
   790	var chipRects = [];
   791	var sheetBtns = null;
   792	
   793	function drawBar() {
   794	  var w = L.w, h = L.h, bh = L.bar, by = h - bh;
   795	  ctx.fillStyle = '#0e1420';
   796	  ctx.fillRect(0, by, w, bh);
   797	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)';
   798	  ctx.fillRect(0, by, w, 1);
   799	
   800	  if (G.selTower) { drawSheet(by, bh); return; }
   801	  sheetBtns = null;
   802	
   803	  chipRects = [];
   804	  var n = CHIPS.length;
   805	  var gap = 4, cw = (w - gap * (n + 1)) / n;
   806	  var chH = bh - 26;
   807	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   808	    var key = CHIPS[i], d = T[key];
   809	    var x = gap + i * (cw + gap), y = by + 8;
   810	    chipRects.push({ x: x, y: y, w: cw, h: chH, key: key });
   811	    var afford = G.gold >= d.cost;
   812	    var selc = G.pending === key;
   813	    ctx.fillStyle = selc ? d.col2 : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
   814	    rr(x, y, cw, chH, 8); ctx.fill();
   815	    if (selc) { ctx.strokeStyle = d.col; ctx.lineWidth = 2; rr(x, y, cw, chH, 8); ctx.stroke(); }
   816	    ctx.globalAlpha = afford ? 1 : 0.35;
   817	    // icon
   818	    var icx = x + cw / 2, icy = y + chH * 0.36, ir = Math.min(cw, chH) * 0.20;
   819	    ctx.fillStyle = d.col;
   820	    if (key === 'wall') { rr(icx - ir, icy - ir * 0.8, ir * 2, ir * 1.6, 2); ctx.fill(); }
--- bulwark input/end ---
   820	    if (key === 'wall') { rr(icx - ir, icy - ir * 0.8, ir * 2, ir * 1.6, 2); ctx.fill(); }
   821	    else if (key === 'bank') { rr(icx - ir, icy - ir * 0.8, ir * 2, ir * 1.6, 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = d.col2; ctx.font = '700 11px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText('$', icx, icy); }
   822	    else { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(icx, icy, ir, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill(); }
   823	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   824	    ctx.fillStyle = '#dce8ff';
   825	    ctx.font = '700 9px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   826	    ctx.fillText(d.name, icx, y + chH * 0.68);
   827	    ctx.fillStyle = afford ? '#ffe066' : '#ff8a8a';
   828	    ctx.font = '700 10px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   829	    ctx.fillText(d.cost + 'g', icx, y + chH * 0.87);
   830	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   831	  }
   832	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(190,210,240,0.45)';
   833	  ctx.font = '500 10px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   834	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   835	  ctx.fillText(G.sel >= 0 ? 'Now tap a chip to build here' : 'Tap an empty cell, then a chip · tap a tower to upgrade', w / 2, h - 9);
   836	}
   837	
   838	function drawSheet(by, bh) {
   839	  var tw = G.selTower, d = T[tw.t], st = statOf(tw), w = L.w;
   840	  ctx.fillStyle = '#131b2b';
   841	  ctx.fillRect(0, by, w, bh);
   842	  ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   843	  ctx.fillStyle = d.col;
   844	  ctx.font = '700 13px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   845	  ctx.fillText(d.name + '  Lv' + tw.lv, 12, by + 16);
   846	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(210,225,250,0.7)';
   847	  ctx.font = '500 10px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   848	  var line = tw.t === 'bank'

   908	/* ---------------- input ---------------- */
   909	function hit(b, x, y, pad) {
   910	  pad = pad === undefined ? 8 : pad;
   911	  return b && x >= b.x - pad && x <= b.x + b.w + pad && y >= b.y - pad && y <= b.y + b.h + pad;
   912	}

   914	function onTap(px, py) {
   915	  E.audio.init();
   916	  G.kb = false;
   917	
   918	  if (G.phase !== 'play') {
   919	    if (endBtns && hit(endBtns.again, px, py)) { newGame(); return; }
   920	    if (endBtns && endBtns.cont && hit(endBtns.cont, px, py)) { G.endless = true; G.phase = 'play'; G.prep = 9; return; }
   921	    return;
   922	  }
   923	
   924	  if (hit(sndBtn, px, py, 10)) { E.audio.toggle(); return; }
   925	  if (py < L.hud) {
   926	    if (hit(goBtn, px, py) && !G.active) {
   927	      var bonus = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(G.prep) * 2);
   928	      if (bonus > 0) { G.gold += bonus; floatT(L.w / 2, L.gy + 30, '+' + bonus + 'g early', '#7ee081'); }
   929	      G.prep = 0; startWave();
   930	    }
   931	    return;
   932	  }
   933	
   934	  // sheet
   935	  if (G.selTower && sheetBtns) {
   936	    if (hit(sheetBtns.up, px, py, 4)) { upgrade(G.selTower); return; }
   937	    if (hit(sheetBtns.sell, px, py, 4)) { sell(G.selTower); return; }
   938	    if (hit(sheetBtns.close, px, py, 4)) { G.selTower = null; return; }
   939	  }
   940	
   941	  // chips
   942	  if (py > L.h - L.bar && !G.selTower) {
   943	    for (var i = 0; i < chipRects.length; i++) {
   944	      var r = chipRects[i];
   945	      if (hit(r, px, py, 3)) {
   946	        G.pending = r.key;
   947	        if (G.sel >= 0) tryBuild(G.sel, r.key);
   948	        else toast('PICK A CELL');
   949	        return;
   950	      }
   951	    }
   952	    return;
   953	  }
   954	
   955	  // field
   956	  var ci = cellAt(px, py);
   957	  if (ci < 0) { G.sel = -1; G.selTower = null; return; }
   958	  var tw = towerAt(ci);
   959	  if (tw) { G.selTower = tw; G.sel = -1; return; }
   960	  G.selTower = null;
   961	  if (G.map.g[ci] === E.EMPTY && ci !== G.map.entry && ci !== G.map.exit) {
   962	    if (G.sel === ci) tryBuild(ci, G.pending);
   963	    else G.sel = ci;
   964	  } else if (G.map.g[ci] === E.WALL) {
   965	    // tap a wall to remove for half refund
   966	    G.map.g[ci] = E.EMPTY; G.map.solve();
   967	    for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) G.creeps[k].tx = undefined;
   968	    G.gold += Math.floor(T.wall.cost * 0.6);
   969	    E.audio.coin();
   970	    G.sel = -1;
   971	  } else { G.sel = -1; }
   972	}

   992	  if (G.phase !== 'play') {
   993	    if (k === 'r' || k === 'enter' || k === ' ') { newGame(); e.preventDefault(); }
   994	    if (k === 'c' && G.phase === 'win') { G.endless = true; G.phase = 'play'; G.prep = 9; }
   995	    return;
   996	  }
   997	  if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') { G.kb = true; G.kx = Math.max(0, G.kx - 1); e.preventDefault(); }
   998	  else if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') { G.kb = true; G.kx = Math.min(L.cols - 1, G.kx + 1); e.preventDefault(); }
   999	  else if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w') { G.kb = true; G.ky = Math.max(0, G.ky - 1); e.preventDefault(); }
  1000	  else if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') { G.kb = true; G.ky = Math.min(L.rows - 1, G.ky + 1); e.preventDefault(); }
  1001	  else if (k === ' ' || k === 'enter') {
  1002	    e.preventDefault();
  1003	    G.kb = true;
  1004	    var ci = G.ky * L.cols + G.kx;
  1005	    var tw = towerAt(ci);
  1006	    if (tw) G.selTower = (G.selTower === tw ? null : tw);
  1007	    else { G.selTower = null; tryBuild(ci, G.pending); }
  1008	  }
  1009	  else if (k >= '1' && k <= '6') { G.pending = CHIPS[parseInt(k, 10) - 1]; toast(T[G.pending].name + ' SELECTED'); }
  1010	  else if (k === 'u') { var t2 = G.selTower || towerAt(G.ky * L.cols + G.kx); if (t2) upgrade(t2); }
  1011	  else if (k === 'x') { var t3 = G.selTower || towerAt(G.ky * L.cols + G.kx); if (t3) sell(t3); }
  1012	  else if (k === 'g') { if (!G.active) { G.gold += Math.max(0, Math.ceil(G.prep) * 2); G.prep = 0; startWave(); } }
  1013	  else if (k === 'r') newGame();
  1014	  else if (k === 'm') E.audio.toggle();
  1015	});

  1018	var last = 0;
  1019	function frame(ts) {
  1020	  if (!last) last = ts;
  1021	  var dt = Math.min(0.05, (ts - last) / 1000);
  1022	  last = ts;
  1023	  if (started) { update(dt); draw(); }
  1024	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  1025	}

--- engine ---
     1	/* Bulwark - engine: rng, audio, math, map gen, pathfinding */
     2	'use strict';
     3	
     4	var E = {};
     5	
     6	/* ---------- rng ---------- */
     7	E.rng = function (seed) {
     8	  var s = seed >>> 0;
     9	  return function () {
    10	    s |= 0; s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    11	    var t = Math.imul(s ^ (s >>> 15), 1 | s);
    12	    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    13	    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    14	  };
    15	};
    16	
    17	/* ---------- math ---------- */
    18	E.clamp = function (v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); };
    19	E.lerp = function (a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; };
    20	E.dist2 = function (ax, ay, bx, by) { var dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by; return dx * dx + dy * dy; };
    21	
    22	/* ---------- audio (WebAudio only, no files) ---------- */
    23	E.audio = (function () {
    24	  var ctx = null, master = null, on = true;
    25	  function init() {
    26	    if (ctx) return;
    27	    var AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    28	    if (!AC) { on = false; return; }
    29	    try {
    30	      ctx = new AC();
    31	      master = ctx.createGain();
    32	      master.gain.value = 0.22;
    33	      master.connect(ctx.destination);
    34	    } catch (e) { on = false; }
    35	  }
    36	  function blip(freq, dur, type, vol, slide) {
    37	    if (!on || !ctx) return;
    38	    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume();
    39	    var t = ctx.currentTime;
    40	    var o = ctx.createOscillator(), g = ctx.createGain();
    41	    o.type = type || 'square';
    42	    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    43	    if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, freq * slide), t + dur);
    44	    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
    45	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || 0.5, t + 0.006);
    46	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    47	    o.connect(g); g.connect(master);
    48	    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    49	  }
    50	  function noise(dur, vol, f0) {
    51	    if (!on || !ctx) return;
    52	    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume();
    53	    var n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
    54	    var buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
    55	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
    56	    var src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf;
    57	    var bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.frequency.value = f0 || 900; bp.Q.value = 1.2;
    58	    var g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol || 0.4;
    59	    src.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(master);
    60	    src.start();
    61	  }
    62	  return {
    63	    init: init,
    64	    toggle: function () { on = !on; if (on) init(); return on; },
    65	    isOn: function () { return on; },
    66	    shoot: function () { blip(680, 0.05, 'square', 0.16, 0.5); },
    67	    zap: function () { blip(1400, 0.09, 'sawtooth', 0.14, 0.3); },
    68	    boom: function () { noise(0.22, 0.4, 220); },
    69	    frost: function () { blip(320, 0.09, 'sine', 0.14, 1.8); },
    70	    kill: function () { blip(220, 0.09, 'triangle', 0.22, 2.2); },
    71	    build: function () { blip(440, 0.07, 'square', 0.22, 1.6); blip(660, 0.09, 'square', 0.14, 1.4); },
    72	    deny: function () { blip(150, 0.13, 'sawtooth', 0.2, 0.7); },
    73	    leak: function () { blip(180, 0.3, 'sawtooth', 0.3, 0.4); noise(0.25, 0.3, 320); },
    74	    coin: function () { blip(880, 0.06, 'square', 0.14, 1.5); blip(1320, 0.08, 'square', 0.1, 1.2); },
    75	    wave: function () { blip(300, 0.12, 'triangle', 0.2, 1.9); },
    76	    win: function () { [523, 659, 784, 1046].forEach(function (f, i) { setTimeout(function () { blip(f, 0.22, 'triangle', 0.22, 1); }, i * 120); }); },
    77	    lose: function () { [400, 320, 250, 180].forEach(function (f, i) { setTimeout(function () { blip(f, 0.3, 'sawtooth', 0.24, 0.7); }, i * 150); }); }
    78	  };
    79	})();
    80	
    81	/* ---------- map ---------- */
    82	/* cell codes */
    83	E.EMPTY = 0; E.ROCK = 1; E.WALL = 2; E.TOWER = 3;
    84	
    85	E.Map = function (cols, rows, seed) {
    86	  this.cols = cols; this.rows = rows;
    87	  this.n = cols * rows;
    88	  this.g = new Uint8Array(this.n);
    89	  this.dist = new Int32Array(this.n);
    90	  this.next = new Int32Array(this.n);
    91	  var r = E.rng(seed);
    92	  this.entryC = 1 + Math.floor(r() * (cols - 2));
    93	  this.exitC = 1 + Math.floor(r() * (cols - 2));
    94	  if (Math.abs(this.entryC - this.exitC) < 2) this.exitC = (this.exitC + 3) % (cols - 2) + 1;
    95	  this.entry = this.entryC;                  // index in top row
    96	  this.exit = (rows - 1) * cols + this.exitC; // index in bottom row
    97	  // rock blobs
    98	  var blobs = 3 + Math.floor(r() * 4);
    99	  for (var b = 0; b < blobs; b++) {
   100	    var cx = Math.floor(r() * cols), cy = 2 + Math.floor(r() * (rows - 4));
   101	    var sz = 2 + Math.floor(r() * 4);
   102	    for (var k = 0; k < sz; k++) {
   103	      var x = cx + Math.round((r() - 0.5) * 2.4), y = cy + Math.round((r() - 0.5) * 2.4);
   104	      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= cols || y >= rows) continue;
   105	      var i = y * cols + x;
   106	      if (y === 0 || y === rows - 1) continue;
   107	      if (x === this.entryC && y < 2) continue;
   108	      if (x === this.exitC && y > rows - 3) continue;
   109	      this.g[i] = E.ROCK;
   110	    }
   111	  }
   112	  // keep entry/exit lanes clear
   113	  this.g[this.entry] = E.EMPTY;
   114	  this.g[this.exit] = E.EMPTY;
   115	  this.g[this.entry + cols] = E.EMPTY;
   116	  this.g[this.exit - cols] = E.EMPTY;
   117	  if (!this.solve()) { // rare: carve a corridor
   118	    for (var y2 = 0; y2 < rows; y2++) this.g[y2 * cols + this.entryC] = E.EMPTY;
   119	    for (var x2 = 0; x2 < cols; x2++) this.g[(rows - 1) * cols + x2] = E.EMPTY;
   120	    this.solve();
   121	  }
   122	};
   123	
   124	E.Map.prototype.idx = function (x, y) { return y * this.cols + x; };
   125	E.Map.prototype.walkable = function (i) { return this.g[i] === E.EMPTY; };
   126	
   127	/* BFS distance field from exit; fills dist + next. returns true if entry reachable */
   128	E.Map.prototype.solve = function (override, ovVal) {
   129	  var cols = this.cols, rows = this.rows, n = this.n;
   130	  var d = this.dist, nx = this.next, g = this.g;
   131	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { d[i] = -1; nx[i] = -1; }
   132	  var q = new Int32Array(n), head = 0, tail = 0;
   133	  var blocked = function (j) {
   134	    var v = (override === j) ? ovVal : g[j];
   135	    return v !== E.EMPTY;
   136	  };
   137	  if (blocked(this.exit)) return false;
   138	  d[this.exit] = 0; q[tail++] = this.exit;
   139	  while (head < tail) {
   140	    var c = q[head++], cd = d[c];

exec

   572	      ctx.strokeStyle = fx.col; ctx.globalAlpha = al * 0.9; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   573	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(fx.x), sy(fx.y), fx.r * cell * (1.3 - al * 0.35), 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   574	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   575	    }
   576	  }
   577	
   578	  // bullets
   579	  for (var b = 0; b < G.bullets.length; b++) {
   580	    var bu = G.bullets[b];
   581	    ctx.fillStyle = bu.col;
   582	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(bu.x), sy(bu.y), bu.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   583	  }
   584	
   585	  // creeps
   586	  for (var c = 0; c < G.creeps.length; c++) drawCreep(G.creeps[c]);
   587	
   588	  // particles
   589	  for (var p = 0; p < G.parts.length; p++) {
   590	    var q = G.parts[p];
   591	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, q.life / q.max);
   592	    ctx.fillStyle = q.c;
   593	    ctx.fillRect(q.x - q.r / 2, q.y - q.r / 2, q.r, q.r);
   594	  }
   595	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   596	
   597	  // float texts
   598	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   599	  for (var tx = 0; tx < G.texts.length; tx++) {
   600	    var ft = G.texts[tx];
   601	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, ft.life / ft.max);
   602	    ctx.fillStyle = ft.c;
   603	    ctx.font = '700 13px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   604	    ctx.fillText(ft.t, ft.x, ft.y);
   605	  }
   606	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   607	
   608	  ctx.restore();
   609	
   610	  drawHUD();
   611	  drawBar();
   612	  if (G.phase !== 'play') drawEnd();
   613	}
   614	
   615	function drawPath() {
   616	  var i = G.map.entry, cell = L.cell, guard = 0;
   617	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,190,255,0.18)';
   618	  ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(3, cell * 0.42);
   619	  ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   620	  ctx.beginPath();
   621	  var cc = cellCenter(i);
   622	  ctx.moveTo(sx(cc.x), sy(cc.y));
   623	  while (i >= 0 && i !== G.map.exit && guard++ < 600) {
   624	    i = G.map.next[i];
   625	    if (i < 0) break;
   626	    var p = cellCenter(i);
   627	    ctx.lineTo(sx(p.x), sy(p.y));
   628	  }
   629	  ctx.stroke();
   630	  ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
   631	}
   632	
   633	function drawPort(i, col, label) {
   634	  var cell = L.cell, p = cellCenter(i);
   635	  var px = sx(p.x), py = sy(p.y);
   636	  ctx.fillStyle = col;
   637	  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22 + 0.12 * Math.sin(G.time * 3);
   638	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 1, py - cell / 2 + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2, 5); ctx.fill();
   639	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   640	  ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   641	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 1, py - cell / 2 + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2, 5); ctx.stroke();
   642	  ctx.fillStyle = col;
   643	  ctx.font = '700 9px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   644	  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   645	  ctx.fillText(label, px, py);
   646	}
   647	
   648	function drawTower(tw) {
   649	  var d = T[tw.t], cell = L.cell;
   650	  var px = sx(tw.x), py = sy(tw.y);
   651	  var pulse = Math.max(0, tw.pulse);
   652	  ctx.fillStyle = d.col2;
   653	  rr(px - cell / 2 + 2, py - cell / 2 + 2, cell - 4, cell - 4, 6); ctx.fill();
   654	  var r = cell * (0.30 + pulse * 0.05);
   655	  ctx.fillStyle = d.col;
   656	  if (tw.t === 'bank') {
   657	    rr(px - r, py - r * 0.8, r * 2, r * 1.6, 3); ctx.fill();
   658	    ctx.fillStyle = d.col2;
   659	    ctx.font = '700 ' + Math.round(cell * 0.4) + 'px -apple-system,sans-serif';
   660	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   661	    ctx.fillText('$', px, py + 1);
   662	  } else {
   663	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   664	    ctx.save();
   665	    ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(tw.ang);
   666	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0f18';
   667	    ctx.fillRect(r * 0.3, -cell * 0.075, cell * 0.30, cell * 0.15);
   668	    ctx.restore();
   669	  }
   670	  // level pips
   671	  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
   672	  for (var l = 0; l < tw.lv; l++) {
   673	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
   674	    ctx.fillRect(px - cell / 2 + 3 + l * 4.5, py + cell / 2 - 6, 3, 3);
   675	  }
   676	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   677	}
   678	
   679	function drawCreep(c) {
   680	  var cell = L.cell, px = sx(c.cx), py = sy(c.cy);
   681	  var r = c.r * cell;
   682	  if (c.fly) {
   683	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.28)';
   684	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(px + 5, py + 7, r * 0.9, r * 0.5, 0, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   685	  }
   686	  ctx.fillStyle = c.flash > 0 ? '#ffffff' : c.col;
   687	  if (c.boss) {
   688	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(G.time * 0.7);
   689	    ctx.beginPath();
   690	    for (var k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
   691	      var a = k / 6 * 6.283;
   692	      ctx[k ? 'lineTo' : 'moveTo'](Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r);
   693	    }
   694	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   695	  } else if (c.fly) {
   696	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(c.ang);
   697	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.7, -r * 0.8); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.3, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.7, r * 0.8);
   698	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   699	  } else if (c.type === 'tank') {
   700	    rr(px - r, py - r, r * 2, r * 2, 3); ctx.fill();
   701	  } else {
   702	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   703	  }
   704	  if (c.slowF < 1) {
   705	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#6fd8ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   706	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r + 2.5, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   707	  }
   708	  if (c.sh > 0) {
   709	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,190,255,' + (0.35 + 0.55 * (c.sh / c.shMax)) + ')';
   710	    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   711	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r + 4, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   712	  }
   713	  // hp bar
   714	  if (c.hp < c.hpMax) {
   715	    var bw = r * 2.2;
   716	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
   717	    ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, py - r - 7, bw, 3.5);
   718	    ctx.fillStyle = c.hp / c.hpMax > 0.4 ? '#7ee081' : '#ff6b6b';
   719	    ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, py - r - 7, bw * Math.max(0, c.hp / c.hpMax), 3.5);
   720	  }
   721	}

   723	/* ------------- HUD ------------- */
   724	var goBtn = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
   725	var sndBtn = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };

   727	function drawHUD() {
   728	  var w = L.w, hud = L.hud;
   729	  ctx.fillStyle = '#0e1420';
   730	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, hud);
   731	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)';
   732	  ctx.fillRect(0, hud - 1, w, 1);
   733	  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   734	
   735	  var pad = 10, cy = hud * 0.36;
   736	  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   737	  ctx.font = '700 15px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   738	  ctx.fillStyle = G.lives <= 5 ? '#ff6b6b' : '#e6f0ff';
   739	  ctx.fillText('♥ ' + G.lives, pad, cy);
   740	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe066';
   741	  ctx.fillText('◆ ' + Math.floor(G.gold), pad + 62, cy);
   742	  var wn = Math.max(1, G.wave);
   743	  ctx.fillStyle = (wn % 10 === 0 && G.active) ? '#ff5cc8' : '#cfe3ff';
   744	  ctx.fillText('W ' + wn + (G.endless ? '' : '/30'), pad + 140, cy);
   745	
   746	  ctx.font = '500 10px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   747	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,220,255,0.5)';
   748	  ctx.fillText('BEST ' + best, pad + 200, cy);
   749	  if (G.msgT > 0) {
   750	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + Math.min(1, G.msgT) + ')';
   751	    ctx.font = '700 11px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   752	    ctx.fillText(G.msg, pad, hud * 0.75);
   753	  } else if (G.hint) {
   754	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,220,255,0.65)';
   755	    ctx.font = '500 11px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   756	    ctx.fillText('Tap a cell, then a chip — maze them in.', pad, hud * 0.75);
   757	  }
   758	
   759	  // GO / wave state
   760	  goBtn = { x: w - 90, y: 7, w: 82, h: hud - 14 };
   761	
   762	  // sound (left of GO)
   763	  sndBtn = { x: goBtn.x - 34, y: 7 + (goBtn.h - 26) / 2, w: 28, h: 26 };
   764	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
   765	  rr(sndBtn.x, sndBtn.y, sndBtn.w, sndBtn.h, 5); ctx.fill();
   766	  ctx.fillStyle = E.audio.isOn() ? '#7ee081' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
   767	  ctx.font = '700 9px -apple-system,sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   768	  ctx.fillText(E.audio.isOn() ? 'SND' : 'OFF', sndBtn.x + sndBtn.w / 2, sndBtn.y + sndBtn.h / 2);
   769	
   770	  if (!G.active && G.phase === 'play') {
   771	    var pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(G.time * 5);
   772	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(126,224,129,' + (0.2 + pulse * 0.2) + ')';
   773	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.fill();
   774	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#7ee081'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   775	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.stroke();
   776	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d9ffdc'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   777	    ctx.font = '700 12px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   778	    ctx.fillText('GO  ' + Math.ceil(Math.max(0, G.prep)) + 's', goBtn.x + goBtn.w / 2, goBtn.y + goBtn.h / 2);
   779	  } else {
   780	    var left = G.spawnQ.length - G.spawnI + G.creeps.length;
   781	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
   782	    rr(goBtn.x, goBtn.y, goBtn.w, goBtn.h, 6); ctx.fill();

   784	    ctx.font = '700 12px -apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif';
   785	    ctx.fillText('LEFT ' + left, goBtn.x + goBtn.w / 2, goBtn.y + goBtn.h / 2);
   786	  }
   787	}

   789	/* ------------- build bar / tower sheet ------------- */
   790	var chipRects = [];
   908	/* ---------------- input ---------------- */
   909	function hit(b, x, y, pad) {
   910	  pad = pad === undefined ? 8 : pad;
   911	  return b && x >= b.x - pad && x <= b.x + b.w + pad && y >= b.y - pad && y <= b.y + b.h + pad;
   912	}
   913	
   914	function onTap(px, py) {
   915	  E.audio.init();
   916	  G.kb = false;
   917	
   918	  if (G.phase !== 'play') {
   919	    if (endBtns && hit(endBtns.again, px, py)) { newGame(); return; }
   920	    if (endBtns && endBtns.cont && hit(endBtns.cont, px, py)) { G.endless = true; G.phase = 'play'; G.prep = 9; return; }
   921	    return;
   922	  }
   923	
   924	  if (hit(sndBtn, px, py, 10)) { E.audio.toggle(); return; }
   925	  if (py < L.hud) {
   926	    if (hit(goBtn, px, py) && !G.active) {
   927	      var bonus = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(G.prep) * 2);
   928	      if (bonus > 0) { G.gold += bonus; floatT(L.w / 2, L.gy + 30, '+' + bonus + 'g early', '#7ee081'); }
   929	      G.prep = 0; startWave();
   930	    }
   931	    return;
   932	  }
   933	
   934	  // sheet
   935	  if (G.selTower && sheetBtns) {
   936	    if (hit(sheetBtns.up, px, py, 4)) { upgrade(G.selTower); return; }
   937	    if (hit(sheetBtns.sell, px, py, 4)) { sell(G.selTower); return; }
   938	    if (hit(sheetBtns.close, px, py, 4)) { G.selTower = null; return; }
   939	  }
   940	
   941	  // chips
   942	  if (py > L.h - L.bar && !G.selTower) {
   943	    for (var i = 0; i < chipRects.length; i++) {
   944	      var r = chipRects[i];
   945	      if (hit(r, px, py, 3)) {
   946	        G.pending = r.key;
   947	        if (G.sel >= 0) tryBuild(G.sel, r.key);
   948	        else toast('PICK A CELL');
   949	        return;
   950	      }
   951	    }
   952	    return;
   953	  }
   954	
   955	  // field
   956	  var ci = cellAt(px, py);
   957	  if (ci < 0) { G.sel = -1; G.selTower = null; return; }
   958	  var tw = towerAt(ci);
   959	  if (tw) { G.selTower = tw; G.sel = -1; return; }
   960	  G.selTower = null;
   961	  if (G.map.g[ci] === E.EMPTY && ci !== G.map.entry && ci !== G.map.exit) {
   962	    if (G.sel === ci) tryBuild(ci, G.pending);
   963	    else G.sel = ci;
   964	  } else if (G.map.g[ci] === E.WALL) {
   965	    // tap a wall to remove for half refund
   966	    G.map.g[ci] = E.EMPTY; G.map.solve();
   967	    for (var k = 0; k < G.creeps.length; k++) G.creeps[k].tx = undefined;
   968	    G.gold += Math.floor(T.wall.cost * 0.6);
   969	    E.audio.coin();
   970	    G.sel = -1;
   971	  } else { G.sel = -1; }
   972	}
   973	
   974	function pos(e) {
   975	  var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   976	  return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
   977	}
   978	cv.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {
   979	  e.preventDefault();
   980	  var t = e.changedTouches[0], r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   981	  onTap(t.clientX - r.left, t.clientY - r.top);
   982	}, { passive: false });
   983	cv.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   984	cv.addEventListener('touchend', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   985	cv.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { var p = pos(e); onTap(p.x, p.y); });
   986	document.addEventListener('gesturestart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   987	document.addEventListener('contextmenu', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   988	
   989	document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   990	  var k = e.key.toLowerCase();
   991	  E.audio.init();
   992	  if (G.phase !== 'play') {
   993	    if (k === 'r' || k === 'enter' || k === ' ') { newGame(); e.preventDefault(); }
   994	    if (k === 'c' && G.phase === 'win') { G.endless = true; G.phase = 'play'; G.prep = 9; }
   995	    return;
   996	  }
   997	  if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') { G.kb = true; G.kx = Math.max(0, G.kx - 1); e.preventDefault(); }
   998	  else if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') { G.kb = true; G.kx = Math.min(L.cols - 1, G.kx + 1); e.preventDefault(); }
   999	  else if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w') { G.kb = true; G.ky = Math.max(0, G.ky - 1); e.preventDefault(); }
  1000	  else if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') { G.kb = true; G.ky = Math.min(L.rows - 1, G.ky + 1); e.preventDefault(); }
  1001	  else if (k === ' ' || k === 'enter') {
  1002	    e.preventDefault();
  1003	    G.kb = true;
  1004	    var ci = G.ky * L.cols + G.kx;
  1005	    var tw = towerAt(ci);
  1006	    if (tw) G.selTower = (G.selTower === tw ? null : tw);
  1007	    else { G.selTower = null; tryBuild(ci, G.pending); }
  1008	  }
  1009	  else if (k >= '1' && k <= '6') { G.pending = CHIPS[parseInt(k, 10) - 1]; toast(T[G.pending].name + ' SELECTED'); }
  1010	  else if (k === 'u') { var t2 = G.selTower || towerAt(G.ky * L.cols + G.kx); if (t2) upgrade(t2); }
  1011	  else if (k === 'x') { var t3 = G.selTower || towerAt(G.ky * L.cols + G.kx); if (t3) sell(t3); }
  1012	  else if (k === 'g') { if (!G.active) { G.gold += Math.max(0, Math.ceil(G.prep) * 2); G.prep = 0; startWave(); } }
  1013	  else if (k === 'r') newGame();
  1014	  else if (k === 'm') E.audio.toggle();
  1015	});

    10	  var rotEl = document.getElementById('rot');
    11	  var W = 800, H = 400, SC = 1, portrait = false;

    13	  function resize() {
    14	    W = wrap.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    15	    H = wrap.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    16	    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    17	    var long = Math.max(W, H);

    30	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- layout
    31	  var UI = {};
    32	  function layout() {
    33	    var r = Math.max(38, Math.min(58, H * 0.155));
    34	    UI.gas = { x: W - r - 22, y: H - r - 20, r: r };
    35	    UI.boost = { x: W - r - 30, y: H - r * 2.7 - 26, r: Math.max(30, r * 0.7) };
    36	    UI.brake = { x: r + 20, y: H - r - 20, r: r * 0.86 };
