    40	
    41	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- audio
    42	  var AU = { ctx: null, on: false };
    43	  function audioInit() {
    44	    if (AU.ctx) { if (AU.ctx.state === 'suspended') AU.ctx.resume(); return; }
    45	    var AC = g.AudioContext || g.webkitAudioContext;
    46	    if (!AC) return;
    47	    try {
    48	      var a = new AC();
    49	      var master = a.createGain(); master.gain.value = 0.5; master.connect(a.destination);
    50	      var osc = a.createOscillator(); osc.type = 'sawtooth'; osc.frequency.value = 70;
    51	      var osc2 = a.createOscillator(); osc2.type = 'square'; osc2.frequency.value = 35;
    52	      var lp = a.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 900;
    53	      var eg = a.createGain(); eg.gain.value = 0;
    54	      osc.connect(lp); osc2.connect(lp); lp.connect(eg); eg.connect(master);
    55	      osc.start(); osc2.start();
    56	      // noise buffer
    57	      var nb = a.createBuffer(1, a.sampleRate * 0.5, a.sampleRate), d = nb.getChannelData(0);
    58	      for (var i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / d.length);
    59	      AU.ctx = a; AU.master = master; AU.osc = osc; AU.osc2 = osc2; AU.eg = eg; AU.lp = lp; AU.noise = nb;
    60	      AU.on = true;
    61	    } catch (e) { AU.on = false; }
    62	  }
    63	  function engine(rpm, gas, boost) {
    64	    if (!AU.on) return;
    65	    var t = AU.ctx.currentTime;
    66	    var f = 62 + rpm * 250 + (boost ? 55 : 0);
    67	    AU.osc.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f, t, 0.06);
    68	    AU.osc2.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f * 0.5, t, 0.06);
    69	    AU.lp.frequency.setTargetAtTime(500 + rpm * 1600 + (gas ? 500 : 0), t, 0.08);
    70	    AU.eg.gain.setTargetAtTime(gas ? 0.075 : 0.026, t, 0.08);
    71	  }
    72	  function thud(vol, dur, freq) {
    73	    if (!AU.on) return;
    74	    var a = AU.ctx, t = a.currentTime;
    75	    var s = a.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = AU.noise;
    76	    var f = a.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'bandpass'; f.frequency.value = freq || 220; f.Q.value = 0.8;
    77	    var gn = a.createGain(); gn.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t);
    78	    gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0007, t + (dur || 0.25));
    79	    s.connect(f); f.connect(gn); gn.connect(AU.master); s.start(t); s.stop(t + (dur || 0.25) + 0.05);
    80	  }
    81	  function blip(f0, f1, dur, vol) {
    82	    if (!AU.on) return;
    83	    var a = AU.ctx, t = a.currentTime;
    84	    var o = a.createOscillator(); o.type = 'triangle';
    85	    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur);
    86	    var gn = a.createGain(); gn.gain.setValueAtTime(vol || 0.15, t);
    87	    gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + dur);
    88	    o.connect(gn); gn.connect(AU.master); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- input
    92	  var inp = { gas: false, brake: false, boost: false, lean: 0 };
    93	  var keys = {};
    94	  var ptrs = {};   // id -> {role, y0, lean}
    95	  var uiTapped = null;
    96	
    97	  function pos(e) {
    98	    var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
    99	    return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
   100	  }
   101	  function inCircle(p, c) { var dx = p.x - c.x, dy = p.y - c.y; return dx * dx + dy * dy <= (c.r + 12) * (c.r + 12); }
   102	  function inRect(p, r) { return p.x >= r.x && p.x <= r.x + r.w && p.y >= r.y && p.y <= r.y + r.h; }
   103	
   104	  function down(id, p) {
   105	    audioInit();
   106	    if (S.mode !== 'run' && S.mode !== 'crash') { uiTapped = p; return; }
   107	    var role = 'none';
   108	    if (inCircle(p, UI.boost)) role = 'boost';
   109	    else if (p.x > W * 0.5) role = 'gas';
   110	    else { role = 'lean'; if (inCircle(p, UI.brake)) role = 'leanbrake'; }
   111	    ptrs[id] = { role: role, y0: p.y, lean: 0 };
   112	    apply();
   113	  }
   114	  function move(id, p) {
   115	    var t = ptrs[id]; if (!t) return;
   116	    if (t.role === 'lean' || t.role === 'leanbrake') {
   117	      t.lean = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (t.y0 - p.y) / 62));
   118	      if (t.role === 'leanbrake' && Math.abs(t.y0 - p.y) > 26) t.role = 'lean';
   119	    } else if (t.role === 'gas' && inCircle(p, UI.boost)) t.role = 'boost';
   120	    apply();
   121	  }
   122	  function up(id) { delete ptrs[id]; apply(); }
   123	
   124	  function apply() {
   125	    var gas = false, br = false, bo = false, ln = 0, has = false;
   126	    for (var k in ptrs) {
   127	      var t = ptrs[k];
   128	      if (t.role === 'gas') gas = true;
   129	      else if (t.role === 'boost') { bo = true; gas = true; }
   130	      else if (t.role === 'leanbrake') { br = true; ln += t.lean; has = true; }
   131	      else if (t.role === 'lean') { ln += t.lean; has = true; }
   132	    }
   133	    inp.tGas = gas; inp.tBrake = br; inp.tBoost = bo;
   134	    inp.tLean = has ? Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ln)) : 0;
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  function pd(e) { e.preventDefault(); try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (x) { } down(e.pointerId, pos(e)); }
   138	  function pm(e) { e.preventDefault(); move(e.pointerId, pos(e)); }
   139	  function pu(e) { e.preventDefault(); up(e.pointerId); }
   140	  if (window.PointerEvent) {
   141	    cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', pd);
   142	    cv.addEventListener('pointermove', pm);
   143	    cv.addEventListener('pointerup', pu);
   144	    cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', pu);
   145	  } else {
   146	    cv.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {
   147	      e.preventDefault();
   148	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) { var t = e.changedTouches[i]; down(t.identifier, pos(t)); }
   149	    }, { passive: false });
   150	    cv.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) {
   151	      e.preventDefault();
   152	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) { var t = e.changedTouches[i]; move(t.identifier, pos(t)); }
   153	    }, { passive: false });
   154	    var tend = function (e) {
   155	      e.preventDefault();
   156	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) up(e.changedTouches[i].identifier);
   157	    };
   158	    cv.addEventListener('touchend', tend, { passive: false });
   159	    cv.addEventListener('touchcancel', tend, { passive: false });
   160	    cv.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); down('m', pos(e)); });
   161	    cv.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) { if (ptrs['m']) { e.preventDefault(); move('m', pos(e)); } });
   162	    window.addEventListener('mouseup', function () { up('m'); });
   163	  }
   164	  document.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   165	  document.addEventListener('gesturestart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   166	
   167	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   168	    audioInit();
   169	    keys[e.code] = true;
   170	    if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'Space', 'Enter', 'ShiftLeft', 'ShiftRight'].indexOf(e.code) >= 0) e.preventDefault();
   171	    if (e.code === 'KeyR') restartTrack();
   172	    if ((e.code === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') && (S.mode === 'result' || S.mode === 'medal')) advance(0);
   173	    if (e.code === 'Escape' && S.mode === 'medal') advance(1);
   174	  });
   175	  window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) { keys[e.code] = false; });
   176	  window.addEventListener('blur', function () { keys = {}; ptrs = {}; apply(); });
   177	
   178	  function readInput() {
   179	    var kg = keys['ArrowRight'] || keys['KeyD'];
   180	    var kb = keys['ArrowLeft'] || keys['KeyA'];
   181	    var kbo = keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight'] || keys['Space'];
   182	    var kl = (keys['ArrowUp'] || keys['KeyW'] ? 1 : 0) + (keys['ArrowDown'] || keys['KeyS'] ? -1 : 0);
   183	    inp.gas = !!(inp.tGas || kg);
   184	    inp.brake = !!(inp.tBrake || kb);
   185	    inp.boost = !!(inp.tBoost || (kbo && inp.gas));
   186	    inp.lean = kl !== 0 ? kl : (inp.tLean || 0);
   187	  }
   188	
   189	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- state
   190	  var EVENTS = 8;
   191	  var S = {
   192	    mode: 'run', evt: 0, ti: 0, times: [null, null, null], bonus: [0, 0, 0],
   193	    t: 0, tracks: null, timer: 0, shake: 0, flash: 0, flashCol: '#fff',
   194	    crashT: 0, msg: '', msgT: 0, hint: 6.5, camX: 0, camY: 0, cp: 0, best: {}
   195	  };
   196	  var bike = new DR.Bike();
   197	  var parts = [];
   198	  var out = {};
   199	
   200	  function loadBest() {
   201	    try { S.best = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dirtrocket.best.v1') || '{}') || {}; }
   202	    catch (e) { S.best = {}; }
   203	  }
   204	  function saveBest() {
   205	    try { localStorage.setItem('dirtrocket.best.v1', JSON.stringify(S.best)); } catch (e) { }
   206	  }
   207	  loadBest();
   208	
   209	  function buildEvent(idx) {
   210	    S.evt = ((idx % EVENTS) + EVENTS) % EVENTS;
   211	    S.tracks = [];
   212	    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) S.tracks.push(DR.makeTrack((S.evt + 1) * 7919 + i * 1313 + 17));
   213	    S.times = [null, null, null]; S.bonus = [0, 0, 0];
   214	    S.ti = 0;
   215	    startTrack();
   216	  }
   217	  function startTrack() {
   218	    var tr = S.tracks[S.ti];
   219	    bike.reset(120, tr.heightAt(120) - (DR.BK.AY + DR.BK.REST + DR.BK.WR), tr.slopeAt(120));
   220	    bike.vx = 0;
   221	    S.timer = 0; S.mode = 'run'; S.cp = 120; S.crashT = 0; S.stuck = 0; S.lastCrashX = -1e9;
   222	    S.camX = bike.x; S.camY = bike.y;
   223	    parts.length = 0;
   224	    S.msg = ''; S.msgT = 0;
   225	  }
   226	  function restartTrack() {
   227	    if (S.mode === 'medal') return;
   228	    startTrack();
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  function medalFor(total, par) {
   232	    if (total <= par * 1.00) return 3;
   233	    if (total <= par * 1.15) return 2;
   234	    if (total <= par * 1.35) return 1;
   235	    return 0;
   236	  }
   237	  var MEDNAME = ['NO MEDAL', 'BRONZE', 'SILVER', 'GOLD'];
   238	  var MEDCOL = ['#7d8798', '#c1743a', '#c9d2de', '#ffc63d'];
   239	
   240	  function eventPar() { var p = 0; for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) p += S.tracks[i].par; return p; }
   241	  function eventTotal() {
   242	    var t = 0; for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) t += (S.times[i] || 0) - S.bonus[i];
   243	    return Math.max(0.1, t);
   244	  }
   245	
   246	  function finishTrack() {
   247	    S.times[S.ti] = S.timer;
   248	    S.bonus[S.ti] = Math.min(2.5, (bike.wheelie / 20) * 0.012);
   249	    S.mode = 'result';
   250	    blip(500, 900, 0.25, 0.2);
   251	  }
   252	
   253	  function advance(alt) {
   254	    if (S.mode === 'result') {
   255	      if (S.ti < 2) { S.ti++; startTrack(); }
   256	      else {
   257	        S.mode = 'medal';
   258	        var tot = eventTotal();
   259	        var key = 'e' + S.evt;
   260	        if (!S.best[key] || tot < S.best[key]) { S.best[key] = tot; S.newBest = true; saveBest(); }
--- dirt game state/update ---
   260	        if (!S.best[key] || tot < S.best[key]) { S.best[key] = tot; S.newBest = true; saveBest(); }
   261	        else S.newBest = false;
   262	        blip(320, 780, 0.4, 0.22);
   263	      }
   264	    } else if (S.mode === 'medal') {
   265	      if (alt) buildEvent(S.evt); else buildEvent(S.evt + 1);
   266	    }
   267	  }
   268	
   269	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- particles
   270	  function spawn(x, y, vx, vy, life, size, col, grav) {
   271	    if (parts.length > 210) return;
   272	    parts.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: vx, vy: vy, l: life, m: life, s: size, c: col, g: grav === undefined ? 900 : grav });
   273	  }
   274	  function stepParts(dt) {
   275	    for (var i = parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   276	      var p = parts[i];
   277	      p.l -= dt; if (p.l <= 0) { parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   278	      p.vy += p.g * dt; p.vx *= 0.99;
   279	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   280	    }
   281	  }
   282	
   283	  // nearest gentle patch at or behind x, so a remount never drops you onto a cliff
   284	  function safeSpot(tr, x) {
   285	    for (var d = 0; d < 1200; d += 15) {
   286	      var q = x - d;
   287	      if (q < 80) break;
   288	      if (Math.abs(tr.slopeAt(q)) < 0.22 && Math.abs(tr.slopeAt(q - 34)) < 0.3 &&
   289	        Math.abs(tr.slopeAt(q + 34)) < 0.3) return q;
   290	    }
   291	    return Math.max(80, x - 60);
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  function wheelWorld(i) {
   295	    var ca = Math.cos(bike.a), sa = Math.sin(bike.a);
   296	    var ox = (i === 0 ? -DR.BK.AX : DR.BK.AX);
   297	    var oy = DR.BK.AY + DR.BK.REST - Math.min(bike.pen[i], 16);
   298	    return { x: bike.x + ox * ca + (-sa) * oy, y: bike.y + ox * sa + ca * oy };
   299	  }
   300	
   301	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- update
   302	  function update(dt) {
   303	    readInput();
   304	    var tr = S.tracks[S.ti];
   305	
   306	    if (S.mode === 'run') {
   307	      S.timer += dt;
   308	      var preX = bike.x;
   309	      bike.step(dt, tr, inp, out);
   310	
   311	      // effects from physics events
   312	      if (out.landed === 'clean') { S.flash = 0.3; S.flashCol = '#7dff9b'; S.msg = 'CLEAN LANDING +'; S.msgT = 1.1; S.shake = Math.min(9, out.impact * 0.005); thud(0.22, 0.16, 260); }
   313	      else if (out.landed === 'wobble') { S.flash = 0.3; S.flashCol = '#ffb03a'; S.msg = 'WOBBLE'; S.msgT = 0.9; S.shake = 11; thud(0.4, 0.3, 150); }
   314	      else if (out.landed === 'ok') { S.shake = Math.min(6, out.impact * 0.004); thud(0.16, 0.14, 200); }
   315	
   316	      // dust
   317	      if (out.dust > 0 && bike.grounded[0]) {
   318	        var w0 = wheelWorld(0);
   319	        var mud = tr.isMud(w0.x);
   320	        for (var d = 0; d < (bike.boosting ? 3 : 1); d++)
   321	          spawn(w0.x - 6, w0.y + 8, -bike.vx * 0.28 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 90, -Math.random() * 130,
   322	            0.34 + Math.random() * 0.3, 2 + Math.random() * 3, mud ? '#5b4530' : '#9c8362', 620);
   323	      }
   324	      if (bike.boosting) {
   325	        var wb = wheelWorld(0);
   326	        spawn(wb.x - 10, wb.y - 6, -bike.vx * 0.1 - 120 - Math.random() * 90, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60,
   327	          0.18 + Math.random() * 0.12, 3 + Math.random() * 4, Math.random() < 0.5 ? '#ff8a2a' : '#ffd45e', -30);
   328	      }
   329	
   330	      // checkpoint: only on gentle, two-wheel-planted ground
   331	      if (bike.grounded[0] && bike.grounded[1] && Math.abs(bike.a) < 0.4 &&
   332	        Math.abs(tr.slopeAt(bike.x)) < 0.3 && bike.x > S.cp + 30) S.cp = bike.x - 20;
   333	
   334	      if (bike.crashed) {
   335	        S.stuck = (Math.abs(bike.x - (S.lastCrashX || -1e9)) < 340) ? Math.min(3, (S.stuck || 0) + 1) : 0;
   336	        S.lastCrashX = bike.x;
   337	        S.mode = 'crash'; S.crashT = 2.0; S.shake = 18; S.flash = 0.45; S.flashCol = '#ff4d4d';
   338	        thud(0.6, 0.55, 120); blip(260, 70, 0.35, 0.18);
   339	        for (var c = 0; c < 26; c++)
   340	          spawn(bike.x, bike.y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 460, -Math.random() * 380 - 40,
   341	            0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5, 2 + Math.random() * 4, Math.random() < 0.5 ? '#d8dee9' : '#8a6d4c', 900);
   342	      } else if (bike.x >= tr.finishX) {
   343	        finishTrack();
   344	      }
   345	      engine(bike.rpm, inp.gas, bike.boosting);
   346	
   347	    } else if (S.mode === 'crash') {
   348	      S.crashT -= dt; S.timer += dt;
   349	      engine(0.05, false, false);
   350	      if (S.crashT <= 0) {
   351	        var cx = Math.max(60, S.cp);
   352	        bike.reset(cx, tr.heightAt(cx) - (DR.BK.AY + DR.BK.REST + DR.BK.WR) - 2, tr.slopeAt(cx));
   353	        var wk = bike.wheelie; bike.wheelie = wk; // keep wheelie credit
   354	        S.mode = 'run';
   355	      }
   356	    } else {
   357	      engine(0.05, false, false);
   358	    }
   359	
   360	    stepParts(dt);
   361	    S.shake *= Math.pow(0.0025, dt);
   362	    S.flash = Math.max(0, S.flash - dt * 1.6);
   363	    S.msgT = Math.max(0, S.msgT - dt);
   364	    S.hint = Math.max(0, S.hint - dt);
   365	
   366	    // camera
   367	    var tx = bike.x + Math.min(180, Math.abs(bike.vx) * 0.22) * (bike.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
   368	    var ty = bike.y - 30;
   369	    var k = 1 - Math.pow(0.0012, dt);
   370	    S.camX += (tx - S.camX) * k;
   371	    S.camY += (ty - S.camY) * Math.min(1, k * 0.75);
   372	
   373	    // UI taps for panels
   374	    if (uiTapped) {
   375	      var p = uiTapped; uiTapped = null;
   376	      if (S.mode === 'result') advance(0);
   377	      else if (S.mode === 'medal') {
   378	        if (inRect(p, UI.btnA)) advance(1);
   379	        else if (inRect(p, UI.btnB)) advance(0);
   380	      }
   381	    }
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- render
   385	  var ZOOM = 1, OX = 0, OY = 0;
   386	  function setCam() {
   387	    ZOOM = Math.max(0.55, Math.min(1.2, H / 430));
   388	    OX = W * 0.34; OY = H * 0.58;
   389	  }
   390	  function sx(wx) { return (wx - S.camX) * ZOOM + OX; }
   391	  function sy(wy) { return (wy - S.camY) * ZOOM + OY; }
   392	
   393	  function roundRect(c, x, y, w, h, r) {
   394	    c.beginPath();
   395	    c.moveTo(x + r, y); c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   396	    c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); c.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); c.closePath();
   397	  }
   398	
   399	  var skyGrad = null, skyKey = '';
   400	  function render() {
   401	    setCam();
   402	    var tr = S.tracks[S.ti];
   403	    ctx.save();
   404	    var sh = S.shake;
   405	    if (sh > 0.3) ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * sh, (Math.random() - 0.5) * sh);
   406	
   407	    // sky
   408	    var key = W + 'x' + H + '|' + S.evt;
   409	    if (skyKey !== key) {
   410	      skyGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   411	      var tint = [['#1a2740', '#3c4f6b', '#7d7a68'], ['#241d38', '#4a3550', '#8a6a5c'],
   412	      ['#12303a', '#2f5c62', '#8fa07a'], ['#2a1c22', '#5c3a35', '#a3765a']][S.evt % 4];
   413	      skyGrad.addColorStop(0, tint[0]); skyGrad.addColorStop(0.55, tint[1]); skyGrad.addColorStop(1, tint[2]);
   414	      skyKey = key;
   415	    }
   416	    ctx.fillStyle = skyGrad; ctx.fillRect(-40, -40, W + 80, H + 80);
   417	
   418	    // sun
   419	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe9b0';
   420	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * 0.78, H * 0.22, 62, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   421	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   422	
   423	    drawHills(0.14, H * 0.62, 70, 'rgba(30,42,60,0.72)', 0.0016);
   424	    drawHills(0.3, H * 0.72, 46, 'rgba(44,56,72,0.8)', 0.0031);
   425	
   426	    drawTerrain(tr);
   427	    drawMarks(tr);
   428	    drawFinish(tr);
   429	    drawParts();
   430	    if (!bike.crashed || S.crashT > 1.2) drawBike();
   431	    else drawWreck();
   432	
   433	    ctx.restore();
   434	
   435	    if (S.flash > 0) {
   436	      ctx.globalAlpha = S.flash * 0.35; ctx.fillStyle = S.flashCol;
   437	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   438	    }
   439	    drawHUD(tr);
   440	    if (S.mode === 'run' || S.mode === 'crash') drawControls();
   441	    if (S.mode === 'result') drawResult();
   442	    if (S.mode === 'medal') drawMedal();
   443	  }
   444	
   445	  function drawHills(par, base, amp, col, freq) {
   446	    ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath();
   447	    var ox = S.camX * par;
   448	    ctx.moveTo(-20, H + 20);
   449	    for (var x = -20; x <= W + 20; x += 16) {
   450	      var wx = (x + ox);
   451	      var y = base - amp * (0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(wx * freq) * Math.cos(wx * freq * 0.43 + 1.7));
   452	      ctx.lineTo(x, y);
   453	    }
   454	    ctx.lineTo(W + 20, H + 20); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   455	  }
   456	
   457	  function drawTerrain(tr) {
   458	    var DXs = tr.DX;
   459	    var wl = S.camX - OX / ZOOM - 40, wr = S.camX + (W - OX) / ZOOM + 40;
   460	    var i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(wl / DXs)), i1 = Math.min(tr.h.length - 1, Math.ceil(wr / DXs));
   461	    ctx.beginPath();
   462	    ctx.moveTo(sx(i0 * DXs), H + 60);
   463	    for (var i = i0; i <= i1; i++) ctx.lineTo(sx(i * DXs), sy(tr.h[i]));
   464	    ctx.lineTo(sx(i1 * DXs), H + 60);
   465	    ctx.closePath();
   466	    var gg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, sy(tr.h[i0]) - 60, 0, H);
   467	    gg.addColorStop(0, '#7a5f3f'); gg.addColorStop(0.18, '#5d4830'); gg.addColorStop(1, '#2c2117');
   468	    ctx.fillStyle = gg; ctx.fill();
   469	
   470	    // surface line
   471	    ctx.beginPath();
   472	    for (var j = i0; j <= i1; j++) { var X = sx(j * DXs), Y = sy(tr.h[j]); if (j === i0) ctx.moveTo(X, Y); else ctx.lineTo(X, Y); }
   473	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#a3855c'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, 3 * ZOOM); ctx.stroke();
   474	
   475	    // mud overlays
   476	    for (var m = 0; m < tr.mud.length; m++) {
   477	      var a = tr.mud[m][0], b = tr.mud[m][1];
   478	      if (b < wl || a > wr) continue;
   479	      ctx.beginPath();
   480	      var ia = Math.max(0, Math.floor(a / DXs)), ib = Math.min(tr.h.length - 1, Math.ceil(b / DXs));
   481	      ctx.moveTo(sx(ia * DXs), sy(tr.h[ia]) + 26 * ZOOM);
   482	      for (var q = ia; q <= ib; q++) ctx.lineTo(sx(q * DXs), sy(tr.h[q]));
   483	      ctx.lineTo(sx(ib * DXs), sy(tr.h[ib]) + 26 * ZOOM);
   484	      ctx.closePath();
   485	      ctx.fillStyle = '#3a2c1c'; ctx.fill();
   486	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#241a10'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   487	    }
   488	  }
   489	
   490	  function drawMarks(tr) {
   491	    var wl = S.camX - 500, wr = S.camX + 900;
   492	    ctx.font = '700 10px system-ui,sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   493	    for (var i = 0; i < tr.marks.length; i++) {
   494	      var mk = tr.marks[i];
   495	      if (mk.x < wl || mk.x > wr) continue;
   496	      var X = sx(mk.x), Y = sy(tr.heightAt(mk.x));
   497	      var lab = mk.kind === 'jump' ? 'GAP' : mk.kind === 'table' ? 'TABLE' : mk.kind === 'mud' ? 'MUD' : 'WHOOPS';
   498	      var col = mk.kind === 'jump' ? '#ff6b4a' : mk.kind === 'mud' ? '#8b6b3f' : '#5fc8e8';
   499	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a3140'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   500	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(X, Y); ctx.lineTo(X, Y - 46 * ZOOM); ctx.stroke();
   501	      ctx.fillStyle = col;
   502	      roundRect(ctx, X - 22, Y - 62 * ZOOM, 44, 15, 3); ctx.fill();
   503	      ctx.fillStyle = '#141821'; ctx.fillText(lab, X, Y - 62 * ZOOM + 11);
   504	    }
   505	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  function drawFinish(tr) {
   509	    var X = sx(tr.finishX);
   510	    if (X < -120 || X > W + 120) return;
   511	    var Y = sy(tr.heightAt(tr.finishX));
   512	    var hgt = 120 * ZOOM;
   513	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6ecf5';
   514	    ctx.fillRect(X - 4, Y - hgt, 6, hgt);
   515	    ctx.fillRect(X + 62, Y - hgt, 6, hgt);
   516	    for (var r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (var c = 0; c < 6; c++) {
   517	      ctx.fillStyle = ((r + c) % 2) ? '#161b24' : '#f2f6fb';
   518	      ctx.fillRect(X - 4 + c * 12, Y - hgt + r * 10, 12, 10);
   519	    }
   520	  }
--- dirt game render/end ---
   520	  }
   521	
   522	  function drawParts() {
   523	    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
   524	      var p = parts[i];
   525	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p.l / p.m));
   526	      ctx.fillStyle = p.c;
   527	      var s = p.s * ZOOM;
   528	      ctx.fillRect(sx(p.x) - s * 0.5, sy(p.y) - s * 0.5, s, s);
   529	    }
   530	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   531	  }
   532	
   533	  function drawBike() {
   534	    var B = DR.BK;
   535	    ctx.save();
   536	    ctx.translate(sx(bike.x), sy(bike.y));
   537	    ctx.scale(ZOOM, ZOOM);
   538	    ctx.rotate(bike.a);
   539	
   540	    // wheels + swingarm/fork
   541	    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   542	      var ox = (i === 0 ? -B.AX : B.AX);
   543	      var oy = B.AY + B.REST - Math.min(bike.pen[i], 16);
   544	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#3b4454'; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   545	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ox * 0.45, -2); ctx.lineTo(ox, oy); ctx.stroke();
   546	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ox, oy, B.WR, 0, 6.2832);
   547	      ctx.fillStyle = '#14181f'; ctx.fill();
   548	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#2f3846'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
   549	      // spokes spin
   550	      var sp = bike.x * 0.06 + (i * 1.1);
   551	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#66738a'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   552	      ctx.beginPath();
   553	      for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
   554	        var an = sp + k * 1.047;
   555	        ctx.moveTo(ox + Math.cos(an) * 3, oy + Math.sin(an) * 3);
   556	        ctx.lineTo(ox + Math.cos(an) * (B.WR - 3), oy + Math.sin(an) * (B.WR - 3));
   557	      }
   558	      ctx.stroke();
   559	    }
   560	
   561	    // chassis
   562	    ctx.fillStyle = bike.boosting ? '#ff9a3c' : '#e04a2f';
   563	    ctx.beginPath();
   564	    ctx.moveTo(-24, 2); ctx.lineTo(-12, -10); ctx.lineTo(14, -12); ctx.lineTo(26, -2);
   565	    ctx.lineTo(18, 6); ctx.lineTo(-14, 8); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   566	    ctx.fillStyle = '#2a3140';
   567	    ctx.fillRect(-22, -14, 16, 6);           // seat
   568	    ctx.fillRect(16, -18, 5, 8);             // bar riser
   569	
   570	    // rider
   571	    var ln = inp.lean;
   572	    var hx = 2 - ln * 7, hy = -34 + Math.abs(ln) * 2;
   573	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#2f6fb5'; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   574	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-14, -12); ctx.lineTo(-4, -22); ctx.lineTo(hx, hy + 8); ctx.stroke();  // legs/torso
   575	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(hx, hy + 8); ctx.lineTo(18, -14); ctx.stroke();                        // arms
   576	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f0f4fa';
   577	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx, hy, 7, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   578	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e04a2f';
   579	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx + 3, hy + 1, 4, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   580	
   581	    ctx.restore();
   582	
   583	    // wheelie glow
   584	    if (bike.grounded[0] && !bike.grounded[1] && bike.a < -0.22) {
   585	      var w0 = wheelWorld(0);
   586	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd45e'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   587	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx(w0.x), sy(w0.y), 20 * ZOOM, 0, 6.2832); ctx.stroke();
   588	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   589	    }
   590	  }
   591	
   592	  function drawWreck() {
   593	    ctx.save();
   594	    ctx.translate(sx(bike.x), sy(bike.y)); ctx.scale(ZOOM, ZOOM); ctx.rotate(bike.a + 0.6);
   595	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8a3a28';
   596	    ctx.fillRect(-22, -6, 44, 12);
   597	    ctx.fillStyle = '#14181f';
   598	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-26, 8, 12, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   599	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(24, 6, 12, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   600	    ctx.restore();
   601	  }

   603	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- HUD
   604	  function fmt(t) {
   605	    if (t == null) return '--.--';
   606	    return (t < 10 ? '0' : '') + t.toFixed(2);
   607	  }
   608	  function panel(x, y, w, h, a) {
   609	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(14,18,26,' + (a || 0.62) + ')';
   610	    roundRect(ctx, x, y, w, h, 8); ctx.fill();
   611	  }
   612	
   613	  function drawHUD(tr) {
   614	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   615	    panel(10, 10, 168, 54);
   616	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa0b8'; ctx.font = '700 11px system-ui,sans-serif';
   617	    ctx.fillText('EVENT ' + (S.evt + 1) + '  ·  TRACK ' + (S.ti + 1) + '/3', 20, 27);
   618	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f2f6fb'; ctx.font = '700 26px system-ui,sans-serif';
   619	    ctx.fillText(fmt(S.timer), 20, 55);
   620	
   621	    // progress bar
   622	    var pw = Math.min(230, W * 0.3);
   623	    var px = W * 0.5 - pw * 0.5;
   624	    panel(px - 6, 12, pw + 12, 20);
   625	    ctx.fillStyle = '#2b3444'; roundRect(ctx, px, 18, pw, 8, 4); ctx.fill();
   626	    var pr = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, bike.x / tr.finishX));
   627	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffb03a'; roundRect(ctx, px, 18, Math.max(4, pw * pr), 8, 4); ctx.fill();
   628	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6ecf5';
   629	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px + pw * pr, 22, 4.5, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   630	
   631	    // best
   632	    var bk = S.best['e' + S.evt];
   633	    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
   634	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa0b8'; ctx.font = '700 11px system-ui,sans-serif';
   635	    ctx.fillText(bk ? 'BEST EVENT ' + fmt(bk) : 'NO BEST YET', W - 14, 26);
   636	    ctx.fillStyle = '#cfd9e6'; ctx.font = '700 13px system-ui,sans-serif';
   637	    ctx.fillText(Math.round(Math.abs(bike.vx) * 0.11) + ' km/h', W - 14, 44);
   638	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   639	
   640	    // wheelie meter
   641	    var wm = bike.wheelie / 20;
   642	    if (wm > 0.5) {
   643	      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   644	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd45e'; ctx.font = '700 12px system-ui,sans-serif';
   645	      ctx.fillText('WHEELIE ' + wm.toFixed(0) + ' m', 20, 82);
   646	    }
   647	
   648	    // messages
   649	    if (S.msgT > 0 && S.mode === 'run') {
   650	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   651	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, S.msgT * 2);
   652	      ctx.fillStyle = S.msg.indexOf('CLEAN') >= 0 ? '#7dff9b' : '#ffb03a';
   653	      ctx.font = '800 20px system-ui,sans-serif';
   654	      ctx.fillText(S.msg, W * 0.5, H * 0.3);
   655	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   656	    }
   657	
   658	    if (S.mode === 'crash') {
   659	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   660	      panel(W * 0.5 - 110, H * 0.36, 220, 62, 0.78);
   661	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff6b5a'; ctx.font = '800 22px system-ui,sans-serif';
   662	      ctx.fillText('CRASH', W * 0.5, H * 0.36 + 28);
   663	      ctx.fillStyle = '#cfd9e6'; ctx.font = '700 13px system-ui,sans-serif';
   664	      ctx.fillText('remount ' + S.crashT.toFixed(1) + 's', W * 0.5, H * 0.36 + 50);
   665	      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   666	    }
   667	
   668	    if (S.hint > 0 && S.mode === 'run' && S.ti === 0) {
   669	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, S.hint / 1.5);
   670	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   671	      panel(W * 0.5 - 190, H - 44, 380, 30, 0.7);
   672	      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6ecf5'; ctx.font = '700 13px system-ui,sans-serif';
   673	      ctx.fillText('HOLD GAS · DRAG LEFT SIDE UP/DOWN TO LEAN · LAND FLAT', W * 0.5, H - 24);
   674	      ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   675	    }
   676	  }
   677	
   678	  function ring(c, x, y, r, frac, col, bg) {
   679	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.strokeStyle = bg; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.stroke();
   680	    if (frac > 0.001) {
   681	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, -1.5708, -1.5708 + 6.2832 * frac);
   682	      ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.stroke();
   683	    }
   684	  }
   685	
   686	  function drawControls() {
   687	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   688	    // gas
   689	    var b = UI.gas;
   690	    ctx.globalAlpha = inp.gas ? 0.92 : 0.55;
   691	    ctx.fillStyle = inp.gas ? '#3fd07a' : '#20303f';
   692	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   693	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#6ff0a6'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
   694	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = '#eafff2'; ctx.font = '800 15px system-ui,sans-serif';
   695	    ctx.fillText('GAS', b.x, b.y);
   696	
   697	    // boost + heat ring
   698	    b = UI.boost;
   699	    var lockd = bike.lock;
   700	    ctx.globalAlpha = bike.boosting ? 0.95 : 0.55;
   701	    ctx.fillStyle = lockd ? '#54303a' : (bike.boosting ? '#ff8a2a' : '#2c2434');
   702	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   703	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   704	    ring(ctx, b.x, b.y, b.r + 5, bike.heat, lockd ? '#ff4d4d' : (bike.heat > 0.75 ? '#ffb03a' : '#5fc8e8'), 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)');
   705	    ctx.fillStyle = lockd ? '#ffb3b3' : '#ffe9c8'; ctx.font = '800 12px system-ui,sans-serif';
   706	    ctx.fillText(lockd ? 'HOT' : 'BOOST', b.x, b.y);
   707	
   708	    // brake
   709	    b = UI.brake;
   710	    ctx.globalAlpha = inp.brake ? 0.9 : 0.5;
   711	    ctx.fillStyle = inp.brake ? '#e04a2f' : '#20303f';
   712	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   713	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff8a72'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
   714	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffeae5'; ctx.font = '800 14px system-ui,sans-serif';
   715	    ctx.fillText('BRAKE', b.x, b.y);
   716	
   717	    // lean indicator
   718	    var lx = 26, ly = H * 0.42, lh = 72;
   719	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
   720	    ctx.fillStyle = '#1a2331'; roundRect(ctx, lx - 9, ly - lh * 0.5, 18, lh, 9); ctx.fill();
   721	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   722	    ctx.fillStyle = inp.lean > 0 ? '#ffd45e' : (inp.lean < 0 ? '#5fc8e8' : '#5a6a80');
   723	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(lx, ly - inp.lean * lh * 0.42, 7, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
   724	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55; ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa0b8'; ctx.font = '700 9px system-ui,sans-serif';
   725	    ctx.fillText('BACK', lx, ly - lh * 0.5 - 10);
   726	    ctx.fillText('FWD', lx, ly + lh * 0.5 + 10);
   727	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   728	    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   729	  }

   749	  function drawMedal() {
   750	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,11,17,0.82)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   751	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   752	    var tot = eventTotal(), par = eventPar(), m = medalFor(tot, par);
   753	    var top = Math.max(14, H * 0.06);
   754	    panel(W * 0.5 - 200, top, 400, H * 0.62, 0.9);
   755	    ctx.fillStyle = MEDCOL[m]; ctx.font = '800 26px system-ui,sans-serif';
   756	    ctx.fillText(MEDNAME[m], W * 0.5, top + 34);
   757	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa0b8'; ctx.font = '700 11px system-ui,sans-serif';
   758	    ctx.fillText('EVENT ' + (S.evt + 1) + ' COMPLETE', W * 0.5, top + 52);
   759	
   760	    var ry = top + 76;
   761	    ctx.font = '700 13px system-ui,sans-serif';
   762	    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   763	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa0b8'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   764	      ctx.fillText('TRACK ' + (i + 1), W * 0.5 - 150, ry + i * 20);
   765	      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6ecf5'; ctx.textAlign = 'right';
   766	      ctx.fillText(fmt(S.times[i]) + '  (-' + S.bonus[i].toFixed(2) + ')', W * 0.5 + 150, ry + i * 20);
   767	    }
   768	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   769	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f2f6fb'; ctx.font = '800 30px system-ui,sans-serif';
   770	    ctx.fillText('TOTAL ' + fmt(tot), W * 0.5, ry + 84);
   771	    ctx.fillStyle = S.newBest ? '#7dff9b' : '#8fa0b8'; ctx.font = '700 12px system-ui,sans-serif';
   772	    ctx.fillText(S.newBest ? 'NEW BEST!' : 'best ' + fmt(S.best['e' + S.evt]) + '   ·   gold ' + fmt(par), W * 0.5, ry + 104);
   773	
   774	    UI.btnA.y = Math.min(H - 60, ry + 122); UI.btnB.y = UI.btnA.y;
   775	    button(UI.btnA, 'RETRY', '#e04a2f');
   776	    button(UI.btnB, 'NEXT EVENT', '#3fd07a');
   777	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   778	  }
   779	
   780	  function button(b, label, col) {
   781	    ctx.fillStyle = col; roundRect(ctx, b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, 9); ctx.fill();
   782	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0d1016'; ctx.font = '800 15px system-ui,sans-serif';
   783	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   784	    ctx.fillText(label, b.x + b.w * 0.5, b.y + b.h * 0.5);
   785	    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
   786	  }
   787	
   788	  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- loop
   789	  var last = 0;
   790	  function frame(ts) {
   791	    if (!last) last = ts;
   792	    var dt = (ts - last) / 1000; last = ts;
   793	    if (dt > 0.05) dt = 0.05;
   794	    if (dt > 0) update(dt);
   795	    render();
   796	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   797	  }
   798	
   799	  resize();
   800	  buildEvent(0);
   801	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   802	})(window);
--- bike ---
     1	/* Dirt Rocket - two-wheel suspension physics */
     2	(function (g) {
     3	  'use strict';
     4	
     5	  var GRAV = 1700;
     6	  var INERTIA = 1200;
     7	  var AX = 27;          // half wheelbase (body frame)
     8	  var AY = 6;           // axle offset below chassis centre
     9	  var REST = 15;        // suspension rest length
    10	  var WR = 13;          // wheel radius
    11	  var KS = 130, CS = 13;      // suspension spring / damper
    12	  var MU = 1.9;               // traction coefficient
    13	  var DRIVE = 1450, BOOSTF = 1150, BRAKEF = 1500;
    14	  var AIRDRAG = 0.0010;
    15	  var MAXPEN = 34;
    16	
    17	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
    18	  function wrapAng(a) { while (a > Math.PI) a -= 6.283185307; while (a < -Math.PI) a += 6.283185307; return a; }
    19	
    20	  function Bike() {
    21	    this.reset(0, 0, 0);
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  Bike.prototype.reset = function (x, y, ang) {
    25	    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
    26	    this.a = ang || 0; this.w = 0;
    27	    this.pen = [0, 0];        // per-wheel compression (visual)
    28	    this.grounded = [false, false];
    29	    this.airTime = 0;
    30	    this.heat = 0; this.lock = false;
    31	    this.crashed = false; this.crashKind = '';
    32	    this.wheelie = 0;         // px of wheelie this run
    33	    this.rpm = 0;
    34	    this.inMud = false;
    35	    this.lastImpact = 0;
    36	    this.flipTotal = 0;
    37	  };
    38	
    39	  // returns event string ('' | 'clean' | 'ok' | 'wobble' | 'crash') for this frame
    40	  Bike.prototype.step = function (dt, tr, inp, out) {
    41	    var subs = clamp(Math.ceil(dt / 0.004), 1, 8);
    42	    var h = dt / subs;
    43	    out.landed = ''; out.impact = 0; out.dust = 0; out.spark = 0;
    44	    for (var s = 0; s < subs; s++) this.sub(h, tr, inp, out);
    45	    return out;
    46	  };
    47	
    48	  Bike.prototype.sub = function (dt, tr, inp, out) {
    49	    var ca = Math.cos(this.a), sa = Math.sin(this.a);
    50	    // body axes (world y is DOWN): right = (ca,sa), down = (-sa, ca)
    51	    var fx = 0, fy = GRAV, tq = 0;
    52	    var wasAir = !this.grounded[0] && !this.grounded[1];
    53	    var gcount = 0;
    54	    this.inMud = false;
    55	
    56	    // ----- boost / heat
    57	    var boosting = false;
    58	    if (this.lock) { if (this.heat < 0.34) this.lock = false; }
    59	    if (inp.boost && !this.lock && inp.gas) {
    60	      boosting = true;
    61	      this.heat += dt * 0.44;
    62	      if (this.heat >= 1) { this.heat = 1; this.lock = true; }
    63	    } else {
    64	      this.heat -= dt * (this.lock ? 0.42 : 0.32);
    65	      if (this.heat < 0) this.heat = 0;
    66	    }
    67	    this.boosting = boosting;
    68	
    69	    // ----- wheels
    70	    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    71	      var ox = (i === 0 ? -AX : AX);
    72	      // axle in world
    73	      var axw = this.x + ox * ca + (-sa) * (AY + REST);
    74	      var ayw = this.y + ox * sa + (ca) * (AY + REST);
    75	      var gy = tr.heightAt(axw);
    76	      var pen = (ayw + WR) - gy;
    77	      this.grounded[i] = false;
    78	      if (pen > 0) {
    79	        pen = Math.min(pen, MAXPEN);
    80	        var sl = tr.slopeAt(axw);
    81	        var nx = Math.sin(sl), ny = -Math.cos(sl);   // ground normal (points up)
    82	        var tx = Math.cos(sl), ty = Math.sin(sl);    // forward tangent
    83	        // contact patch (forces act at the ground, not the axle)
    84	        var rx = axw - this.x, ry = (ayw + WR - pen) - this.y;
    85	        var pvx = this.vx - this.w * ry, pvy = this.vy + this.w * rx;
    86	        var vn = pvx * nx + pvy * ny;
    87	        var vt = pvx * tx + pvy * ty;
    88	
    89	        var N = KS * pen - CS * vn * 1.0;
    90	        if (N < 0) N = 0;
    91	        if (N > 9000) N = 9000;
    92	        this.pen[i] = pen;
    93	        this.grounded[i] = true; gcount++;
    94	        this.gslope = sl;
    95	
    96	        var mud = tr.isMud(axw);
    97	        if (mud) this.inMud = true;
    98	        var grip = MU * (mud ? 0.62 : 1) * N;
    99	
   100	        var ftx = 0;
   101	        // drive (rear only)
   102	        if (i === 0 && inp.gas) {
   103	          ftx += DRIVE * (1 - clamp(Math.abs(vt) / 1400, 0, 0.88));
   104	        }
   105	        // brake
   106	        if (inp.brake) {
   107	          var bf = BRAKEF * (i === 0 ? 0.45 : 0.55);
   108	          ftx -= clamp(vt * 40, -bf, bf);
   109	        }
   110	        // rolling resistance + mud drag
   111	        ftx -= vt * (mud ? 1.35 : 0.16);
   112	        // clamp by traction
   113	        ftx = clamp(ftx, -grip, grip);
   114	
   115	        fx += N * nx + ftx * tx;
   116	        fy += N * ny + ftx * ty;
   117	        tq += rx * (N * ny + ftx * ty) - ry * (N * nx + ftx * tx);
   118	
   119	        // impact bookkeeping
   120	        if (wasAir) {
   121	          var diff = Math.abs(wrapAng(this.a - sl));
   122	          var vimp = Math.abs(vn);
   123	          out.impact = Math.max(out.impact, vimp);
   124	          if (diff > 1.05 || (vimp > 1250 && diff > 0.42)) {
   125	            this.crash('faceplant'); out.landed = 'crash';
   126	          } else if (diff > 0.60) {
   127	            this.vx *= 0.62; this.vy *= 0.5; this.w *= 0.4; out.landed = 'wobble';
   128	          } else if (diff < 0.24 && vimp > 160) {
   129	            var sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
   130	            var bl = Math.min(70, 40 + vimp * 0.05);
   131	            this.vx += tx * bl; this.vy += ty * bl;
   132	            out.landed = 'clean';
   133	          } else if (vimp > 120) {
   134	            out.landed = out.landed || 'ok';
   135	          }
   136	          this.flipTotal = 0;
   137	        }
   138	      } else {
   139	        this.pen[i] *= 0.85;
   140	      }
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    // ----- boost thrust: pushes through the frame, not the tyre (no loop-out torque)
   144	    if (boosting) {
   145	      var bf2 = BOOSTF * (gcount > 0 ? 1 : 0.55);
   146	      fx += ca * bf2; fy += sa * bf2;
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    // ----- rider lean / pitch
   150	    var lean = inp.lean;
   151	    if (gcount > 0) {
   152	      // anti-loop: authority fades as the nose climbs past ~35 degrees
   153	      var fade = lean > 0 ? 1 - 0.85 * clamp((-this.a - 0.6) / 0.85, 0, 1) : 1;
   154	      tq -= lean * 12000 * fade;
   155	      // upright-ish assist so it is controllable
   156	      this.w *= (1 - 3.2 * dt);
   157	    } else {
   158	      tq -= lean * 7000;
   159	      this.w *= (1 - 2.4 * dt);
   160	      this.airTime += dt;
   161	      this.flipTotal += Math.abs(this.w) * dt;
   162	    }
   163	    if (gcount > 0) this.airTime = 0;
   164	
   165	    // ----- air drag
   166	    var sp2 = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
   167	    fx -= this.vx * sp2 * AIRDRAG;
   168	    fy -= this.vy * sp2 * AIRDRAG;
   169	
   170	    // ----- integrate
   171	    this.vx += fx * dt; this.vy += fy * dt;
   172	    this.w += (tq / INERTIA) * dt;
   173	    this.w = clamp(this.w, -7, 7);
   174	    this.x += this.vx * dt; this.y += this.vy * dt;
   175	    this.a = wrapAng(this.a + this.w * dt);
   176	
   177	    // ----- ground pitch limits: a wheel on the dirt cannot loop or endo past the stops
   178	    if (gcount > 0) {
   179	      var gs = this.gslope || 0;
   180	      var lo = gs - 1.02, hi = gs + 0.95;
   181	      if (this.a < lo) { this.a = lo; if (this.w < 0) this.w *= -0.15; }
   182	      else if (this.a > hi) { this.a = hi; if (this.w > 0) this.w *= -0.15; }
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    // ----- wheelie credit (rear down, front up, nose raised)
   186	    if (this.grounded[0] && !this.grounded[1] && this.a < -0.22 && this.vx > 60) {
   187	      this.wheelie += this.vx * dt;
   188	      out.dust += 1;
   189	    }
   190	    if (this.grounded[0] && inp.gas) out.dust += 1;
   191	
   192	    this.rpm = clamp(Math.abs(this.vx) / 900, 0, 1);
   193	
   194	    // ----- chassis / rider hitting dirt = crash
   195	    if (!this.crashed) {
   196	      var pts = [[-17, -7], [17, -7], [0, -24], [0, -6]];
   197	      for (var k = 0; k < pts.length; k++) {
   198	        var px = this.x + pts[k][0] * ca + (-sa) * pts[k][1];
   199	        var py = this.y + pts[k][0] * sa + (ca) * pts[k][1];
   200	        if (py > tr.heightAt(px) - 1) { this.crash('slam'); out.landed = 'crash'; break; }
   201	      }
   202	      if (Math.abs(this.a) > 2.05) { this.crash('flip'); out.landed = 'crash'; }
   203	    }
   204	  };
   205	
   206	  Bike.prototype.crash = function (kind) {
   207	    if (this.crashed) return;
   208	    this.crashed = true; this.crashKind = kind;
   209	  };
   210	
   211	  g.DR = g.DR || {};
   212	  g.DR.Bike = Bike;
   213	  g.DR.BK = { AX: AX, AY: AY, REST: REST, WR: WR };
   214	})(window);
--- track ---
     1	/* Dirt Rocket - seeded track generation */
     2	(function (g) {
     3	  'use strict';
     4	
     5	  function mulberry32(a) {
     6	    a = a >>> 0;
     7	    return function () {
     8	      a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
     9	      var t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    10	      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    11	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    12	    };
    13	  }
    14	
    15	  var DX = 10; // sample spacing in world px
    16	
    17	  function smooth(t) { return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); }
    18	
    19	  function makeTrack(seed) {
    20	    var rnd = mulberry32(seed);
    21	    var R = function (a, b) { return a + rnd() * (b - a); };
    22	    var h = [0];
    23	    var cur = 0;
    24	    var mud = [];      // [x0,x1]
    25	    var marks = [];    // {x, kind}
    26	    var jumps = 0;
    27	
    28	    function seg(len, fn) {
    29	      var n = Math.max(1, Math.round(len / DX));
    30	      var base = cur;
    31	      for (var i = 1; i <= n; i++) h.push(base + fn(i / n));
    32	      cur = base + fn(1);
    33	      return n * DX;
    34	    }
    35	    function x() { return (h.length - 1) * DX; }
    36	
    37	    // ---- start straight
    38	    seg(560, function () { return 0; });
    39	
    40	    var target = 8200 + Math.floor(R(0, 1800));
    41	    var kinds = ['rollers', 'table', 'kicker', 'whoops', 'mud', 'rollers', 'kicker', 'table'];
    42	    var last = -1;
    43	    var guard = 0;
    44	
    45	    while (x() < target && guard++ < 60) {
    46	      var k = kinds[Math.floor(rnd() * kinds.length)];
    47	      if (k === last && rnd() < 0.8) k = 'rollers';
    48	      last = k;
    49	
    50	      // keep the ribbon inside a sane band
    51	      var drift = R(-70, 70);
    52	      if (cur < -260) drift = R(20, 90);
    53	      if (cur > 260) drift = R(-90, -20);
    54	
    55	      if (k === 'rollers') {
    56	        var cyc = Math.round(R(1, 3)), amp = R(18, 46), ph = rnd() * 6.283;
    57	        seg(R(520, 900), function (t) {
    58	          return drift * smooth(t) + amp * (Math.sin(ph + t * 6.283 * cyc) - Math.sin(ph));
    59	        });
    60	
    61	      } else if (k === 'whoops') {
    62	        var n2 = Math.round(R(5, 8)), a2 = R(12, 21);
    63	        marks.push({ x: x() + 40, kind: 'whoops' });
    64	        seg(n2 * 108, function (t) {
    65	          return drift * smooth(t) - a2 * (1 - Math.cos(t * 6.283 * n2)) * 0.5 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t));
    66	        });
    67	
    68	      } else if (k === 'mud') {
    69	        var mx0 = x();
    70	        marks.push({ x: mx0 + 20, kind: 'mud' });
    71	        var ln = R(280, 460);
    72	        seg(ln, function (t) { return drift * 0.4 * smooth(t) + 6 * Math.sin(t * 20); });
    73	        mud.push([mx0, x()]);
    74	        seg(R(180, 300), function (t) { return drift * 0.6 * smooth(t); });
    75	
    76	      } else if (k === 'table') {
    77	        var th = R(62, 108);
    78	        marks.push({ x: x() + 30, kind: 'table' });
    79	        seg(R(185, 235), function (t) { return -th * smooth(t); });          // ramp up
    80	        seg(R(150, 260), function (t) { return 0; });                         // deck
    81	        seg(R(175, 225), function (t) { return (th + drift * 0.3) * smooth(t); }); // ramp down
    82	        seg(R(220, 340), function (t) { return drift * 0.4 * smooth(t); });
    83	
    84	      } else { // kicker with a real gap + landing ramp
    85	        jumps++;
    86	        var jh = R(78, 132);
    87	        var gap = R(190, 340);
    88	        marks.push({ x: x() + 30, kind: 'jump' });
    89	        seg(R(215, 275), function (t) { return -jh * t * t; });                      // launch ramp
    90	        seg(60, function (t) { return (jh + 34) * smooth(t); });                      // face of the gap
    91	        seg(gap, function (t) { return 0; });                                         // pit floor
    92	        seg(R(200, 260), function (t) { return -(jh * 0.72 + 34) * smooth(t); });     // landing ramp up
    93	        seg(R(230, 340), function (t) { return (jh * 0.72 + drift * 0.3) * smooth(t); }); // landing downslope
    94	        seg(R(180, 280), function (t) { return drift * 0.3 * smooth(t); });
    95	      }
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    // ---- finish straight
    99	    var finishX = x() + 260;
   100	    seg(560, function (t) { return -cur * 0.35 * smooth(t); });
   101	
   102	    var len = (h.length - 1) * DX;
   103	
   104	    function heightAt(wx) {
   105	      var f = wx / DX;
   106	      if (f <= 0) return h[0];
   107	      if (f >= h.length - 1) return h[h.length - 1];
   108	      var i = f | 0, u = f - i;
   109	      return h[i] + (h[i + 1] - h[i]) * u;
   110	    }
   111	    function slopeAt(wx) {
   112	      var a = heightAt(wx - 9), b = heightAt(wx + 9);
   113	      return Math.atan2(b - a, 18);
   114	    }
   115	    function isMud(wx) {
   116	      for (var i = 0; i < mud.length; i++) if (wx >= mud[i][0] && wx <= mud[i][1]) return true;
   117	      return false;
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    return {
   121	      seed: seed, DX: DX, h: h, len: len, finishX: finishX,
   122	      mud: mud, marks: marks, jumps: jumps,
   123	      par: len / 520 + jumps * 0.3,
   124	      heightAt: heightAt, slopeAt: slopeAt, isMud: isMud
   125	    };
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  g.DR = g.DR || {};
   129	  g.DR.makeTrack = makeTrack;
   130	  g.DR.rng = mulberry32;
   131	})(window);

   522	  function drawParts() {
   523	    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {

   529	    }

   531	  }

   533	  function drawBike() {
   534	    var B = DR.BK;
   535	    ctx.save();
   536	    ctx.translate(sx(bike.x), sy(bike.y));
   537	    ctx.scale(ZOOM, ZOOM);
   538	    ctx.rotate(bike.a);
   539	
   540	    // wheels + swingarm/fork
   540	    // wheels + swingarm/fork
   541	    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   542	      var ox = (i === 0 ? -B.AX : B.AX);
   543	      var oy = B.AY + B.REST - Math.min(bike.pen[i], 16);
   544	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#3b4454'; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   545	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ox * 0.45, -2); ctx.lineTo(ox, oy); ctx.stroke();
   546	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ox, oy, B.WR, 0, 6.2832);
   547	      ctx.fillStyle = '#14181f'; ctx.fill();
   548	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#2f3846'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
   549	      // spokes spin
   550	      var sp = bike.x * 0.06 + (i * 1.1);
   551	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#66738a'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   552	      ctx.beginPath();
   553	      for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
   554	        var an = sp + k * 1.047;
   555	        ctx.moveTo(ox + Math.cos(an) * 3, oy + Math.sin(an) * 3);
   556	        ctx.lineTo(ox + Math.cos(an) * (B.WR - 3), oy + Math.sin(an) * (B.WR - 3));
   557	      }
   558	      ctx.stroke();
   559	    }
   560	
   561	    // chassis
   562	    ctx.fillStyle = bike.boosting ? '#ff9a3c' : '#e04a2f';
   563	    ctx.beginPath();
   564	    ctx.moveTo(-24, 2); ctx.lineTo(-12, -10); ctx.lineTo(14, -12); ctx.lineTo(26, -2);
   565	    ctx.lineTo(18, 6); ctx.lineTo(-14, 8); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();

   580

   582

   678	  function ring(c, x, y, r, frac, col, bg) {
   679	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.strokeStyle = bg; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.stroke();
   680	    if (frac > 0.001) {
   681	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, -1.5708, -1.5708 + 6.2832 * frac);
   682	      ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.stroke();
   683	    }
   684	  }

     4	var TAU = Math.PI * 2;
     5	function clamp(v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
     6	function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
     7	function dist2(ax, ay, bx, by) { var dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by; return dx * dx + dy * dy; }
     8	
     9	/* deterministic seeded rng (mulberry32) */
    10	function makeRng(seed) {
    11	  var a = seed >>> 0;
    12	  return function () {
    13	    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    14	    var t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    15	    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    16	    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    17	  };
    18	}

    21	var View = {
    22	  cv: null, ctx: null, w: 390, h: 700, scale: 1,
    23	  init: function () {
    24	    this.cv = document.getElementById('c');
    25	    this.ctx = this.cv.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
    26	    this.resize();
    27	    window.addEventListener('resize', this.resize.bind(this));
    28	    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () {
    29	      setTimeout(View.resize.bind(View), 250);
    30	    });
    31	  },
    32	  resize: function () {
    33	    var w = Math.max(240, window.innerWidth | 0);
    34	    var h = Math.max(320, window.innerHeight | 0);
    35	    this.w = w; this.h = h;

    37	    var longAxis = Math.max(w, h);
    38	    if (longAxis * s > 960) s = 960 / longAxis;

    44	  },

    51	/* ---------- input: dual floating sticks + keyboard ---------- */
    52	var Input = {
    53	  move: { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 },
    54	  aim: { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 },
    55	  sticks: {},          /* pointerId -> stick */
    56	  keys: {},
    57	  bombEdge: false,
    58	  anyEdge: false,      /* any press this frame (restart) */
    59	  R: 46,               /* stick travel radius */
    60	  bomb: { x: 0, y: 0, r: 30, hit: 42 },
    61	  init: function () {
    62	    var cv = View.cv, self = this;
    63	    var opt = { passive: false };
    64	    cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { self.down(e); e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    65	    cv.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { self.moveEv(e); e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    66	    cv.addEventListener('pointerup', function (e) { self.up(e); e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    67	    cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', function (e) { self.up(e); e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    68	    document.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    69	    document.addEventListener('gesturestart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, opt);
    70	    window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    71	      if (e.repeat) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
    72	      self.keys[e.code] = 1; self.anyEdge = true; Snd.init();
    73	      if (e.code === 'Space' || e.code === 'KeyB') self.bombEdge = true;
    74	      if (['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Space'].indexOf(e.code) >= 0) e.preventDefault();
    75	    });
    76	    window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) { self.keys[e.code] = 0; });
    77	    window.addEventListener('blur', function () { self.keys = {}; self.sticks = {}; });
    78	  },
    79	  down: function (e) {
    80	    Snd.init();
    81	    this.anyEdge = true;
    82	    var x = e.clientX, y = e.clientY;
    83	    if (dist2(x, y, this.bomb.x, this.bomb.y) < this.bomb.hit * this.bomb.hit) {
    84	      this.bombEdge = true; return;
    85	    }
    86	    this.sticks[e.pointerId] = { side: x < View.w * 0.5 ? 0 : 1, ox: x, oy: y, x: x, y: y };
    87	  },
    88	  moveEv: function (e) {
    89	    var s = this.sticks[e.pointerId];
    90	    if (!s) return;
    91	    s.x = e.clientX; s.y = e.clientY;
    92	    /* let the origin trail if pushed beyond travel radius */
    93	    var dx = s.x - s.ox, dy = s.y - s.oy, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    94	    if (d > this.R) { s.ox = s.x - dx / d * this.R; s.oy = s.y - dy / d * this.R; }
    95	  },
    96	  up: function (e) { delete this.sticks[e.pointerId]; },
    97	  /* build axis vectors for this frame */
    98	  poll: function () {
    99	    var m = this.move, a = this.aim;
   100	    m.x = m.y = m.mag = 0; a.x = a.y = a.mag = 0;
   101	    for (var id in this.sticks) {
   102	      var s = this.sticks[id];
   103	      var dx = s.x - s.ox, dy = s.y - s.oy, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   104	      if (d < 7) continue;
   105	      var mg = Math.min(d, this.R) / this.R;
   106	      var t = s.side === 0 ? m : a;
   107	      t.x = dx / d; t.y = dy / d; t.mag = mg;
   108	    }
   109	    var k = this.keys, kx = 0, ky = 0;
   110	    if (k['KeyA']) kx -= 1; if (k['KeyD']) kx += 1;
   111	    if (k['KeyW']) ky -= 1; if (k['KeyS']) ky += 1;
   112	    if (kx || ky) { var kd = Math.hypot(kx, ky); m.x = kx / kd; m.y = ky / kd; m.mag = 1; }
   113	    var ax = 0, ay = 0;
   114	    if (k['ArrowLeft']) ax -= 1; if (k['ArrowRight']) ax += 1;
   115	    if (k['ArrowUp']) ay -= 1; if (k['ArrowDown']) ay += 1;
   116	    if (ax || ay) { var ad = Math.hypot(ax, ay); a.x = ax / ad; a.y = ay / ad; a.mag = 1; }
   117	  },
   118	  drawSticks: function (c) {
   119	    for (var id in this.sticks) {
   120	      var s = this.sticks[id];
   121	      var dx = s.x - s.ox, dy = s.y - s.oy, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   122	      var kx = s.ox, ky = s.oy;
   123	      if (d > 0) { var m = Math.min(d, this.R); kx = s.ox + dx / d * m; ky = s.oy + dy / d * m; }
   124	      var col = s.side === 0 ? '90,230,255' : '255,120,190';
   125	      c.lineWidth = 2;
   126	      c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(' + col + ',0.28)';
   127	      c.beginPath(); c.arc(s.ox, s.oy, this.R, 0, TAU); c.stroke();
   128	      c.fillStyle = 'rgba(' + col + ',0.20)';
   129	      c.beginPath(); c.arc(kx, ky, 20, 0, TAU); c.fill();
   130	      c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(' + col + ',0.7)';
   131	      c.beginPath(); c.arc(kx, ky, 20, 0, TAU); c.stroke();
   132	    }
   133	  }
   134	};
