   136	/* ---------- audio: WebAudio only, synthesized ---------- */
   137	var Snd = {
   138	  ctx: null, master: null, last: 0, muted: false,
   139	  init: function () {
   140	    if (this.ctx) { if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume(); return; }
   141	    try {
   142	      var AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
   143	      if (!AC) return;
   144	      this.ctx = new AC();
   145	      this.master = this.ctx.createGain();
   146	      this.master.gain.value = 0.28;
   147	      this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
   148	    } catch (e) { this.ctx = null; }
   149	  },
   150	  tone: function (f0, f1, dur, type, vol) {
   151	    if (!this.ctx || this.muted) return;
   152	    var t = this.ctx.currentTime;
   153	    if (t - this.last < 0.012) return;
   154	    this.last = t;
   155	    var o = this.ctx.createOscillator(), g = this.ctx.createGain();
   156	    o.type = type || 'square';
   157	    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
   158	    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, f1), t + dur);
   159	    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
   160	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || 0.3, t + 0.008);
   161	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
   162	    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
   163	    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
   164	  },
   165	  noise: function (dur, vol, f) {
   166	    if (!this.ctx || this.muted) return;
   167	    var t = this.ctx.currentTime, n = Math.floor(this.ctx.sampleRate * dur);
   168	    var buf = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, n, this.ctx.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
   169	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
   170	    var src = this.ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf;
   171	    var bp = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'lowpass'; bp.frequency.value = f || 900;
   172	    var g = this.ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol || 0.3;
   173	    src.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
   174	    src.start(t);
   175	  },
   176	  shoot: function () { this.tone(760, 320, 0.06, 'square', 0.12); },
   177	  hit: function () { this.tone(320, 120, 0.07, 'sawtooth', 0.14); },
   178	  boom: function () { this.noise(0.35, 0.4, 700); this.tone(180, 40, 0.3, 'triangle', 0.22); },
   179	  pop: function () { this.tone(900, 1400, 0.05, 'triangle', 0.1); },
   180	  die: function () { this.noise(0.6, 0.5, 500); this.tone(220, 30, 0.7, 'sawtooth', 0.25); },
   181	  wave: function () { this.tone(420, 880, 0.22, 'triangle', 0.18); },
   182	  bombS: function () { this.noise(0.7, 0.6, 1400); this.tone(90, 25, 0.8, 'sine', 0.3); }
   183	};
   184	
   185	/* ---------- particles ---------- */
   186	var Particles = {
   187	  list: [], max: 420,
   188	  clear: function () { this.list.length = 0; },
   189	  burst: function (x, y, n, col, spd, life, size) {
   190	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   191	      if (this.list.length >= this.max) break;
   192	      var a = Math.random() * TAU, s = spd * (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.85);
   193	      this.list.push({
   194	        x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s,
   195	        l: life * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.6), m: life, c: col, s: size || 2.2
   196	      });
   197	    }
   198	  },
   199	  update: function (dt) {
   200	    var L = this.list;
   201	    for (var i = L.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   202	      var p = L[i];
   203	      p.l -= dt;
   204	      if (p.l <= 0) { L[i] = L[L.length - 1]; L.pop(); continue; }
   205	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   206	      p.vx *= 1 - 1.9 * dt; p.vy *= 1 - 1.9 * dt;
   207	    }
   208	  },
   209	  draw: function (c) {
   210	    var L = this.list;
   211	    for (var i = 0; i < L.length; i++) {
   212	      var p = L[i], a = clamp(p.l / p.m, 0, 1);
   213	      c.globalAlpha = a;
   214	      c.fillStyle = p.c;
   215	      var s = p.s * (0.4 + a * 0.8);
   216	      c.fillRect(p.x - s * 0.5, p.y - s * 0.5, s, s);
   217	    }
   218	    c.globalAlpha = 1;
   219	  }
   220	};
   221	
   222	/* ---------- shockwave ripples that warp the arena grid ---------- */
   223	var Ripples = {
   224	  list: [],
   225	  add: function (x, y, amp) {
   226	    if (this.list.length > 6) this.list.shift();
   227	    this.list.push({ x: x, y: y, t: 0, amp: amp });
   228	  },
   229	  clear: function () { this.list.length = 0; },
   230	  update: function (dt) {
   231	    for (var i = this.list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   232	      var r = this.list[i]; r.t += dt;
   233	      if (r.t > 1.5) this.list.splice(i, 1);
   234	    }
   235	  },
   236	  disp: function (x, y, out) {
   237	    var dx = 0, dy = 0, L = this.list;
   238	    for (var i = 0; i < L.length; i++) {
   239	      var r = L[i];
   240	      var ex = x - r.x, ey = y - r.y;
   241	      var d = Math.sqrt(ex * ex + ey * ey) + 0.001;
   242	      var front = r.t * 300;
   243	      var diff = d - front;
   244	      if (diff > 130 || diff < -130) continue;
   245	      var k = diff / 55;
   246	      var f = r.amp * Math.exp(-k * k) * (1 - r.t / 1.5);
   247	      dx += ex / d * f; dy += ey / d * f;
   248	    }
   249	    out[0] = dx; out[1] = dy;
   250	  }
   251	};
--- vector game 1 ---
     1	'use strict';
     2	/* Vector Storm - gameplay */
     3	
     4	var G = {
     5	  state: 'play',           /* play | dead | over */
     6	  arena: { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 },
     7	  player: null,
     8	  bullets: [], enemies: [], crystals: [], drops: [],
     9	  wave: 0, waveTimer: 0, banner: 0, bannerTxt: '',
    10	  score: 0, best: 0, lives: 3, bombs: 1,
    11	  shards: 0, mult: 1, peak: 1,
    12	  shake: 0, hint: 6, seed: 1, rng: null,
    13	  respawn: 0, invuln: 0, over: 0, flash: 0
    14	};
    15	
    16	var COL = {
    17	  drifter: '#ff5f6d', weaver: '#ffd166', snake: '#7bffab',
    18	  spawner: '#c08bff', mini: '#ff9d5f', well: '#5f8bff',
    19	  player: '#66f0ff', bullet: '#ffffff', crystal: '#8affe0', bomb: '#ff7bd5'
    20	};
    21	
    22	/* ================= setup ================= */
    23	function layout() {
    24	  var W = View.w, H = View.h;
    25	  G.arena.x = 7; G.arena.y = 52;
    26	  G.arena.w = W - 14; G.arena.h = H - 66;
    27	  Input.bomb.x = W * 0.5; Input.bomb.y = H - 46;
    28	}
    29	
    30	function reset(full) {
    31	  layout();
    32	  G.bullets.length = 0; G.enemies.length = 0; G.crystals.length = 0; G.drops.length = 0;
    33	  Particles.clear(); Ripples.clear();
    34	  var a = G.arena;
    35	  G.player = { x: a.x + a.w / 2, y: a.y + a.h * 0.62, vx: 0, vy: 0, r: 10, ang: -Math.PI / 2, cd: 0 };
    36	  if (full) {
    37	    G.seed = (Date.now() ^ (Math.random() * 1e9)) >>> 0;
    38	    G.rng = makeRng(G.seed);
    39	    G.score = 0; G.lives = 3; G.bombs = 1; G.shards = 0; G.mult = 1; G.peak = 1;
    40	    Input.sticks = {}; G.shake = 0; G.flash = 0;
    41	    G.wave = 0; G.waveTimer = 0.9; G.state = 'play'; G.hint = 6; G.over = 0;
    42	  }
    43	  G.invuln = 2.0; G.respawn = 0;
    44	}
    45	
    46	function boot() {
    47	  View.init(); Input.init(); layout();
    48	  try { G.best = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('vectorstorm.best') || '0', 10) || 0; } catch (e) { G.best = 0; }
    49	  reset(true);
    50	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    51	}
    52	
    53	/* ================= spawn grammar ================= */
    54	var FAMILY = [
    55	  { t: 'drifter', cost: 1, from: 1 },
    56	  { t: 'weaver', cost: 2, from: 2 },
    57	  { t: 'snake', cost: 4, from: 4 },
    58	  { t: 'spawner', cost: 5, from: 6 },
    59	  { t: 'well', cost: 6, from: 8 }
    60	];
    61	
    62	function spawnWave() {
    63	  G.wave++;
    64	  var r = G.rng, a = G.arena;
    65	  var budget = 3 + G.wave * 1.7;
    66	  var pool = FAMILY.filter(function (f) { return G.wave >= f.from; });
    67	  var guard = 0;
    68	  while (budget > 0 && guard++ < 40 && G.enemies.length < 52) {
    69	    /* pick a family, biased toward newer (harder) ones as waves climb */
    70	    var w = [], tot = 0;
    71	    for (var i = 0; i < pool.length; i++) {
    72	      var age = G.wave - pool[i].from;
    73	      var wt = 1 + Math.min(age, 6) * 0.35 + i * 0.25;
    74	      if (pool[i].cost > budget + 1) wt = 0.02;
    75	      w.push(wt); tot += wt;
    76	    }
    77	    var pick = r() * tot, f = pool[0];
    78	    for (var j = 0; j < pool.length; j++) { pick -= w[j]; if (pick <= 0) { f = pool[j]; break; } }
    79	
    80	    /* formation rule */
    81	    var forms = ['cluster', 'ring', 'edge', 'corners'];
    82	    var form = forms[(r() * forms.length) | 0];
    83	    var count = 1;
    84	    if (f.cost <= 2) count = 2 + ((r() * 4) | 0);
    85	    else if (f.cost === 4) count = 1 + ((r() * 2) | 0);
    86	    count = Math.max(1, Math.min(count, Math.ceil(budget / f.cost)));
    87	
    88	    var cx = a.x + 40 + r() * (a.w - 80), cy = a.y + 40 + r() * (a.h - 80);
    89	    var rad = 40 + r() * 70, base = r() * TAU;
    90	    for (var k = 0; k < count; k++) {
    91	      var x, y;
    92	      if (form === 'ring') { var an = base + k / count * TAU; x = cx + Math.cos(an) * rad; y = cy + Math.sin(an) * rad; }
    93	      else if (form === 'cluster') { x = cx + (r() - 0.5) * 70; y = cy + (r() - 0.5) * 70; }
    94	      else if (form === 'corners') {
    95	        var q = (k + ((r() * 4) | 0)) % 4;
    96	        x = a.x + 46 + (q & 1 ? a.w - 92 : 0); y = a.y + 46 + (q & 2 ? a.h - 92 : 0);
    97	      } else {
    98	        var side = (r() * 4) | 0;
    99	        var tt = (k + 1) / (count + 1);
   100	        if (side === 0) { x = a.x + a.w * tt; y = a.y + 34; }
   101	        else if (side === 1) { x = a.x + a.w * tt; y = a.y + a.h - 34; }
   102	        else if (side === 2) { x = a.x + 34; y = a.y + a.h * tt; }
   103	        else { x = a.x + a.w - 34; y = a.y + a.h * tt; }
   104	      }
   105	      x = clamp(x, a.x + 26, a.x + a.w - 26);
   106	      y = clamp(y, a.y + 26, a.y + a.h - 26);
   107	      /* never drop one right on top of the player */
   108	      if (dist2(x, y, G.player.x, G.player.y) < 110 * 110) { y = y > G.player.y ? y + 110 : y - 110; y = clamp(y, a.y + 26, a.y + a.h - 26); }
   109	      makeEnemy(f.t, x, y);
   110	      budget -= f.cost;
   111	    }
   112	  }
   113	  G.banner = 1.6; G.bannerTxt = 'WAVE ' + G.wave;
   114	  Snd.wave();
   115	}
   116	
   117	function makeEnemy(t, x, y) {
   118	  var sp = 1 + G.wave * 0.03;
   119	  var e = { t: t, x: x, y: y, vx: 0, vy: 0, born: 0.7, ph: Math.random() * TAU, ang: 0 };
   120	  if (t === 'drifter') { e.r = 12; e.hp = 1; e.spd = 62 * sp; e.pts = 10; }
   121	  else if (t === 'mini') { e.r = 7; e.hp = 1; e.spd = 120 * sp; e.pts = 8; e.born = 0.35; }
   122	  else if (t === 'weaver') { e.r = 12; e.hp = 2; e.spd = 96 * sp; e.pts = 25; }
   123	  else if (t === 'snake') {
   124	    e.r = 10; e.hp = 1; e.spd = 108 * sp; e.pts = 15; e.ang = Math.random() * TAU;
   125	    e.segs = [];
   126	    var n = 5 + ((Math.random() * 4) | 0);
   127	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) e.segs.push({ x: x, y: y });
   128	  }
   129	  else if (t === 'spawner') { e.r = 18; e.hp = 7; e.spd = 30 * sp; e.pts = 120; e.emit = 1.6; e.kids = 0; }
   130	  else if (t === 'well') { e.r = 20; e.hp = 12; e.spd = 22 * sp; e.pts = 200; }
   131	  G.enemies.push(e);
   132	  return e;
   133	}
   134	
   135	/* ================= gameplay ================= */
   136	function fire(ax, ay) {
   137	  var p = G.player;
   138	  var sp = 640, spread = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.07;
   139	  var ca = Math.cos(spread), sa = Math.sin(spread);
   140	  var dx = ax * ca - ay * sa, dy = ax * sa + ay * ca;
   141	  G.bullets.push({ x: p.x + dx * 14, y: p.y + dy * 14, vx: dx * sp, vy: dy * sp, l: 1.4, r: 3.5 });
   142	  p.cd = 0.105;
   143	  p.vx -= dx * 22; p.vy -= dy * 22;
   144	  Snd.shoot();
   145	}
   146	
   147	function killEnemy(e, i, byBomb) {
   148	  var pts = Math.round(e.pts * G.mult);
   149	  G.score += pts;
   150	  Particles.burst(e.x, e.y, e.t === 'mini' ? 6 : 14, COL[e.t] || '#fff', 190, 0.5, 3);
   151	  G.shake = Math.min(18, G.shake + (e.t === 'well' || e.t === 'spawner' ? 12 : 3.5));
   152	  if (e.t === 'well' || e.t === 'spawner') { Ripples.add(e.x, e.y, 22); Snd.boom(); }
   153	  else Snd.hit();
   154	  /* crystals */
   155	  var n = e.t === 'mini' ? 1 : (e.t === 'well' ? 8 : (e.t === 'spawner' ? 6 : 2));
   156	  for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
   157	    var a = Math.random() * TAU, s = 40 + Math.random() * 90;
   158	    G.crystals.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, l: 9 });
   159	  }
   160	  if (e.t !== 'mini' && !byBomb && G.rng() < 0.045 && G.bombs < 3) {
   161	    G.drops.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, l: 12, ph: 0 });
   162	  }
   163	  if (e.owner) e.owner.kids--;
   164	  G.enemies.splice(i, 1);
   165	}
   166	
   167	function playerDie() {
   168	  var p = G.player;
   169	  Particles.burst(p.x, p.y, 46, COL.player, 300, 0.9, 3.5);
   170	  Ripples.add(p.x, p.y, 30);
   171	  G.shake = 24; G.flash = 0.5;
   172	  Snd.die();
   173	  G.lives--; G.mult = 1; G.shards = 0;
   174	  G.crystals.length = 0;
   175	  if (G.lives <= 0) {
   176	    G.state = 'over'; G.over = 0;
   177	    if (G.score > G.best) { G.best = G.score; try { localStorage.setItem('vectorstorm.best', String(G.best)); } catch (e) {} }
   178	  } else {
   179	    G.state = 'dead'; G.respawn = 1.1;
   180	  }
   181	}
   182	
   183	function useBomb() {
   184	  if (G.bombs <= 0 || G.state !== 'play') return;
   185	  G.bombs--;
   186	  var p = G.player;
   187	  Ripples.add(p.x, p.y, 46); G.shake = 26; G.flash = 0.7;
   188	  Snd.bombS();
   189	  for (var i = G.enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) killEnemy(G.enemies[i], i, true);
   190	  Particles.burst(p.x, p.y, 70, COL.bomb, 420, 0.9, 4);
   191	}
   192	
   193	function update(dt) {
   194	  layout();
   195	  Input.poll();
   196	  var a = G.arena, p = G.player, i, e;
   197	
   198	  if (Input.bombEdge) { useBomb(); Input.bombEdge = false; }
   199	  if (G.hint > 0) G.hint -= dt;
   200	  if (G.banner > 0) G.banner -= dt;
   201	  if (G.flash > 0) G.flash -= dt * 2.2;
   202	  G.shake *= 1 - Math.min(1, 6 * dt);
   203	
   204	  if (G.state === 'over') {
   205	    G.over += dt;
   206	    if (G.over > 0.6 && Input.anyEdge) { reset(true); }
   207	    Input.anyEdge = false;
   208	    Particles.update(dt); Ripples.update(dt);
   209	    return;
   210	  }
   211	  Input.anyEdge = false;
   212	
   213	  if (G.state === 'dead') {
   214	    G.respawn -= dt;
   215	    Particles.update(dt); Ripples.update(dt);
   216	    updateCrystals(dt, true);
   217	    if (G.respawn <= 0) {
   218	      p.x = a.x + a.w / 2; p.y = a.y + a.h * 0.62; p.vx = p.vy = 0;
   219	      G.invuln = 2.2; G.state = 'play';
   220	      /* clear space around respawn */
   221	      for (i = G.enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   222	        if (dist2(G.enemies[i].x, G.enemies[i].y, p.x, p.y) < 90 * 90) killEnemy(G.enemies[i], i, true);
   223	      }
   224	    }
   225	    /* enemies keep moving while dead, minus contact */
   226	    updateEnemies(dt, false);
   227	    return;
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  if (G.invuln > 0) G.invuln -= dt;
   231	
   232	  /* --- player --- */
   233	  var m = Input.move;
   234	  var acc = 1900, maxv = 268;
   235	  if (m.mag > 0) { p.vx += m.x * m.mag * acc * dt; p.vy += m.y * m.mag * acc * dt; }
   236	  var damp = 1 - Math.min(1, (m.mag > 0 ? 6 : 11) * dt);
   237	  p.vx *= damp; p.vy *= damp;
   238	  var sp = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
   239	  if (sp > maxv) { p.vx = p.vx / sp * maxv; p.vy = p.vy / sp * maxv; }
   240	  p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   241	  if (p.x < a.x + p.r) { p.x = a.x + p.r; p.vx *= -0.35; }
   242	  if (p.x > a.x + a.w - p.r) { p.x = a.x + a.w - p.r; p.vx *= -0.35; }
   243	  if (p.y < a.y + p.r) { p.y = a.y + p.r; p.vy *= -0.35; }
   244	  if (p.y > a.y + a.h - p.r) { p.y = a.y + a.h - p.r; p.vy *= -0.35; }
   245	
   246	  /* --- shooting --- */
   247	  var aim = Input.aim;
   248	  p.cd -= dt;
   249	  if (aim.mag > 0.28) {
   250	    p.ang = Math.atan2(aim.y, aim.x);
   251	    if (p.cd <= 0) fire(aim.x, aim.y);
   252	  } else if (m.mag > 0.1) {
   253	    p.ang = lerp2ang(p.ang, Math.atan2(m.y, m.x), 12 * dt);
   254	  }
   255	  if (Math.random() < dt * 22) Particles.burst(p.x - Math.cos(p.ang) * 9, p.y - Math.sin(p.ang) * 9, 1, '#2ea9c8', 40, 0.28, 2);
   256	
   257	  /* --- bullets --- */
   258	  for (i = G.bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   259	    var b = G.bullets[i];
   260	    /* wells bend bullets */
--- vector game 2 ---
   260	    /* wells bend bullets */
   261	    for (var q = 0; q < G.enemies.length; q++) {
   262	      var wv = G.enemies[q];
   263	      if (wv.t !== 'well' || wv.born > 0) continue;
   264	      var wdx = wv.x - b.x, wdy = wv.y - b.y, wd = Math.hypot(wdx, wdy) + 1;
   265	      if (wd < 190) { var f = 12000 / (wd * wd); b.vx += wdx / wd * f * 60 * dt; b.vy += wdy / wd * f * 60 * dt; }
   266	    }
   267	    b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt; b.l -= dt;
   268	    if (b.l <= 0 || b.x < a.x || b.x > a.x + a.w || b.y < a.y || b.y > a.y + a.h) {
   269	      if (b.l > 0) { Particles.burst(b.x, b.y, 3, '#8fd8ff', 90, 0.2, 2); }
   270	      G.bullets.splice(i, 1);
   271	    }
   272	  }
   273	
   274	  updateEnemies(dt, true);
   275	  updateCrystals(dt, false);
   276	
   277	  /* --- bomb pickups --- */
   278	  for (i = G.drops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   279	    var d = G.drops[i]; d.l -= dt; d.ph += dt * 3;
   280	    if (d.l <= 0) { G.drops.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   281	    if (dist2(d.x, d.y, p.x, p.y) < 26 * 26) {
   282	      G.bombs = Math.min(3, G.bombs + 1);
   283	      Particles.burst(d.x, d.y, 16, COL.bomb, 160, 0.5, 3);
   284	      Snd.pop(); G.drops.splice(i, 1);
   285	    }
   286	  }
   287	
   288	  Particles.update(dt); Ripples.update(dt);
   289	
   290	  /* --- wave flow --- */
   291	  if (G.enemies.length === 0) {
   292	    G.waveTimer -= dt;
   293	    if (G.waveTimer <= 0) { spawnWave(); G.waveTimer = 1.1; }
   294	  } else G.waveTimer = 1.1;
   295	}
   296	
   297	function lerp2ang(a, b, t) {
   298	  var d = ((b - a + Math.PI * 3) % TAU) - Math.PI;
   299	  return a + d * clamp(t, 0, 1);
   300	}
   301	
   302	function updateCrystals(dt, noPickup) {
   303	  var p = G.player;
   304	  for (var i = G.crystals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   305	    var c = G.crystals[i];
   306	    c.l -= dt;
   307	    if (c.l <= 0) { G.crystals.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   308	    var dx = p.x - c.x, dy = p.y - c.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) + 0.001;
   309	    if (!noPickup && d < 150) {
   310	      var pull = 900 / Math.max(24, d);
   311	      c.vx += dx / d * pull * dt * 9; c.vy += dy / d * pull * dt * 9;
   312	    }
   313	    c.vx *= 1 - 1.4 * dt; c.vy *= 1 - 1.4 * dt;
   314	    c.x += c.vx * dt; c.y += c.vy * dt;
   315	    if (!noPickup && d < 18) {
   316	      G.crystals.splice(i, 1);
   317	      G.shards++;
   318	      G.mult = Math.min(25, 1 + Math.floor(G.shards / 8));
   319	      if (G.mult > G.peak) G.peak = G.mult;
   320	      G.score += Math.round(2 * G.mult);
   321	      Particles.burst(c.x, c.y, 4, COL.crystal, 110, 0.3, 2);
   322	      if (G.shards % 8 === 0) Snd.pop();
   323	    }
   324	  }
   325	}
   326	
   327	function updateEnemies(dt, canHurt) {
   328	  var a = G.arena, p = G.player, i, j;
   329	  for (i = G.enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   330	    var e = G.enemies[i];
   331	    if (e.born > 0) { e.born -= dt; continue; }
   332	    e.ph += dt;
   333	    var dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) + 0.001;
   334	
   335	    if (e.t === 'drifter' || e.t === 'mini') {
   336	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx / d * e.spd, 2.2 * dt);
   337	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy / d * e.spd, 2.2 * dt);
   338	      e.ang += dt * 2;
   339	    } else if (e.t === 'weaver') {
   340	      var tx = dx / d, ty = dy / d;
   341	      /* dodge: perpendicular kick away from close incoming bullets */
   342	      var dodgeX = 0, dodgeY = 0;
   343	      for (j = 0; j < G.bullets.length; j++) {
   344	        var b = G.bullets[j];
   345	        var bx = e.x - b.x, by = e.y - b.y, bd = Math.hypot(bx, by);
   346	        if (bd < 100) {
   347	          var dot = (b.vx * bx + b.vy * by) / (bd + 0.001);
   348	          if (dot > 0) { dodgeX += -b.vy / 640 * (1 - bd / 100) * 2.4; dodgeY += b.vx / 640 * (1 - bd / 100) * 2.4; }
   349	        }
   350	      }
   351	      var wob = Math.sin(e.ph * 3.4) * 0.7;
   352	      var vx = tx + (-ty) * wob + dodgeX * 2.4;
   353	      var vy = ty + (tx) * wob + dodgeY * 2.4;
   354	      var vl = Math.hypot(vx, vy) + 0.001;
   355	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, vx / vl * e.spd, 6 * dt);
   356	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, vy / vl * e.spd, 6 * dt);
   357	      e.ang += dt * 4;
   358	    } else if (e.t === 'snake') {
   359	      var want = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
   360	      e.ang = lerp2ang(e.ang, want, 2.0 * dt);
   361	      e.vx = Math.cos(e.ang) * e.spd; e.vy = Math.sin(e.ang) * e.spd;
   362	    } else if (e.t === 'spawner') {
   363	      var away = d < 190 ? -1 : 1;
   364	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx / d * e.spd * away + Math.cos(e.ph * 0.8) * 22, 1.6 * dt);
   365	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy / d * e.spd * away + Math.sin(e.ph * 0.9) * 22, 1.6 * dt);
   366	      e.emit -= dt;
   367	      if (e.emit <= 0) {
   368	        e.emit = 1.5 + Math.random() * 0.9;
   369	        if (e.kids < 5 && G.enemies.length < 56) {
   370	          var an = Math.random() * TAU;
   371	          var k = makeEnemy('mini', e.x + Math.cos(an) * 24, e.y + Math.sin(an) * 24);
   372	          k.owner = e; e.kids++;
   373	          Particles.burst(k.x, k.y, 6, COL.mini, 120, 0.3, 2);
   374	        }
   375	      }
   376	    } else if (e.t === 'well') {
   377	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx / d * e.spd, 1.0 * dt);
   378	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy / d * e.spd, 1.0 * dt);
   379	      e.ang += dt * 1.4;
   380	      /* pull the player */
   381	      if (d < 260) {
   382	        var f = 34000 / Math.max(2600, d * d);
   383	        p.vx += dx / d * f * 60 * dt; p.vy += dy / d * f * 60 * dt;
   384	      }
   385	      if (Math.random() < dt * 30) {
   386	        var pa = Math.random() * TAU, pr = 60 + Math.random() * 90;
   387	        Particles.burst(e.x + Math.cos(pa) * pr, e.y + Math.sin(pa) * pr, 1, '#8fb0ff', 10, 0.55, 2);
   388	      }
   389	    }
   390	
   391	    e.x += e.vx * dt; e.y += e.vy * dt;
   392	
   393	    /* arena bounds */
   394	    if (e.t === 'snake') {
   395	      if (e.x < a.x + e.r || e.x > a.x + a.w - e.r) { e.ang = Math.PI - e.ang; e.x = clamp(e.x, a.x + e.r, a.x + a.w - e.r); }
   396	      if (e.y < a.y + e.r || e.y > a.y + a.h - e.r) { e.ang = -e.ang; e.y = clamp(e.y, a.y + e.r, a.y + a.h - e.r); }
   397	      /* segment trail */
   398	      var px = e.x, py = e.y;
   399	      for (j = 0; j < e.segs.length; j++) {
   400	        var s = e.segs[j];
   401	        var sdx = px - s.x, sdy = py - s.y, sd = Math.hypot(sdx, sdy);
   402	        var spac = 13;
   403	        if (sd > spac) { s.x += sdx / sd * (sd - spac); s.y += sdy / sd * (sd - spac); }
   404	        px = s.x; py = s.y;
   405	      }
   406	    } else {
   407	      if (e.x < a.x + e.r) { e.x = a.x + e.r; e.vx = Math.abs(e.vx); }
   408	      if (e.x > a.x + a.w - e.r) { e.x = a.x + a.w - e.r; e.vx = -Math.abs(e.vx); }
   409	      if (e.y < a.y + e.r) { e.y = a.y + e.r; e.vy = Math.abs(e.vy); }
   410	      if (e.y > a.y + a.h - e.r) { e.y = a.y + a.h - e.r; e.vy = -Math.abs(e.vy); }
   411	    }
   412	
   413	    /* bullets vs enemy */
   414	    var dead = false;
   415	    for (j = G.bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   416	      var bl = G.bullets[j];
   417	      var hitSeg = -1;
   418	      if (dist2(bl.x, bl.y, e.x, e.y) < (e.r + bl.r) * (e.r + bl.r)) hitSeg = 0;
   419	      else if (e.t === 'snake') {
   420	        for (var si = 0; si < e.segs.length; si++) {
   421	          if (dist2(bl.x, bl.y, e.segs[si].x, e.segs[si].y) < (9 + bl.r) * (9 + bl.r)) { hitSeg = si + 1; break; }
   422	        }
   423	      }
   424	      if (hitSeg < 0) continue;
   425	      Particles.burst(bl.x, bl.y, 4, '#ffffff', 130, 0.22, 2.4);
   426	      G.bullets.splice(j, 1);
   427	      if (e.t === 'snake' && hitSeg > 0) {
   428	        /* sever: destroy that segment and everything behind it */
   429	        var lost = e.segs.length - (hitSeg - 1);
   430	        for (var z = e.segs.length - 1; z >= hitSeg - 1; z--) {
   431	          var sg = e.segs[z];
   432	          Particles.burst(sg.x, sg.y, 6, COL.snake, 150, 0.4, 2.6);
   433	          G.crystals.push({ x: sg.x, y: sg.y, vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 90, vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 90, l: 9 });
   434	          e.segs.pop();
   435	        }
   436	        G.score += Math.round(15 * lost * G.mult);
   437	        Snd.hit(); G.shake = Math.min(16, G.shake + 2);
   438	        continue;
   439	      }
   440	      e.hp--;
   441	      e.hurt = 0.12;
   442	      if (e.hp <= 0) { killEnemy(e, i, false); dead = true; break; }
   443	      else Snd.hit();
   444	    }
   445	    if (dead) continue;
   446	    if (e.hurt > 0) e.hurt -= dt;
   447	
   448	    /* enemy vs player */
   449	    if (canHurt && G.invuln <= 0 && G.state === 'play') {
   450	      var hitR = e.r + p.r - 3;
   451	      var hitP = dist2(e.x, e.y, p.x, p.y) < hitR * hitR;
   452	      if (!hitP && e.t === 'snake') {
   453	        for (var s2 = 0; s2 < e.segs.length; s2++) {
   454	          if (dist2(e.segs[s2].x, e.segs[s2].y, p.x, p.y) < (9 + p.r - 3) * (9 + p.r - 3)) { hitP = true; break; }
   455	        }
   456	      }
   457	      if (hitP) { playerDie(); return; }
   458	    }
   459	  }
   460	}
   461	
   462	/* ================= rendering ================= */
   463	var _d = [0, 0];
   464	var _wellGrad = null;
   465	
   466	function drawGrid(c) {
   467	  var a = G.arena;
   468	  var step = 48, samp = 24;
   469	  c.lineWidth = 1;
   470	  c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,120,190,0.30)';
   471	  var x, y, first;
   472	  for (x = a.x; x <= a.x + a.w + 1; x += step) {
   473	    var xx = Math.min(x, a.x + a.w);
   474	    c.beginPath(); first = true;
   475	    for (y = a.y; y <= a.y + a.h; y += samp) {
   476	      Ripples.disp(xx, y, _d);
   477	      if (first) { c.moveTo(xx + _d[0], y + _d[1]); first = false; }
   478	      else c.lineTo(xx + _d[0], y + _d[1]);
   479	    }
   480	    c.stroke();
   481	  }
   482	  for (y = a.y; y <= a.y + a.h + 1; y += step) {
   483	    var yy = Math.min(y, a.y + a.h);
   484	    c.beginPath(); first = true;
   485	    for (x = a.x; x <= a.x + a.w; x += samp) {
   486	      Ripples.disp(x, yy, _d);
   487	      if (first) { c.moveTo(x + _d[0], yy + _d[1]); first = false; }
   488	      else c.lineTo(x + _d[0], yy + _d[1]);
   489	    }
   490	    c.stroke();
   491	  }
   492	  /* arena border */
   493	  c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,220,255,0.20)'; c.lineWidth = 6;
   494	  c.strokeRect(a.x, a.y, a.w, a.h);
   495	  c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,240,255,0.85)'; c.lineWidth = 1.6;
   496	  c.strokeRect(a.x, a.y, a.w, a.h);
   497	}
   498	
   499	function poly(c, cx, cy, r, n, rot, col, fillA) {
   500	  c.beginPath();
   501	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   502	    var an = rot + i / n * TAU;
   503	    var px = cx + Math.cos(an) * r, py = cy + Math.sin(an) * r;
   504	    if (i === 0) c.moveTo(px, py); else c.lineTo(px, py);
   505	  }
   506	  c.closePath();
   507	  if (fillA) { c.globalAlpha = fillA; c.fillStyle = col; c.fill(); c.globalAlpha = 1; }
   508	  c.lineWidth = 3.4; c.globalAlpha = 0.28; c.strokeStyle = col; c.stroke();
   509	  c.globalAlpha = 1; c.lineWidth = 1.6; c.stroke();
   510	}
   511	
   512	function drawEnemy(c, e) {
   513	  var col = COL[e.t] || '#fff';
   514	  if (e.born > 0) {
   515	    var t = 1 - e.born / (e.t === 'mini' ? 0.35 : 0.7);
   516	    c.globalAlpha = 0.25 + t * 0.5;
   517	    c.strokeStyle = col; c.lineWidth = 2;
   518	    c.beginPath(); c.arc(e.x, e.y, e.r + (1 - t) * 26, 0, TAU); c.stroke();
   519	    c.globalAlpha = 1;
   520	    return;
   521	  }
   522	  if (e.hurt > 0) col = '#ffffff';
   523	  if (e.t === 'drifter') poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 4, e.ang, col, 0.16);
   524	  else if (e.t === 'mini') poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 3, e.ang, col, 0.2);
   525	  else if (e.t === 'weaver') {
   526	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 3, e.ang, col, 0.14);
   527	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r * 0.55, 3, -e.ang, col, 0.3);
   528	  } else if (e.t === 'snake') {
   529	    for (var i = e.segs.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   530	      var s = e.segs[i], f = 1 - i / (e.segs.length + 1) * 0.55;
   531	      c.globalAlpha = 0.55 * f + 0.2;
   532	      c.fillStyle = col;
   533	      c.beginPath(); c.arc(s.x, s.y, 8 * f + 2, 0, TAU); c.fill();
   534	      c.globalAlpha = 1;
   535	      c.strokeStyle = col; c.lineWidth = 1.4;
   536	      c.beginPath(); c.arc(s.x, s.y, 8 * f + 2, 0, TAU); c.stroke();
   537	    }
   538	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 5, e.ang, col, 0.4);
   539	  } else if (e.t === 'spawner') {
   540	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 6, e.ph * 0.6, col, 0.14);
   541	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r * 0.5 + Math.sin(e.ph * 4) * 2, 6, -e.ph, col, 0.35);
   542	  } else if (e.t === 'well') {
   543	    /* gradient built once at the origin, then translated into place */
   544	    if (!_wellGrad) {
   545	      _wellGrad = c.createRadialGradient(0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 130);
   546	      _wellGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(95,139,255,0.42)');
   547	      _wellGrad.addColorStop(0.45, 'rgba(60,80,190,0.13)');
   548	      _wellGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
   549	    }
   550	    c.save(); c.translate(e.x, e.y);
   551	    c.fillStyle = _wellGrad;
   552	    c.beginPath(); c.arc(0, 0, 130, 0, TAU); c.fill();
   553	    c.restore();
   554	    c.fillStyle = '#04050e';
   555	    c.beginPath(); c.arc(e.x, e.y, e.r * 0.72, 0, TAU); c.fill();
   556	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r, 8, e.ang, col, 0);
   557	    poly(c, e.x, e.y, e.r * 1.5, 3, -e.ang * 1.7, col, 0);
   558	  }
   559	}
   560	
--- vector game 3 ---
   560	
   561	function drawPlayer(c) {
   562	  var p = G.player;
   563	  if (G.state === 'dead' || G.state === 'over') return;
   564	  if (G.invuln > 0 && (Math.floor(G.invuln * 12) & 1)) return;
   565	  c.save();
   566	  c.translate(p.x, p.y); c.rotate(p.ang);
   567	  c.beginPath();
   568	  c.moveTo(14, 0); c.lineTo(-9, 8.5); c.lineTo(-5, 0); c.lineTo(-9, -8.5);
   569	  c.closePath();
   570	  c.globalAlpha = 0.22; c.fillStyle = COL.player; c.fill(); c.globalAlpha = 1;
   571	  c.lineWidth = 3.6; c.globalAlpha = 0.3; c.strokeStyle = COL.player; c.stroke();
   572	  c.globalAlpha = 1; c.lineWidth = 1.7; c.strokeStyle = '#e8ffff'; c.stroke();
   573	  c.restore();
   574	}
   575	
   576	function drawHud(c) {
   577	  var W = View.w, H = View.h;
   578	  c.textAlign = 'left'; c.textBaseline = 'top';
   579	  c.font = 'bold 22px monospace';
   580	  c.fillStyle = '#dff6ff';
   581	  c.fillText(String(G.score), 12, 12);
   582	  c.font = 'bold 13px monospace';
   583	  c.fillStyle = 'rgba(160,200,225,0.8)';
   584	  c.fillText('BEST ' + G.best, 12, 36);
   585	
   586	  c.textAlign = 'right';
   587	  c.font = 'bold 20px monospace';
   588	  c.fillStyle = G.mult > 1 ? '#8affe0' : 'rgba(150,190,215,0.75)';
   589	  c.fillText('x' + G.mult, W - 12, 12);
   590	  c.font = 'bold 13px monospace';
   591	  c.fillStyle = 'rgba(160,200,225,0.8)';
   592	  c.fillText('WAVE ' + Math.max(1, G.wave), W - 12, 36);
   593	
   594	  /* lives pips */
   595	  c.textAlign = 'center';
   596	  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   597	    var lx = W * 0.5 - 22 + i * 22, ly = 22;
   598	    c.beginPath();
   599	    c.moveTo(lx + 7, ly); c.lineTo(lx - 5, ly + 5); c.lineTo(lx - 5, ly - 5);
   600	    c.closePath();
   601	    if (i < G.lives) { c.fillStyle = COL.player; c.globalAlpha = 0.85; c.fill(); c.globalAlpha = 1; }
   602	    else { c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,180,205,0.4)'; c.lineWidth = 1.2; c.stroke(); }
   603	  }
   604	
   605	  /* bomb button */
   606	  var b = Input.bomb;
   607	  c.globalAlpha = G.bombs > 0 ? 1 : 0.28;
   608	  c.beginPath(); c.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, TAU);
   609	  c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,123,213,0.13)'; c.fill();
   610	  c.strokeStyle = COL.bomb; c.lineWidth = 2; c.stroke();
   611	  c.fillStyle = COL.bomb; c.font = 'bold 12px monospace'; c.textBaseline = 'middle';
   612	  c.fillText('BOMB', b.x, b.y - 6);
   613	  c.font = 'bold 15px monospace';
   614	  c.fillText(String(G.bombs), b.x, b.y + 9);
   615	  c.globalAlpha = 1;
   616	  c.textBaseline = 'top';
   617	}
   618	
   619	function draw() {
   620	  var c = View.ctx, W = View.w, H = View.h;
   621	  View.begin();
   622	  c.fillStyle = '#05060c';
   623	  c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   624	
   625	  var sx = 0, sy = 0;
   626	  if (G.shake > 0.3) { sx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * G.shake; sy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * G.shake; }
   627	  c.save();
   628	  c.translate(sx, sy);
   629	
   630	  drawGrid(c);
   631	
   632	  /* crystals */
   633	  for (var i = 0; i < G.crystals.length; i++) {
   634	    var cr = G.crystals[i];
   635	    if (cr.l < 2 && (Math.floor(cr.l * 8) & 1)) continue;
   636	    c.fillStyle = COL.crystal; c.globalAlpha = 0.85;
   637	    c.beginPath();
   638	    c.moveTo(cr.x, cr.y - 4.5); c.lineTo(cr.x + 3.4, cr.y); c.lineTo(cr.x, cr.y + 4.5); c.lineTo(cr.x - 3.4, cr.y);
   639	    c.closePath(); c.fill(); c.globalAlpha = 1;
   640	  }
   641	  /* bomb pickups */
   642	  for (i = 0; i < G.drops.length; i++) {
   643	    var d = G.drops[i];
   644	    if (d.l < 3 && (Math.floor(d.l * 8) & 1)) continue;
   645	    poly(c, d.x, d.y, 9 + Math.sin(d.ph) * 1.6, 6, d.ph * 0.5, COL.bomb, 0.3);
   646	  }
   647	
   648	  Particles.draw(c);
   649	  for (i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) drawEnemy(c, G.enemies[i]);
   650	
   651	  /* bullets */
   652	  c.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
   653	  for (i = 0; i < G.bullets.length; i++) {
   654	    var b = G.bullets[i];
   655	    c.globalAlpha = 0.3;
   656	    c.fillRect(b.x - 2.8, b.y - 2.8, 5.6, 5.6);
   657	    c.globalAlpha = 1;
   658	    c.fillRect(b.x - 1.5, b.y - 1.5, 3, 3);
   659	  }
   660	  drawPlayer(c);
   661	  c.restore();
   662	
   663	  drawHud(c);
   664	  Input.drawSticks(c);
   665	
   666	  /* banner */
   667	  if (G.banner > 0 && G.state === 'play') {
   668	    var al = Math.min(1, G.banner / 0.5);
   669	    c.globalAlpha = al * 0.9;
   670	    c.textAlign = 'center'; c.textBaseline = 'middle';
   671	    c.font = 'bold 30px monospace'; c.fillStyle = '#9be8ff';
   672	    c.fillText(G.bannerTxt, W * 0.5, G.arena.y + G.arena.h * 0.32);
   673	    c.globalAlpha = 1; c.textBaseline = 'top';
   674	  }
   675	
   676	  /* one-line hint */
   677	  if (G.hint > 0) {
   678	    c.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, G.hint / 1.5) * 0.85;
   679	    c.textAlign = 'center';
   680	    c.font = 'bold 13px monospace'; c.fillStyle = '#bfe6ff';
   681	    c.fillText('DRAG LEFT TO MOVE  ·  DRAG RIGHT TO SHOOT', W * 0.5, G.arena.y + G.arena.h - 92);
   682	    c.globalAlpha = 1;
   683	  }
   684	
   685	  if (G.flash > 0) {
   686	    c.globalAlpha = clamp(G.flash, 0, 1) * 0.35;
   687	    c.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); c.globalAlpha = 1;
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  if (G.state === 'over') {
   691	    c.fillStyle = 'rgba(3,5,12,0.82)'; c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   692	    c.textAlign = 'center'; c.textBaseline = 'middle';
   693	    var cy = H * 0.42;
   694	    c.font = 'bold 34px monospace'; c.fillStyle = '#ff5f6d';
   695	    c.fillText('GAME OVER', W * 0.5, cy - 74);

   712	/* ================= loop ================= */
   713	var _last = 0;
   714	function frame(ts) {
   715	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   716	  if (!_last) _last = ts;
   717	  var dt = (ts - _last) / 1000;
   718	  _last = ts;
   719	  if (dt > 1 / 20) dt = 1 / 20;
   720	  if (dt <= 0) dt = 1 / 60;
   721	  update(dt);
   722	  draw();
   723	}

   193	function update(dt) {
   194	  layout();
   195	  Input.poll();
   196	  var a = G.arena, p = G.player, i, e;
   197	
   198	  if (Input.bombEdge) { useBomb(); Input.bombEdge = false; }
   199	  if (G.hint > 0) G.hint -= dt;
   200	  if (G.banner > 0) G.banner -= dt;
   201	  if (G.flash > 0) G.flash -= dt * 2.2;
   202	  G.shake *= 1 - Math.min(1, 6 * dt);
   203	
   204	  if (G.state === 'over') {
   205	    G.over += dt;
   206	    if (G.over > 0.6 && Input.anyEdge) { reset(true); }
   207	    Input.anyEdge = false;

   209	    return;
   210	  }
   211	  Input.anyEdge = false;
   212	
   213	  if (G.state === 'dead') {
   214	    G.respawn -= dt;

   216	    updateCrystals(dt, true);

   218	      p.x = a.x + a.w / 2; p.y = a.y + a.h * 0.62; p.vx = p.vy = 0;

   220	      /* clear space around respawn */
   221	      for (i = G.enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {

   223	      }
   224	    }
   225	    /* enemies keep moving while dead, minus contact */
   226	    updateEnemies(dt, false);
   227	    return;
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  if (G.invuln > 0) G.invuln -= dt;

   232	  /* --- player --- */

   235	  if (m.mag > 0) { p.vx += m.x * m.mag * acc * dt; p.vy += m.y * m.mag * acc * dt; }

   243	  if (p.y < a.y + p.r) { p.y = a.y + p.r; p.vy *= -0.35; }
   244	  if (p.y > a.y + a.h - p.r) { p.y = a.y + a.h - p.r; p.vy *= -0.35; }
   245

   252	  } else if (m.mag > 0.1) {

   295	}

   297	function lerp2ang(a, b, t) {
   298	  var d = ((b - a + Math.PI * 3) % TAU) - Math.PI;
   299	  return a + d * clamp(t, 0, 1);

    66	  function mulberry32(seed) {
    67	    return function random() {
    68	      let t = seed += 0x6D2B79F5;
    69	      t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    70	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    71	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    72	    };
    73	  }

   208	  function requestTurn(snake, next) {
   209	    if (!next || (!next.x && !next.y)) return;
   210	    if (next.x === -snake.dir.x && next.y === -snake.dir.y) return;
   211	    snake.nextDir = copyDir(next);

   216	  function handleTurn(name) {
   217	    const next = turnFromName(name);
   218	    if (roundState === 'play' && player && player.alive) requestTurn(player, next);

   222	  function pointToSegmentDistance(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by) {
   223	    const dx = bx - ax;
   224	    const dy = by - ay;
   225	    const lenSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
   226	    let t = lenSq ? ((px - ax) * dx + (py - ay) * dy) / lenSq : 0;
   227	    t = clamp(t, 0, 1);
   228	    const x = ax + t * dx;
   229	    const y = ay + t * dy;
   230	    const xx = px - x;
   230	    const xx = px - x;
   231	    const yy = py - y;
   232	    return Math.sqrt(xx * xx + yy * yy);
   233	  }
   234	
   235	  function segmentHitsGate(ax, ay, bx, by, g) {
   236	    if (g.axis === 'h') {
   237	      if (Math.max(ax, bx) < g.start || Math.min(ax, bx) > g.end) return false;
   238	      return pointToSegmentDistance(g.start, g.pos, ax, ay, bx, by) < .48 ||
   239	        pointToSegmentDistance(g.end, g.pos, ax, ay, bx, by) < .48 ||
   240	        ((ay - g.pos) * (by - g.pos) <= 0 && Math.min(ax, bx) <= g.end && Math.max(ax, bx) >= g.start);
   241	    }
   242	    if (Math.max(ay, by) < g.start || Math.min(ay, by) > g.end) return false;
   243	    return pointToSegmentDistance(g.pos, g.start, ax, ay, bx, by) < .48 ||
   244	      pointToSegmentDistance(g.pos, g.end, ax, ay, bx, by) < .48 ||
   245	      ((ax - g.pos) * (bx - g.pos) <= 0 && Math.min(ay, by) <= g.end && Math.max(ay, by) >= g.start);
   246	  }
   247	
   248	  function hitsArena(ax, ay, bx, by) {
   249	    const b = currentBounds();
   250	    return bx < b.left + .08 || bx > b.right - .08 || by < b.top + .08 || by > b.bottom - .08;
   251	  }
   252	
   253	  function hitsGate(ax, ay, bx, by) {
   254	    for (const g of gates()) if (!gateOpen(g) && segmentHitsGate(ax, ay, bx, by, g)) return true;
   255	    return false;
   256	  }
   257	
   258	  function hitsTrails(snake, ax, ay, bx, by, forPlanning) {
   259	    const now = elapsed;
   260	    const check = (other) => {
   261	      if (!other.trail.length) return false;
   262	      for (let i = 0; i < other.trail.length; i++) {
   263	        const point = other.trail[i];
   264	        const age = now - point.t;
   265	        if (age > WALL_TTL || (other === snake && age < (forPlanning ? .55 : .28))) continue;
   266	        if (pointToSegmentDistance(point.x, point.y, ax, ay, bx, by) < .49) return true;
   267	      }
   268	      return false;
   269	    };
   270	    if (check(snake)) return true;
   271	    if (check(player) && snake !== player) return true;
   272	    for (const rival of rivals) if (rival !== snake && check(rival)) return true;
   273	    return false;
   274	  }
   275	
   276	  function blockedFor(snake, ax, ay, bx, by, planning) {
   277	    return hitsArena(ax, ay, bx, by) || hitsGate(ax, ay, bx, by) || hitsTrails(snake, ax, ay, bx, by, planning);
   278	  }
   279	
   280	  function trimTrail(snake) {
   281	    const cutoff = elapsed - WALL_TTL;
   282	    while (snake.trail.length > 1 && snake.trail[0].t < cutoff) snake.trail.shift();
   283	  }
   284	
   285	  function bodyWindow(snake) { return 2.7 + snake.lengthBonus * .34; }
   286	
   287	  function sampleTrail(snake) {
   288	    if (dist(snake, snake.lastSample) >= SAMPLE_DISTANCE) {
   289	      snake.trail.push({ x: snake.x, y: snake.y, t: elapsed });
   290	      snake.lastSample = { x: snake.x, y: snake.y };
   291	    }
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  function moveSnake(snake, dt) {
   295	    if (!snake.alive) { trimTrail(snake); return; }
   296	    if (snake.nextDir.x !== -snake.dir.x || snake.nextDir.y !== -snake.dir.y) snake.dir = copyDir(snake.nextDir);
   297	    snake.boost = Math.max(0, snake.boost - dt);
   298	    snake.padCooldown = Math.max(0, snake.padCooldown - dt);
   299	    const speed = (snake.ai ? 5.0 : 5.55) * (snake.boost > 0 ? 1.36 : 1);
   300	    const nx = snake.x + snake.dir.x * speed * dt;
   301	    const ny = snake.y + snake.dir.y * speed * dt;
   302	    if (blockedFor(snake, snake.x, snake.y, nx, ny, false)) {
   303	      destroySnake(snake);
   304	      return;
   305	    }
   306	    snake.x = nx;
   307	    snake.y = ny;
   308	    sampleTrail(snake);
   309	    trimTrail(snake);
   310	  }
   311	
   312	  function applySpeedPad(snake) {
   313	    if (!snake.alive) return;
   314	    for (const pad of SPEED_PADS) {
   315	      if (Math.hypot(snake.x - pad.x, snake.y - pad.y) < .8) {
   316	        snake.boost = Math.max(snake.boost, 1.25);
   317	        if (snake.padCooldown <= 0) {
   318	          snake.padCooldown = .45;
   319	          burst(pad.x, pad.y, snake.color, snake.ai ? 3 : 7, .65);
   320	          shake = Math.max(shake, .045);
   321	        }
   322	        break;
   323	      }
   324	    }
   325	  }
   326	
   327	  function chooseAiTurn(snake) {
   328	    if (!player || !player.alive) return;
   329	    const choices = [leftOf(snake.dir), copyDir(snake.dir), rightOf(snake.dir)];
   330	    const lead = 2.1 + clamp(player.lengthBonus, 0, 3) * .22;
   331	    const target = {
   332	      x: player.x + player.dir.x * lead,
   333	      y: player.y + player.dir.y * lead
   334	    };
   335	    let bestChoice = snake.dir;
   336	    let bestValue = Infinity;
   337	    for (let i = 0; i < choices.length; i++) {
   338	      const d = choices[i];
   339	      const far = 1.15 + rng() * .45;
   340	      const tx = snake.x + d.x * far;
   341	      const ty = snake.y + d.y * far;
   342	      if (blockedFor(snake, snake.x, snake.y, tx, ty, true)) continue;
   343	      const value = Math.hypot(tx - target.x, ty - target.y) + (i === 1 ? 0 : .08) + rng() * .4;
   344	      if (value < bestValue) { bestValue = value; bestChoice = d; }
   345	    }
   346	    requestTurn(snake, bestChoice);
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function collectPips(snake) {
   350	    for (let i = pips.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   351	      if (dist(snake, pips[i]) < .77) {
   352	        const pip = pips[i];
   353	        pips.splice(i, 1);
   354	        snake.lengthBonus += snake.ai ? .28 : .38;
   355	        if (!snake.ai) { pipsCollected += pip.value; burst(pip.x, pip.y, snake.color, 9, 1.3); shake = Math.max(shake, .08); }
   356	        else burst(pip.x, pip.y, snake.color, 4, .7);
   357	      }
   358	    }
   359	  }
   360	
   361	  function destroySnake(snake) {
   362	    if (!snake.alive) return;
   363	    snake.alive = false;
   364	    snake.deathFlash = .8;
   365	    burst(snake.x, snake.y, snake.color, snake.ai ? 17 : 28, snake.ai ? 1.7 : 2.1);
   366	    shake = Math.max(shake, snake.ai ? .13 : .32);
   367	    if (snake.ai) rivalKills++;
   368	    else endGame();
   369	  }
   370	
   371	  function endGame() {
   372	    if (roundState === 'dead') return;
   373	    roundState = 'dead';
   374	    updateHud();
   375	    messageTitle.textContent = 'LINE LOST';
   376	    messageCopy.textContent = `Score ${score()}  •  best ${best}`;
   377	    message.querySelector('.restart').textContent = 'TAP OR PRESS SPACE TO RETRY';
   378	    message.className = 'show dead';
   379	  }
   380	
   381	  function clearRound() {
   382	    if (roundState !== 'play') return;
   383	    roundState = 'clear';
   384	    roundClearTimer = 1.25;
   385	    round++;
   386	    updateHud();
   387	    messageTitle.textContent = 'ROUND CLEAR';
   388	    messageCopy.textContent = `The arena grows a new edge.  ${Math.min(3, round)} hunters next.`;
   389	    message.querySelector('.restart').textContent = 'NEXT RUN IN A MOMENT';
   390	    message.className = 'show';
   391	    burst(player.x, player.y, player.color, 20, 1.8);
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  function update(dt) {
   395	    elapsed += dt;
   396	    for (const particle of particles) {
   397	      particle.life -= dt;
   398	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
   399	      particle.y += particle.vy * dt;
   400	      particle.vx *= Math.pow(.04, dt);
   401	      particle.vy *= Math.pow(.04, dt);
   402	    }
   403	    particles = particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   404	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.9);
   405	    if (roundState === 'dead') { trimTrail(player); for (const rival of rivals) trimTrail(rival); return; }
   406	    if (roundState === 'clear') {
   407	      roundClearTimer -= dt;
   408	      trimTrail(player);
   409	      for (const rival of rivals) trimTrail(rival);
   410	      if (roundClearTimer <= 0) beginRound();
   411	      return;
   412	    }
   413	    roundTime += dt;
   414	    if (player.alive) {
   415	      moveSnake(player, dt);
   416	      if (player.alive) { applySpeedPad(player); collectPips(player); }
   417	    }
   418	    for (const rival of rivals) {
   419	      if (!rival.alive) { trimTrail(rival); continue; }
   420	      rival.think -= dt;
   421	      if (rival.think <= 0) { chooseAiTurn(rival); rival.think = .26 + rng() * .32; }
   422	      moveSnake(rival, dt);
   423	      if (rival.alive) { applySpeedPad(rival); collectPips(rival); }
   424	    }
   425	    if (player.alive && rivals.every(rival => !rival.alive)) clearRound();
   426	    updateHud();
   427	  }
   428	
   429	  function burst(x, y, color, count, force) {
   430	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   431	      const a = rand(0, TAU);
   432	      const speed = rand(.35, 1.8) * force;
   433	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed, life: rand(.25, .72), max: .72, size: rand(.035, .11), color });
   434	    }
   435	    if (particles.length > 150) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 150);
   436	  }
   437	
   438	  function draw() {
   439	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
   440	    ctx.fillStyle = '#070b16';
   441	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewW, viewH);
   442	    ctx.save();
   443	    const jx = Math.sin(elapsed * 47.1) * shake * scale * .45;
   444	    const jy = Math.cos(elapsed * 39.7) * shake * scale * .45;
   445	    ctx.translate(ox + jx, oy + jy);
   446	    ctx.scale(scale, scale);
   447	    drawArena();
   448	    drawPips();
   449	    drawTrails();
   450	    drawParticles();
   451	    ctx.restore();
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function drawArena() {
   455	    const b = currentBounds();
   456	    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   457	    grad.addColorStop(0, '#0d1a2c');
   458	    grad.addColorStop(1, '#08101d');
   459	    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
   460	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   461	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(82,218,255,.025)';
   462	    ctx.fillRect(b.left, b.top, b.right - b.left, b.bottom - b.top);
   463	    ctx.save();
   464	    ctx.beginPath();
   465	    ctx.rect(b.left, b.top, b.right - b.left, b.bottom - b.top);
   466	    ctx.clip();
   467	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(117,184,211,.08)';
   468	    ctx.lineWidth = .035;
   469	    for (let x = Math.ceil(b.left); x <= b.right; x++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, b.top); ctx.lineTo(x, b.bottom); ctx.stroke(); }
   470	    for (let y = Math.ceil(b.top); y <= b.bottom; y++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(b.left, y); ctx.lineTo(b.right, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   470	    for (let y = Math.ceil(b.top); y <= b.bottom; y++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(b.left, y); ctx.lineTo(b.right, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   471	    ctx.restore();
   472	    const shrink = clamp((roundTime - 26) / 20, 0, 1);
   473	    ctx.strokeStyle = shrink > 0 ? rgba('255,102,142', .56 + shrink * .27) : '#3ac6e878';
   474	    ctx.lineWidth = .16;
   475	    ctx.shadowColor = shrink > 0 ? '#ff668e' : '#39dfff';
   476	    ctx.shadowBlur = .55 + shrink * .65;
   477	    ctx.strokeRect(b.left, b.top, b.right - b.left, b.bottom - b.top);
   478	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   479	    drawSpeedPads();
   480	    for (const g of gates()) drawGate(g);
   481	    if (shrink > 0) {
   482	      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('255,102,142', .025 + shrink * .035);
   483	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, b.top);
   484	      ctx.fillRect(0, b.bottom, W, H - b.bottom);
   485	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, b.left, H);
   486	      ctx.fillRect(b.right, 0, W - b.right, H);
   487	    }
   488	  }
   489	
   490	  function drawSpeedPads() {
   491	    for (const pad of SPEED_PADS) {
   492	      const pulse = .88 + Math.sin(elapsed * 4.5 + pad.phase) * .08;
   493	      ctx.save();
   494	      ctx.translate(pad.x, pad.y);
   495	      ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4);
   496	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(85,231,255,.07)';
   497	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(85,231,255,.56)';
   498	      ctx.lineWidth = .1;
   499	      ctx.shadowColor = '#55e7ff';
   500	      ctx.shadowBlur = .45;
   501	      ctx.fillRect(-.58 * pulse, -.58 * pulse, 1.16 * pulse, 1.16 * pulse);
   502	      ctx.strokeRect(-.58 * pulse, -.58 * pulse, 1.16 * pulse, 1.16 * pulse);
   503	      ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 4);
   504	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(218,251,255,.84)';
   505	      ctx.beginPath();
   506	      ctx.moveTo(-.23, .18); ctx.lineTo(.02, -.18); ctx.lineTo(.02, -.03); ctx.lineTo(.24, -.03);
   507	      ctx.lineTo(-.07, .31); ctx.lineTo(-.07, .13); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   508	      ctx.restore();
   509	    }
   510	  }
   511	
   512	  function drawGate(g) {
   513	    const open = gateOpen(g);
   514	    const t = .35 + Math.sin(elapsed * 3 + g.offset) * .08;
   515	    ctx.save();
   516	    ctx.lineWidth = open ? .08 : .22;
   517	    ctx.strokeStyle = open ? rgba('85,231,255', .22) : rgba('255,200,92', .86);
   518	    ctx.shadowColor = open ? '#55e7ff' : '#ffc85c';
   519	    ctx.shadowBlur = open ? .35 : .8;
   520	    ctx.setLineDash(open ? [.5, .48] : [.9, .18]);
   521	    ctx.beginPath();
   522	    if (g.axis === 'h') { ctx.moveTo(g.start, g.pos); ctx.lineTo(g.end, g.pos); }
   523	    else { ctx.moveTo(g.pos, g.start); ctx.lineTo(g.pos, g.end); }
   524	    ctx.stroke();
   525	    ctx.setLineDash([]);
   526	    if (!open) {
   527	      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('255,200,92', .12 + t * .05);
   528	      if (g.axis === 'h') ctx.fillRect(g.start, g.pos - .19, g.end - g.start, .38);
   529	      else ctx.fillRect(g.pos - .19, g.start, .38, g.end - g.start);
   530	    }
   531	    ctx.restore();
   532	  }
   533	
   534	  function drawPips() {
   535	    for (const pip of pips) {
   536	      const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 4 + pip.pulse) * .12;
   537	      ctx.save();
   538	      ctx.translate(pip.x, pip.y);
   539	      ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4);
   540	      ctx.fillStyle = '#f8fbff';
   541	      ctx.shadowColor = '#a9f4ff';
   542	      ctx.shadowBlur = .65;
   543	      ctx.fillRect(-.23 * pulse, -.23 * pulse, .46 * pulse, .46 * pulse);
   544	      ctx.fillStyle = '#55e7ff';
   545	      ctx.fillRect(-.10 * pulse, -.10 * pulse, .20 * pulse, .20 * pulse);
   546	      ctx.restore();
   547	    }
   548	  }
   549	
   550	  function drawTrails() {
   551	    const snakes = [player, ...rivals];
   552	    for (const snake of snakes) {
   553	      if (!snake) continue;
   554	      const bodyTime = bodyWindow(snake);
   555	      ctx.save();
   556	      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   557	      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   558	      const wallBuckets = [
   559	        [bodyTime, bodyTime + 2.4, .34],
   560	        [bodyTime + 2.4, bodyTime + 5.0, .25],
   561	        [bodyTime + 5.0, bodyTime + 7.6, .16],
   562	        [bodyTime + 7.6, WALL_TTL + .1, .07]
   563	      ];
   564	      for (const bucket of wallBuckets) {
   565	        ctx.beginPath();
   566	        let hasPath = false;
   567	        for (let i = 1; i < snake.trail.length; i++) {
   568	          const a = snake.trail[i - 1];
   569	          const b = snake.trail[i];
   570	          const age = elapsed - b.t;
   571	          if (age < bucket[0] || age >= bucket[1] || age > WALL_TTL) continue;
   572	          if (!hasPath) { ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); hasPath = true; }
   573	          ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
   574	        }
   575	        if (hasPath) {
   576	          ctx.lineWidth = .68;
   577	          ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(snake.color.rgb, bucket[2]);
   578	          ctx.stroke();
   579	        }
   580	      }
   581	      ctx.beginPath();
   582	      let hasBody = false;
   583	      for (let i = 1; i < snake.trail.length; i++) {
   584	        const a = snake.trail[i - 1];
   585	        const b = snake.trail[i];
   586	        if (elapsed - b.t > bodyTime) continue;
   587	        if (!hasBody) { ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); hasBody = true; }
   588	        ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
   589	      }
   590	      if (hasBody) {
   591	        ctx.lineWidth = .92;
   592	        ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(snake.color.rgb, .62);
   593	        ctx.shadowColor = snake.color.main;
   594	        ctx.shadowBlur = 1.1;
   595	        ctx.stroke();
   596	      }
   597	      ctx.restore();
   598	      if (snake.alive) {
   599	        ctx.save();
   600	        ctx.translate(snake.x, snake.y);
   601	        ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(snake.dir.y, snake.dir.x));
   602	        ctx.fillStyle = snake.color.main;
   603	        ctx.shadowColor = snake.color.main;
   604	        ctx.shadowBlur = 1.15;
   605	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, .52, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   606	        ctx.fillStyle = '#07101b';
   607	        ctx.fillRect(.12, -.23, .18, .15);
   608	        ctx.fillRect(.12, .08, .18, .15);
   609	        ctx.restore();
   610	      }
   611	    }
   612	  }
   613	
   614	  function drawParticles() {
   615	    for (const particle of particles) {
   616	      const life = clamp(particle.life / particle.max, 0, 1);
   617	      ctx.fillStyle = rgba(particle.color.rgb, life * .82);
   618	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(particle.x, particle.y, particle.size * (.5 + life), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   619	    }
   620	  }
   621	
   622	  function frame(now) {
   623	    const dt = Math.min(.045, Math.max(0, (now - lastFrame) / 1000));
   624	    lastFrame = now;
   625	    update(dt);
   626	    draw();
   627	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   628	  }
   629	
   630	  function unlockAudio() {
   631	    if (!audioContext) {
   632	      try { audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (_) { audioContext = null; }
   633	    }
   634	    if (audioContext && audioContext.state === 'suspended') audioContext.resume();
   635	  }
   636	
   637	  function pointerTurn(x, y, start) {
   638	    if (!start) return;
   639	    const dx = x - start.x;
   640	    const dy = y - start.y;
   641	    if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < 22) return;
   642	    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) handleTurn(dx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left');
   643	    else handleTurn(dy > 0 ? 'down' : 'up');
   644	  }
   645	
   646	  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', (event) => {
   647	    unlockAudio();
   648	    const touch = event.changedTouches[0];
   649	    pointerStart = { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY };
   650	    event.preventDefault();
   651	  }, { passive: false });
   652	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   653	  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', (event) => {
   654	    const touch = event.changedTouches[0];
   655	    pointerTurn(touch.clientX, touch.clientY, pointerStart);
   656	    if (roundState === 'dead' && pointerStart && Math.hypot(touch.clientX - pointerStart.x, touch.clientY - pointerStart.y) < 22) startGame();
   657	    pointerStart = null;
   658	    event.preventDefault();
   659	  }, { passive: false });
   660	  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => { mouseStart = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY }; });
   661	  canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', (event) => {
   662	    pointerTurn(event.clientX, event.clientY, mouseStart);
   663	    if (roundState === 'dead' && mouseStart && Math.hypot(event.clientX - mouseStart.x, event.clientY - mouseStart.y) < 22) startGame();
   664	    mouseStart = null;
   665	  });
   666	  message.addEventListener('click', () => { if (roundState === 'dead') startGame(); });
   667	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
   668	    const keys = { ArrowUp: 'up', w: 'up', W: 'up', ArrowDown: 'down', s: 'down', S: 'down', ArrowLeft: 'left', a: 'left', A: 'left', ArrowRight: 'right', d: 'right', D: 'right' };
   669	    if (keys[event.key]) { event.preventDefault(); handleTurn(keys[event.key]); }
   670	    if ((event.key === ' ' || event.key === 'Enter') && roundState === 'dead') { event.preventDefault(); startGame(); }
   671	  });
   672	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   673	
   674	  resize();
   675	  startGame();
   676	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   677	})();

   261	    function startAudio() {
   262	      if (audioContext) {
   263	        if (audioContext.state === 'suspended') audioContext.resume();
   264	        return;
   265	      }
   266	      try {
   267	        audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
   268	      } catch (_) { audioContext = null; }
