   271	    function tone(frequency, duration, type = 'sine', volume = .025) {
   272	      if (!audioContext) return;
   273	      try {
   274	        const now = audioContext.currentTime;
   275	        const oscillator = audioContext.createOscillator();
   276	        const gain = audioContext.createGain();
   277	        oscillator.type = type;
   278	        oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);
   279	        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume, now);
   280	        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001, now + duration);
   281	        oscillator.connect(gain).connect(audioContext.destination);
   282	        oscillator.start(now);
   283	        oscillator.stop(now + duration);
   284	      } catch (_) { /* Audio is a bonus, never a dependency. */ }
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    function burst(x, y, color, count = 8, power = 1) {
   288	      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   289	        const a = hash(elapsed * 30 + i * 4.31 + x) * Math.PI * 2;
   290	        const s = (25 + hash(i * 6.4 + y) * 55) * power;
   291	        particles.push({
   292	          x, y,
   293	          vx: Math.cos(a) * s,
   294	          vy: Math.sin(a) * s,
   295	          life: .35 + hash(i * 3.7) * .35,
   296	          max: .7,
   297	          color,
   298	          size: 2 + hash(i * 9.8) * 3
   299	        });
   300	      }
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    function fail(reason) {
   304	      if (state !== 'running') return;
   305	      state = 'failed';
   306	      saveBest(distance + coins);
   307	      flash = 1;
   308	      shake = 12;
   309	      const y = rowScreenY(player.row) + ROW_H / 2;
   310	      burst(player.x, y, reason === 'water' ? '#76e4ff' : '#ff756e', 22, 1.25);
   311	      tone(reason === 'water' ? 110 : 72, .24, 'sawtooth', .045);
   312	    }
   313	
   314	    function attemptHop(dx, dy) {
   315	      if (state !== 'running' || player.hop) return;
   316	      startAudio();
   317	      const fromRow = player.row;
   318	      const toRow = Math.max(0, fromRow + dy);
   319	      const toCol = Math.max(0, Math.min(COLS - 1, player.col + dx));
   320	      if (toRow === fromRow && toCol === player.col) return;
   321	      ensureRows(toRow + 10);
   322	      player.hop = {
   323	        fromRow,
   324	        toRow,
   325	        fromX: player.x,
   326	        toX: cellX(toCol),
   327	        toCol,
   328	        t: 0,
   329	        duration: .17
   330	      };
   331	      idle = 0;
   332	      eagle = null;
   333	      burst(player.x, rowScreenY(fromRow) + ROW_H / 2, colors.mint, 5, .45);
   334	      tone(dy > 0 ? 360 : 250, .055, 'triangle', .018);
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    function finishHop() {
   338	      const hop = player.hop;
   339	      if (!hop) return;
   340	      player.row = hop.toRow;
   341	      player.col = hop.toCol;
   342	      player.x = hop.toX;
   343	      player.visualRow = player.row;
   344	      player.hop = null;
   345	      distance = Math.max(distance, player.row);
   346	      const row = makeRow(player.row);
   347	      if (row.coin && !row.coin.collected && wrappedDistance(player.x, cellX(row.coin.col)) < 22) {
   348	        row.coin.collected = true;
   349	        coins++;
   350	        flash = .45;
   351	        shake = 3;
   352	        burst(player.x, rowScreenY(player.row) + ROW_H / 2, colors.yellow, 12, .75);
   353	        tone(720, .11, 'square', .035);
   354	      }
   355	      evaluateDanger();
   356	    }
   357	
   358	    function supportAt(row, x, time) {
   359	      for (const support of row.supports) {
   360	        const sx = wrap(support.x + support.speed * time * .001);
   361	        const sinking = support.pad && Math.sin(time * .00155 + support.phase) > .78;
   362	        if (!sinking && wrappedDistance(sx, x) < support.width / 2 - 2) return true;
   363	      }
   364	      return false;
   365	    }
   366	
   367	    function trainInfo(row, time) {
   368	      const t = (time * .001 * row.train.speed + row.train.phase) % 6;
   369	      const warning = t < 1.35;
   370	      const active = t >= 1.35 && t < 3.35;
   371	      const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (t - 1.35) / 2));
   372	      const x = row.train.direction > 0
   373	        ? -150 + progress * (BASE_W + 300)
   374	        : BASE_W + 150 - progress * (BASE_W + 300);
   375	      return { warning, active, x };
   376	    }
   377	
   378	    function evaluateDanger() {
   379	      if (state !== 'running' || player.hop) return;
   380	      const row = makeRow(player.row);
   380	      const row = makeRow(player.row);
   381	      if (row.type === 'river' && !supportAt(row, player.x, elapsed)) {
   382	        fail('water');
   383	        return;
   384	      }
   385	      if (row.type === 'road') {
   386	        for (const vehicle of row.vehicles) {
   387	          const x = wrap(vehicle.x + vehicle.speed * elapsed * .001);
   388	          if (wrappedDistance(player.x, x) < vehicle.width / 2 + 13) {
   389	            fail('road');
   390	            return;
   391	          }
   392	        }
   393	      }
   394	      if (row.type === 'rail') {
   395	        const train = trainInfo(row, elapsed);
   396	        if (train.active && wrappedDistance(player.x, train.x) < 62) fail('rail');
   397	      }
   398	    }
   399	
   400	    function update(dt) {
   401	      if (state !== 'running') {
   402	        flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 1.7);
   403	        updateParticles(dt);
   404	        return;
   405	      }
   406	      elapsed += dt * 1000;
   407	      idle += dt;
   408	
   409	      if (player.hop) {
   410	        const hop = player.hop;
   411	        hop.t += dt / hop.duration;
   412	        const t = Math.min(1, hop.t);
   413	        const eased = t * (2 - t);
   414	        player.x = lerp(hop.fromX, hop.toX, eased);
   415	        player.visualRow = lerp(hop.fromRow, hop.toRow, eased);
   416	        if (t >= 1) finishHop();
   417	      } else {
   418	        const row = makeRow(player.row);
   419	        if (row.type === 'river') {
   420	          player.x = wrap(player.x + row.current * dt);
   421	          player.col = nearestCol(player.x);
   422	        }
   423	        evaluateDanger();
   424	      }
   425	
   426	      const targetCamera = Math.max(0, (player.hop ? player.hop.toRow : player.row) - 2.1);
   427	      cameraRow = lerp(cameraRow, targetCamera, 1 - Math.pow(.0001, dt));
   428	      if (idle > 7.2 && !eagle) {
   429	        eagle = { t: 0, side: hash(elapsed) > .5 ? 1 : -1 };
   430	        tone(170, .18, 'triangle', .025);
   431	      }
   432	      if (eagle) {
   433	        eagle.t += dt;
   434	        if (eagle.t > 1.65) fail('eagle');
   435	      }
   436	      shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 30);
   437	      flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 1.9);
   438	      updateParticles(dt);
   439	    }
   440	
   441	    function updateParticles(dt) {
   442	      for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   443	        const p = particles[i];
   444	        p.life -= dt;
   445	        if (p.life <= 0) { particles.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   446	        p.x += p.vx * dt;
   447	        p.y += p.vy * dt;
   448	        p.vy += 90 * dt;
   449	      }
   450	    }
   451	
   452	    function drawSky() {
   453	      const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, BASE_H);
   454	      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#09182b');
   455	      gradient.addColorStop(.5, '#102b3d');
   456	      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#0d1c2d');
   457	      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   458	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, BASE_W, BASE_H);
   459	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(116,243,176,.07)';
   460	      for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
   461	        const x = hash(i * 7.5) * BASE_W;
   462	        const y = wrap(hash(i * 2.2) * 900 - elapsed * (.004 + hash(i) * .006), BASE_H);
   463	        ctx.beginPath();
   464	        ctx.arc(x, y, 1 + hash(i * 8) * 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   465	        ctx.fill();
   466	      }
   467	    }
   468	
   469	    function drawMedian(row, y) {
   470	      const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, y + ROW_H);
   471	      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#2c6959');
   472	      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#1d4d49');
   473	      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   474	      ctx.fillRect(0, y, BASE_W, ROW_H);
   475	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(190,255,217,.16)';
   476	      for (let i = -1; i < 9; i++) {
   477	        ctx.beginPath();
   478	        ctx.arc(i * 57 + (row.index % 2) * 20, y + 18 + hash(i + row.index) * 27, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   479	        ctx.fill();
   480	      }
   481	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(203,255,224,.2)';
   482	      ctx.fillRect(0, y + ROW_H - 2, BASE_W, 2);
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    function drawRoad(row, y) {
   486	      ctx.fillStyle = colors.road;
   487	      ctx.fillRect(0, y, BASE_W, ROW_H);
   488	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.07)';
   489	      ctx.fillRect(0, y + 3, BASE_W, 2);
   490	      ctx.fillRect(0, y + ROW_H - 5, BASE_W, 2);
   491	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(190,213,227,.22)';
   492	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   493	      ctx.setLineDash([25, 19]);
   494	      ctx.beginPath();
   495	      ctx.moveTo(0, y + ROW_H / 2);
   496	      ctx.lineTo(BASE_W, y + ROW_H / 2);
   497	      ctx.stroke();
   498	      ctx.setLineDash([]);
   499	      for (const vehicle of row.vehicles) {
   500	        const x = wrap(vehicle.x + vehicle.speed * elapsed * .001);
   501	        drawVehicle(x, y + ROW_H / 2, vehicle);
   502	        if (x < vehicle.width) drawVehicle(x + BASE_W, y + ROW_H / 2, vehicle);
   503	        if (x > BASE_W - vehicle.width) drawVehicle(x - BASE_W, y + ROW_H / 2, vehicle);
   504	      }
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    function drawVehicle(x, y, vehicle) {
   508	      const w = vehicle.width;
   509	      const h = vehicle.height;
   510	      ctx.save();
   511	      ctx.translate(x, y);
   512	      ctx.shadowColor = vehicle.truck ? 'rgba(255,161,90,.3)' : 'rgba(255,104,114,.28)';
   513	      ctx.shadowBlur = 11;
   514	      ctx.fillStyle = vehicle.truck ? '#d8794f' : '#cf5667';
   515	      roundedRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 7);
   516	      ctx.fill();
   517	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   518	      ctx.fillStyle = vehicle.truck ? '#f0bc78' : '#8cc4cc';
   519	      roundedRect(vehicle.truck ? -w * .18 : -w * .12, -h * .38, w * .43, h * .38, 3);
   520	      ctx.fill();
   521	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(14,34,48,.65)';
   522	      ctx.fillRect(-w * .4, h * .28, w * .18, 4);
   523	      ctx.fillRect(w * .22, h * .28, w * .18, 4);
   524	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffda73';
   525	      ctx.fillRect(vehicle.speed > 0 ? w * .35 : -w * .43, -3, 4, 6);
   526	      ctx.restore();
   527	    }
   528	
   529	    function drawRiver(row, y) {
   530	      const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, y + ROW_H);
   531	      gradient.addColorStop(0, colors.water2);
   532	      gradient.addColorStop(1, colors.water);
   533	      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   534	      ctx.fillRect(0, y, BASE_W, ROW_H);
   535	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(193,251,255,.18)';
   536	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   537	      for (let line = 0; line < 3; line++) {
   538	        ctx.beginPath();
   539	        for (let x = -12; x < BASE_W + 18; x += 14) {
   540	          const waveY = y + 11 + line * 21 + Math.sin(x * .08 + elapsed * .002 + line) * 2.5;
   541	          if (x === -12) ctx.moveTo(x, waveY); else ctx.lineTo(x, waveY);
   542	        }
   543	        ctx.stroke();
   544	      }
   545	      for (const support of row.supports) {
   546	        const x = wrap(support.x + support.speed * elapsed * .001);
   547	        const sinking = support.pad && Math.sin(elapsed * .00155 + support.phase) > .78;
   548	        drawSupport(x, y + ROW_H / 2 + Math.sin(elapsed * .002 + support.phase) * 2, support, sinking);
   549	        if (x < support.width) drawSupport(x + BASE_W, y + ROW_H / 2, support, sinking);
   550	        if (x > BASE_W - support.width) drawSupport(x - BASE_W, y + ROW_H / 2, support, sinking);
   551	      }
   552	    }
   553	
   554	    function drawSupport(x, y, support, sinking) {
   555	      ctx.save();
   556	      ctx.translate(x, y + (sinking ? 7 : 0));
   557	      ctx.globalAlpha = sinking ? .25 : 1;
   558	      if (support.pad) {
   559	        ctx.fillStyle = '#91d36d';
   560	        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(177,255,116,.33)';
   561	        ctx.shadowBlur = 9;
   562	        ctx.beginPath();
   563	        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, support.width / 2, 15, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   564	        ctx.fill();
   565	        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   566	        ctx.strokeStyle = '#3b875c';
   567	        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   568	        ctx.beginPath();
   569	        ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(support.width * .25, -7);
   570	        ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(-support.width * .28, 7);
   571	        ctx.stroke();
   572	      } else {
   573	        ctx.fillStyle = '#8f6044';
   574	        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(50,28,20,.4)';
   575	        ctx.shadowBlur = 7;
   576	        roundedRect(-support.width / 2, -12, support.width, 24, 9);
   577	        ctx.fill();
   578	        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   579	        ctx.strokeStyle = '#c28a58';
   580	        ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   581	        ctx.beginPath();
   582	        ctx.moveTo(-support.width * .25, -9); ctx.lineTo(-support.width * .3, 9);
   583	        ctx.moveTo(support.width * .12, -9); ctx.lineTo(support.width * .07, 9);
   584	        ctx.stroke();
   585	      }
   586	      ctx.restore();
   587	    }
   588	
   589	    function drawRail(row, y) {
   590	      ctx.fillStyle = colors.rail;
   591	      ctx.fillRect(0, y, BASE_W, ROW_H);
   592	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.05)';
   593	      ctx.fillRect(0, y + 5, BASE_W, 2);
   594	      ctx.fillRect(0, y + ROW_H - 7, BASE_W, 2);
   595	      for (const railY of [y + 16, y + 46]) {
   596	        ctx.fillStyle = '#151b27';
   597	        ctx.fillRect(0, railY - 5, BASE_W, 10);
   598	        ctx.strokeStyle = '#bcc3bc';
   599	        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   600	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, railY); ctx.lineTo(BASE_W, railY); ctx.stroke();
   601	        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(231,198,117,.42)';
   602	        ctx.lineWidth = 4;
   603	        ctx.setLineDash([8, 18]);
   604	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, railY - 8); ctx.lineTo(BASE_W, railY - 8); ctx.stroke();
   605	        ctx.setLineDash([]);
   606	      }
   607	      const train = trainInfo(row, elapsed);
   608	      for (const x of [22, BASE_W - 22]) {
   609	        ctx.fillStyle = train.warning ? '#ff715f' : '#45605b';
   610	        ctx.shadowColor = train.warning ? '#ff5d4e' : 'transparent';
   611	        ctx.shadowBlur = train.warning ? 13 : 0;
   612	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y + ROW_H / 2, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   613	        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   614	      }
   615	      if (train.active) drawTrain(train.x, y + ROW_H / 2, row.train.direction);
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    function drawTrain(x, y, direction) {
   619	      for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {
   620	        const cx = x + i * 54;
   620	        const cx = x + i * 54;
   621	        ctx.save();
   622	        ctx.translate(cx, y);
   623	        ctx.fillStyle = '#7c5367';
   624	        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,102,121,.4)';
   625	        ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   626	        roundedRect(-29, -16, 58, 32, 6); ctx.fill();
   627	        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   628	        ctx.fillStyle = '#f2ba69';
   629	        ctx.fillRect(-20, -7, 10, 7); ctx.fillRect(5, -7, 10, 7);
   630	        ctx.fillStyle = direction > 0 ? '#ffda75' : '#ff6e68';
   631	        ctx.fillRect(direction > 0 ? 21 : -25, 5, 4, 5);
   632	        ctx.restore();
   633	      }
   634	    }
   635	
   636	    function drawCoin(row, y) {
   637	      if (!row.coin || row.coin.collected) return;
   638	      const x = cellX(row.coin.col);
   639	      const bob = Math.sin(elapsed * .006 + row.coin.phase) * 3;
   640	      ctx.save();
   641	      ctx.translate(x, y + ROW_H / 2 + bob);
   642	      ctx.rotate(Math.sin(elapsed * .004 + row.coin.phase) * .16);
   643	      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,214,104,.85)';
   644	      ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   645	      ctx.fillStyle = colors.yellow;
   646	      ctx.beginPath();
   647	      ctx.moveTo(0, -11); ctx.lineTo(8, -3); ctx.lineTo(6, 8); ctx.lineTo(0, 12);
   648	      ctx.lineTo(-6, 8); ctx.lineTo(-8, -3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   649	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   650	      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3ae';
   651	      ctx.fillRect(-2, -5, 4, 9);
   652	      ctx.restore();
   653	    }
   654	
   655	    function drawWorld() {
   656	      const topRow = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cameraRow - (PLAYER_ANCHOR - TOP_H) / ROW_H) - 2);
   657	      const bottomRow = Math.ceil(cameraRow + (BASE_H - TOP_H) / ROW_H) + 2;
   658	      ensureRows(bottomRow + 8);
   659	      ctx.save();
   660	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, TOP_H, BASE_W, BASE_H - TOP_H); ctx.clip();
   661	      for (let i = topRow; i <= bottomRow; i++) {
   662	        const row = makeRow(i);
   663	        const y = rowScreenY(i);
   664	        if (row.type === 'median') drawMedian(row, y);
   665	        else if (row.type === 'road') drawRoad(row, y);
   666	        else if (row.type === 'river') drawRiver(row, y);
   667	        else drawRail(row, y);
   668	        drawCoin(row, y);
   669	      }
   670	      ctx.restore();
   671	    }
   672	
   673	    function drawPlayer() {
   674	      const hop = player.hop;
   675	      const t = hop ? Math.min(1, hop.t) : 0;
   676	      const lift = hop ? Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 13 : 0;
   677	      const x = player.x;
   678	      const y = rowScreenY(player.visualRow) + ROW_H / 2 - lift;
   679	      const squash = hop ? 1 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * .12 : 1;
   680	      ctx.save();
   681	      ctx.translate(x, y);
   682	      ctx.scale(1 / squash, squash);
   683	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.34)';
   684	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 19, 19, 6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   685	      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(116,243,176,.72)';
   686	      ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
   687	      ctx.fillStyle = colors.mint;
   688	      roundedRect(-16, -17, 32, 32, 10); ctx.fill();
   689	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   690	      ctx.fillStyle = '#16352f';
   691	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-6, -3, 3.6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.arc(6, -3, 3.6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   692	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ecfff1';
   693	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-5, -4, 1.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.arc(7, -4, 1.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   694	      ctx.fillStyle = '#4bc58f';
   695	      roundedRect(-10, 8, 20, 5, 3); ctx.fill();
   696	      ctx.restore();
   697	    }
   698	
   699	    function drawEagle() {
   700	      if (!eagle) return;
   701	      const t = Math.min(1, eagle.t / 1.65);
   702	      const x = eagle.side > 0 ? lerp(-70, player.x, t) : lerp(BASE_W + 70, player.x, t);
   703	      const y = rowScreenY(player.row) + ROW_H / 2 - 130 * Math.sin(t * Math.PI) - 20;
   704	      ctx.save();
   705	      ctx.translate(x, y);
   706	      ctx.rotate(eagle.side > 0 ? .12 : -.12);
   707	      ctx.fillStyle = '#d5e4d3';
   708	      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(217,255,226,.45)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   709	      ctx.beginPath();
   710	      ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-17, -19, -45, -8); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-28, 7, -7, 8);
   711	      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-4, 20, 0, 25); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(4, 20, 7, 8);
   712	      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(28, 7, 45, -8); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(17, -19, 0, 0); ctx.fill();
   713	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   714	      ctx.fillStyle = '#f4ca6d';
   715	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(eagle.side > 0 ? 14 : -14, -2); ctx.lineTo(eagle.side > 0 ? 26 : -26, 2); ctx.lineTo(eagle.side > 0 ? 14 : -14, 6); ctx.fill();
   716	      ctx.restore();
   717	    }
   718	
   719	    function drawParticles() {
   720	      for (const p of particles) {
   721	        const alpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.max);
   722	        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
   723	        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
   724	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size * alpha, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   725	      }
   726	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   727	    }
   728	
   729	    function drawButton(button, symbol, active) {
   730	      ctx.save();
   731	      ctx.globalAlpha = active ? .86 : .48;
   732	      ctx.fillStyle = active ? 'rgba(116,243,176,.24)' : 'rgba(9,18,31,.68)';
   733	      ctx.strokeStyle = active ? 'rgba(192,255,224,.58)' : 'rgba(192,255,224,.17)';
   734	      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   735	      roundedRect(button.x, button.y, button.w, button.h, 14); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   736	      ctx.fillStyle = active ? colors.mint2 : 'rgba(220,255,236,.75)';
   737	      ctx.font = '700 24px system-ui, sans-serif';
   738	      ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   739	      ctx.fillText(symbol, button.x + button.w / 2, button.y + button.h / 2 - 1);
   740	      ctx.restore();
   741	    }
   742	
   743	    function drawHUD() {
   744	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,13,25,.93)';
   745	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, BASE_W, TOP_H);

   798	      const dy = shake ? (hash(elapsed * 17) - .5) * shake : 0;

   814	        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, BASE_W, BASE_H);
   815	      }

   817	
   818	    function frame(now) {
   819	      const dt = Math.min(.034, Math.max(0, (now - lastTime) / 1000 || 0));
   820	      lastTime = now;
   821	      update(dt);
   822	      render();
   823	      requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   824	    }
   825	
   826	    function resize() {
   827	      const viewportW = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth);
   828	      const viewportH = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight);
   829	      cssScale = Math.min(viewportW / BASE_W, viewportH / BASE_H);
   830	      const drawW = BASE_W * cssScale;
   831	      const drawH = BASE_H * cssScale;
   832	      const deviceScale = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   833	      const cappedScale = Math.min(deviceScale, 960 / Math.max(drawW, drawH));
   834	      renderScale = Math.max(.5, cappedScale);
   835	      canvas.style.width = drawW + 'px';
   836	      canvas.style.height = drawH + 'px';
   837	      canvas.style.left = ((viewportW - drawW) / 2) + 'px';
   838	      canvas.style.top = ((viewportH - drawH) / 2) + 'px';
   839	      canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(drawW * renderScale));
   840	      canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(drawH * renderScale));
   841	      ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / BASE_W, 0, 0, canvas.height / BASE_H, 0, 0);
   842	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
   843	    }
   844	
   845	    function pointFromEvent(event) {
   846	      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   847	      return {
   848	        x: (event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width * BASE_W,
   849	        y: (event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height * BASE_H
   850	      };
   850	      };
   851	    }
   852	
   853	    function directionAt(x, y) {
   854	      for (const key of Object.keys(buttons)) {
   855	        const b = buttons[key];
   856	        if (x >= b.x - 8 && x <= b.x + b.w + 8 && y >= b.y - 8 && y <= b.y + b.h + 8) return b;
   857	      }
   858	      return null;
   859	    }
   860	
   861	    function handlePointerDown(event) {
   862	      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' && event.button !== 0) return;
   863	      event.preventDefault();
   864	      startAudio();
   865	      if (state === 'failed') {
   866	        resetGame();
   867	        pointer = null;
   868	        return;
   869	      }
   870	      const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   871	      pointer = { id: event.pointerId, x: point.x, y: point.y };
   872	      try { canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { /* Safari may not expose capture. */ }
   873	    }
   874	
   875	    function handlePointerUp(event) {
   876	      if (!pointer || pointer.id !== event.pointerId) return;
   877	      event.preventDefault();
   878	      const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   879	      const dx = point.x - pointer.x;
   880	      const dy = point.y - pointer.y;
   881	      const distanceMoved = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   882	      pointer = null;
   883	      if (distanceMoved > 22) {
   884	        if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) attemptHop(dx > 0 ? 1 : -1, 0);
   885	        else attemptHop(0, dy < 0 ? 1 : -1);
   886	        return;
   887	      }
   888	      const button = directionAt(point.x, point.y);
   889	      if (button) attemptHop(button.dx, button.dy);
   890	    }
   891	
   892	    function handlePointerCancel(event) {
   893	      if (pointer && pointer.id === event.pointerId) pointer = null;
   894	    }
   895	
   896	    function handleKey(event) {
   897	      const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   898	      const direction = {
   899	        arrowup: [0, 1], w: [0, 1],
   900	        arrowdown: [0, -1], s: [0, -1],
   901	        arrowleft: [-1, 0], a: [-1, 0],
   902	        arrowright: [1, 0], d: [1, 0]
   903	      }[key];
   904	      if (direction || key === ' ' || key === 'enter') event.preventDefault();
   905	      if (state === 'failed' && (direction || key === ' ' || key === 'enter')) {
   906	        resetGame();
   907	        return;
   908	      }
   909	      if (direction) attemptHop(direction[0], direction[1]);
   910	    }
   911	
   912	    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { passive: false });
   913	    canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp, { passive: false });
   914	    canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerCancel, { passive: false });
   915	    canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   916	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKey, { passive: false });
   917	    window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   918	    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', resize, { passive: true });
   919	
   920	    resize();
   921	    resetGame();
   922	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   923	  })();
   924	  </script>
   925	</body>
   926	</html>

The audit is complete at source level. I found no syntax errors in the JavaScript entrypoints; the remaining checks are line-reference reconciliation and consolidating clean checks versus actionable findings. The main actionable classes are orientation overlays that don’t pause simulation, single-record touch state in several games, sub-48px canvas/UI targets, and a few bounded/unbounded mobile performance concerns.
All 10 source trees have been reviewed and line references cross-checked; final report is ready.
  ✓ Inventory the 10 game codebases and identify entrypoints/input/render/state files
  ✓ Review each game against controls, reset/win, viewport/orientation, performance, and keyboard criteria
  ✓ Cross-check line references and produce concise per-game findings and verdicts
codex
Read-only source QA; no files changed. Endless loops were accepted per spec.

### tubeshock

- MEDIUM/0.98 `tubeshock/game.js:588` — A second touch overwrites the active `pointerId`, so multi-touch can steal/interrupt rim control.
- CLEAN — Surge/restart targets are ≥48px; reset, fixed no-scroll viewport, performance, and keyboard fallback are sound.

Verdict: FAIL — multi-touch safety issue only.

### stacklock

- MEDIUM/0.99 `stacklock/game.js:466` — One global pointer record lets a second touch overwrite the first and misclassify the eventual swipe/tap.
- LOW/0.99 `stacklock/style.css:69` — Marathon/Sprint buttons are only 32px tall.
- CLEAN — Core controls are 56px; reset, viewport, performance, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: FAIL — multi-touch and undersized mode controls.

### mazerunner-prime

- HIGH/0.99 `mazerunner-prime/game.js:92` — Landscape rotate overlay appears, but the simulation continues underneath it.
- MEDIUM/0.99 `mazerunner-prime/game.js:580` — Swipe tracking has no pointer identity; multi-touch can overwrite and consume another gesture.
- MEDIUM/0.97 `mazerunner-prime/game.js:371` — Restart does not clear elapsed time, particles, or shake state, allowing carryover effects.
- CLEAN — Swipe queue, 48px direction pads, reset reachability, viewport, and keyboard fallback otherwise pass.

Verdict: FAIL — orientation pause, multi-touch, and incomplete reset.

### corridor-crawl

- MEDIUM/0.98 `corridor-crawl/game.js:29` — At 390px width, map tiles are about 43px, below the 48px touch-target requirement.
- MEDIUM/0.99 `corridor-crawl/game.js:776` — Touch handling stores one global press and uses `changedTouches[0]`; simultaneous touches can overwrite/end another action.
- LOW/0.98 `corridor-crawl/game.js:706` — Mute hit area is 46×46px.
- CLEAN — Item slots, long-press inspect, win/death/restart reset, viewport, performance, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: FAIL — undersized map/mute targets and multi-touch issue.

### spire-ascent

- MEDIUM/0.99 `spire-ascent/game.js:140` — Touch handling ignores touch identity; multiple touches can trigger unintended jump/dash sequences.
- LOW/0.98 `spire-ascent/game.js:75` — Reset does not clear `lastTap`; a quick first tap after restart can be misread as a double-tap dash.
- CLEAN — Full-surface jump, restart/fail flow, portrait viewport fit, keyboard fallback, and performance pass.

Verdict: FAIL — multi-touch and narrow reset-state issue.

### bulwark

- MEDIUM/0.99 `bulwark/game.js:31` — Grid cells are roughly 35px wide at 390px viewport width, below the 48px target requirement.
- MEDIUM/0.99 `bulwark/game.js:978` — Each touch is acted on immediately without pointer identity; multi-touch can cause multiple builds/selections.
- LOW/0.99 `bulwark/game.js:763` — Sound control hit area is 48×46px.
- LOW/0.94 `bulwark/game.js:293` — Every tower scans every creep each frame; chain targeting adds further nested scans at higher waves.
- CLEAN — Build/upgrade/wall/GO controls, win/lose/restart, viewport, and keyboard fallback otherwise pass.

Verdict: FAIL — touch-target, multi-touch, and scaling-performance issues.

### dirt-rocket

- HIGH/0.99 `dirt-rocket/game.js:23` — Portrait mode only displays the rotate overlay; physics still advances in `update()` while the overlay blocks the canvas.
- CLEAN — Landscape controls, per-pointer multi-touch handling, ≥48px controls, track reset/finish, viewport, performance, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: FAIL — mandatory orientation pause is missing.

### vector-storm

- LOW/0.92 `vector-storm/game.js:258` — Bullet/well processing and enemy collision both scale as bullet-count × enemy-count; bounded but a mobile performance red flag.
- LOW/0.98 `vector-storm/game.js:40` — Full reset clears sticks but not held keyboard state, so a key held through game-over can carry into the next run.
- CLEAN — Twin-stick pointer IDs, bomb hit area, fail/restart, viewport, no-scroll behavior, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: PASS WITH LOW-RISK FINDINGS.

### serpentine

- MEDIUM/0.99 `serpentine/game.js:646` — Single global touch state with `changedTouches[0]` allows multi-touch gestures to overwrite or double-process turns.
- MEDIUM/0.97 `serpentine/game.js:258` — Collision/planning and rendering repeatedly scan every trail point for every snake; the 10.5-second trail window is a mobile hot path.
- CLEAN — Swipe coverage, restart flow, viewport, no scroll, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: FAIL — multi-touch and trail-scaling performance issues.

### crossfire-hopper

- HIGH/0.99 `crossfire-hopper/index.html:51` — Landscape rotate hint is displayed, but the game loop continues running underneath it.
- MEDIUM/0.97 `crossfire-hopper/index.html:240` — Generated rows are stored in a `Map` indefinitely with no culling; endless play causes unbounded memory growth.
- LOW/0.99 `crossfire-hopper/index.html:871` — Only one pointer record is retained, so concurrent touches cancel/lose each other.
- CLEAN — Swipe/arrows, 48px buttons with padding, fail/restart, 390px fit, no scroll, and keyboard fallback pass.

Verdict: FAIL — orientation pause and unbounded row retention.
